| M192b | The elementals murmur of Ramuh's return to the forest. | *Lord of Levin* · 2.3 · main | "The Elder Seedseer informs us that the elementals themselves murmur of the Lord of Levin's return to the forest." | #192 |
| M192c | Yda expected the summoning, just not so soon. | *Lord of Levin* · 2.3 · main | "Oh, it didn't. I was pretty sure it was going to happen─just not this soon." | #192 |
| M192d | Papalymo thinks the refugee influx alarmed the sylphs into hastening the summoning. | *Lord of Levin* · 2.3 · main | "'Tis like that the sylphs perceived this panicked migration as yet another invasion, and quickened their efforts to summon their god." | #192 |
| M192e | The sylphs first summoned Ramuh after the Calamity, when the Garleans came through the forest. | *Lord of Levin* · 2.3 · main | "They already summoned Ramuh once right after the Calamity, but that was because the Garleans came tramping through the forest..." | #192 |
| M192f | The Elder Seedseer asks you to represent Gridania in a negotiation with Ramuh. | *Lord of Levin* · 2.3 · main | "I would ask therefore that you represent us in this most delicate of negotiations." | #192 |
| M192g | Ramuh is no raging avatar of destruction. | *Lord of Levin* · 2.3 · main | "Unlike the other primals you have encountered, Lord Ramuh is no raging avatar of destruction." | #192 |
| M192h | Minfilia warns the meeting may not be amicable. | *Lord of Levin* · 2.3 · main | "Steel yourself for the worst. While I hope for a peaceful resolution to this matter, experience suggests that your meeting with the Lord of Levin will prove less than amicable." | #192 |
| M193a | Ramuh says the sylphs turned to him for succor, and pronounces sentence on humankind. | *Levin an Impression* · 2.3 · main | "Thus did they turn to me for succor. The sentence I pronounce upon thy kind is just." | #193 |
| M193b | Ramuh sees something more in you than the Bringer of Light. | *Levin an Impression* · 2.3 · main | "Thou standest apart from thy kin. Thou art the Bringer of Light, aye...but there is something more in thee..." | #193 |
| M193c | You defeat Ramuh and earn the right to champion peace in the Black Shroud. | *Levin an Impression* · 2.3 · main | "You have defeated the Lord of Levin, and thus earned the right to champion the cause of peace in [[the Black Shroud]]." | #193 |
| M193d | Ramuh judges you a worthy champion and charges you with excising the sin in man's heart. | *Levin an Impression* · 2.3 · main | "And I judge thee a worthy champion. The task of excising the sin that hath taken root in man's heart is thine." | #193 |
| M193e | Urianger glimpses a possible chink in the Ascians' armor. | *Levin an Impression* · 2.3 · main | "If I mistake not, it may yet prove a chink in the eternal armor of the Ascians." | #193 |
| M193f | A sylph guide fears the fate of his tempered brethren. | *Levin an Impression* · 2.3 · main | "for fear of suffering the same fate as his tempered brethren." | #193 |
| M194a | The Elder Seedseer sees the Alliance straining under self-interest. | *What Little Gods Are Made Of* · 2.3 · main | "Our shared struggle against the Empire should have served to seal our union, yet the ties which bind the Alliance strain under the weight of gross self-interest." | #194 |
| M194b | Urianger names the white-clad Ascian as Elidibus. | *What Little Gods Are Made Of* · 2.3 · main | "I speak of the Ascian clad in white, Elidibus." | #194 |
| M194c | To the Ascians, physical destruction is only a temporary inconvenience. | *What Little Gods Are Made Of* · 2.3 · main | "that the Ascians are eternal beings, to whom physical destruction is as a temporary inconvenience." | #194 |
| M194d | The ascended Sahagin had no Crystal of Darkness to flee by, so Leviathan consumed him. | *What Little Gods Are Made Of* · 2.3 · main | "The Sahagin ascended to an immortal state, but he did not possess a Crystal of Darkness through which to flee this realm. Thus was he consumed by Leviathan." | #194 |
| M194e | A Crystal of Darkness, used to control Thancred, may be a gateway to that place. | *What Little Gods Are Made Of* · 2.3 · main | "The Ascians took control of Thancred by means of a Crystal of Darkness─an artifact which, if our theories are correct, serves as a gateway to the place I have just described." | #194 |
| M194f | The Scions lack a way to trap and destroy an Ascian soul. | *What Little Gods Are Made Of* · 2.3 · main | "At present, we lack a viable means to entrap and extinguish an Ascian soul." | #194 |
| M194g | Urianger departs to confer with the sages of Sharlayan. | *What Little Gods Are Made Of* · 2.3 · main | "I will depart at once to convene with the sages of Sharlayan." | #194 |
| M194h | Elidibus says the gifted must be nurtured to forge the final key. | *What Little Gods Are Made Of* · 2.3 · main | "All that remains is to nurture the strength of the gifted─to forge the final key." | #194 |
| M194i | Lahabrea says the one true god shall come again. | *What Little Gods Are Made Of* · 2.3 · main | "That which was shattered shall be made anew...and the one true god shall come again." | #194 |
| M194j | A Sharlayan survey party seeks the fate of the Students of Baldesion. | *What Little Gods Are Made Of* · 2.3 · main | "Even now, a Sharlayan survey party seeketh to ascertain the fate of the Students of Baldesion." | #194 |
| M195a | A letter thanks the Scions for quelling the primal threat. | *Guardian of Eorzea* · 2.3 · main | "Minfilia informs you that a letter has arrived expressing gratitude for [[the Scions]]' efforts in quelling the [[primal]] threat." | #195 |
| M195b | It names every defeated primal, ending with Ramuh. | *Guardian of Eorzea* · 2.3 · main | "It mentions, in particular, the names of all of the defeated primals─Ifrit, Titan, Garuda, Good King Moggle Mog XII, Leviathan, and now Ramuh." | #195 |
| M195c | Minfilia expects the Ascians to sow chaos until they are stopped. | *Guardian of Eorzea* · 2.3 · main | "The Ascians may be relied upon to sow chaos in this realm until such time as they are forced to stop." | #195 |
| M195d | Alphinaud wants a new organization that works beyond nations and borders. | *Guardian of Eorzea* · 2.3 · main | "is a new, expanded organization that operates beyond the limitations of nations and borders." | #195 |
| M195e | Alphinaud sought to understand why his grandfather gave his life. | *Guardian of Eorzea* · 2.3 · main | "I sought to grasp the reason why Grandfather willingly sacrificed himself to protect it." | #195 |
| M195f | Alphinaud vows to take up the mantle of guardian of Eorzea. | *Guardian of Eorzea* · 2.3 · main | "I, too, will endeavor to take up the mantle of guardian of Eorzea, and champion these people who toil towards a better world." | #195 |
| M196a | Alphinaud plans a realm-wide Grand Company. | *Recruiting the Realm* · 2.3 · main | "Alphinaud lays before you the plan to establish a realm-wide [[Grand Company]]." | #196 |
| M196b | The trial company is placed under the Antecedent's authority to ease fears of misuse. | *Recruiting the Realm* · 2.3 · main | "I mean to assuage fears of its misuse by placing the fledgling organization under the authority of the Antecedent." | #196 |
| M196c | Riol, once of the Company of Heroes, joins. | *Recruiting the Realm* · 2.3 · main | "a member of the now defunct [[Company of Heroes]]." | #196 |
| M196d | Much of the company's funding came from the Syndicate. | *Recruiting the Realm* · 2.3 · main | "Does it not concern you that a significant proportion of its financial backing came from the coffers of the Syndicate?" | #196 |
| M196e | Under Ul'dahn law there is no evidence to convict Teledji. | *Recruiting the Realm* · 2.3 · main | "as Ul'dahn law now stands, we lack the evidence to convict him of any crime." | #196 |
| M196f | The sultana blesses the trial company. | *Recruiting the Realm* · 2.3 · main | "As you have no doubt surmised, the sultana also graced my trial company with her blessings." | #196 |
| M196g | Every coin the Syndicate gave was traced and recorded. | *Recruiting the Realm* · 2.3 · main | "Thus was the origin of every coin of their contribution carefully scrutinized and recorded." | #196 |
| M196h | Laurentius, whose crimes you exposed, joins. | *Recruiting the Realm* · 2.3 · main | "It is I, Laurentius, the man whose crimes you exposed." | #196 |
| M197a | The trial company's arrival strains Slafborn and Revenant's Toll. | *Heretical Harassment* · 2.3 · main | "Minfilia expresses concern that the bustling chaos surrounding the official commencement of [[Alphinaud]]'s trial company has placed undue stress on [[Slafborn]] and his efforts to supervise the growing population of Revenant's Toll." | #197 |
| M197b | Coerthan heretics robbed a supply shipment near the Mor Dhona border. | *Heretical Harassment* · 2.3 · main | "a band of [[Coerthas|Coerthan]] heretics near the border to [[Mor Dhona]]." | #197 |
| M197c | Slafborn says heretics make it more than theft; Ishgard must be told. | *Heretical Harassment* · 2.3 · main | "If heretics are involved, then this is like to be more than a case of simple thievery. Ishgard will need to be informed." | #197 |
| M197d | You go to Coerthas to help House Fortemps track the stolen shipment. | *Heretical Harassment* · 2.3 · main | "Slafborn asks that you travel to Coerthas and aid [[House Fortemps]] in tracking down the stolen shipment meant for the frontier hands of Revenant's Toll." | #197 |
| M197e | Haurchefant was about to open an urgent missive from Revenant's Toll. | *Heretical Harassment* · 2.3 · main | "I was, in fact, on the verge of opening an urgent missive from Revenant's Toll, and I suspect the timing of your arrival is no coincidence..." | #197 |
| M198a | Haurchefant sends you to Lord Drillemont at Whitebrim. | *When the Cold Sets In* · 2.3 · main | "Reasoning that your best chance to track down the supply thieves lies in [[Whitebrim]] and its heretic-infested environs, the garrison commander suggests you bring the matter before Lord [[Drillemont]]." | #198 |
| M198b | The heretics' new unity is credited to their leader, the Lady Iceheart. | *When the Cold Sets In* · 2.3 · main | "This new unity of purpose we attribute to their leader─the Lady “Iceheart.”" | #198 |
| M198c | No one knows Iceheart's birthname. | *When the Cold Sets In* · 2.3 · main | "we have yet to learn so much as her birthname." | #198 |
| M198d | The heretics revere Iceheart and would die rather than betray her. | *When the Cold Sets In* · 2.3 · main | "the heretics speak of Iceheart in reverent tones, and would gladly embrace death rather than betray her." | #198 |
| M198e | Drillemont finds the heretics somehow changed. | *When the Cold Sets In* · 2.3 · main | "But 'tis not merely their newfound organization that confounds us: the heretics themselves seem somehow ''changed''." | #198 |
| M198f | The heretics spoke of returning to Snowcloak. | *When the Cold Sets In* · 2.3 · main | "Before darkness claimed me, however, I distinctly heard the words, “return to Snowcloak.”" | #198 |
| M198g | A knight glimpses a woman on the cliff who may be Iceheart. | *When the Cold Sets In* · 2.3 · main | "Could it be Iceheart herself come to sneer at our folly!?" | #198 |
| M198h | Haurchefant hands the matter to the Temple Knights. | *When the Cold Sets In* · 2.3 · main | "I am bound by duty to inform the Temple Knights and request that they bring this matter to its conclusion." | #198 |
| M198i | Haurchefant sends a second shipment to Revenant's Toll. | *When the Cold Sets In* · 2.3 · main | "I have already made arrangements for a second shipment to be sent to Revenant's Toll." | #198 |
| M199a | Tataru is buried in the ceremony's small details. | *Brave New Companions* · 2.3 · main | "He is concerned, however, that [[Tataru]] yet seems buried under a mountain of small details." | #199 |
| M199b | Ilberd will lead the recruits as captain. | *Brave New Companions* · 2.3 · main | "I don't believe we've met. I am Ilberd, and I will have the honor of leading my fellow recruits as captain." | #199 |
| M199c | Alphinaud declares the recruits Crystal Braves. | *Brave New Companions* · 2.3 · main | "From this moment forth, I declare you Crystal Braves!" | #199 |
| M199d | Hydaelyn has spoken to neither you nor Minfilia since the final battle with the Empire. | *Brave New Companions* · 2.3 · main | "the Antecedent realizes that [[Hydaelyn]] has spoken to neither of you since the final battle with [[Garlean Empire|the Empire]]." | #199 |
| M199e | Minfilia's friend Krile survived the loss of the Isle of Val, though she has not woken. | *Brave New Companions* · 2.3 · main | "Truly!? Krile is alive!? Thank the Twelve... … When I learned of the loss of the Isle of Val, I dared not hope that my friend had survived. Yet by some miracle, it would seem she has. She's still to regain consciousness, it is true, but better that than death." | #199 |
| M199f | The isle's destruction came close to Ultima's magnitude; Minfilia wonders if the Ascians are to blame. | *Brave New Companions* · 2.3 · main | "It appears the aetheric disturbance which accompanied the isle's destruction is of a magnitude alarmingly close to that of Ultima. Could the Ascians be responsible for this devastation as well?" | #199 |
| M199g | Archbishop Thordan holds a ceremony with the Temple Knights, with Lahabrea and Elidibus behind him. | *Brave New Companions* · 2.3 · main | "The scene shifts to [[Archbishop Thordan VII]] conducting a ceremony with the Temple Knights. [[Lahabrea]] and [[Elidibus]] are behind him." | #199 |
| M199h | Minfilia prays that Louisoix still watches over them. | *Brave New Companions* · 2.3 · main | "Louisoix... I pray you yet watch over us..." | #199 |
| M200a | Minfilia asks you to help Alphinaud run the newly formed Crystal Braves. | *Traitor in the Midst* · 2.4 · main | "Conscious of the many practical difficulties facing the newly formed Crystal Braves, Minfilia asks that you place yourself at Alphinaud's disposal." | #200 |
| M200b | Ilberd reveals that a Garlean spy known as the Ivy has infiltrated one of the Grand Companies. | *Traitor in the Midst* · 2.4 · main | "Ilberd reveals that a [[Garlean]] spy known as [[the Ivy]] has infiltrated one of the [[Grand Companies]]." | #200 |
| M200c | A suspect in the Immortal Flames proved to be only one of the many in the spy's employ. | *Traitor in the Midst* · 2.4 · main | "A suspect was identified among [[the Immortal Flames]], but the man proved to be but one of many in the agent's employ." | #200 |
| M200d | The Ivy's conspirators are the vines by which the spy learns secrets without being exposed. | *Traitor in the Midst* · 2.4 · main | "These conspirators are the vines by which he learns our secrets, all without exposing himself." | #200 |
| M200e | Raubahn and Ilberd have been friends and rivals since before the fall of Ala Mhigo. | *Traitor in the Midst* · 2.4 · main | "You learn that Raubahn and Ilberd have been friends and rivals since before the fall of [[Ala Mhigo]]." | #200 |
| M200f | Raubahn is a Flame General. | *Traitor in the Midst* · 2.4 · main | "Flame General... You wear the mantle well, old friend." | #200 |
| M200g | Raubahn wonders whether the Monetarists have been in league with the Empire from the first. | *Traitor in the Midst* · 2.4 · main | "Could it be that the Monetarists have been in league with the Empire from the first?" | #200 |
| M200h | Ilberd fears the Empire will soon resume its march on Eorzea. | *Traitor in the Midst* · 2.4 · main | "Garlemald's war of succession nears its end, and it is feared that the Empire will soon resume its march on Eorzea." | #200 |
| M201a | A unit of Crystal Braves was ambushed by Garlean forces. | *Back and Fourth* · 2.4 · main | "a unit of [[Crystal Braves]] was ambushed by Garlean forces during your absence." | #201 |
| M201b | The captured Brave is rescued with no further losses. | *Back and Fourth* · 2.4 · main | "Thanks to your aid, our comrade Rhesh is safe, and we suffered no further casualties." | #201 |
| M201c | An Ishgardian dignitary has granted Alphinaud an audience and asked for you. | *Back and Fourth* · 2.4 · main | "I have been granted an audience with an Ishgardian dignitary, you see, and the man expressly requested your presence." | #201 |
| M201d | Ilberd vows not to rest until the Ivy is caught. | *Back and Fourth* · 2.4 · main | "And it may yet take time, but I will not rest until we have identified and apprehended the Ivy." | #201 |
| M201e | Alianne of the 4th Unit waits near Castrum Centri. | *Back and Fourth* · 2.4 · main | "Alianne of the 4th lies in wait in the Tangle, near the entrance to Castrum Centri." | #201 |
| M201f | One of the ambushed unit's members was taken to Castrum Centri. | *Back and Fourth* · 2.4 · main | "one of the unit's members was captured and taken to [[Castrum Centri]]" | #201 |
| M202a | Ser Aymeric, lord commander of the Temple Knights, represents the Holy See. | *Coming to Terms* · 2.4 · main | "Alphinaud has been granted an audience with Ser Aymeric, lord commander of the [[Ishgard]]ian [[Temple Knights]], who will be acting as a representative of the [[Holy See]]." | #202 |
| M202b | The Holy See has no intention of rejoining the Eorzean Alliance. | *Coming to Terms* · 2.4 · main | "it becomes apparent that the Holy See has no intention of rejoining the [[Eorzean Alliance]]" | #202 |
| M202c | Ishgard's astrologians foretell Midgardsormr's return, and Aymeric wants the Scions to help watch the great wyrm's remains. | *Coming to Terms* · 2.4 · main | "You learn that the [[astrologian]]s of Ishgard have foretold the return of [[Midgardsormr]]─the fallen guardian of [[Silvertear Falls]]─and that Ser Aymeric wishes to enlist [[the Scions]]' aid in keeping watch over the great wyrm's remains." | #202 |
| M202d | In exchange, Aymeric will keep House Fortemps' aid shipments flowing to Revenant's Toll. | *Coming to Terms* · 2.4 · main | "In exchange, he will ensure that the shipments of aid from [[House Fortemps]] to [[Revenant's Toll]] will continue unabated." | #202 |
| M202e | Iceheart and her followers waylay the latest shipment. | *Coming to Terms* · 2.4 · main | "only to learn moments after that the latest shipment has been waylaid by [[Iceheart]] and her followers..." | #202 |
| M202f | Ishgard's forces are wholly committed to the conflict with the Dravanians. | *Coming to Terms* · 2.4 · main | "Even were that not the case, our forces are wholly committed to the Dravanian conflict." | #202 |
| M202h | The dragon star waxes bright, and there are whispers that it portends Midgardsormr's resurrection. | *Coming to Terms* · 2.4 · main | "The dragon star waxes unnaturally bright, and there are whispers that it portends the resurrection of Midgardsormr." | #202 |
| M202i | Since the Battle of Silvertear Skies, Ishgard has kept her neighbors at arm's length. | *Coming to Terms* · 2.4 · main | "Ever since the [[Battle of Silvertear Skies]], the city–state of Ishgard has kept her neighbors at arm's length." | #202 |
| M203a | In the ambush every knight was slain and every crate seized. | *The Intercession of Saints* · 2.4 · main | "'Twas an ambush─swift and ruthless. In an instant, every knight was slain, every crate seized." | #203 |
| M203b | Disguised as pilgrims, Iceheart and her brethren slaughtered the caravan's guards. | *The Intercession of Saints* · 2.4 · main | "Disguised as pilgrims, Iceheart and her brethren slaughtered the caravan's guards, and the young man only survived by feigning his own death." | #203 |
| M203c | Iceheart's people spoke of deliverance and resurrection. | *The Intercession of Saints* · 2.4 · main | "Iceheart's people spoke of deliverance and resurrection." | #203 |
| M203d | Ishgard calls Shiva a traitor to kith and kin who lay down with dragons. | *The Intercession of Saints* · 2.4 · main | "Shiva was a traitor to kith and kin─an apostate who ''lay down with dragons''." | #203 |
| M203e | Alphinaud concludes that Iceheart and her followers mean to summon Shiva as a beast tribe would a primal. | *The Intercession of Saints* · 2.4 · main | "Iceheart and her followers intend to ''summon'' Shiva, as a beast tribe would a primal!" | #203 |
| M203f | Alphinaud turns the whole order to preventing the summoning. | *The Intercession of Saints* · 2.4 · main | "Our entire order must turn its efforts to the prevention of this summoning." | #203 |
| M203g | Shiva is the heretics' patron saint. | *The Intercession of Saints* · 2.4 · main | "Wait─you say she is their ''patron'' saint?" | #203 |
| M203h | Iceheart is a silver-haired woman. | *The Intercession of Saints* · 2.4 · main | "Slowly she approached, her silver hair flowing in the wind like melting snow." | #203 |
| M204a | Drillemont's scouts lost the heretic pilgrims at Snowcloak. | *Strength in Unity* · 2.4 · main | "Alas, our scouts lost track of them at Snowcloak─that selfsame frozen wall which we speculated might conceal the heretics' hideaway." | #204 |
| M204b | Aymeric's knights join the hunt. | *Strength in Unity* · 2.4 · main | "I have heard enough. My knights will join the hunt." | #204 |
| M204c | Alphinaud summons a unit of Crystal Braves to Whitebrim Front. | *Strength in Unity* · 2.4 · main | "I took the liberty of summoning a unit of Crystal Braves to Whitebrim Front." | #204 |
| M204d | The Scions join the hunt for Iceheart. | *Strength in Unity* · 2.4 · main | "The Scions of the Seventh Dawn wish to join in the hunt for Iceheart?" | #204 |
| M205a | A covert check of Whitebrim Front finds one possible heretic. | *Dark Words, Dark Deeds* · 2.4 · main | "we identified only one possible heretic." | #205 |
| M205b | The heretics already know the movements of the Temple Knights and the Crystal Braves. | *Dark Words, Dark Deeds* · 2.4 · main | "the movements of the [[Temple Knights]] and the [[Crystal Braves]] are already known to them." | #205 |
| M205c | The suspect is detained. | *Dark Words, Dark Deeds* · 2.4 · main | "I shall have the man detained at once. His guilt is plain." | #205 |
| M205d | Drillemont means to root out the heretics. | *Dark Words, Dark Deeds* · 2.4 · main | "which means it is key to ''our'' plans that they be rooted out and put to the sword." | #205 |
| M206a | The knights of House Durendaire lie slain in Boulder Downs. | *First Blood* · 2.4 · main | "the knights of House Durendaire slain after what must have been a desperate battle with" | #206 |
| M206b | A recovered parchment is a timetable of the watches at Snowcloak. | *First Blood* · 2.4 · main | "The parchment you recovered is a timetable of the guardsmen's watches at our outpost in Snowcloak." | #206 |
| M206c | The traitor passed the heretics something of value. | *First Blood* · 2.4 · main | "it seems the traitor you followed managed to pass the heretics something of value after all" | #206 |
| M206d | The heretics may already be in position. | *First Blood* · 2.4 · main | "Of course, it will be changed ''now'', but the heretics may already be in position." | #206 |
| M207a | Aymeric had suspected the heretics were plotting an attack. | *The Path of the Righteous* · 2.4 · main | "Truth be told, we suspected the heretics might be plotting such an attack." | #207 |
| M207b | Alphinaud finds the entrance to Iceheart's lair among the tunnels. | *The Path of the Righteous* · 2.4 · main | "Amongst this warren of tunnels lies the entrance to Iceheart's lair!" | #207 |
| M207c | Iceheart was given the name Ysayle but earned the name Iceheart. | *The Path of the Righteous* · 2.4 · main | "I was given the name Ysayle, but I earned the name Iceheart." | #207 |
| M207d | Iceheart vows to end the war between dragon and man, no matter the cost. | *The Path of the Righteous* · 2.4 · main | "I will bring an end to this war between dragon and man─no matter the cost." | #207 |
| M207e | Iceheart escapes, but you drive the heretics from their lair. | *The Path of the Righteous* · 2.4 · main | "Though you failed to apprehend Iceheart, you have driven the heretics from their lair." | #207 |
| M207f | Minfilia and Urianger search House Fortemps' archives for word of Shiva. | *The Path of the Righteous* · 2.4 · main | "Minfilia and Urianger have been busy perusing the archives of House Fortemps, gathering what information they can on Shiva." | #207 |
| M208a | Minfilia is certain Shiva was a real, living woman. | *For the Greater Good* · 2.4 · main | "Shiva was real─a living, breathing woman─of that there can be no doubt." | #208 |
| M208b | Iceheart likely had the second aetheryte destroyed to keep anyone from following. | *For the Greater Good* · 2.4 · main | "I can only presume she had it destroyed to prevent us from following her." | #208 |
| M208c | The Garlean spy may have been helping Iceheart. | *For the Greater Good* · 2.4 · main | "it seems our favorite Garlean spy may have been providing assistance to Iceheart." | #208 |
| M208d | Ilberd and Alphinaud had sought the captured merchant in their hunt for the Ivy. | *For the Greater Good* · 2.4 · main | "they had been seeking the merchant in connection with their investigation into" | #208 |
| M208e | Yugiri offers to accompany the Scions until the investigation is complete. | *For the Greater Good* · 2.4 · main | "If you will allow it, I would accompany you until the investigation is complete." | #208 |
| M208f | Yugiri and her shinobi helped uncover the merchant's link to the heretics. | *For the Greater Good* · 2.4 · main | "Were it not for the efforts of Lady Yugiri and her shinobi, we might never have discovered this connection." | #208 |
| M208g | Yugiri's people long to strike back against the Empire. | *For the Greater Good* · 2.4 · main | "We long to strike back against the Empire and weaken their influence in this land─" | #208 |
| M208h | Yuyuhase leads the 3rd Unit Braves in pursuit of the heretics. | *For the Greater Good* · 2.4 · main | "the 3rd Unit Braves are already in pursuit. Yuyuhase leaves to join them" | #208 |
| M208i | A dead heretic's plan has them withdraw to the Observatorium. | *For the Greater Good* · 2.4 · main | "Withdraw in groups of three or less to the Observatorium." | #208 |
| M209a | A Flame at Revenant's Toll sold secrets to the merchant, who passed them to the heretics. | *Tendrils of Intrigue* · 2.4 · main | "had been selling secrets to the merchant you apprehended, who had in turn been passing information to the heretics." | #209 |
| M209b | The Ivy covered the trail with powers held only by high-ranking Flames. | *Tendrils of Intrigue* · 2.4 · main | "In a desperate bid to cover his tracks, the Ivy resorted to exercising certain administrative powers available only to high-ranking members of the Immortal Flames." | #209 |
| M209c | The Garlean war of succession ends with Varis, the late emperor's grandson, crowned. | *Tendrils of Intrigue* · 2.4 · main | "the crowning of [[Varis zos Galvus]], the [[Solus zos Galvus|late emperor]]'s grandson." | #209 |
| M209d | Varis tore the crown from his uncle's grasp. | *Tendrils of Intrigue* · 2.4 · main | "So, young Varis has torn the crown from his uncle's grasp, and taken his place at the head of the Empire." | #209 |
| M209e | Until another path is found, Iceheart's sanctum is out of reach. | *Tendrils of Intrigue* · 2.4 · main | "until another path to her sanctum is found, she will remain beyond our reach." | #209 |
| M209f | Ilberd says the Ivy has been unmasked. | *Tendrils of Intrigue* · 2.4 · main | "Doubtless the commander has informed you, but we have unmasked the Ivy." | #209 |
| M209g | The Alliance must entice Ishgard back into the fold. | *Tendrils of Intrigue* · 2.4 · main | "find a way to entice the long-estranged [[Ishgard]]ians back into the fold" | #209 |
| M209h | Kan-E-Senna opens the council on the change in the Garlean Empire. | *Tendrils of Intrigue* · 2.4 · main | "Change has come to the Garlean Empire, and we must discuss the implications." | #209 |
| M209i | The Alliance leaders convene at the Lotus Stand in Old Gridania. | *Tendrils of Intrigue* · 2.4 · main | "The council of Alliance leaders will convene at [[the Lotus Stand]] in [[Old Gridania]]." | #209 |
| M210a | Ilberd names Flame Marshal Eline, Raubahn's second-in-command, as the spy. | *Chasing Ivy* · 2.4 · main | "The spy we have been seeking all this time is none other than Flame Marshal Eline Roaille, Raubahn's second-in-command." | #210 |
| M210b | Eline is of Ishgardian birth, the highest-ranked foreigner in the Immortal Flames. | *Chasing Ivy* · 2.4 · main | "Of Ishgardian birth, she is the highest-ranked foreigner in the Immortal Flames" | #210 |
| M210c | Ilberd wonders whether the Ivy has a hold over the Monetarists. | *Chasing Ivy* · 2.4 · main | "Could it be that the Ivy has some hold over the Monetarists?" | #210 |
| M210d | Eline should have stayed at the Hall of Flames while Raubahn attended the council. | *Chasing Ivy* · 2.4 · main | "While Raubahn attended the council of Alliance leaders, she ''should'' have remained in the Hall of Flames." | #210 |
| M210e | A Doman shinobi saw through Eline's merchant disguise. | *Chasing Ivy* · 2.4 · main | "She was attired as a merchant, but these eyes are not so easily deceived." | #210 |
| M211a | Devices from the Ironworks disrupt Garlean communications. | *In Flagrante Delicto* · 2.4 · main | "our ingenious friends at Garlond Ironworks have provided us with devices which disrupt these communications" | #211 |
| M211b | The Garleans send agents to destroy the devices. | *In Flagrante Delicto* · 2.4 · main | "the Garleans have sent agents with a mind to destroy the devices and render such direct contact unnecessary." | #211 |
| M211c | Ilberd arrests Eline for treason and espionage. | *In Flagrante Delicto* · 2.4 · main | "Flame Marshal Eline Roaille! You are under arrest for treason and espionage!" | #211 |
| M211d | Eline offers no struggle, saying she has nothing to hide. | *In Flagrante Delicto* · 2.4 · main | "I will offer you no struggle. After all, I have naught to hide." | #211 |
| M211e | Yugiri reads Eline's surrender as knowing her position is untenable, not as innocence. | *In Flagrante Delicto* · 2.4 · main | "Despite her claims, her surrender does not bespeak innocence, but an awareness that her position is untenable." | #211 |
| M211f | Minfilia reports progress toward a way into Iceheart's sanctum. | *In Flagrante Delicto* · 2.4 · main | "I am pleased to report that we have made progress in our efforts to find a way into Iceheart's sanctum" | #211 |
| M212a | Iceheart means to summon Shiva with the crystals stolen from the House Fortemps caravan. | *A Simple Plan* · 2.4 · main | "It is there that we suspect she means to summon Shiva using the crystals she stole from the House Fortemps caravan." | #212 |
| M212b | Minfilia expects the result to be something more akin to a primal. | *A Simple Plan* · 2.4 · main | "it seems likely that the result will be something more akin to a primal." | #212 |
| M212c | Moenbryda is an accomplished Sharlayan scholar and an authority on aetheryte technologies. | *A Simple Plan* · 2.4 · main | "Moenbryda is an accomplished Sharlayan scholar and an authority on aetheryte technologies." | #212 |
| M212d | Moenbryda has been researching white auracite to capture Ascian souls. | *A Simple Plan* · 2.4 · main | "a material she has been researching for the purpose of capturing [[Ascian]] souls." | #212 |
| M212e | Moenbryda proposes using an aetherial current to recreate the beacon. | *A Simple Plan* · 2.4 · main | "We use the current to recreate the beacon." | #212 |
| M212f | Everyone who has tried to teleport this way has died. | *A Simple Plan* · 2.4 · main | "Every person who has attempted to teleport in this fashion has died in the process." | #212 |
| M213a | Through Moenbryda's white auracite the aetheryte wakes, opening a path to Iceheart's sanctuary. | *The Instruments of Our Deliverance* · 2.4 · main | "you attune to the giant crystal, opening a path to [[Iceheart]]'s sanctuary...probably." | #213 |
| M213b | Iceheart offers her own body as a vessel for Shiva's soul. | *The Instruments of Our Deliverance* · 2.4 · main | "Iceheart offers her own body as a vessel for Shiva's soul." | #213 |
| M213c | The battle ends with the maiden kneeling in the snow, cursing your ignorance. | *The Instruments of Our Deliverance* · 2.4 · main | "the battle ends as it began, with the young maiden kneeling in the snow, cursing your ignorance." | #213 |
| M213d | Iceheart tells you to seek the Keeper of the Lake and see with eyes unclouded. | *The Instruments of Our Deliverance* · 2.4 · main | "Seek the Keeper of the Lake. See with eyes unclouded." | #213 |
| M213e | An unseen figure remarks that Lahabrea will be pleased. | *The Instruments of Our Deliverance* · 2.4 · main | "Nevertheless, Lahabrea will be pleased." | #213 |
| M213f | You wonder whether Hydaelyn speaks to your enemy too. | *The Instruments of Our Deliverance* · 2.4 · main | "Could it be that [[Hydaelyn]] speaks to your enemy too?" | #213 |
| M214a | The Holy See ordered its knights to observe and act only in self-defense. | *The Road Less Traveled* · 2.4 · main | "the Holy See decreed that we were to observe, and that military action should be taken only in self-defense." | #214 |
| M214b | Ishgard wants Revenant's Toll to flourish as an obstacle to its enemies from the south. | *The Road Less Traveled* · 2.4 · main | "Ishgard desires to see Revenant's Toll flourish, as it would present a troublesome obstacle to our enemies from the south." | #214 |
| M214c | Aymeric introduces Lucia, his second-in-command, who will represent him. | *The Road Less Traveled* · 2.4 · main | "Ser Aymeric then formally introduces his second-in-command, [[Lucia]], who will act as his representative when he is otherwise indisposed." | #214 |
| M214d | Lucia, not of Ishgardian birth, owes no allegiance to any noble house. | *The Road Less Traveled* · 2.4 · main | "Not being of Ishgardian birth, she owes no allegiance to any noble house, making her as near to incorruptible as one can find in my homeland." | #214 |
| M214e | Haurchefant is distressed that you did not involve him. | *The Road Less Traveled* · 2.4 · main | "Lord [[Haurchefant]] is more than a little distressed that you did not think to involve him in your daring endeavor." | #214 |
| M214f | Iceheart has escaped, but Aymeric says she cannot run forever. | *The Road Less Traveled* · 2.4 · main | "Aye, there is the matter of Iceheart's escape, but she cannot run forever." | #214 |
| M214g | Alphinaud suspects Ishgard of manipulating its neighbors. | *The Road Less Traveled* · 2.4 · main | "Ishgard is not wont to aid its neighbors, but that does not preclude it from manipulating them to serve its own interests." | #214 |
| M214h | Ishgard shares what it learned from the heretics' attacks and the captured merchant. | *The Road Less Traveled* · 2.4 · main | "The results of our investigation into the heretics' recent attacks, as well as our interrogation of the merchant you detained." | #214 |
| M214i | Aymeric calls the arrangement mutually beneficial, born of necessity. | *The Road Less Traveled* · 2.4 · main | "Ours is a mutually beneficial arrangement, lest we forget. One born of necessity." | #214 |
| M215a | Not only beast tribes but civilized peoples can summon primals. | *Eyes Unclouded* · 2.4 · main | "it was not only the beast tribes of Eorzea that could summon primals, but her civilized peoples as well" | #215 |
| M215b | The Alliance could face the combined might of all Garlemald. | *Eyes Unclouded* · 2.4 · main | "we could well find ourselves facing the combined might of ''all Garlemald''" | #215 |
| M215c | Minfilia wonders whether Iceheart has the Echo. | *Eyes Unclouded* · 2.4 · main | "If Iceheart is blessed with the power of the Echo, she will doubtless have used it to further her goals." | #215 |
| M215d | Moenbryda says what Iceheart summoned was a primal. | *Eyes Unclouded* · 2.4 · main | "but I ''will'' say that what she summoned was a primal." | #215 |
| M215e | Midgardsormr was a king amongst kings who reigned for centuries. | *Eyes Unclouded* · 2.4 · main | "Midgardsormr was a king amongst kings who reigned for centuries on end" | #215 |
| M215f | Ishgard's astrologians fear Midgardsormr may soon rise from the dead. | *Eyes Unclouded* · 2.4 · main | "that [[Ishgard]]ian [[astrologian]]s fear may soon rise from the dead" | #215 |
| M215g | The Scions have another reason to watch the Keeper of the Lake. | *Eyes Unclouded* · 2.4 · main | "It would seem we have yet another reason to stand watch over the Keeper of the Lake." | #215 |
| M215h | Urianger notes that few know the secrets of summoning, and a single party profits from spreading them. | *Eyes Unclouded* · 2.4 · main | "Few are privy to the secrets of summoning, and but a single party standeth to profit from their dissemination." | #215 |
| M216a | The turnkey and two guards are found slain after Eline's escape. | *The Reason Roaille* · 2.4 · main | "The turnkey was found slain along with two guards." | #216 |
| M216b | Eline had inside help. | *The Reason Roaille* · 2.4 · main | "'Tis plain she received inside help." | #216 |
| M216c | Eline makes for Castrum Meridianum. | *The Reason Roaille* · 2.4 · main | "She makes for Castrum Meridianum, I am certain of it." | #216 |
| M216d | Ilberd's men bind the recaptured Eline. | *The Reason Roaille* · 2.4 · main | "Bind her! And do it ''tight''!" | #216 |
| M216e | Eline speaks of the injustice lowborn Ishgardians endure. | *The Reason Roaille* · 2.4 · main | "the injustice that we lowborn Ishgardians must endure!" | #216 |
| M216f | Ilberd tells Eline that he sold his sword, but she sold her comrades. | *The Reason Roaille* · 2.4 · main | "But whereas I sold my sword, you, Marshal─you sold your comrades." | #216 |
| M216g | Eline is sent back to Ul'dah to face justice. | *The Reason Roaille* · 2.4 · main | "You will return to Ul'dah to face justice" | #216 |
| M216h | Teledji has the Blades watch Raubahn and the Flames day and night. | *The Reason Roaille* · 2.4 · main | "Have the Blades watch him and the Flames day and night." | #216 |
| M216i | Teledji orders an East Aldenard trade route severed to keep Lolorito occupied. | *The Reason Roaille* · 2.4 · main | "Sever one of the East Aldenard trade routes. That ought to keep Lolorito occupied for a while." | #216 |
| M217a | Eline aided the Scions at the time of the order's founding. | *Let Us Cling Together* · 2.4 · main | "Together with Raubahn, Eline lent us much-needed aid at the time of our order's founding." | #217 |
| M217b | White auracite holds aether only for a short while. | *Let Us Cling Together* · 2.4 · main | "The stone can only store aether for a short while before expelling its contents." | #217 |
| M217c | An Ascian soul may be undone by a concentrated burst of pure aether. | *Let Us Cling Together* · 2.4 · main | "'Tis our belief that an Ascian soul may be permanently undone if smitten by a sufficiently concentrated burst of pure aether." | #217 |
| M217d | The Isle of Val has been erased from existence. | *Let Us Cling Together* · 2.4 · main | "According to the report, the isle has been erased from existence." | #217 |
| M217e | Even as she served the Empire, Eline sold imperial secrets to a faction in Eorzea. | *Let Us Cling Together* · 2.4 · main | "We've learned that even as Roaille served the Empire's interests, she sold imperial secrets to a certain faction in Eorzea." | #217 |
| M217f | Raubahn concludes that Emperor Varis was Eline's true master. | *Let Us Cling Together* · 2.4 · main | "This could only be the former high legatus of the Garlean Army, now known as Emperor Varis zos Galvus." | #217 |
| M217g | Ilberd calls Eline a triple agent. | *Let Us Cling Together* · 2.4 · main | "Triple, if you consider her services to van Baelsar and the new emperor as separate." | #217 |
| M217h | Nanamo declares that Ul'dah will become a true republic, ending Royalists and Monetarists alike. | *Let Us Cling Together* · 2.4 · main | "When the time is ripe, the nation shall become a true republic. Both Royalists and Monetarists shall cease to be." | #217 |
| M217i | Nanamo asks Raubahn's forgiveness for casting aside all he has toiled for in her name. | *Let Us Cling Together* · 2.4 · main | "Raubahn... Forgive me for casting aside all that you have toiled for in my name..." | #217 |
| M217j | Raubahn swears to purge the land of the Monetarists. | *Let Us Cling Together* · 2.4 · main | "One day, I will purge this land of your sickness! Before the eyes of the Twelve, I swear it!" | #217 |
| M217k | Nanamo asks the Alliance leaders to help Raubahn preserve order. | *Let Us Cling Together* · 2.4 · main | "Help Raubahn to preserve order, and protect the people." | #217 |
| M217l | Nanamo is the last monarch in the line of Ul. | *Let Us Cling Together* · 2.4 · main | "As the last monarch in the line of Ul, I make unto you this request." | #217 |
| M218a | The Immortal Flames struggle after one of their highest-ranking officers proved a Garlean agent. | *Good Intentions* · 2.5 · main | "As expected, the Immortal Flames have been struggling to cope with the revelation that one of their highest-ranking officers was a Garlean agent." | #218 |
| M218b | Shiva will not be the last primal, and ties with Ishgard are tenuous. | *Good Intentions* · 2.5 · main | "We both know full well that Saint Shiva will not be the last primal we face...and our relationship with Ishgard is still tenuous at best." | #218 |
| M218c | How to form an aetherial blade at will remains unsolved. | *Good Intentions* · 2.5 · main | "Alas, the key problem─how to form an aetherial blade at will─remains unsolved." | #218 |
| M218d | Riol, tracing weapons used by disgruntled refugees, learns of a large shipment. | *Good Intentions* · 2.5 · main | "Riol, who has been attempting to trace the source of some weapons wielded by disgruntled refugees, has learned of a large shipment which may be intended for the same purpose." | #218 |
| M218e | Alphinaud sends you to meet Captain Ilberd at Highbridge. | *Good Intentions* · 2.5 · main | "Then it is settled. Rendezvous with Captain Ilberd at Highbridge and intervene when the exchange takes place." | #218 |
| M218f | Teledji and his Monetarist ilk turn the situation to their advantage. | *Good Intentions* · 2.5 · main | "Teledji Adeledji and his Monetarist ilk have wasted no time in attempting to turn the situation to their advantage." | #218 |
| M219a | Ilberd's 1st Unit is to block the clients' escape. | *Bait and Switch* · 2.5 · main | "Ilberd states that the 1st Unit will establish a perimeter, preventing the clients' escape, while you and he seize the black marketeer and his weapons." | #219 |
| M219b | The black marketeer fought back and one of Ilberd's men killed him. | *Bait and Switch* · 2.5 · main | "The captain explains that the black marketeer fought back with a hidden blade, and that one of his subordinates, fearing for his safety, slew the man." | #219 |
| M219c | The clients slipped past the perimeter. | *Bait and Switch* · 2.5 · main | "though we know not how, they slipped past our perimeter." | #219 |
| M219d | The promised massive shipment turns out to be one crate. | *Bait and Switch* · 2.5 · main | "the information we received indicated a massive shipment, and this is anything but..." | #219 |
| M220a | Yuyuhase blames Riol's intelligence. | *Best-Laid Schemes* · 2.5 · main | "Clearly, Riol is unfamiliar with the ways of Ul'dahn merchants, who ever strive to present themselves as greater than they are." | #220 |
| M220b | Riol insists his information was reliable. | *Best-Laid Schemes* · 2.5 · main | "Our information was reliable, godsdammit! I ''know'' he purchased those weapons!" | #220 |
| M220c | Riol notes the 1st and 3rd Units now spend freely. | *Best-Laid Schemes* · 2.5 · main | "'Course these days the money flows like water, an' the 1st an' 3rd get the shiniest new toys..." | #220 |
| M220d | A guest from Ishgard asks to see you. | *Best-Laid Schemes* · 2.5 · main | "a guest from [[Ishgard]] has requested your presence." | #220 |
| M221a | Lucia comes on Aymeric's behalf to ask your help. | *The Rising Chorus* · 2.5 · main | "Waiting for you in the solar is [[Lucia]], Ser [[Aymeric]]'s second-in-command, who has come on his behalf to request your assistance with a matter of grave import." | #221 |
| M221b | The Observatorium's astrologians read the signs as Midgardsormr's imminent resurrection. | *The Rising Chorus* · 2.5 · main | "The [[astrologian]]s of [[the Observatorium]] have interpreted recent signs as an indication of [[Midgardsormr]]'s imminent resurrection." | #221 |
| M221c | The explosion that followed Eorzea's victory claimed the great wyrm's life. | *The Rising Chorus* · 2.5 · main | "Yet this victory came at a great cost, for the explosion which followed claimed the life of the great wyrm as well." | #221 |
| M221d | Atop the wreck of the Agrius you confront the fallen guardian of Silvertear Falls. | *The Rising Chorus* · 2.5 · main | "atop the wreckage of the ''Agrius'' you confront the fallen guardian of [[Silvertear Falls]]" | #221 |
| M221e | Hydaelyn marks you as Her chosen, and Midgardsormr strips you of the Mothercrystal's blessing. | *The Rising Chorus* · 2.5 · main | "only to hesitate when [[Hydaelyn]] intervenes and marks you as one of Her chosen. Midgardsormr then relents─but not before somehow stripping you of [[the Mothercrystal]]'s blessing." | #221 |
| M221f | A covenant binds Midgardsormr to you. | *The Rising Chorus* · 2.5 · main | "Revealing that a covenant binds him to you" | #221 |
| M221g | Midgardsormr says his children now sing of Ishgard's destruction. | *The Rising Chorus* · 2.5 · main | "the wyrm lord flatly states that his children now sing of Ishgard's imminent destruction" | #221 |
| M221h | The Scions keep most of the conversation with Midgardsormr secret. | *The Rising Chorus* · 2.5 · main | "it would be best to keep the majority of your conversation with Midgardsormr a secret" | #221 |
| M221i | Lucia thanks you for confirming that a Dravanian attack is coming. | *The Rising Chorus* · 2.5 · main | "for bringing confirmation of the impending [[Dravanian]] attack" | #221 |
| M221j | Until Ishgard asks for aid, the Scions can only watch and wait. | *The Rising Chorus* · 2.5 · main | "Until such time as they choose to request aid, you and the others have no choice but to watch and wait." | #221 |
| M222a | An Ascian is immortal because its soul does not return to the aetherial realm when its host falls. | *Aether on Demand* · 2.5 · main | "An Ascian is an immortal because its soul doesn't return to the aetherial realm when its host is defeated." | #222 |
| M222b | Moenbryda plans to siphon energy from corrupted crystals across Eorzea. | *Aether on Demand* · 2.5 · main | "by siphoning energy from the corrupted crystals found across [[Eorzea]]." | #222 |
| M222c | The XIVth Legion lost the Empire's support, but was replenished from Castrum Centri. | *Aether on Demand* · 2.5 · main | "Having lost the Empire's support, the XIVth Legion lacks the resources to sustain itself. … Not long after our forces withdrew, however, their ranks were replenished by reinforcements from Castrum Centri." | #222 |
| M222d | Minfilia sends Alphinaud, with Thancred and Papalymo, to support the Crystal Braves. | *Aether on Demand* · 2.5 · main | "Pray join the Crystal Braves and lend them your support. Thancred and Papalymo shall accompany you." | #222 |
| M222e | Crates at the plant hold explosives of imperial design. | *Aether on Demand* · 2.5 · main | "these boxes contain powerful explosive devices. Devices of imperial design, no less." | #222 |
| M223a | The imperial squads are too few to take and hold the plant. | *On the Counteroffensive* · 2.5 · main | "According to the reconnaissance report that just came in, the squads seen leaving the castrum are too few in number to effectively take and hold our position." | #223 |
| M223b | Ilberd marches the 3rd Unit out of Ul'dah to the plant. | *On the Counteroffensive* · 2.5 · main | "The 3rd was getting restless holed up in Ul'dah, so I marched them out here to fill any holes in the plant's defenses and add their numbers to your counterattack." | #223 |
| M223c | The plant is no longer in immediate danger. | *On the Counteroffensive* · 2.5 · main | "The Amajina engineers will be most relieved to hear that the plant is no longer in any immediate danger." | #223 |
| M223d | Yuyuhase reports that many Garleans won't make it back to the castrum. | *On the Counteroffensive* · 2.5 · main | "I'll take that to mean that, between us, more than a few Garleans won't be making it back to the castrum." | #223 |
| M224a | An Ascian introduces himself as Nabriales. | *An Uninvited Ascian* · 2.5 · main | "We meet at last, Warrior of Light. I am Nabriales..." | #224 |
| M224b | The blessing of Light had kept the Ascians out of the Scions' domain. | *An Uninvited Ascian* · 2.5 · main | "The blessing of Light kept you and your fellow meddlers safe. It was that which prevented my kind from entering your domain." | #224 |
| M224c | The staff Nabriales seeks is Tupsimati, the relic Louisoix once wielded. | *An Uninvited Ascian* · 2.5 · main | "Moenbryda realizes that the staff Nabriales spoke of is likely [[Tupsimati]], the relic once wielded by [[Louisoix]] himself." | #224 |
| M224d | Nabriales escapes through a rift with Minfilia and the relic. | *An Uninvited Ascian* · 2.5 · main | "He then tears open a dimensional rift, through which he escapes with both Minfilia and the relic she holds." | #224 |
| M224e | Minfilia realizes the Isle of Val and the scholars' fate were Nabriales's doing. | *An Uninvited Ascian* · 2.5 · main | "Then it was ''your'' doing! The Isle of Val, the scholars─all of it!" | #224 |
| M224f | You defeat Nabriales and rescue Minfilia. | *An Uninvited Ascian* · 2.5 · main | "You have defeated Nabriales and rescued Minfilia." | #224 |
| M224g | Minfilia traps the weakened Ascian's soul in white auracite. | *An Uninvited Ascian* · 2.5 · main | "Minfilia succeeds in trapping the soul of the weakened Ascian within the chunk of [[White Auracite|white auracite]]." | #224 |
| M224h | Moenbryda spends her own life energy so your blade can pierce Nabriales. | *An Uninvited Ascian* · 2.5 · main | "The wounded scholar then expends her own life energy in a brilliant burst of aether, allowing your glowing blade to at last pierce Nabriales's essence." | #224 |
| M224i | Nabriales means to use the staff's power to bring about a new Rejoining. | *An Uninvited Ascian* · 2.5 · main | "the Ascian reveals his plans to use the power yet hidden within the staff to bring about a new [[Rejoining]]" | #224 |
| M224j | Minfilia keeps a device left by Moenbryda. | *An Uninvited Ascian* · 2.5 · main | "This device is a legacy of Moenbryda's toils and sacrifice." | #224 |
| M224k | Nabriales reaches the Rising Stones' solar, where Minfilia holds the broken Tupsimati. | *An Uninvited Ascian* · 2.5 · main | "You return to the Rising Stones to find Nabriales already in [[The Solar|the solar]] with Minfilia, the Antecedent clutching the shards of the broken Tupsimati." | #224 |
| M224l | Louisoix was branded a pariah in his own land. | *An Uninvited Ascian* · 2.5 · main | "Yet this great soul, whom all should rightly have honored, was branded a pariah in his own land." | #224 |
| M225a | Moenbryda was a native of Sharlayan, so the Scions gather at the mark of the Scholar. | *In Memory of Moenbryda* · 2.5 · main | "As Moenbryda was a native of Sharlayan, I feel it is only fitting that we gather before the mark of the Scholar." | #225 |
| M225b | Midgardsormr's voice calls it life for death, a fair exchange, and promises other bargains. | *In Memory of Moenbryda* · 2.5 · main | "Life for death. A fair exchange. … Other bargains will be struck." | #225 |
| M225c | Minfilia names the dead: Louisoix, Moenbryda and the Scions at the Waking Sands. | *In Memory of Moenbryda* · 2.5 · main | "The battles we have fought have taken the lives of so many. Louisoix. Moenbryda. Our fellows at the Waking Sands." | #225 |
| M225d | Minfilia calls you her pillar of strength. | *In Memory of Moenbryda* · 2.5 · main | "You are ''my'' pillar of strength, and I fear I shall have need of your steadfast presence in the days to come..." | #225 |
| M226a | Minfilia is concerned for Yda after Moenbryda's death. | *Mask of Grief* · 2.55 · main | "Minfilia expresses concern over [[Yda]]'s emotional state following the loss of [[Moenbryda]]." | #226 |
| M226b | Yda ordered a rare flower in memory of her friend. | *Mask of Grief* · 2.55 · main | "You learn that she has ordered a [[Moon Daisy|rare flower]] in memory of her friend" | #226 |
| M226c | As Yda lays the flower at the mark of the Scholar, Dravanian wings darken the sky. | *Mask of Grief* · 2.55 · main | "As Yda places the moon daisy before the mark of the Scholar, the skies over Rathefrost are suddenly darkened by the passing of [[Dravanian]] wings." | #226 |
| M226d | An envoy from the Holy See is on the way with an urgent report. | *Mask of Grief* · 2.55 · main | "an envoy from the [[Holy See]] is already en route to [[the Scions]]' headquarters, bearing an urgent report..." | #226 |
| M227a | The envoy from Ishgard is Lucia, Aymeric's second-in-command. | *Defenders for Ishgard* · 2.55 · main | "The envoy from Ishgard turns out to be [[Lucia]], Ser [[Aymeric]]'s second-in-command." | #227 |
| M227b | The Dravanians were forced back, at the cost of many Ishgardian lives. | *Defenders for Ishgard* · 2.55 · main | "Though the beasts were ultimately forced into retreat, our victory─if such it may be called─came at the cost of many of our own." | #227 |
| M227c | Lucia seeks the aid of the Crystal Braves and the Grand Companies. | *Defenders for Ishgard* · 2.55 · main | "has come to seek the aid of the [[Crystal Braves]] and the [[Grand Companies]] in bolstering her city's defenses." | #227 |
| M227d | Minfilia warns that any support will be conditional. | *Defenders for Ishgard* · 2.55 · main | "In the event that your plea does receive a favorable answer, know that any support is certain to be conditional." | #227 |
| M227e | Alphinaud warns the Braves' commitment to Ul'dah must come first. | *Defenders for Ishgard* · 2.55 · main | "I must warn you, however, that the involvement of the Crystal Braves is by no means assured─our commitment to quelling the unrest in Ul'dah must take precedence." | #227 |
| M227f | A heretic force broke the outer defenses and dispelled the first of Ishgard's wards. | *Defenders for Ishgard* · 2.55 · main | "a heretic force has broken through the outer defenses and dispelled the first layer of arcane wards protecting the city of Ishgard." | #227 |
| M227g | Lady Iceheart and her followers attacked the knights at the Gates of Judgement. | *Defenders for Ishgard* · 2.55 · main | "You learn from the knights stationed at the Gates of Judgement that they were attacked by Lady [[Iceheart]] and her followers." | #227 |
| M227h | Iceheart says the sons must answer for their fathers' misdeeds. | *Defenders for Ishgard* · 2.55 · main | "The sons must answer for their fathers' misdeeds." | #227 |
| M228a | Iceheart and the Dravanians share one goal: reclaiming Nidhogg's stolen eye. | *The Wyrm's Roar* · 2.55 · main | "'Twould seem that Lady Iceheart and the Dravanians share a common goal─the reclamation of Nidhogg's stolen eye." | #228 |
| M228b | Haldrath took the Eye from Nidhogg's skull, and it is a treasured relic of Ishgard. | *The Wyrm's Roar* · 2.55 · main | "The trophy Haldrath plucked from the bested wyrm's skull─known simply as “the Eye”─is a treasured relic of Ishgard, and one possessed of untold power." | #228 |
| M228c | Nidhogg has terrorized Coerthas since Ishgard's founding. | *The Wyrm's Roar* · 2.55 · main | "Though once defeated by the hero Haldrath, the beast is said to have terrorized Coerthas since Ishgard's founding." | #228 |
| M228d | Aymeric presents Ishgard's Azure Dragoon, Estinien. | *The Wyrm's Roar* · 2.55 · main | "May I present to you Ishgard's Azure Dragoon." | #228 |
| M228e | Through the Eye, Estinien has become the vessel of Nidhogg's strength. | *The Wyrm's Roar* · 2.55 · main | "Through the power of the Eye, I have become the vessel of Nidhogg's strength." | #228 |
| M228f | Nidhogg means to hurl his Horde against Daniffen's Collar and shatter the remaining wards. | *The Wyrm's Roar* · 2.55 · main | "He means to hurl his Horde against Daniffen's Collar, and shatter the remaining wards that protect the city." | #228 |
| M228g | If Ishgard falls, the Empire would claim Coerthas. | *The Wyrm's Roar* · 2.55 · main | "Until the Garleans' arrival, that is─for you may be certain that the Empire would not hesitate to claim Coerthas." | #228 |
| M228h | Alphinaud pledges that Eorzea will not leave Ishgard to face the Horde alone. | *The Wyrm's Roar* · 2.55 · main | "we of Eorzea will not abandon our Ishgardian brothers to face the coming Horde alone." | #228 |
| M229a | The main Dravanian host advances on Ishgard. | *Committed to the Cause* · 2.55 · main | "Indeed. The main host advances upon Ishgard as we speak." | #229 |
| M229b | Ishgard's defenses have been nullified. | *Committed to the Cause* · 2.55 · main | "Whose vaunted defenses have been nullified." | #229 |
| M229c | The Scions and the Crystal Braves commit to the defense of Ishgard. | *Committed to the Cause* · 2.55 · main | "Along with the [[Crystal Braves]], [[the Scions of the Seventh Dawn]] have committed themselves to the defense of Ishgard." | #229 |
| M229d | Minfilia makes plain that the Scions would be directly intervening in the war. | *Committed to the Cause* · 2.55 · main | "Let there be no ambiguity about what has been proposed. We would be directly intervening in the war." | #229 |
| M229e | Riol is uneasy about private words between Ilberd and Yuyuhase. | *Committed to the Cause* · 2.55 · main | "it appears that [[Riol]] is more concerned by the private words passing between [[Ilberd]] and [[Yuyuhase]]" | #229 |
| M229f | Alphinaud will ask the Alliance council to contribute forces. | *Committed to the Cause* · 2.55 · main | "I shall inform the council of our intentions, and request that they contribute their own forces to the defense of Ishgard." | #229 |
| M230a | Minfilia asks you to gather adventurers for the coming siege. | *Volunteer Dragonslayers* · 2.55 · main | "I would have you reach out to your fellow adventurers, and gather what allies you may for the coming siege." | #230 |
| M230b | The Adventurers' Guild will have no official presence at the siege. | *Volunteer Dragonslayers* · 2.55 · main | "There will be no official Adventurers' Guild presence at this siege." | #230 |
| M230c | Wilred, the youth from Little Ala Mhigo, has disappeared. | *Volunteer Dragonslayers* · 2.55 · main | "the disappearance of [[Wilred]], the youth from [[Little Ala Mhigo]]." | #230 |
| M230d | Black-market weapons went missing from the Braves' vault when Wilred stopped turning up for duty. | *Volunteer Dragonslayers* · 2.55 · main | "Around the time Wilred stopped turnin' up for duty, them black market weapons went missin' from our vault in Ul'dah..." | #230 |
| M230e | Riol believes a villain other than Wilred is behind it. | *Volunteer Dragonslayers* · 2.55 · main | "Aye, there's a villain at the end o' this trail I've stumbled on, an' I'll bet me good eye it ain't young Wilred." | #230 |
| M230f | Wilred had reported that the Braves' numbers did not add up. | *Volunteer Dragonslayers* · 2.55 · main | "Somethin' about the Braves' numbers not addin' up..." | #230 |
| M230g | Only three or four volunteers come forward. | *Volunteer Dragonslayers* · 2.55 · main | "One or two, eh? Well, added to mine, that makes three or four, not including Slafborn's friends!" | #230 |
| M231a | The Alliance leaders refuse meaningful assistance and suggest hiring elite adventurers. | *An Allied Perspective* · 2.55 · main | "the leaders refuse to provide meaningful assistance, and suggest instead that you assemble a contingent of elite [[adventurer]]s" | #231 |
| M231b | Merlwyb puts Limsa first, then the Alliance, then Ishgard. | *An Allied Perspective* · 2.55 · main | "Lest you doubt, Limsa comes first, then the Alliance, and finally─circumstances permitting─Ishgard and the rest." | #231 |
| M231c | Raubahn proposes leaving Ul'dah's security to the Brass Blades to send a larger force. | *An Allied Perspective* · 2.55 · main | "If we entrust the security of Ul'dah to the Brass Blades for a time, we can dispatch a larger force to Ishgard." | #231 |
| M231d | Merlwyb gives Alphinaud the Crystal Braves to command. | *An Allied Perspective* · 2.55 · main | "The Crystal Braves are yours to command, Alphinaud. Do what you will." | #231 |
| M231e | The Dravanians have begun their assault on Coerthas. | *An Allied Perspective* · 2.55 · main | "It would seem the [[Dravanians]] have begun their assault on [[Coerthas]]." | #231 |
| M231f | Alphinaud sends the Braves to join House Durendaire at the Steel Vigil. | *An Allied Perspective* · 2.55 · main | "Move our forces into position. Your unit will join House Durendaire at the Steel Vigil." | #231 |
| M231g | The adventurer reinforcements are decimated. | *An Allied Perspective* · 2.55 · main | "''Decimated'', you say? Dravanian devils! Damn their scaly hides!" | #231 |
| M231h | Kan-E-Senna fears Teledji and his ilk would seize Gridania's territories in Carteneau. | *An Allied Perspective* · 2.55 · main | "We dare not leave our interests in Carteneau undefended as well. Teledji Adeledji and his ilk would seize control of the territories in our absence." | #231 |
| M231i | Kan-E-Senna can commit only a token force. | *An Allied Perspective* · 2.55 · main | "Would that we could commit more than a token force to this cause." | #231 |
| M231j | Kan-E-Senna speaks for Gridania at the council. | *An Allied Perspective* · 2.55 · main | "Gridania does not object." | #231 |
| M232a | Iceheart's damage to Ishgard's barrier lets the dragons land on the Steps of Faith. | *The Steps of Faith* · 2.55 · main | "the damage [[Iceheart]] inflicted on Ishgard's arcane barrier has allowed the [[dragon]]s to land directly upon [[the Steps of Faith]]" | #232 |
| M232b | The bridge is the foundation of the wards of Daniffen's Collar. | *The Steps of Faith* · 2.55 · main | "More than simply a means to cross the Sea of Clouds, the bridge serves as a foundation for the arcane wards that form the barrier we call Daniffen's Collar." | #232 |
| M232c | Estinien sees one of Nidhogg's greatest champions slain. | *The Steps of Faith* · 2.55 · main | "One of Nidhogg's greatest champions...slain." | #232 |
| M232d | You drive the Dravanian Horde from the Steps of Faith. | *The Steps of Faith* · 2.55 · main | "You have succeeded in driving the Dravanian Horde from the Steps of Faith." | #232 |
| M232e | Alphinaud declares the stage set for Ishgard's return to the Alliance. | *The Steps of Faith* · 2.55 · main | "Alphinaud triumphantly declares that the stage has been set for Ishgard's return to the [[Eorzean Alliance]]" | #232 |
| M232f | Aymeric thanks you on behalf of the Holy See and the people of Ishgard. | *The Steps of Faith* · 2.55 · main | "On behalf of the Holy See and the people of Ishgard, I offer you my humble thanks." | #232 |
| M232g | The siege is ended. | *The Steps of Faith* · 2.55 · main | "Ishgard's weary defenders breathe a sigh of relief. The siege is ended." | #232 |
| M232h | Papalymo and Yda join the rearguard. | *The Steps of Faith* · 2.55 · main | "I think it prudent we divide into two groups. Yda and I will join the rearguard." | #232 |
| M232i | You aid the Temple Knights in defending the bridge against the Dravanian Horde. | *The Steps of Faith* · 2.55 · main | "aid the [[Temple Knights]] in defending the vital structure against the [[Dravanian Horde]]" | #232 |
| M233a | A royal invitation asks the Scions to a feast at Ul'dah's Royal Palace in honor of Ishgard's victory. | *Administrative Decision* · 2.55 · main | "invites the Scions of the Seventh Dawn to attend a feast at the Royal Palace of Ul'dah...in honor of Ishgard's recent victory." | #233 |
| M233b | The Alliance leaders sent sellswords, many of whom died before the siege began. | *Administrative Decision* · 2.55 · main | "All three refused to deploy their standing armies, preferring to send hastily assembled companies of sellswords, many of whom perished ere the siege began." | #233 |
| M233c | Minfilia suspects the feast is a chance to press for Ishgard's return to the Alliance. | *Administrative Decision* · 2.55 · main | "Such an occasion would present a fine opportunity to press for Ishgard's return to the Eorzean Alliance." | #233 |
| M233d | Tataru asks for leave to study a fighting discipline. | *Administrative Decision* · 2.55 · main | "Citing a need for some measure of martial prowess, Tataru requests a leave of absence to study her chosen discipline." | #233 |
| M233e | Tataru wants the power to defend herself. | *Administrative Decision* · 2.55 · main | "And so it seems only right that I, too, should acquire the power to defend myself!" | #233 |
| M233f | Minfilia is in no mood to celebrate so soon after Moenbryda's death. | *Administrative Decision* · 2.55 · main | "We have scarce had time to mourn Moenbryda, and I, for one, am in no mood to carouse." | #233 |
| M234a | Minfilia suspects Tataru has taken up arcanima. | *An Unexpected Ambition* · 2.55 · main | "Minfilia surmises that [[Tataru]] may have taken up the [[Arcanist|study of arcanima]]." | #234 |
| M234b | Tataru's Carbuncle abandons her to wild beasts. | *An Unexpected Ambition* · 2.55 · main | "Wild beasts soon approach, and she steels herself for battle─only to be abandoned by her [[Carbuncle]]." | #234 |
| M234ba | The Scions employ scholars to take aetheric readings across Eorzea. | *Where We Are Needed* · 2.55 · main | "employ [[scholar]]s to travel across [[Eorzea]] taking [[aether]]ic readings" | #234b |
| M234bb | A scholar finds that ambient aether has decreased since the Calamity. | *Where We Are Needed* · 2.55 · main | "there has been a widespread decrease in ambient aether concentrations following [[the Calamity]]" | #234b |
| M234bc | The scholar wants more measurements. | *Where We Are Needed* · 2.55 · main | "We must take additional measurements to confirm my assessment." | #234b |
| M234c | Tataru admits she will never be a warrior. | *An Unexpected Ambition* · 2.55 · main | "I'll never be a warrior like you or the others." | #234 |
| M235a | Tataru means to make charms for the Scions from the blood pearls she harvested. | *Ancient Ways, Timeless Wants* · 2.55 · main | "Tataru reveals her intent to use the blood pearls she has harvested to fashion charms for her fellow [[Scions]]." | #235 |
| M235b | Tataru hopes the charms will bring everyone home. | *Ancient Ways, Timeless Wants* · 2.55 · main | "I thought I might make charms for everyone...so that no matter where they go, they will always come home." | #235 |
| M235ba | The scholar finds the body of Wilred, the young Ala Mhigan you recruited to the Crystal Braves. | *The Least among Us* · 2.55 · main | "the young [[Ala Mhigan]] whom you personally recruited to the [[Crystal Braves]]" | #235b |
| M235bb | Wilred was murdered, by whom and why no one knows. | *The Least among Us* · 2.55 · main | "they conclude that the boy was murdered─though by whom and for what reason they can only speculate" | #235b |
| M235bc | No Crystal Brave operation was known in the area. | *The Least among Us* · 2.55 · main | "I've heard nothing of any Crystal Brave operations in this area." | #235b |
| M235bd | Minfilia will send word to Alphinaud. | *The Least among Us* · 2.55 · main | "I will send word to Alphinaud at once." | #235b |
| M235c | Minfilia concludes Tataru benefited from the attempt. | *Ancient Ways, Timeless Wants* · 2.55 · main | "Minfilia concludes that despite Tataru's failure to become an [[arcanist]], she nevertheless benefited from the experience." | #235 |
| M238a | Minfilia leaves for Ul'dah to meet the other Scions. | *A Time to Every Purpose* · 2.55 · main | "And so she departs for [[Ul'dah]] to rendezvous with the other Scions, instructing you to follow when you are ready." | #238 |
| M238b | Riol has evidence that Teledji bribed members of the Braves' 1st and 3rd Units. | *A Time to Every Purpose* · 2.55 · main | "uncovered evidence that [[Teledji Adeledji]] has bribed members of the 1st and 3rd Units" | #238 |
| M238c | The Braves' donors were fronts owned by the Mirage Trust. | *A Time to Every Purpose* · 2.55 · main | "all were owned by the Mirage Trust." | #238 |
| M238d | Riol believes the bribed Braves killed Wilred. | *A Time to Every Purpose* · 2.55 · main | "I'd wager me right eye they're the ones what did for Wilred." | #238 |
| M238e | Riol vows to purge the traitors from the Braves. | *A Time to Every Purpose* · 2.55 · main | "We'll purge every one o' them traitorous bastards from our ranks!" | #238 |
| M238f | Four nations prepare to celebrate Ishgard's defense. | *A Time to Every Purpose* · 2.55 · main | "As four great nations stand poised to commemorate the successful defense of [[Ishgard]]" | #238 |
| M238g | You are needed at the Quicksand. | *A Time to Every Purpose* · 2.55 · main | "It would seem that your presence is required at [[the Quicksand]]" | #238 |
| M239a | Alianne of the 4th asks for a private meeting to show you something. | *Come, but Not Gone* · 2.55 · main | "[[Alianne]] of the 4th has requested a private meeting so that she might show you something of importance." | #239 |
| M239b | Alianne never comes, and you find a vial at your feet. | *Come, but Not Gone* · 2.55 · main | "you chance to catch sight of [[Clouded Vial|a vial]] at your feet" | #239 |
| M239c | Another Brave has seen no sign of Alianne. | *Come, but Not Gone* · 2.55 · main | "He tells you that he has seen no sign of Alianne on his patrol" | #239 |
| M239d | Nanamo laments that through her weakness the House of Ul has all but vanished. | *Come, but Not Gone* · 2.55 · main | "Through my weakness, the glorious House of Ul has all but disappeared beneath the sands." | #239 |
| M239e | Another Brave knows her as Lieutenant Alianne. | *Come, but Not Gone* · 2.55 · main | "Lieutenant Alianne? Why no, I haven't seen her about..." | #239 |
| M240a | Nanamo tells you she means to abdicate and establish a republic. | *The Parting Glass* · 2.55 · main | "In private, [[Nanamo|the sultana]] reveals to you her intent to abdicate the throne and establish a fair and equitable republic." | #240 |
| M240b | Nanamo asks you to support Raubahn in the days ahead. | *The Parting Glass* · 2.55 · main | "she asks that you support [[Raubahn]] however you can in the days ahead" | #240 |
| M240c | Nanamo drinks the wine and collapses. | *The Parting Glass* · 2.55 · main | "Nanamo drinks the wine and collapses." | #240 |
| M240d | Teledji proclaims the sultana murdered with poison. | *The Parting Glass* · 2.55 · main | "[[Teledji]] proclaims to all and sundry that Her Royal Majesty Nanamo Ul Namo has been murdered with poison" | #240 |
| M240e | Captain Ilberd delivers you bound, accused of regicide. | *The Parting Glass* · 2.55 · main | "Captain [[Ilberd]] of the [[Crystal Braves]] delivers you bound into [[the Fragrant Chamber]] and declares that you stand accused of regicide." | #240 |
| M240f | Yuyuhase tells Alphinaud the Braves strive for the salvation of Eorzea. | *The Parting Glass* · 2.55 · main | "Why, that which we have striven for all along, Commander: the salvation of Eorzea." | #240 |
| M240g | An urgent message from the Holy See arrives. | *The Parting Glass* · 2.55 · main | "We have received an urgent message from the Holy See." | #240 |
| M240h | Teledji goads Raubahn, who cuts him down. | *The Parting Glass* · 2.55 · main | "Unable to resist, he goads the Bull of Ala Mhigo into action, and is cleft in twain for his troubles" | #240 |
| M240i | When Raubahn advances on Lolorito, Ilberd severs his arm. | *The Parting Glass* · 2.55 · main | "Raubahn then advances upon [[Lolorito]]─only to be taken unawares by Ilberd, who severs his arm with a single practiced stroke." | #240 |
| M240j | Ilberd claims credit for the sultana's murder. | *The Parting Glass* · 2.55 · main | "going so far as to claim credit for Nanamo's murder" | #240 |
| M240k | Raubahn bids you find out who is behind the plot. | *The Parting Glass* · 2.55 · main | "Find out who is behind this plot! It is the only way! Now go!" | #240 |
| M240l | Pipin is Vice Marshal of the Immortal Flames and Raubahn's adopted son. | *The Parting Glass* · 2.55 · main | "Together with [[Pipin Tarupin]]─Vice Marshal of [[the Immortal Flames]], and Raubahn's adopted son─you flee to [[Black Brush]]" | #240 |
| M240m | Cid comes to spirit you away aboard the Enterprise. | *The Parting Glass* · 2.55 · main | "who is prepared to spirit you away from [[Thanalan]] aboard the ''Enterprise''" | #240 |
| M240n | You land near Camp Dragonhead, and Alphinaud proposes seeking Haurchefant's counsel. | *The Parting Glass* · 2.55 · main | "you disembark near [[Camp Dragonhead]], where a downcast Alphinaud proposes you seek Lord [[Haurchefant]]'s counsel." | #240 |
| M240o | Pipin swears to do what he can to clear your names. | *The Parting Glass* · 2.55 · main | "who swears to do what he can to clear your names" | #240 |
| M240q | Raubahn bids you and the Scions escape and clear your names. | *The Parting Glass* · 2.55 · main | "Raubahn bids you and [[the Scions]] escape─to clear your names and see justice done" | #240 |
| M240r | One by one, your comrades stay behind to hold back the pursuit. | *The Parting Glass* · 2.55 · main | "One by one, your comrades choose to remain and hold back the tide of pursuers" | #240 |
| M240s | Pipin is Raubahn's adopted son. | *The Parting Glass* · 2.55 · main | "Yes, I am his son. Adopted, of course." | #240 |
| M240t | Yda stays to hold off the pursuers. | *The Parting Glass* · 2.55 · main | "The rest of you go on ahead. I'll handle this lot!" | #240 |
| M240u | Papalymo stays with Yda to hold the pursuers. | *The Parting Glass* · 2.55 · main | "We will hold our pursuers here. Hurry, now! Find this tunnel of Thancred's!" | #240 |
| M240v | Y'shtola means to bring the tunnel down on the pursuers. | *The Parting Glass* · 2.55 · main | "Numbers will count for little when I bring the tunnel down upon their heads." | #240 |
| M241a | The Dravanians renewed their assault on Ishgard, and wyverns broke into the city. | *Before the Dawn* · 2.55 · main | "the [[Dravanian]]s renewed their assault on [[Ishgard]] in your absence, and that several [[wyvern]]s broke free into the city proper" | 2.55 |
| M241b | Your petition for asylum will wait until the threat to Ishgard diminishes. | *Before the Dawn* · 2.55 · main | "your petition for asylum will not be heard until the threat to Ishgard has diminished" | 2.55 |
| M241c | Aymeric is back from Ul'dah and again leads Ishgard's defense. | *Before the Dawn* · 2.55 · main | "Ser Aymeric is safely returned from Ul'dah and once more leads the defense of Ishgard." | 2.55 |
| M241d | House Fortemps will aid no one who comes searching for you. | *Before the Dawn* · 2.55 · main | "any who come here in search of you will receive no aid from House Fortemps." | 2.55 |
| M241e | Raubahn is shown in a prison cell. | *Before the Dawn* · 2.55 · main | "A short scene plays of Raubahn in a prison cell." | 2.55 |
| M241f | Elidibus says Nabriales is no more. | *Before the Dawn* · 2.55 · main | "Nabriales is no more." | 2.55 |
| M241g | Lahabrea says the Ardor was not Nabriales's to invoke, and his demise was his own making. | *Before the Dawn* · 2.55 · main | "The Ardor was not his to invoke. His demise was of his own making." | 2.55 |
| M241h | Elidibus turns their thoughts to the northern lands. | *Before the Dawn* · 2.55 · main | "The northern lands, then?" | 2.55 |
| M241i | At Ishgard's gates Midgardsormr says you will find only delusion, despair and death. | *Before the Dawn* · 2.55 · main | "Delusion. Despair. Death. Thou shalt find naught else here." | 2.55 |
| M241j | The Temple Knights slew the wyverns. | *Before the Dawn* · 2.55 · main | "The Temple Knights succeeded in slaying the beasts" | 2.55 |
| M242a | Count Edmont has made you, Alphinaud and Tataru wards of House Fortemps. | *Coming to Ishgard* · 3.0 · main | "Count Edmont has decreed that the three of you be taken in as wards of House Fortemps." | #242 |
| M242b | Haurchefant offers the family manor as the Scions' new headquarters. | *Coming to Ishgard* · 3.0 · main | "Pray consider our manor your new headquarters" | #242 |
| M242c | Ishgard is devoted to Halone and ruled by Archbishop Thordan VII of the Ishgardian Orthodox Church. | *Coming to Ishgard* · 3.0 · main | "A great and proud nation devoted to Halone, the Fury, ruled by Thordan VII, Archbishop of the Ishgardian Orthodox Church..." | #242 |
| M242d | After a thousand years of war, Ishgard had forgotten peace. | *Coming to Ishgard* · 3.0 · main | "A land that after a thousand years of war had forgotten what it was to be at peace." | #242 |
| M242e | The Scions, betrayed by the Crystal Braves, fled the victory banquet and are condemned as murderers. | *Coming to Ishgard* · 3.0 · main | "are now condemned as murderers─betrayed by the [[Crystal Braves]] and forced to flee the banquet held to celebrate the very victory" | #242 |
| M242f | Your comrades stayed behind one by one, and you alone escaped to rejoin Alphinaud and take refuge in Coerthas. | *Coming to Ishgard* · 3.0 · main | "until you alone emerged from the tunnels beneath [[Ul'dah]] to be reunited with Alphinaud and seek refuge in [[Coerthas]]" | #242 |
| M242g | Haurchefant says any missing allies who are found will be welcome at the manor. | *Coming to Ishgard* · 3.0 · main | "should any of your missing allies be found─as I am certain they shall─they will of course be welcome to join you there." | #242 |
| M242h | Fortemps Manor is Haurchefant's family home. | *Coming to Ishgard* · 3.0 · main | "Allow me to present to you my family home: Fortemps Manor!" | #242 |
| M242i | Ishgard has closed its borders for the war, and Edmont's decision will raise eyebrows among the other High Houses. | *Coming to Ishgard* · 3.0 · main | "'Tis true that Ishgard's first thought has ever been the war effort, hence the closing of our borders... … Granted, my decision will have raised eyebrows in the Vault and in the halls of the other High Houses." | #242 |
| M242j | Wyverns recently wrought destruction in Ishgard's Foundation. | *Coming to Ishgard* · 3.0 · main | "As you arrive in [[Foundation]], you find that the destruction wrought by the [[wyvern]]s is still fresh." | #242 |
| M243a | Ishgard's borders have been closed for decades, so many of its people are unused to foreigners. | *Taking in the Sights* · 3.0 · main | "Given that our borders have remained closed for decades, many of my countrymen are unaccustomed to treating with foreigners." | #243 |
| M243b | Archbishop Thordan VII lives in and rules from the Holy Vault. | *Taking in the Sights* · 3.0 · main | "wherein [[Thordan VII]], Archbishop of the [[Ishgardian Orthodox Church]], resides and rules." | #243 |
| M243c | Alphinaud and Tataru resolve to learn more of Ishgard. | *Taking in the Sights* · 3.0 · main | "Alphinaud and [[Tataru]] resolve to learn more of Ishgard." | #243 |
