| M244a | The Brume suffered worst in the wyvern attack, while the Pillars had the High Houses and Temple Knights to protect them. | *The Better Half* · 3.0 · main | "it was the Brume that got the worst of it, while the Pillars enjoyed the protection of the High Houses and the Temple Knights." | #244 |
| M244b | Ser Aymeric has ordered his knights to strengthen Ishgard's defenses, and every house is asked to help. | *The Better Half* · 3.0 · main | "Ser Aymeric has ordered his knights to strengthen our defenses, and each house has been called upon to lend what support it can." | #244 |
| M244c | Edmont accepts the Scions' help, hoping to secure provisions from abroad. | *The Better Half* · 3.0 · main | "Such qualities may prove most useful to us in our efforts to secure provisions from foreign sources" | #244 |
| M244d | Edmont asks you to help his sons, Artoirel and Emmanellain, with their duties. | *The Better Half* · 3.0 · main | "to aid Artoirel and Emmanellain in their duties." | #244 |
| M244e | Edmont's two sons greet the offer with indignation and mirth. | *The Better Half* · 3.0 · main | "unlike his two sons, who greet your proposal with a mixture of indignation and mirth." | #244 |
| M244f | By granting the Scions patronage, House Fortemps has taken a great risk. | *The Better Half* · 3.0 · main | "By granting us patronage, House Fortemps has taken a tremendous risk." | #244 |
| M244g | Tataru stays at the Forgotten Knight tavern to gather news, perhaps of the missing Scions. | *The Better Half* · 3.0 · main | "[[Tataru]] proposes that she stay behind and ingratiate herself with the regulars." | #244 |
| M245a | Artoirel, Edmont's eldest son, is sent to aid Houses Durendaire and Dzemael, despite the old rivalries between Ishgard's noble families. | *Over the Wall* · 3.0 · main | "Ser Artoirel de Fortemps, eldest son of [[Count Edmont de Fortemps]], did not expect to have company on his mission to aid houses [[House Durendaire|Durendaire]] and [[House Dzemael|Dzemael]] … given the ancient rivalries between [[Ishgard]]'s noble families" | #245 |
| M245b | Artoirel takes your company as a sign his father does not trust him to work alone. | *Over the Wall* · 3.0 · main | "the count has given Artoirel cause to believe that his father does not trust him to carry out his duties alone." | #245 |
| M245ba | Emmanellain, Edmont's second son, longs to escape his elder brother's shadow. | *Onwards and Upwards* · 3.0 · main | "has long dreamed of emerging from beneath his elder brother's shadow" | #245b |
| M245bb | Emmanellain hopes his deeds will raise him above Artoirel in the people's eyes. | *Onwards and Upwards* · 3.0 · main | "so that the tales of my great deeds can win me a place in the people's hearts─or better yet, raise me higher in their estimations than Artoirel!" | #245b |
| M245bc | Emmanellain's mission is to aid House Haillenarte. | *Onwards and Upwards* · 3.0 · main | "we are to aid the good men and women of House Haillenarte." | #245b |
| M245bd | Camp Cloudtop was built to warn of Dravanian incursions. | *Onwards and Upwards* · 3.0 · main | "Camp Cloudtop was established to provide early warning of impending Dravanian incursions." | #245b |
| M245be | Ser Laniaitte of House Haillenarte commands Camp Cloudtop. | *Onwards and Upwards* · 3.0 · main | "I am Ser Laniaitte de Haillenarte, commander of the forces stationed here at Camp Cloudtop." | #245b |
| M245bf | The camp's great airship, the Protector, is under repair, hampered by skirmishes with the Vanu Vanu. | *Onwards and Upwards* · 3.0 · main | "our efforts have been hampered by an indigenous beast tribe known as the Vanu Vanu, with whom we have had several skirmishes already." | #245b |
| M245bg | Laniaitte is the elder sister of Francel, whose unjust execution you prevented. | *Onwards and Upwards* · 3.0 · main | "Lady Laniaitte explains that she is the elder sister of Lord [[Francel]], whose unjust execution you prevented during your search for the ''Enterprise'' long ago." | #245b |
| M245bh | Laniaitte thinks little of Emmanellain, who does not notice. | *Onwards and Upwards* · 3.0 · main | "though the young lord seems oblivious to her low opinion of him." | #245b |
| M245bi | Emmanellain resents being born second in a society where the eldest inherits all. | *Onwards and Upwards* · 3.0 · main | "What ill fortune to be born second in a society where the eldest inherits all." | #245b |
| M245c | The Holy See wills that the houses set aside their differences. | *Over the Wall* · 3.0 · main | "it is the will of the Holy See that we set aside our differences at this crucial juncture." | #245 |
| M245d | Falcon's Nest was abandoned in the Ishgardian withdrawal after the Calamity. | *Over the Wall* · 3.0 · main | "which was abandoned during the Ishgardian withdrawal shortly after [[the Calamity]]." | #245 |
| M245e | The Holy See has ordered the western highlands reclaimed and the heretics expelled. | *Over the Wall* · 3.0 · main | "The Holy See has commanded the reclamation of the western highlands and the expulsion of heretical elements." | #245 |
| M245f | Lady Iceheart and her kind have roamed the western highlands unchecked since then. | *Over the Wall* · 3.0 · main | "Lady Iceheart and her kind have walked these lands with impunity ever since." | #245 |
| M245g | Artoirel pledges House Fortemps to the Durendaire knight commanding Falcon's Nest. | *Over the Wall* · 3.0 · main | "As promised, we of House Fortemps are at your disposal, Ser Redwald." | #245 |
| M246a | House Dzemael's craftsmen are rebuilding Falcon's Nest. | *Work in Progress* · 3.0 · main | "Several craftsmen in service to House Dzemael are contributing to the restoration of Falcon's Nest." | #246 |
| M246b | You slay the icicle sprites sabotaging the work. | *Work in Progress* · 3.0 · main | "It would seem that Rothe was correct, as you discover and slay numerous icicle sprites within the walls of Falcon's Nest." | #246 |
| M246ba | Laniaitte keeps Emmanellain out of trouble with a watch for Dravanian outflyers. | *An Indispensable Ally* · 3.0 · main | "Tell him...tell him that I would like him to keep watch for...Dravanian outflyers." | #246b |
| M246bb | Laniaitte spares Emmanellain's feelings only for the close ties between Houses Haillenarte and Fortemps. | *An Indispensable Ally* · 3.0 · main | "Were it not for the close relationship between [[House Haillenarte]] and [[House Fortemps]], Lady Laniaitte would not feel obligated to spare Lord Emmanellain's feelings." | #246b |
| M247ba | After half a year of peace, the Vanu turned hostile for no known reason. | *Meeting the Neighbors* · 3.0 · main | "There was peace between our peoples for half a year, until suddenly, for reasons unknown, their attitudes towards us changed." | #247b |
| M247bb | The Vanu attack the camp's foragers unprovoked. | *Meeting the Neighbors* · 3.0 · main | "They began to attack our foraging parties without provocation, forcing us to rely more heavily on supplies flown in from Ishgard." | #247b |
| M247bc | You drive off Vanu on patrol. | *Meeting the Neighbors* · 3.0 · main | "As expected, you spy several Vanu during your patrol and are forced to drive them off." | #247b |
| M248a | A mason at the Pike and a knight at the Anvil look out for each other, though decorum keeps the knight from showing it. | *From on High* · 3.0 · main | "Torn between decorum and her desire to do right by the hardworking mason" | #248 |
| M248b | A sudden development has the commander of Falcon's Nest calling on everyone, you and Artoirel included. | *From on High* · 3.0 · main | "I have a task to which I would commit all able-bodied men and women─you and Lord Artoirel included." | #248 |
| M248ba | Camp Cloudtop has never recorded a Dravanian intrusion. | *Sense of Urgency* · 3.0 · main | "Despite being built as an observation post, we have yet to record a single intrusion by the Horde." | #248b |
| M248bb | Emmanellain demands a grand mission befitting his station. | *Sense of Urgency* · 3.0 · main | "Lady Laniaitte ''will'' give me a grand mission as befits my station!" | #248b |
| M249a | A reconnaissance party sent north to find the heretics' hideaway has gone silent. | *Reconnaissance Lost* · 3.0 · main | "We have lost contact with a reconnaissance party sent north to Riversmeet in search of the heretics' hideaway." | #249 |
| M249b | The sole survivor says heretics ambushed the party in a blizzard. | *Reconnaissance Lost* · 3.0 · main | "ambushed by the heretics in the midst of a furious blizzard" | #249 |
| M249ba | Laniaitte sends you and Emmanellain to fetch a spring crystal for water. | *Hope Springs Eternal* · 3.0 · main | "I would have you and Emmanellain bring one back to us." | #249b |
| M249bb | Emmanellain runs off, and does not come back. | *Hope Springs Eternal* · 3.0 · main | "It would seem you were the first to return and present him with an entry, for Lord Emmanellain is nowhere to be seen..." | #249b |
| M249c | The survivor says the heretics' hideaway lies to the north. | *Reconnaissance Lost* · 3.0 · main | "the heretics' hideaway lies somewhere to the north." | #249 |
| M249d | You and Artoirel save the knight. | *Reconnaissance Lost* · 3.0 · main | "Three heretics slain and one knight saved, just like that." | #249 |
| M250a | Artoirel sends you to follow the heretics' trail alone. | *At the End of Our Hope* · 3.0 · main | "Accordingly, Lord Artoirel assigns you the unenviable task of following the trail alone" | #250 |
| M250b | In a lair below Gorgagne Mills you meet an old foe, Lady Iceheart. | *At the End of Our Hope* · 3.0 · main | "Within the secret lair hidden below [[Gorgagne Mills]], you are reunited with an old foe: the Lady [[Iceheart]]." | #250 |
| M250ba | The Vanu Vanu capture Emmanellain. | *A Series of Unfortunate Events* · 3.0 · main | "Honoroit screams that Lord Emmanellain has been captured by the [[Vanu Vanu]]." | #250b |
| M250bb | Haurchefant arrives with knights to help free him, but the Vanu surround you. | *A Series of Unfortunate Events* · 3.0 · main | "who arrives in the nick of time with a party of knights … However, the Vanu are too numerous, and you soon find yourselves surrounded with no means of escape." | #250b |
| M250bc | The Vanu chieftain calls forth Bismarck, Lord of the Mists, a primal. | *A Series of Unfortunate Events* · 3.0 · main | "[[Bismarck]], Lord of the Mists, the chieftain names it, and Haurchefant realizes with horror that the creature is a [[primal]]." | #250b |
| M250bd | Cid swoops in aboard the Enterprise and carries you away. | *A Series of Unfortunate Events* · 3.0 · main | "swoops down aboard the ''Enterprise'' and spirits you away." | #250b |
| M250be | The Holy See has asked the Ironworks for help with its airship, the Protector. | *A Series of Unfortunate Events* · 3.0 · main | "The Holy See has petitioned the Ironworks for assistance with this creation of theirs, the ''Protector''." | #250b |
| M250bf | Cid promises to speak with you again soon. | *A Series of Unfortunate Events* · 3.0 · main | "Don't worry, old friend─we'll speak again soon, I promise." | #250b |
| M250c | An ancient wyrm in your company reveals himself to Iceheart, to her shock. | *At the End of Our Hope* · 3.0 · main | "reveals himself to the heretic leader, who is shocked to see the ancient wyrm in your company." | #250 |
| M250d | Iceheart has the Echo, which showed her the truth of the Dragonsong War through a great wyrm's eyes. | *At the End of Our Hope* · 3.0 · main | "she too is blessed with [[the Echo]], and that it allowed her to see the truth of the [[Dragonsong War]] through the eyes of [[Hraesvelgr]]" | #250 |
| M250e | Iceheart says she was chosen to bring dragon and man together again. | *At the End of Our Hope* · 3.0 · main | "I was chosen to deliver this revelation to the people─to bring dragon and man together, as they once were, and should ever be!" | #250 |
| M250f | Iceheart helped the Dravanians breach Ishgard's wards, and their slaughter leaves her doubting. | *At the End of Our Hope* · 3.0 · main | "the indiscriminate slaughter committed by the [[Dravanian Horde|Dravanians]] whom she helped to breach the wards, her doubts soon resurface" | #250 |
| M250g | Iceheart swears to break the cycle and restore peace. | *At the End of Our Hope* · 3.0 · main | "I will see this cycle broken and peace restored." | #250 |
| M250h | You let Iceheart escape. | *At the End of Our Hope* · 3.0 · main | "nor of the Lady Iceheart, whom you allowed to escape." | #250 |
| M251a | Falcon's Nest's commander releases you and Artoirel, and will report your deeds to the Holy See. | *Knights Be Not Proud* · 3.0 · main | "affirms his intent to deliver a glowing report to the [[Holy See]]" | #251 |
| M251b | Haurchefant is Artoirel's bastard half-brother. | *Knights Be Not Proud* · 3.0 · main | "Lord [[Haurchefant]]─his bastard half-brother─holds you in such high esteem." | #251 |
| M251ba | The Vanu have summoned a primal, which must lie behind their hostility. | *A Reward Long in Coming* · 3.0 · main | "Lady Laniaitte is shocked to hear that the Vanu have summoned a primal, and all agree that its presence must be behind the recent rise in Vanu hostility." | #251b |
| M251bb | Laniaitte praises you for rescuing Emmanellain from a doom of his own making. | *A Reward Long in Coming* · 3.0 · main | "praises you for rescuing the young lord from a doom of his own making." | #251b |
| M251bc | Haurchefant goes to tell Ser Aymeric of the Congregation what happened. | *A Reward Long in Coming* · 3.0 · main | "I must make for the Congregation and inform Ser Aymeric of all that has occurred." | #251b |
| M251bd | Edmont scolds Emmanellain for his recklessness. | *A Reward Long in Coming* · 3.0 · main | "having received a dressing down from his father for his recklessness." | #251b |
| M251c | Artoirel calls Haurchefant the legacy of their father's only indiscretion, whom his mother never acknowledged. | *Knights Be Not Proud* · 3.0 · main | "the legacy of my father's sole indiscretion. … Even on her deathbed, she refused to acknowledge him." | #251 |
| M251d | Artoirel admits he half hoped you would fail. | *Knights Be Not Proud* · 3.0 · main | "I told myself it was to be a test, but part of me...part of me hoped that you would fail." | #251 |
| M251e | Artoirel swears never to doubt you again. | *Knights Be Not Proud* · 3.0 · main | "swears never to doubt you again." | #251 |
| M259a | Ser Grinnaux of the Heavens' Ward has arrested Alphinaud and Tataru for fomenting heresy. | *Divine Intervention* · 3.0 · main | "have been arrested by a knight of the [[Heavens' Ward]]—one “[[Ser Grinnaux]]”—on the charge of fomenting heresy." | #259 |
| M259b | The Heavens' Ward is the archbishop's personal guard: twelve knights who answer only to him. | *Divine Intervention* · 3.0 · main | "the personal guard of the [[Thordan VII|archbishop]]. These twelve chosen sers answer to none save the archbishop himself, and their testimony is unimpeachable." | #259 |
| M259c | Only a trial by combat can disprove the charge. | *Divine Intervention* · 3.0 · main | "by granting victory in a trial by combat between the accuser and the accused." | #259 |
| M259d | Grinnaux is a son of House Dzemael. | *Divine Intervention* · 3.0 · main | "Alas, he is also a son of House Dzemael." | #259 |
| M259e | Edmont suspects another member of House Dzemael is manipulating Grinnaux. | *Divine Intervention* · 3.0 · main | "'Tis like that one such member of House Dzemael is manipulating Ser Grinnaux, for he is not a man to dabble in politics of his own volition." | #259 |
| M259f | Edmont believes the charges are a political ploy against House Fortemps. | *Divine Intervention* · 3.0 · main | "The count believes the charges leveled against your friends to be nothing more than a political ploy to defame [[House Fortemps]]." | #259 |
| M259g | Aymeric, the lord commander, believes them innocent but cannot overrule the Heavens' Ward. | *Divine Intervention* · 3.0 · main | "Though Ser Aymeric believes your friends to be innocent, he is powerless to intervene as the knight who accused them is a member of the Heavens' Ward" | #259 |
| M259h | Edmont sends you to the lord commander, Ser Aymeric. | *Divine Intervention* · 3.0 · main | "pay the lord commander a visit at the [[Congregation of Our Knights Most Heavenly]]" | #259 |
| M259i | You defeat Grinnaux and his second in the trial, and your friends go free. | *Divine Intervention* · 3.0 · main | "you defeat Ser Grinnaux and [[Ser Paulecrain]], thus earning your comrades their freedom." | #259 |
| M259j | Haurchefant gives you a black chocobo that can fly. | *Divine Intervention* · 3.0 · main | "a fully trained [[Black Chocobo|black chocobo]]" | #259 |
| M259k | Edmont has heard of all you did at Falcon's Nest and Camp Cloudtop. | *Divine Intervention* · 3.0 · main | "Having been informed of all you accomplished during your time in [[Falcon's Nest]] and [[Camp Cloudtop]], Count [[Edmont]] wishes to express his heartfelt gratitude." | #259 |
| M260a | Thordan VII apologizes for your friends' wrongful arrest. | *Disclosure* · 3.0 · main | "Thordan VII warmly welcomes you and apologizes for the misunderstanding that led to Alphinaud and Tataru's incarceration." | #260 |
| M260b | Ser Zephirin, of the Heavens' Ward, admits the Ward acted on false information. | *Disclosure* · 3.0 · main | "Regrettably, it would appear that we of the Heavens' Ward were in receipt of erroneous information." | #260 |
| M260c | Thordan says the Ascians offered Ishgard power to keep fighting the dragons. | *Disclosure* · 3.0 · main | "Those harbingers of chaos and strife offered us power, that we might continue our war against the dragons." | #260 |
| M260d | Thordan says he pays the Ascians lip service while plotting against them, and asks your help. | *Disclosure* · 3.0 · main | "he is paying lip service to their wishes while plotting to move against them. He asks for your help in this endeavor" | #260 |
| M260e | Alphinaud is shocked that the Ascians' influence reaches Ishgard. | *Disclosure* · 3.0 · main | "Alphinaud is shocked to learn that the Ascians' influence has also spread to [[Ishgard]]." | #260 |
| M260f | Raubahn, the deposed Flame General, is to be executed, and Alphinaud resolves to rescue him. | *Disclosure* · 3.0 · main | "It would seem that [[Raubahn]] is to be executed for crimes against the sultanate. Determined not to lose yet another ally, Alphinaud resolves to do everything in his power to rescue the deposed Flame General." | #260 |
| M261a | Merlwyb remains a stout ally of the Scions. | *Flame General Affairs* · 3.0 · main | "who remains a stout ally to the [[Scions]]" | #261 |
| M261b | The Crystal Braves have moved Raubahn somewhere unknown ahead of his execution. | *Flame General Affairs* · 3.0 · main | "[[Raubahn]] has been moved to an unknown location ahead of his impending execution—by none other than the [[Crystal Braves]]." | #261 |
| M261c | Merlwyb and the Elder Seedseer made the Syndicate keep the Scions' alleged crimes quiet until proof is found. | *Flame General Affairs* · 3.0 · main | "On Marshal Tarupin's urging, the Elder Seedseer and I demanded that the Syndicate suppress news of the Scions' alleged crimes until such time as irrefutable evidence could be found." | #261 |
| M261d | The sultana meant to abdicate and make way for an Ul'dahn republic. | *Flame General Affairs* · 3.0 · main | "There, she revealed her intent to announce her abdication, that she might pave the way for the establishment of an Ul'dahn republic." | #261 |
| M261e | Alphinaud suspects Lolorito and Teledji plotted the sultana's assassination. | *Flame General Affairs* · 3.0 · main | "Lolorito and Teledji plotted the sultana's assassination in the hope of maintaining the constitutional status quo...?" | #261 |
| M261f | Ul'dah claims the sultana is convalescing from illness. | *Flame General Affairs* · 3.0 · main | "To allay any suspicion that may arise from Her Grace's absence, they have issued a statement to the effect that she is presently convalescing from illness." | #261 |
| M261g | Merlwyb cannot send troops into Ul'dah without ending the Alliance. | *Flame General Affairs* · 3.0 · main | "But I cannot send troops into Ul'dah. 'Twould spell the end of the Alliance, and war, like as not." | #261 |
| M261h | Alphinaud takes responsibility for the Crystal Braves he founded. | *Flame General Affairs* · 3.0 · main | "It was I who founded the Crystal Braves, and I who must take responsibility for their actions." | #261 |
| M261i | The Crystal Braves turned on the Domans at the victory feast and took the Rising Stones by force. | *Flame General Affairs* · 3.0 · main | "Even as the victory feast was taking place, the Crystal Braves turned upon us and took the Rising Stones by force." | #261 |
| M261j | The shinobi of Doma are gathering intelligence on Raubahn's whereabouts. | *Flame General Affairs* · 3.0 · main | "the shinobi of [[Doma]] have been frantically gathering intelligence on the Flame General's whereabouts" | #261 |
| M262a | A unit of Crystal Braves has left the Rising Stones to escort Raubahn to his execution. | *In Search of Raubahn* · 3.0 · main | "Their purpose: to escort [[Raubahn]] to his execution." | #262 |
| M262b | The Domans are shadowing the Braves. | *In Search of Raubahn* · 3.0 · main | "This can be no simple coincidence. Their purpose, we believe, is to escort the Flame General to the scene of his execution." | #262 |
| M262c | Ilberd, the traitor, has entered Halatali. | *In Search of Raubahn* · 3.0 · main | "But moments ago, the traitor Ilberd arrived and entered Halatali." | #262 |
| M263a | With Alphinaud and Yugiri you rescue Raubahn from the Crystal Braves. | *Keeping the Flame Alive* · 3.0 · main | "With the aid of [[Alphinaud]] and [[Yugiri]], you have succeeded in rescuing Raubahn from the clutches of the traitorous [[Crystal Braves]]." | #263 |
| M263b | Ilberd denies killing the sultana. | *Keeping the Flame Alive* · 3.0 · main | "'Twas not I who assassinated the sultana, boy!" | #263 |
| M263c | Ilberd says all he wanted was to free his homeland. | *Keeping the Flame Alive* · 3.0 · main | "All I ever wanted was to liberate my homeland, and I ate dirt to make it happen." | #263 |
| M263d | Dewlala, head of the Order of Nald'thal, is a member of the Syndicate. | *Keeping the Flame Alive* · 3.0 · main | "I am Dewlala, head of the Order of Nald'thal, and member of the Syndicate." | #263 |
| M263e | Dewlala confirms Nanamo is still alive, perhaps held in an induced slumber. | *Keeping the Flame Alive* · 3.0 · main | "she confirms Alphinaud's suspicion that [[Nanamo]] is still alive, and goes on to speculate that the sultana may be confined somewhere in a state of induced slumber." | #263 |
| M263f | Dewlala says Nanamo lives because Lolorito willed it. | *Keeping the Flame Alive* · 3.0 · main | "She lives because Lolorito willed it." | #263 |
| M263g | Dewlala believes Teledji plotted the assassination alone and Lolorito exploited it. | *Keeping the Flame Alive* · 3.0 · main | "'Tis my belief that Teledji plotted the sultana's assassination alone, but that Lolorito caught wind of his plot, and exploited it to his own ends." | #263 |
| M263h | Raubahn is brought to the Waking Sands. | *Keeping the Flame Alive* · 3.0 · main | "Upon arriving at the Waking Sands, you are greeted by a number of familiar faces" | #263 |
| M264a | The Dravanian Horde gathers to renew its assault on Ishgard. | *To Siege or Not to Siege* · 3.0 · main | "It would seem the [[Dravanian Horde|Horde]] gathers once more to renew its assault upon the city." | #264 |
| M264b | Raubahn is in safe hands. | *To Siege or Not to Siege* · 3.0 · main | "Tataru? ...Yes, General Raubahn is now in safe hands." | #264 |
| M264c | The Scions lack the evidence to act on Dewlala's claims. | *To Siege or Not to Siege* · 3.0 · main | "we lack the necessary evidence to act upon Dewlala's claims." | #264 |
| M264d | Trappers saw a cloud of dragons big enough to dim the sky. | *To Siege or Not to Siege* · 3.0 · main | "a band of trappers returning from the west claim they saw a cloud of dragons big enough to dim the midday sky!" | #264 |
| M264e | Nidhogg's minions are gathering for war. | *To Siege or Not to Siege* · 3.0 · main | "Nidhogg's minions gather once more for war..." | #264 |
| M264f | Edmont offers the Scions the choice of staying in a besieged Ishgard or seeking refuge elsewhere. | *To Siege or Not to Siege* · 3.0 · main | "Scions of the Seventh Dawn, you are faced with a choice: to remain in a besieged Ishgard, or seek sanctuary elsewhere." | #264 |
| M264g | Alphinaud is tired of running. | *To Siege or Not to Siege* · 3.0 · main | "The count would have us seek shelter from this storm, but I, for one, grow tired of running." | #264 |
| M265a | Alphinaud hopes Iceheart can persuade Nidhogg to abandon his course. | *Alphinaud's Way* · 3.0 · main | "If we can persuade Iceheart to act as our intermediary, we may yet be able to convince Nidhogg to abandon his bloody course." | #265 |
| M265b | Estinien, Ishgard's Azure Dragoon, insists on joining the parley with Nidhogg. | *Alphinaud's Way* · 3.0 · main | "you are approached by [[Estinien]], [[Ishgard]]'s famed [[Azure Dragoon]]. He insists on accompanying you to the proposed parley with [[Nidhogg]]" | #265 |
| M265c | Alphinaud agrees on condition that Estinien uses his lance only if words fail. | *Alphinaud's Way* · 3.0 · main | "I should only turn to your lance if my words failed to find their mark." | #265 |
| M265d | Estinien carries the power of the Eye. | *Alphinaud's Way* · 3.0 · main | "With the power of the Eye at my disposal, and the vaunted strength of the Warrior of Light, we could conceivably slay the beast outright..." | #265 |
| M265e | Aymeric will support any effort that could spare his countrymen's blood. | *Alphinaud's Way* · 3.0 · main | "I will gladly lend my support to any endeavor that could spare the blood of my countrymen" | #265 |
| M265f | Thordan tells Zephirin they will reward their Ascian allies once the dragons are gone. | *Alphinaud's Way* · 3.0 · main | "And when we have rid the world of their pestilence, we shall turn our attention to our Ascian allies─see that they are suitably rewarded for their invaluable assistance." | #265 |
| M265g | Zephirin worries the Paragons may see through their deception. | *Alphinaud's Way* · 3.0 · main | "The Paragons wield powers strange and unknowable. Can we be certain that they will not see through our deception?" | #265 |
| M265h | Zephirin, in a changed voice, swears by the glory of King Thordan. | *Alphinaud's Way* · 3.0 · main | "For the glory of King Thordan..." | #265 |
| M266a | Alphinaud begins the search for Iceheart in the western highlands, where you last met her. | *In Search of Iceheart* · 3.0 · main | "Alphinaud proposes that you begin the search for Lady [[Iceheart]] in the last place you encountered her" | #266 |
| M266b | Tataru keeps searching for the missing Scions. | *In Search of Iceheart* · 3.0 · main | "promises to continue her search for the missing [[Scions]] in your absence." | #266 |
| M266c | Knights are picking over the heretics' abandoned hideaway at Gorgagne Mills. | *In Search of Iceheart* · 3.0 · main | "an expedition sent to pick over the remnants of the heretic hideaway you discovered at [[Gorgagne Mills]]." | #266 |
| M267a | The heretics' letters show Iceheart's followers still use the Akh Afah Amphitheatre as a base. | *From One Heretic to Another* · 3.0 · main | "it would seem the amphitheatre is still being used as a base by Iceheart's minions..." | #267 |
| M268a | The heretics at the amphitheatre refuse to parley and attack. | *Sounding Out the Amphitheatre* · 3.0 · main | "the heretics are not inclined to trust his word, and the encounter quickly devolves into violence" | #268 |
| M268b | The heretics drink the blood of dragons. | *Sounding Out the Amphitheatre* · 3.0 · main | "They partake of the blood of dragons!" | #268 |
| M268c | Iceheart is not at the amphitheatre. | *Sounding Out the Amphitheatre* · 3.0 · main | "I see no sign of Lady Iceheart." | #268 |
| M268d | Estinien sends you to the Convictors at Twinpools. | *Sounding Out the Amphitheatre* · 3.0 · main | "I am told the Convictors have set up camp in Twinpools." | #268 |
| M269a | Heretics win lowborn conscripts with the promise of freedom. | *Camp of the Convictors* · 3.0 · main | "From what I hear, the simple promise of freedom tends to do it." | #269 |
| M269b | Purple smoke in the highlands summons the heretics. | *Camp of the Convictors* · 3.0 · main | "Send up some purple smoke, and the bastards come to you." | #269 |
| M269c | Alphinaud thinks that promise explains how fast Iceheart's following grew. | *Camp of the Convictors* · 3.0 · main | "That may explain how Lady Iceheart was able to expand her following so swiftly." | #269 |
| M270a | Iceheart tells how an Elezen tribe came to claim Coerthas over a millennium ago. | *Purple Flame, Purple Flame* · 3.0 · main | "'Twas more than a millennium past when an Elezen tribe first sought to claim the lands of Coerthas as its home." | #270 |
| M270b | In her tale, the maid Shiva asked the wyrm Hraesvelgr to consume her so their spirits would be joined forever. | *Purple Flame, Purple Flame* · 3.0 · main | "Unable to bear the thought of their separation, the maid bid the wyrm consume her, that their spirits might be entwined for eternity." | #270 |
| M270c | Iceheart says Nidhogg lost an eye to Elezen treachery. | *Purple Flame, Purple Flame* · 3.0 · main | "Nidhogg─he who now stands poised to unleash his wyrmlings upon Ishgard─was the great dragon who lost an eye to Elezen treachery." | #270 |
| M270d | Estinien kept the Eye with him to draw Nidhogg away from Ishgard. | *Purple Flame, Purple Flame* · 3.0 · main | "Indeed, it was for that very reason that I kept it with me as I roamed the land, attempting to draw him away from the city." | #270 |
| M270e | Iceheart agrees to take you to Hraesvelgr. | *Purple Flame, Purple Flame* · 3.0 · main | "You ''still'' believe that a peaceable solution can be found? Very well. I will take you to him." | #270 |
| M270f | Midgardsormr notes that his mistress's blessing on you is stronger than it was. | *Purple Flame, Purple Flame* · 3.0 · main | "Curious... The vestiges of thy mistress's blessing are not as faint as once they were..." | #270 |
| M270g | Your Crystal of Ice flares as you set out for the dragons' homeland. | *Purple Flame, Purple Flame* · 3.0 · main | "As you resolve to depart for the dragons' homeland, the light of your Crystal of Ice suddenly flares once more..." | #270 |
| M271a | Iceheart asks you to use her true name, Ysayle. | *Where the Chocobos Roam* · 3.0 · main | "Lady [[Iceheart]] invites you to use her true name, [[Ysayle]]," | #271 |
| M271b | Sohm Al is the ancient home of dragonkind. | *Where the Chocobos Roam* · 3.0 · main | "Into the deepening shadows of Sohm Al, where lies the ancient home of dragonkind..." | #271 |
| M271c | The path to Sohm Al lies far to the west through Dravania. | *Where the Chocobos Roam* · 3.0 · main | "We now stand on the threshold of Dravania. The path to Sohm Al will take us far to the west" | #271 |
| M271d | The dragons have been murderous of late. | *Where the Chocobos Roam* · 3.0 · main | "The dragons've been absolutely bleedin' ''murderous'' of late." | #271 |
| M272a | The Gnath, a beast tribe, have turned to unprovoked hostility. | *Worse than Dragons* · 3.0 · main | "tells you of the [[beast tribe]] known as the [[Gnath]], and of their recent acts of unprovoked hostility." | #272 |
| M272b | Ysayle fled into Dravania after the Calamity, her family dead and her home gone. | *Worse than Dragons* · 3.0 · main | "My family was dead, my home was gone, and I had fled from the bitter cold into these lands." | #272 |
| M272c | Ysayle says the ruins date from an age when her ancestors and dragonkind lived in peace. | *Worse than Dragons* · 3.0 · main | "It is a remnant of the age when our ancestors and dragonkind lived together in peace." | #272 |
| M272d | Estinien was taught the ruins were built by heretics. | *Worse than Dragons* · 3.0 · main | "I was taught that these buildings were constructed by heretics, in honor of your Dravanian masters." | #272 |
| M272e | Alphinaud says the Echo's visions will settle their dispute. | *Worse than Dragons* · 3.0 · main | "he declares that their debate will presently be settled by the indisputable visions granted you by the Echo." | #272 |
| M273a | Without the help of the dragons at Anyx Trine, the way to Sohm Al's summit stays closed. | *The Trine Towers* · 3.0 · main | "Without their cooperation, she claims, the way to [[Sohm Al]]'s summit will remain closed." | #273 |
| M273b | Vidofnir must stay to protect her kin from the Gnath's god. | *The Trine Towers* · 3.0 · main | "I am bound to remain here, and protect my kin from the Gnath's god." | #273 |
| M273c | The Gnath act under the influence of a primal. | *The Trine Towers* · 3.0 · main | "Suddenly, the [[beastmen]]'s aggression begins to make sense—they act under the influence of a [[primal]]." | #273 |
| M273d | Vidofnir knows Estinien carries Nidhogg's stolen eye. | *The Trine Towers* · 3.0 · main | "when thine own companion beareth Nidhogg's stolen eye!?" | #273 |
| M273e | Alphinaud offers to remove the threat in return for Vidofnir's help. | *The Trine Towers* · 3.0 · main | "if we were to eliminate the threat to your territory, would you consent to Lady Ysayle's request?" | #273 |
| M274a | Ysayle says the Gnath have summoned their god. | *Gifts for the Outcasts* · 3.0 · main | "So, the Gnath have summoned their god... And thus is the mystery of their newfound belligerence laid to rest." | #274 |
| M274b | Ysayle insists she called on Saint Shiva for peace, not for her own ends. | *Gifts for the Outcasts* · 3.0 · main | "I did ''not'' reach out to Saint Shiva to further mine own ends!" | #274 |
| M274c | Outcast Gnath to the northwest trade with Tailfeather's hunters. | *Gifts for the Outcasts* · 3.0 · main | "From time to time, we barter with a small hive to the north and west of here─outcasts from the main tribe, from what I understand." | #274 |
| M275a | The Vath are the Nonmind, outside the Onemind of the Gnath. | *The Nonmind* · 3.0 · main | "You must speak to our chosen leader in words, for we are Vath. We are the Nonmind." | #275 |
| M275b | The Overmind heeded black-robed men and summoned the god against the dragons. | *The Nonmind* · 3.0 · main | "Thus did the Overmind decide to heed the words of the black-robed men." | #275 |
| M275c | The Gnath mean to make Dravanian lands their own. | *The Nonmind* · 3.0 · main | "Dravanian lands would become Gnath lands, and ever larger would grow our domain." | #275 |
| M275d | The god demands more crystals than the war yields. | *The Nonmind* · 3.0 · main | "The price He demands in crystals far exceeds the rewards for this war." | #275 |
| M276a | The Overmind binds the senses of every Gnath of the Onemind. | *A Gnathic Deity* · 3.0 · main | "The senses of the Onemind are enmeshed by the Overmind─what one sees, all see; what one feels, all feel." | #276 |
| M276b | Captives taken near the colony are brought before the Gnath's god. | *A Gnathic Deity* · 3.0 · main | "Should we allow ourselves to be captured in the environs of the colony, the Gnath will bring us before their deity to make an offering of our souls..." | #276 |
| M277a | Alphinaud and Estinien stay behind, unprotected from the primal, while you and Ysayle stage an assault. | *Breaking into Hives* · 3.0 · main | "Lacking any protection from the primal's influence, Alphinaud and [[Estinien]] will remain behind while you and Ysayle mount a mock assault upon the hive." | #277 |
| M278a | Ravana, Master of the Sacred Blades and Wrath of the Colony, comes to claim his sacrifices. | *Lord of the Hive* · 3.0 · main | "Lord [[Ravana]], Master of the Sacred Blades and Wrath of the Colony, arrives to claim his sacrifices." | #278 |
| M278b | The Gnath's prayers for the Dravanians' death gave Ravana form. | *Lord of the Hive* · 3.0 · main | "The Gnath would see the Dravanians slain, and their territory secured─and by such fervent prayers am I now given form and purpose." | #278 |
| M278c | Ysayle challenges Ravana to withdraw his soldiers from Dravanian soil if she wins. | *Lord of the Hive* · 3.0 · main | "And should we emerge the victors, I would have you swear to withdraw your soldiers from Dravanian soil!" | #278 |
| M278d | Ysayle draws on the Gnath's crystals to become Saint Shiva. | *Lord of the Hive* · 3.0 · main | "Ysayle draws upon the energy of the Gnath's hoarded crystals to transform herself into Saint [[Shiva]], and battle is joined." | #278 |
| M278e | Ravana, defeated, vows to call his followers back from the dragons' domain. | *Lord of the Hive* · 3.0 · main | "vows to call his followers back from the dragons' domain." | #278 |
| M278f | A voice warns that warring gods will shake the world into chaos. | *Lord of the Hive* · 3.0 · main | "At their bidding, warring gods will shake the firmament, and your world will be consumed in the swelling storm of chaos..." | #278 |
| M279a | Vidofnir opens the way through Mourn to Sohm Al's summit. | *Mourn in Passing* · 3.0 · main | "hie you unto the deepest reaches of Mourn, and there shall you find Halo─the altar from which you may begin your ascent to the summit of Sohm Al." | #279 |
| M279b | You defeat Tioman, Nidhogg's consort. | *Mourn in Passing* · 3.0 · main | "You have defeated [[Tioman]], the consort of Nidhogg" | #279 |
| M279c | Nidhogg's fury bleeds through the Eye into Estinien. | *Mourn in Passing* · 3.0 · main | "His fury... <gasp> It bleeds through the Eye." | #279 |
| M279d | Vidofnir warns Estinien to keep his stolen power in check. | *Mourn in Passing* · 3.0 · main | "have care to keep the stolen power thou wieldest in check, lest it breed further despair." | #279 |
| M279e | You reach the summit of Sohm Al. | *Mourn in Passing* · 3.0 · main | "You have arrived at the summit of Sohm Al." | #279 |
| M280a | You still must find Hraesvelgr. | *Beyond the Clouds* · 3.0 · main | "Now that we have scaled the mountain, there remains the problem of finding Hraesvelgr himself..." | #280 |
| M280b | A moogle on the summit may know where Hraesvelgr lairs. | *Beyond the Clouds* · 3.0 · main | "If the moogle ''does'' live here, however, he may know where Hraesvelgr makes his lair." | #280 |
| M280c | The moogle vanishes, perhaps hidden by magic. | *Beyond the Clouds* · 3.0 · main | "Could he be concealing himself with magic...?" | #280 |
| M281a | Kan-E-Senna, the Elder Seedseer, joins you as Gridania's ambassador. | *Mountaintop Diplomacy* · 3.0 · main | "If you will have me, I too will accompany you on this venture, and lend my voice to your cause as an ambassador of Gridania." | #281 |
| M281b | Estinien watches Nidhogg, who senses the loss of his consort and the coming of the Eye. | *Mountaintop Diplomacy* · 3.0 · main | "Nidhogg senses both the loss of his consort and the coming of the Eye, and his attention is firmly fixed in this direction." | #281 |
| M281c | Hraesvelgr is Nidhogg's brood-brother. | *Mountaintop Diplomacy* · 3.0 · main | "I can only identify Nidhogg's with any certainty. We could wander the Mists for days, and not know if we were any closer to his brood-brother..." | #281 |
| M281d | Moglin is chieftain of the moogles of the Churning Mists. | *Mountaintop Diplomacy* · 3.0 · main | "I am Moglin, the chieftain of this moogle clan." | #281 |
| M281e | The moogles hid because they smelled dragon blood. | *Mountaintop Diplomacy* · 3.0 · main | "We hid because we smelled dragon blood." | #281 |
| M281f | Moglin says some dragons, like Hraesvelgr, crave peace, and others, like Nidhogg, the opposite. | *Mountaintop Diplomacy* · 3.0 · main | "While some crave peace, such as Hraesvelgr, many others crave the opposite...such as Nidhogg." | #281 |
| M281g | Moglin sets trials to judge you worthy of his help. | *Mountaintop Diplomacy* · 3.0 · main | "It seems that Moglin has devised a number of trials by which he shall judge you worthy of his assistance." | #281 |
| M282a | For Moghan, an alchemist, you gather cloudsbreath for a pom tonic. | *Moghan's Trial* · 3.0 · main | "Oh, yes, these are perfect, kupo! Now I have the main ingredient for my pom tonic." | #282 |
| M282ba | For Mogmug you humble the archaeosaurs pursuing him. | *Mogmug's Trial* · 3.0 · main | "You were able to humble my Dravanian devotees? Wonderful!" | #282b |
| M282ca | For Mogwin you find his painted pebble on Sohm Al's summit. | *Mogwin's Trial* · 3.0 · main | "You search Sohm Al's summit and find Mogwin's painted pebble." | #282c |
| M285a | The trials only kept you busy while the moogles repaired the horn that summons Hraesvelgr. | *Moglin's Judgment* · 3.0 · main | "a means to keep you and your companions occupied while the moogles repaired the horn required to summon [[Hraesvelgr]]." | #285 |
| M285b | The horn must be blown at Zenith, the wyrm's dwelling to the west. | *Moglin's Judgment* · 3.0 · main | "Now, you need simply head to Zenith─the wyrm's dwelling to the west─and give the horn a good toot." | #285 |
| M285c | Your friendship with a Twelveswood moogle earned the moogles' trust. | *Moglin's Judgment* · 3.0 · main | "You were, after all, already friends with a fellow moogle." | #285 |
| M286a | Kan-E-Senna warns that a storm of anguish seethes beneath Estinien's calm. | *Leaving Moghome* · 3.0 · main | "Pray attend closely to your companion, Ser Estinien─beneath that stoical facade, a storm of anguish seethes unseen." | #286 |
| M286b | You bid farewell to Kan-E-Senna and her moogle companion as they leave. | *Leaving Moghome* · 3.0 · main | "before bidding them both a fond farewell. As the Elder Seedseer turns to leave" | #286 |
| M287a | Moghan guides you toward Zenith, warning the road will be fought over. | *The Road to Zenith* · 3.0 · main | "but he warns that the road will not be traveled without conflict." | #287 |
| M287b | Moghan is unsettled by the dragons' unusual aggression. | *The Road to Zenith* · 3.0 · main | "Though Moghan seems unsettled by the dragons' unusual aggression, your journey to Zenith is all but at an end." | #287 |
| M288a | High winds force you to camp and wait. | *Waiting for the Wind to Change* · 3.0 · main | "It would seem you have no choice but to make yourself comfortable, and wait for the weather to turn." | #288 |
| M288b | Ysayle vowed to tear down the Holy See and its falsehoods after meeting Hraesvelgr. | *Waiting for the Wind to Change* · 3.0 · main | "I would never have vowed to tear down the Holy See, and expunge the falsehoods with which it fans the flames of conflict." | #288 |
| M288c | Alphinaud says his hubris invited the betrayal that brought down the Scions. | *Waiting for the Wind to Change* · 3.0 · main | "That hubris invited manipulation and betrayal...and led to the downfall of the Scions." | #288 |
| M288d | Nidhogg killed Estinien's family. | *Waiting for the Wind to Change* · 3.0 · main | "Nidhogg killed my family, and no quirk of history will exonerate him." | #288 |
| M288e | Estinien's word holds only if the parley succeeds. | *Waiting for the Wind to Change* · 3.0 · main | "My word is contingent upon the success of our parley." | #288 |
| M289a | Hraesvelgr says the spirit Ysayle summons is only a shade of her own fancy, not Shiva. | *Heart of Ice* · 3.0 · main | "The spirit thou hast summoned belongeth not to Shiva─'tis but a shade conjured by thine own fancy." | #289 |
| M289b | King Thordan and his knights ambushed Hraesvelgr's brood-sister Ratatoskr and took what he craved from her. | *Heart of Ice* · 3.0 · main | "With the aid of his leal knights, that most noble of Elezen lured my brood-sister Ratatoskr into an ambush, and took from her that which he craved." | #289 |
| M289c | Nidhogg slew Thordan and some of his knights in vengeance. | *Heart of Ice* · 3.0 · main | "But though he slew Thordan and some few of his knights, their fellow butchers knew only too well where to aim their spears" | #289 |
| M289d | That, says Hraesvelgr, began the Dragonsong War, and the Holy See hides it. | *Heart of Ice* · 3.0 · main | "Such is the truth the Holy See would keep hidden. Such was the beginning of the Dragonsong War." | #289 |
| M289e | Heretics who drink dragon blood become dragons in Nidhogg's army. | *Heart of Ice* · 3.0 · main | "those who abandon their loyalties are rewarded with a drink. And thus are they transformed, to join the ranks of Nidhogg's ever-growing army as newborn dragons." | #289 |
| M289f | Alphinaud concludes nothing will deter Nidhogg. | *Heart of Ice* · 3.0 · main | "Nothing will deter Nidhogg from his course." | #289 |
| M290a | Nidhogg's lair, the Aery, lies to the east. | *The Wyrm's Lair* · 3.0 · main | "Nidhogg's lair─the Aery─is said to lie in that direction." | #290 |
| M290b | With the parley failed, Estinien means to kill Nidhogg to save Ishgard. | *The Wyrm's Lair* · 3.0 · main | "But your efforts at parley have come to naught, Lady Iceheart. I would see Ishgard saved, and for that, Nidhogg must die." | #290 |
| M290c | Ysayle is beyond reach, and Estinien leaves her behind. | *The Wyrm's Lair* · 3.0 · main | "Leave her. Your words will not reach her now." | #290 |
| M290d | Nidhogg has walled the Aery behind impenetrable winds. | *The Wyrm's Lair* · 3.0 · main | "You have at last discovered the Aery, but Nidhogg has surrounded his lair with a barrier of impenetrable winds." | #290 |
| M290e | Alphinaud proposes calling on Cid. | *The Wyrm's Lair* · 3.0 · main | "Mayhap it is time we called upon Master Garlond." | #290 |
| M291a | Estinien keeps watch on the Aery until you are ready to set out together. | *New Winds, Old Friends* · 3.0 · main | "Contact me by linkpearl when your preparations are complete, and we shall set forth from Ishgard together." | #291 |
| M291b | Cid is in Ishgard, working on the Holy See's ill-fated airship. | *New Winds, Old Friends* · 3.0 · main | "the latest gossip surrounding Cid and the Holy See's ill-fated [[airship]]." | #291 |
| M291c | The Enterprise is too big to dodge dragons. | *New Winds, Old Friends* · 3.0 · main | "The Dravanians would fly rings around her." | #291 |

| M291e | Tataru brings news of the sultana, and Cid sends you to Ul'dah while the manacutter is finished. | *New Winds, Old Friends* · 3.0 · main | "The receptionist has news of the [[Nanamo Ul Namo|sultana]]! Noting the urgency of the situation, Cid bids you concentrate on matters in [[Ul'dah]] while he and his engineers continue work on the manacutter." | #291 |

| M292d | Lolorito had Meriel give the sultana a sleeping potion in place of poison. | *A General Summons* · 3.0 · main | "Thus did I employ young Meriel here to administer a potent sleeping potion in place of a poison." | #292 |
| M292e | Lolorito had Ilberd lie about the assassination to turn Raubahn's rage on Teledji. | *A General Summons* · 3.0 · main | "I had him lie about the assassination as a means to prime your rage against Teledji." | #292 |
| M292f | Lolorito gives Raubahn the means to wake the sultana. | *A General Summons* · 3.0 · main | "he then presents the Flame General with the means to rouse the sultana from her deathlike state" | #292 |
| M292g | Raubahn is reinstated as general of the Immortal Flames. | *A General Summons* · 3.0 · main | "you are hereby reinstated as general of the Immortal Flames!" | #292 |
| M292h | Teledji's Carteneau Reclamation Bill was a facade for seizing the Allagan machine Omega. | *A General Summons* · 3.0 · main | "Teledji Adeledji's Carteneau Reclamation Bill was no more than a facade" | #292 |
| M292i | Raubahn executed Teledji without trial. | *A General Summons* · 3.0 · main | "Or have you forgotten your ''own'' crime in executing Teledji Adeledji without trial!?" | #292 |

| M293b | Nanamo will soon resume her plan to place Ul'dah's government in its citizens' hands. | *Awakening in Ul'dah* · 3.0 · main | "I believe Her Grace will soon resume her plans to place the government of Ul'dah into the hands of its citizens." | #293 |
| M293c | Lolorito reports that the Empire has built a warship to rival the Agrius. | *Awakening in Ul'dah* · 3.0 · main | "has built a warship to rival the might of the legendary" | #293 |
| M293d | Lolorito urges repairing Teledji's damage and preparing to repel the Empire. | *Awakening in Ul'dah* · 3.0 · main | "I move that it is time to repair the damage caused by Teledji Adeledji, and prepare our great nation to repel the Empire once more." | #293 |

| M293f | Those who sided with Ilberd will be hunted down and held to account. | *Awakening in Ul'dah* · 3.0 · main | "As for those who sided with the traitor, Ilberd, they shall be hunted down and held to account for their crimes." | #293 |
| M293g | Alphinaud will henceforth be simply a member of the Scions. | *Awakening in Ul'dah* · 3.0 · main | "Henceforth, I shall be no more or less than Alphinaud, proud member of the Scions." | #293 |

| M338c | Krile's plan to find Thancred needs Matoya's Crystal Eye. | *Another Time, Another Place* · 3.1 · main | "she explains that she has a plan to locate Thancred. However, it will require the use of Master [[Matoya]]'s Crystal Eye" | #338 |

| M338e | Krile is recently come from the Sharlayan homeland. | *Another Time, Another Place* · 3.1 · main | "Pray allow me to introduce Krile, who is recently come from the Sharlayan motherland." | #338 |
| M338f | Krile hopes to follow a trail Thancred left when Y'shtola sent him away. | *Another Time, Another Place* · 3.1 · main | "Simply put, assuming Thancred left some manner of trail when you whisked him away" | #338 |
| M338g | The Crystal Eye is one of Matoya's most cherished relics. | *Another Time, Another Place* · 3.1 · main | "The Crystal Eye is one of Master Matoya's most cherished relics." | #338 |
| M339a | Krile has the Echo, and the blessing of Light preserved her when the Isle of Val was attacked. | *In the Eye of the Beholder* · 3.1 · main | "Krile reveals not only that she possesses the Echo, but that she has availed herself of the blessing of Light's protection" | #339 |
| M339b | The Students of Baldesion's headquarters and the Isle of Val itself were obliterated by a powerful magick. | *In the Eye of the Beholder* · 3.1 · main | "Regrettably, our order's headquarters—and the isle itself—were obliterated by a magick of immense power." | #339 |
| M339c | Krile befriended Minfilia while studying with the Students. | *In the Eye of the Beholder* · 3.1 · main | "It was in the course of these studies that I met and subsequently befriended Minfilia." | #339 |
| M339d | Matoya's Crystal Eye is an ancient Crystal of Light. | *In the Eye of the Beholder* · 3.1 · main | "You have in your possession an ancient Crystal of Light" | #339 |
| M339e | Matoya agrees to Krile's plan. | *In the Eye of the Beholder* · 3.1 · main | "Having taken a liking to the girl, Matoya consents to Krile's plan" | #339 |
| M339f | In a trance, Krile sees Thancred emerge from the Lifestream near Sohm Al. | *In the Eye of the Beholder* · 3.1 · main | "In a trance, she declares that Thancred emerged from the [[Lifestream]] near the base of [[Sohm Al]]" | #339 |
| M339g | A Vath trader came to Tailfeather seeking garments for a man of refinement. | *In the Eye of the Beholder* · 3.1 · main | "one of a [[Vath]] trader who came to Tailfeather in search of garments fit for a" | #339 |
| M340a | The Vath say Thancred went to Loth ast Gnath when he heard the Gnath had summoned their god again. | *A Little Slow, a Little Late* · 3.1 · main | "Gone to Loth ast Gnath. Not long ago. When he heard the Onemind had summoned our god once more." | #340 |
| M340b | The Gnath have summoned Ravana again. | *A Little Slow, a Little Late* · 3.1 · main | "the [[Gnath]] had summoned forth [[Ravana]] from the aether once more" | #340 |
| M340c | Y'shtola's forbidden magicks sent Thancred into the wilderness. | *A Little Slow, a Little Late* · 3.1 · main | "Had I known you intended to use forbidden magicks to deliver me to some godsforsaken wilderness..." | #340 |
| M340d | A party of adventurers was already beating Ravana when you arrived. | *A Little Slow, a Little Late* · 3.1 · main | "a party of adventurers locked in combat with the Lord of the Hive, and having seized the upper hand no less." | #340 |
| M340e | Their leader names them the Warriors of Darkness, walkers of a different path. | *A Little Slow, a Little Late* · 3.1 · main | "Mark well our faces, Warrior of Light, for we are the Warriors of Darkness. Walkers of a different path." | #340 |
| M340f | Krile saw in the leader's memory a fight with a man in black, perhaps an Ascian. | *A Little Slow, a Little Late* · 3.1 · main | "They were confronting a man in black. An Ascian, I think." | #340 |
| M340g | Thancred learned of Ravana from the Vath. | *A Little Slow, a Little Late* · 3.1 · main | "From them I learned of Ravana, and of the great warrior who had once laid him low." | #340 |
| M341a | Vidofnir is pleased that Ravana has fallen. | *Dreams of the Lost* · 3.1 · main | "Vidofnir is nevertheless pleased to learn of Ravana's defeat." | #341 |
| M341b | Hraesvelgr has mourned for a thousand years his beloved, who gave her life for a peace a king betrayed. | *Dreams of the Lost* · 3.1 · main | "For a thousand years I have mourned my beloved, who gave her life to forge a peace thy king betrayed." | #341 |
| M341c | Vidofnir will journey to Ishgard on behalf of her people. | *Dreams of the Lost* · 3.1 · main | "Let it be known that I, Vidofnir, shall journey unto Ishgard on behalf of my people." | #341 |
| M341d | The Warriors of Darkness have plainly fought primals before. | *Dreams of the Lost* · 3.1 · main | "it seems all but certain that the [[Warriors of Darkness]] have fought such beings before." | #341 |
| M341e | Alphinaud says they have the Echo and are driven to put down primals. | *Dreams of the Lost* · 3.1 · main | "Blessed with the power of the Echo, and driven to put down primals" | #341 |
| M341f | Aymeric will recover from the attack on him, but it was only the beginning. | *Dreams of the Lost* · 3.1 · main | "Though he is like to make a full recovery, the attack was only the beginning" | #341 |
| M341g | The knife of honorable men set the city aflame. | *Dreams of the Lost* · 3.1 · main | "Honorable men, whose knife in the dark was the spark which set the city aflame." | #341 |
| M341h | Hilda says the fires multiply faster than they can be put out. | *Dreams of the Lost* · 3.1 · main | "We've been dowsin' the fires, but for every one we put out, it seems like two more start up." | #341 |
| M341i | Krile goes to ask Matoya's help in the search for Minfilia. | *Dreams of the Lost* · 3.1 · main | "If you have need of me, I shall be with Master Matoya. I would beg her assistance with the search for Minfilia." | #341 |
| M341j | Thancred is saddened that Yda, Papalymo and Minfilia are still missing. | *Dreams of the Lost* · 3.1 · main | "he is saddened to learn that [[Yda]], [[Papalymo]], and [[Minfilia Warde|Minfilia]] are still missing." | #341 |
| M342a | Lucia sets you to finding witnesses and suspects. | *Against the Dying of the Light* · 3.1 · main | "Witnesses must be found; suspects must be identified." | #342 |
| M342b | Refugees are sheltered and fed in the Vault on Aymeric's initiative. | *Against the Dying of the Light* · 3.1 · main | "the [[The Hoplon|Hoplon]] has been inundated with refugees on their way to receive food and shelter in the [[The Vault (Landmark)|Vault]]—an initiative of Ser [[Aymeric]]'s, apparently." | #342 |
| M342c | Thancred tricks a captured arsonist into confessing. | *Against the Dying of the Light* · 3.1 · main | "Thancred tricks the man into confessing by claiming his highborn master betrayed his trust." | #342 |
| M342d | The arsons were orchestrated by a person or persons of influence. | *Against the Dying of the Light* · 3.1 · main | "we have at last confirmed that these arsons were orchestrated by a person or persons of influence." | #342 |
| M342e | Artoirel's father is slandered, and his half-brother is newly dead. | *Against the Dying of the Light* · 3.1 · main | "His father is widely slandered, and his half-brother not yet cold in the grave." | #342 |
| M342f | House Fortemps has known much tragedy. | *Against the Dying of the Light* · 3.1 · main | "Truly, none can deny that [[House Fortemps]] has known much tragedy of late" | #342 |
| M342g | Artoirel brings a salve for Aymeric on his father's behalf. | *Against the Dying of the Light* · 3.1 · main | "who has come on [[Edmont de Fortemps|his father]]'s behalf to deliver a special salve for Ser Aymeric." | #342 |
| M343a | The True Brothers of the Faith seized the Vault and took the refugees there hostage. | *As Goes Light, So Goes Darkness* · 3.1 · main | "a rebel faction calling themselves the True Brothers of the Faith have seized control of the [[The Vault (Landmark)|Vault]], and taken the refugees sheltering within hostage." | #343 |
| M343b | You, Aymeric and Artoirel rescue all but one of the captives. | *As Goes Light, So Goes Darkness* · 3.1 · main | "together you are able to rescue all but one of their captives" | #343 |
| M343c | A priest throws the girl Maelie from the ledge, and a great white dragon, Vidofnir, catches her. | *As Goes Light, So Goes Darkness* · 3.1 · main | "In a final act of defiance, he throws Maelie from the ledge, sending the child tumbling towards the plaza. … a great white dragon swoops down to pluck her from the very jaws of death." | #343 |
| M343d | Nidhogg and his brood have flown for skies unknown. | *As Goes Light, So Goes Darkness* · 3.1 · main | "It would seem [[Nidhogg]] and his brood have flown for skies unknown." | #343 |
| M343e | Vidofnir can say nothing of Estinien. | *As Goes Light, So Goes Darkness* · 3.1 · main | "Such matters are beyond my ken." | #343 |
| M343f | Count Edmont steps aside so that Artoirel will head House Fortemps. | *As Goes Light, So Goes Darkness* · 3.1 · main | "reveals that Count Edmont means to step aside, leaving him to take his father's place as the head of [[House Fortemps]]." | #343 |
| M343g | Edmont would rid the house of suspicion that it joined a coup by Aymeric. | *As Goes Light, So Goes Darkness* · 3.1 · main | "In this way, Count Edmont intends to rid the house of the lingering suspicion that it was complicit in a coup d'etat orchestrated by Ser Aymeric." | #343 |
| M343h | Artoirel vows to follow Haurchefant's example. | *As Goes Light, So Goes Darkness* · 3.1 · main | "And so he vows to follow [[Haurchefant]]'s proud example and labor for the greater good of Ishgard." | #343 |
| M343i | Y'shtola suspects Thancred's aetheric flow is disrupted, keeping him from magicks and teleportation. | *As Goes Light, So Goes Darkness* · 3.1 · main | "his aetheric flow has been disrupted in a manner which would theoretically prevent him from wielding all magicks, teleportation included." | #343 |
| M343j | At the Great Gubal Library, Urianger meets Elidibus and the Warriors' leader. | *As Goes Light, So Goes Darkness* · 3.1 · main | "[Urianger is in the Great Gubal Library. He meets with Elidibus and the Warrior of Darkness.]" | #343 |
| M343k | Elidibus tells Urianger their fates were ordained long ago. | *As Goes Light, So Goes Darkness* · 3.1 · main | "Our fates were ordained long ago, Archon. The Garleans are no exception." | #343 |
| M343l | At an Ala Mhigan Resistance camp, Yda says the Masks will attack once all units are in place. | *As Goes Light, So Goes Darkness* · 3.1 · main | "[At an Ala Mhigan Resistance camp] … The Masks will attack as soon as all units are in position." | #343 |
| M344a | F'lhaminn, Hoary Boulder and Coultenet are coming home to the Scions. | *As It Once Was* · 3.2 · main | "[[Tataru]] proudly informs you that [[F'lhaminn]], [[Hoary Boulder]], and [[Coultenet]]—the three" | #344 |
| M344b | Krile proposes that Hydaelyn's intercession must leave a trace in the aether. | *As It Once Was* · 3.2 · main | "I propose that such intervention, however, subtle, must surely leave some residual trace." | #344 |
| M344c | Krile and Thancred will analyze the aether in Castrum Meridianum. | *As It Once Was* · 3.2 · main | "Krile and I will infiltrate the castrum and analyze the aether therein." | #344 |
| M344d | F'lhaminn asks after her daughter Minfilia. | *As It Once Was* · 3.2 · main | "Eager to speak with her daughter, F'lhaminn asks after Minfilia" | #344 |
| M344e | Thancred calls Minfilia the Scions' guiding light after the Calamity. | *As It Once Was* · 3.2 · main | "In those dark days following the Calamity, she was our guiding light—our hope for a brighter future." | #344 |
| M344f | Alphinaud blames his ambition for the Scions' flight. | *As It Once Was* · 3.2 · main | "'Twas the chaos born of my foolish ambition which forced them to flee." | #344 |
| M344g | Tataru insists Minfilia, Yda and Papalymo will be found. | *As It Once Was* · 3.2 · main | "We'll find them, Alphinaud—I know we will! Minfilia, Yda, Papalymo...every last one!" | #344 |
| M345a | The aether at the Praetorium and the aqueducts bears a signature also found at the Isle of Val. | *The Word of the Mother* · 3.2 · main | "the readings from these two locations yielded a unique aetheric signature—one which was also detected at the [[Isle of Val]]" | #345 |
| M345b | Krile believes Hydaelyn guided Minfilia to her realm. | *The Word of the Mother* · 3.2 · main | "Krile surmises that Hydaelyn—who has a demonstrated capacity to summon the consciousness of those blessed with the Echo to Her realm—may have guided Minfilia" | #345 |
| M345c | The Antitower is a Sharlayan structure overlooking the aetherial sea. | *The Word of the Mother* · 3.2 · main | "I speak of the Antitower—a Sharlayan construction conceived to provide scholars a vantage point over the aetherial sea." | #345 |
| M345d | Matoya keeps Krile behind to restore the ward. | *The Word of the Mother* · 3.2 · main | "she demands that Krile stays behind to help her restore the ward over the door" | #345 |
| M345e | A voice calling itself the Word of the Mother says it was once Minfilia. | *The Word of the Mother* · 3.2 · main | "We are the Word of the Mother. We who were once called" | #345 |
| M345f | Hydaelyn banished the Darkness when it coveted power. | *The Word of the Mother* · 3.2 · main | "But the Darkness coveted power, and the balance was broken. Thus was I forced to banish Him unto the distant heavens" | #345 |
| M345g | Seven times the Ascians have succeeded, and the Darkness grown stronger. | *The Word of the Mother* · 3.2 · main | "Seven times have they succeeded. Seven times has the Darkness grown stronger. Seven times have I failed." | #345 |
| M345h | Krile concludes Minfilia, joined with Hydaelyn, struggles to keep her own form. | *The Word of the Mother* · 3.2 · main | "it is not hard to see why Minfilia, having joined with Her, might struggle to maintain her own form." | #345 |
| M345i | Matoya tells Alphinaud Minfilia threw herself on the fire for the Scions' cause. | *The Word of the Mother* · 3.2 · main | "She threw herself on the fire to fuel your" | #345 |
| M345j | Thancred storms out without a word. | *The Word of the Mother* · 3.2 · main | "while Thancred storms out without uttering a word, leaving you alone with Alphinaud and Matoya" | #345 |
| M345k | The Forum gave Matoya the Antitower's custodianship. | *The Word of the Mother* · 3.2 · main | "Role? Hardly. The Forum foisted the title on me." | #345 |
| M346a | Alphinaud will tell Tataru and Urianger of Minfilia's fate, but asks you to tell no one else, least of all F'lhaminn. | *This War of Ours* · 3.2 · main | "Although [[Alphinaud]] intends to inform [[Tataru]] and [[Urianger]] of [[Minfilia Warde|Minfilia's]] fate, he asks that you share with no one else what you have learned—especially [[F'lhaminn]]." | #346 |
| M346b | Vidofnir's rescue of a child gave hope of peace between man and dragon. | *This War of Ours* · 3.2 · main | "Yet in adversity were we blessed with the promise of peace between man and dragon, through Vidofnir's timely rescue of an innocent child." | #346 |
| M346c | Aymeric is arranging a peace conference at Falcon's Nest, to be attended by Vidofnir. | *This War of Ours* · 3.2 · main | "he has begun making formal arrangements for a peace conference in [[Falcon's Nest]], to be attended by [[Vidofnir]]" | #346 |
| M346d | Aymeric fears Nidhogg, implacably opposed to peace, may disrupt the conference. | *This War of Ours* · 3.2 · main | "Given Nidhogg's implacable opposition to the peace, it is possible that he and his followers may attempt to disrupt proceedings" | #346 |
| M346e | Lucia was sent to Ishgard as a foreign spy. | *This War of Ours* · 3.2 · main | "Dispatched to a foreign nation as a spy, it was her duty to infiltrate [[Ishgard]]ian society for her masters' ends." | #346 |
| M346f | Lucia chose instead to pledge herself to Ishgard's cause and war. | *This War of Ours* · 3.2 · main | "In the end, however, she chose a different path—to pledge herself to another nation's cause, and to join in their war." | #346 |
| M346g | A relief at Falcon's Nest will be unveiled during the conference. | *This War of Ours* · 3.2 · main | "It conceals a relief that will be unveiled during the conference." | #346 |
| M347a | A small party of Convictors has pledged to attack any Dravanians bound for Falcon's Nest. | *Staunch Conviction* · 3.2 · main | "The majority were willing to do so, but a small party have instead pledged to engage any Dravanians bound for Falcon's Nest." | #347 |
| M347b | Lucia rides out with Artoirel, since a lord of House Fortemps will make the Convictors reluctant to oppose her. | *Staunch Conviction* · 3.2 · main | "They should be far more reluctant to oppose us were a lord of House Fortemps present." | #347 |
| M347c | Artoirel asks you to look after Emmanellain. | *Staunch Conviction* · 3.2 · main | "I must ask: would you look after Emmanellain while I am away?" | #347 |
| M347d | Artoirel calls Emmanellain impulsive and lately melancholy. | *Staunch Conviction* · 3.2 · main | "he has ever been an impulsive, changeable child, but recently he seems to have been suffering from a bout of melancholy." | #347 |
| M347e | Lords Artoirel and Emmanellain have volunteered at Falcon's Nest. | *Staunch Conviction* · 3.2 · main | "you may meet lords Artoirel and Emmanellain, who have graciously volunteered their services." | #347 |
| M348a | Emmanellain is forbidden to leave Falcon's Nest. | *Once More, a Favor* · 3.2 · main | "The wasteland beyond the walls must be kept safe for travelers, yet I have been forbidden from leaving Falcon's Nest." | #348 |
| M348b | You find Thancred, who saved two heretics from wolves. | *Once More, a Favor* · 3.2 · main | "You find Thancred, as well as two heretics he seemingly saved from a ravenous wolf pack." | #348 |
| M348c | Thancred says the young lord took advantage of you both. | *Once More, a Favor* · 3.2 · main | "The young lord wasted no time taking advantage of you, did he? And me, I suppose." | #348 |
| M349a | A waitress drugs you in the tavern. | *For Those We Have Lost* · 3.2 · main | "You crumple to the ground, and as all fades to black, you hear her whisper to sleep, to sleep..." | #349 |
| M349b | The woman cries for vengeance on the Dravanians, and Emmanellain, panicking, commands a knight to act. | *For Those We Have Lost* · 3.2 · main | "She cries out for vengeance against the [[Dravania]]ns, and Emmanellain, in a panic, commands a nearby knight to act." | #349 |
| M349c | The knight shoots her, and a second arrow fells her. | *For Those We Have Lost* · 3.2 · main | "As the crowd looks on, the knight shoots, striking her in the arm. … before a second arrow silences her, and she falls from sight." | #349 |
| M349d | The knight says he did as Emmanellain ordered. | *For Those We Have Lost* · 3.2 · main | "I-I only did as you ordered, my lord..." | #349 |
| M349e | The peace conference has no chance in this climate. | *For Those We Have Lost* · 3.2 · main | "the peace conference clearly has no chance of success in this climate." | #349 |
| M349f | The people will remember a young lordling ordering an unarmed protester's execution. | *For Those We Have Lost* · 3.2 · main | "the people will not soon forget the image of a young lordling ordering the public execution of an unarmed protestor" | #349 |
| M350a | The Convictory incident was likely a ruse, and the protest leader yet lives. | *Consequences* · 3.2 · main | "They reveal that the incident at the [[The Convictory|Convictory]] was more than likely a ruse intended to draw them away from the hamlet, and that the protest leader, while gravely wounded, yet lives." | #350 |
| M350b | The protesters infiltrated Falcon's Nest as servants and guests. | *Consequences* · 3.2 · main | "She and her conspirators infiltrated Falcon's Nest posing as servants and guests." | #350 |
| M350c | Artoirel orders Emmanellain back to Ishgard to answer for his actions. | *Consequences* · 3.2 · main | "Lord Artoirel duly orders his brother to return to [[Ishgard]] and answer for his actions" | #350 |
| M350d | Honoroit was beaten for trying to stop the smallfolk leaving. | *Consequences* · 3.2 · main | "he attempted to dissuade the smallfolk from leaving" | #350 |
| M350e | Thancred strikes Emmanellain. | *Consequences* · 3.2 · main | "Thancred, in turn, deals Lord Emmanellain a swingeing blow, snarling that he knows nothing of him and his failures." | #350 |
| M350f | Thancred has tried everything to save those dear to him, and failed. | *Consequences* · 3.2 · main | "I have fought tooth and nail for the people I hold dear—done ''everything'' in my power to save them...and I have failed." | #350 |
| M350g | Lucia suspends the peace conference pending Aymeric's decision. | *Consequences* · 3.2 · main | "I have no choice but to suspend the peace conference, pending Ser Amyeric's final decision." | #350 |
| M350h | Artoirel has detained the Convictors for questioning. | *Consequences* · 3.2 · main | "Accordingly, we have detained them for questioning." | #350 |
| M350i | Lucia is the first commander. | *Consequences* · 3.2 · main | "Recalling that Lord [[Artoirel]] and the [[Lucia|first commander]] should have returned by now" | #350 |
| M350j | Honoroit is Emmanellain's manservant. | *Consequences* · 3.2 · main | "calling for his manservant to accompany him. [[Honoroit]], however, is nowhere to be seen." | #350 |
| M351a | The Alliance proposed joint exercises, which Aymeric would present as a grand melee. | *Choices* · 3.2 · main | "The other members of the [[The Eorzean Alliance|Eorzean Alliance]] have recently proposed joint military exercises, which Ser Aymeric believes could be presented as a" | #351 |
| M351b | Edmont asks that his younger son fight in the melee to make amends. | *Choices* · 3.2 · main | "his only request is that his younger son take part in the melee, that he might make amends for his mistakes in [[Falcon's Nest]]." | #351 |
| M351c | Lucia, likely a Garlean citizen once, has seen such spectacles rally people to the imperial cause. | *Choices* · 3.2 · main | "Doubtless, as a [[Garlean]] citizen, she bore witness to similar spectacles intended to rally the people to the imperial cause" | #351 |
| M351d | Inspired by Aymeric, Emmanellain begs leave to fight in the melee. | *Choices* · 3.2 · main | "So inspired, he begs leave to participate in the grand melee" | #351 |
| M351e | Aymeric's father trusted only himself. | *Choices* · 3.2 · main | "He did not believe the people strong enough to bring about their own deliverance, trusting only in himself." | #351 |
| M351f | People call Aymeric a patricide in the streets. | *Choices* · 3.2 · main | "They decry you as a patricide in the streets." | #351 |
| M351g | You agree to fight for Ishgard. | *Choices* · 3.2 · main | "you agree to fight for Ishgard, and a jubilant Ser Aymeric declares that you shall give the people a spectacle for the ages." | #351 |
| M351h | Thancred recalls that the Ascians sought to profit from the war. | *Choices* · 3.2 · main | "In any event, let us not forget that the Ascians sought to profit from this war." | #351 |
| M352a | The grand melee is held in central Coerthas, outside the city. | *A Spectacle for the Ages* · 3.2 · main | "Lucia informs you that the grand melee is to be held in [[Coerthas Central Highlands|central Coerthas]], just outside the city." | #352 |
| M352b | General Raubahn swept aside all comers and challenged you to single combat. | *A Spectacle for the Ages* · 3.2 · main | "General of the [[Immortal Flames]], the Bull of Ala Mhigo himself, swept aside all comers and challenged you to single combat." | #352 |
| M352c | Aymeric proclaims victory for Ishgard. | *A Spectacle for the Ages* · 3.2 · main | "It was then that Ser [[Aymeric]], raising both sword and voice to the heavens, proclaimed victory for Ishgard, for the day was theirs." | #352 |
| M352d | You bear House Fortemps's colors into battle. | *A Spectacle for the Ages* · 3.2 · main | "we believe the men would find it inspiring if you bore the colors of House Fortemps into battle." | #352 |
| M352e | The melee is opened in the Eorzean Alliance's name. | *A Spectacle for the Ages* · 3.2 · main | "Brave men and women of the Eorzean Alliance. Let the grand melee begin!" | #352 |
| M353a | Honoroit, Emmanellain's manservant, has fully recovered. | *For Those We Can Yet Save* · 3.2 · main | "[[Honoroit]], Lord [[Emmanellain]]'s manservant, has made a full recovery and is eager to resume his duties." | #353 |
| M353b | Aymeric orders the conference reconvened with Vidofnir. | *For Those We Can Yet Save* · 3.2 · main | "Reconvene the conference and summon Vidofnir to Falcon's Nest." | #353 |
| M353c | Aymeric and Vidofnir speak of peace. | *For Those We Can Yet Save* · 3.2 · main | "Ser Aymeric and [[Vidofnir]] come together and speak of peace" | #353 |
| M353d | Estinien drives his lance into Vidofnir's back. | *For Those We Can Yet Save* · 3.2 · main | "[[Estinien]] leaps down from on high and drives his lance into Vidofnir's back" | #353 |
| M353e | Nidhogg's eyes are fused to Estinien, who speaks with a wyrm's voice. | *For Those We Can Yet Save* · 3.2 · main | "two massive, twitching [[Eyes of Nidhogg|eyes]] fused to his arm and shoulder … And when he speaks, it is not with the voice of a man, but a wyrm." | #353 |
| M353f | He declares the final chorus nigh. | *For Those We Can Yet Save* · 3.2 · main | "The final chorus is nigh, and all will be held to account!" | #353 |
| M353g | Vidofnir is carried to Anyx Trine to be cared for. | *For Those We Can Yet Save* · 3.2 · main | "The dragon was spirited away to Anyx Trine to receive care from her brethren." | #353 |
| M353h | Edmont puts Emmanellain at his brother's disposal. | *For Those We Can Yet Save* · 3.2 · main | "Emmanellain—you will place yourself at your brother's disposal. He will be glad of your assistance." | #353 |
| M354a | Though Aymeric and Ishgard may sacrifice Estinien to slay Nidhogg, Alphinaud will not. | *Causes and Costs* · 3.2 · main | "Even if Ser [[Aymeric]] and all of [[Ishgard]] are willing to sacrifice [[Estinien]] to slay [[Nidhogg]], he is not." | #354 |
| M354b | Alphinaud will not sacrifice friends for a cause. | *Causes and Costs* · 3.2 · main | "I do not want to be a man who sacrifices his friends and family for a cause." | #354 |
| M354c | Alphinaud expects Nidhogg to lead the Horde, and Aymeric to do what he must. | *Causes and Costs* · 3.2 · main | "When Nidhogg leads the Horde into battle, Ser Aymeric and his forces will do what they believe must be done." | #354 |
| M354d | Krile felt the wyrm's hatred about Estinien even before his change. | *Causes and Costs* · 3.2 · main | "Even before his transformation, I could feel the wyrm's hatred swirling about Estinien." | #354 |
| M354e | Nidhogg cries for vengeance, and his kin raise the final chorus. | *Causes and Costs* · 3.2 · main | "For in those twilight hours did Nidhogg cry out for vengeance, and his brethern raise their voices for the final chorus of the Dragonsong war." | #354 |
| M354g | Papalymo warns Yda not to place her faith in them too soon. | *Causes and Costs* · 3.2 · main | "Do not be so eager to place your faith in them." | #354 |
| M355a | Y'shtola and Krile conclude Estinien's spirit may survive within Nidhogg's will. | *The Man Within* · 3.3 · main | "concluding at last that Estinien's spirit may indeed have survived, having apparently been enveloped but not consumed by Nidhogg's vengeful will." | #355 |
| M355b | Alphinaud thinks removing Nidhogg's eyes from the armor could free Estinien. | *The Man Within* · 3.3 · main | "Alphinaud speculates that removing the great wyrm's [[Eyes of Nidhogg|eyes]] from the dragoon's armor could potentially free him." | #355 |
| M355c | Krile warns his would-be savior might become Nidhogg's next host. | *The Man Within* · 3.3 · main | "his would-be savior might become Nidhogg's next host" | #355 |
| M355d | Nidhogg's shade, in the Azure Dragoon's flesh, spilled the blood of his own kind. | *The Man Within* · 3.3 · main | "But the lingering shade of Nidhogg, clad in the flesh of the Azure Dragoon, did mark the occasion by spilling the blood of his own kind." | #355 |
| M355e | Fear turned to fury, and the call to arms rang out anew. | *The Man Within* · 3.3 · main | "And when fear gave way to fury, the call to arms rang out anew:" | #355 |
| M356a | Aymeric believes only Hraesvelgr, Nidhogg's brood-brother, can stand against the shade. | *An Ally for Ishgard* · 3.3 · main | "the lord commander believes that there is only one being capable of standing against the vengeful shade—Nidhogg's own brood-brother, [[Hraesvelgr]]." | #356 |
| M356b | No mortal force can repel Nidhogg now that he has both eyes. | *An Ally for Ishgard* · 3.3 · main | "now that Nidhogg is possessed of both his eyes, no mortal force we can muster will repel him." | #356 |
| M356c | Aymeric says the battle with Nidhogg will end his tenure as acting head of church and state. | *An Ally for Ishgard* · 3.3 · main | "the battle with Nidhogg will mark the end of my tenure as the acting head of church and state." | #356 |
| M356d | Estinien reported Hraesvelgr's unwillingness to meddle in men's affairs. | *An Ally for Ishgard* · 3.3 · main | "Estinien's report was most particular about Hraesvelgr's unwillingness to involve himself in the affairs of men." | #356 |
| M356e | Vidofnir's wound was deep but not mortal. | *An Ally for Ishgard* · 3.3 · main | "The thrust was deep, but not mortal. I will heal in time." | #356 |
| M356f | Vidofnir thinks her sire will refuse. | *An Ally for Ishgard* · 3.3 · main | "Folly. Thou know'st as well as I how he will answer." | #356 |
| M357a | Hraesvelgr says men's treacherous forefathers tore down the bridge between man and dragon. | *Winning Over the Wyrm* · 3.3 · main | "a bridge which thy treacherous forefathers saw fit to tear down, as thou well knowest." | #357 |
| M357b | Aymeric admits his father corrupted himself with a primal's power, and he asked you to slay him. | *Winning Over the Wyrm* · 3.3 · main | "When my father corrupted himself and his followers with the power of a primal, I beseeched the Warrior of Light to slay him" | #357 |
