| M357c | Hraesvelgr bore Ysayle because she sought to balance her past sins. | *Winning Over the Wyrm* · 3.3 · main | "The child did lament her past sins, and sought to balance the scales with her remaining days." | #357 |
| M357d | Only when Midgardsormr appears does Hraesvelgr agree to test you. | *Winning Over the Wyrm* · 3.3 · main | "it is not until [[Midgardsormr]] himself appears that Hraesvelgr finally assents to test you and your companions' resolve." | #357 |
| M357e | Hraesvelgr pledges to aid you against Nidhogg. | *Winning Over the Wyrm* · 3.3 · main | "Hraesvelgr commends you and your companions for your unwavering determination, and pledges to aid you in the coming battle with [[Nidhogg]]." | #357 |
| M357f | A roar signals the Dravanian Horde's advance. | *Winning Over the Wyrm* · 3.3 · main | "Scarcely have the words left his lips, however, when a thunderous roar echoes across the sky, signaling the advance of the [[Dravanian Horde]]." | #357 |
| M357g | Hraesvelgr lets his children aid you, and will warn Ishgard of his brood-brother's coming. | *Winning Over the Wyrm* · 3.3 · main | "I permit my children to offer or deny thee aid as they see fit; to warn thy people of my brood-brother's coming." | #357 |
| M358a | Hraesvelgr tries in vain to turn Nidhogg from vengeance. | *An End to the Song* · 3.3 · main | "who attempts in vain to persuade his brother from his vengeful course." | #358 |
| M358b | Hraesvelgr falls beaten onto the bridge. | *An End to the Song* · 3.3 · main | "The city's defenders watch transfixed as Hraesvelgr tumbles onto the bridge, beaten and bloody, Ishgard's fate seemingly sealed." | #358 |
| M358c | Hraesvelgr has given you half his power. | *An End to the Song* · 3.3 · main | "only to learn that his fallen sibling has entrusted you with a gift: one half of his power." | #358 |
| M358d | You subdue Nidhogg's shade. | *An End to the Song* · 3.3 · main | "You have succeeded in subduing Nidhogg's shade." | #358 |
| M358e | You tear Nidhogg's eyes from Estinien and cast them into the abyss. | *An End to the Song* · 3.3 · main | "With one final heave, you succeed in separating man and wyrm, and for an instant you glimpse Nidhogg's disembodied spirit, before casting his eyes into the abyss." | #358 |
| M358f | Nidhogg's spirit is undone by mortal will. | *An End to the Song* · 3.3 · main | "Undone by mortal will. Whither now...?" | #358 |
| M358g | Estinien lives, and Aymeric bears him back to Ishgard. | *An End to the Song* · 3.3 · main | "He lives. ah, Estinien, my ill-fated friend. … you watch as Ser [[Aymeric]] bears him back to Ishgard." | #358 |
| M358h | The Horde scatters after its master falls. | *An End to the Song* · 3.3 · main | "The Horde have sensed the fall of their master, and scattered to the winds." | #358 |
| M359a | Alphinaud counts freeing Estinien the greater feat. | *Heroes of the Hour* · 3.3 · main | "[[Alphinaud]] praises you for your epochal victory over [[Nidhogg]], but admits that freeing [[Estinien]] felt the greater accomplishment." | #359 |
| M359b | Estinien has not yet woken. | *Heroes of the Hour* · 3.3 · main | "[[Lucia]] informs you that Estinien has yet to regain consciousness." | #359 |
| M359c | Estinien's mind and body were held hostage for many days. | *Heroes of the Hour* · 3.3 · main | "But as you know, Estinien's mind and body were held hostage for many days." | #359 |
| M359e | Alphinaud sits at Estinien's bedside. | *Heroes of the Hour* · 3.3 · main | "When he begged to sit at Estinien's side, I could not well refuse him." | #359 |
| M360a | Aymeric abolishes the archbishops' thousand-year rule, opening the way to a republic. | *Litany of Peace* · 3.3 · main | "Echoing Estinien's resolve, Ser Aymeric finally moves to abolish the thousand-year rule of the archbishops, paving the way for a new republic." | #360 |
| M360b | Ishgard is now governed by a House of Commons and a House of Lords, and Aymeric is elected to the Lords' highest seat. | *Litany of Peace* · 3.3 · main | "[[Ishgard]]'s governance is placed in the hands of a newly formed House of Commons and House of Lords. And though Ser Aymeric would like nothing more than to lay down the reins of state, he soon finds himself elected to the highest seat in the latter." | #360 |
| M360c | Estinien lays down the mantle of Azure Dragoon, his hunt at an end. | *Litany of Peace* · 3.3 · main | "Lord Commander, my hunt is at an end. I would lay down the mantle of Azure Dragoon." | #360 |
| M360d | Estinien's hatred began when Nidhogg slew his family. | *Litany of Peace* · 3.3 · main | "the hatred I felt after Nidhogg slew my family, when no path remained save vengeance against dragonkind." | #360 |
| M360e | Aymeric and Estinien were the only survivors of a company a dragon attacked. | *Litany of Peace* · 3.3 · main | "our company was set upon by a dragon, and we were the only two to survive." | #360 |
| M360f | Estinien has disappeared, and Aymeric asks you to carry him a message. | *Litany of Peace* · 3.3 · main | "He then reveals that Estinien has disappeared, and asks that you give him a message should your paths ever chance to cross." | #360 |
| M360h | Elidibus tells the Warriors' leader that the others will play their part, as they will theirs. | *Litany of Peace* · 3.3 · main | "They will play their part, and we will play ours..." | #360 |
| M360i | The Warriors' leader catches Alisaie eavesdropping, and Thancred intervenes. | *Litany of Peace* · 3.3 · main | "notices Alisaie eavesdropping and attacks her but is interrupted by Thancred." | #360 |
| M360j | You escort Hraesvelgr back to Zenith on Midgardsormr's wings. | *Litany of Peace* · 3.3 · main | "borrow [[Midgardsormr]]'s wings to escort [[Hraesvelgr]] back to [[Zenith]]" | #360 |
| M361a | Aymeric, newly lord speaker of the House of Lords, invites you to dine at his estate. | *Promises Kept* · 3.4 · main | "fresh from his appointment as lord speaker of the House of Lords, has extended to you a formal invitation to dine with him at his estate." | #361 |
| M361b | Aymeric is lord speaker of the House of Lords and lord commander of the Temple Knights. | *Promises Kept* · 3.4 · main | "lord speaker of the House of Lords, lord commander of the Temple Knights" | #361 |
| M361c | The wounded maiden is Alisaie, Alphinaud's twin, who long ago parted from her brother. | *Promises Kept* · 3.4 · main | "The maiden is revealed to be none other than [[Alisaie]], [[Alphinaud]]'s twin sister, who had long ago decided to part ways with her brother." | #361 |
| M361d | Thancred, tracking the Warriors of Darkness, found Alisaie on their trail too. | *Promises Kept* · 3.4 · main | "Thancred explains that after tracking the [[Warriors of Darkness]] for some time, he discovered that she too had attempted to learn more of the enigmatic adventurers." | #361 |
| M361e | Alisaie, struck by a poisoned arrow, is near death. | *Promises Kept* · 3.4 · main | "Struck by a poisoned arrow as they fled, Alisaie now teeters on the brink of death" | #361 |
| M361f | Alisaie warns that Garuda will soon be summoned in Xelphatol, to be slain by the Warriors of Darkness. | *Promises Kept* · 3.4 · main | "she musters the strength to warn you of [[Garuda]]'s imminent summoning in [[Xelphatol]]—and of the [[primal]]'s planned death at the hands of the Warriors of Darkness." | #361 |
| M361g | The Warriors of Darkness conspire with the Ascians to goad the Ixal into summoning. | *Promises Kept* · 3.4 · main | "The Warriors of Darkness are conspiring with the [[Ascians]] to goad the [[Ixal]] into summoning their patron deity" | #361 |
| M361h | Ishgard's Lords and Commons offer the Scions patronage. | *Promises Kept* · 3.4 · main | "The Lords and Commons agree on very little, but not a soul in either house begrudges your order this offer of patronage." | #361 |
| M362b | You lay low the Ixali occultist leading the summoning. | *Shadows of the First* · 3.4 · main | "you confront the Ixali occultist overseeing the summoning ritual, and in a pitched battle manage to lay him low" | #362 |
| M362c | With the ritual unfinished, Garuda is no longer a threat. | *Shadows of the First* · 3.4 · main | "So they were unable to see the ritual to its completion... Then Garuda is no longer a threat" | #362 |
| M362d | The Warriors' world held far more Light than Dark. | *Shadows of the First* · 3.4 · main | "But not all worlds hold Light and Dark in equal measure. In ours, the power of Light was greater by far." | #362 |
| M362e | The Warriors joined hands with their former enemies to come to the Source. | *Shadows of the First* · 3.4 · main | "And so we joined hands with our former enemies, and with their aid came here...to the Source." | #362 |
| M362f | They intend another Umbral Calamity to break down the barriers between worlds. | *Shadows of the First* · 3.4 · main | "Here, they intend to bring about another Umbral Calamity, for only through such unbridled destruction may the barriers between planes be broken down" | #362 |
| M362g | Their leader says only the Ardor can save their home. | *Shadows of the First* · 3.4 · main | "For there is but one way to restore the balance and save our home: the Ardor." | #362 |
| M362h | He asks whether you are prepared to do whatever it takes. | *Shadows of the First* · 3.4 · main | "We are prepared to do whatever it takes! Are you?" | #362 |
| M362i | Alphinaud vows the sacrifice ends here. | *Shadows of the First* · 3.4 · main | "The sacrifice, the loss—it ends here." | #362 |
| M363a | Alisaie has regained consciousness. | *Two Sides of a Coin* · 3.4 · main | "I am pleased to report that she has regained consciousness." | #363 |
| M363b | Thancred and Alisaie found evidence that the Ascians keep crystals flowing to the beast tribes. | *Two Sides of a Coin* · 3.4 · main | "Alisaie and I have uncovered evidence that the Ascians have been manipulating certain parties to ensure that a constant stream of crystals flows into the hands of the beast tribes." | #363 |
| M363c | An Ascian observed that the powerless will turn to another god when the old ones fail. | *Two Sides of a Coin* · 3.4 · main | "The Ascian himself observed that once the powerless realize that the old gods have failed them, they will have little recourse but to look to a new one." | #363 |
| M363d | Y'shtola and Krile go to Xelphatol to learn the Ixal's source. | *Two Sides of a Coin* · 3.4 · main | "Agreed. Krile and I shall journey to Xelphatol and learn what we can of the Ixal's source." | #363 |
| M363f | Urianger reports the kobolds may soon summon Titan again. | *Two Sides of a Coin* · 3.4 · main | "the [[kobolds]] of [[Vylbrand]] are rumored to be on the verge of summoning [[Titan]] once more" | #363 |
| M363g | Alisaie asks Urianger to research the Warriors' claims. | *Two Sides of a Coin* · 3.4 · main | "I would ask that you research this for us." | #363 |
| M363h | The Scions reach Vylbrand in the nick of time. | *Two Sides of a Coin* · 3.4 · main | "It would seem that the Scions of the Seventh Dawn have arrived in Vylbrand in the nick of time." | #363 |
| M364a | Commander Bloeidin confirms the kobolds have grown more aggressive. | *Unlikely Allies* · 3.4 · main | "Commander [[Bloeidin]] confirms [[Urianger]]'s report that the [[kobolds]] of [[U'Ghamaro]] have grown more aggressive of late." | #364 |
| M364b | The kobold child is Ga Bu, of the 620th Order. | *Unlikely Allies* · 3.4 · main | "Pickman Ga Bu of the 620th Order!" | #364 |
| M364c | Ga Bu's parents spoke against the patriarch and were taken away. | *Unlikely Allies* · 3.4 · main | "Ga Bu goes on to explain that his parents, along with other like-minded kobolds, spoke out against the patriarch's plans, only to be taken away to serve as" | #364 |
| M364d | The patriarch means to summon the Great Father again. | *Unlikely Allies* · 3.4 · main | "The patriarch is going to summon the Great Father again, and you have to stop him" | #364 |
| M364e | The Scions will infiltrate the stronghold, secure the crystals and free the prisoners. | *Unlikely Allies* · 3.4 · main | "declares that the [[Scions]] will attempt to infiltrate the kobold stronghold, secure the crystals, and free the prisoners." | #364 |
| M365a | The kobolds' crystals were smuggled out of Ishgard. | *The Beast That Mourned at the Heart of the Mountain* · 3.4 · main | "the crate must have been smuggled out of Ishgard and transported across the sea" | #365 |
| M365b | Ga Bu's parents and the other prisoners have already been executed. | *The Beast That Mourned at the Heart of the Mountain* · 3.4 · main | "they and the other prisoners have already been executed" | #365 |
| M365c | Ga Bu's anguish birthed a primal as surely as a ritual. | *The Beast That Mourned at the Heart of the Mountain* · 3.4 · main | "His anguish and despair have served to birth a primal, just as surely as would the supplications of the faithful." | #365 |
| M365d | The Titan born of the child's anguish must be faced. | *The Beast That Mourned at the Heart of the Mountain* · 3.4 · main | "the Titan born of the child's anguish is nevertheless a threat which cannot be ignored" | #365 |
| M365e | Titan is no more. | *The Beast That Mourned at the Heart of the Mountain* · 3.4 · main | "Nevertheless, Titan is no more, and you are free to return to Camp Overlook and the others." | #365 |
| M365f | Ga Bu may have fallen under the primal's influence. | *The Beast That Mourned at the Heart of the Mountain* · 3.4 · main | "Alphinaud gives voice to the unhappy possibility that Ga Bu may have fallen prey to the primal's influence." | #365 |
| M366a | Alisaie entrusts Ga Bu to Commander Bloeidin's care. | *Beneath a Star-filled Sky* · 3.4 · main | "she surrenders him into Commander Bloeidin's care, entreating him to show the young [[kobold]] every kindness." | #366 |
| M366b | Ga Bu may one day recover. | *Beneath a Star-filled Sky* · 3.4 · main | "Ga Bu may one day recover from his trauma." | #366 |
| M366c | A Rejoining would kill everyone on a reflection of the Source. | *Beneath a Star-filled Sky* · 3.4 · main | "a passage which suggests that a [[Rejoining]] would result in the deaths of all those living upon a reflection of the [[The Source|Source]]" | #366 |
| M366d | The Ishgardian smugglers are remnants of the Ivy's network. | *Beneath a Star-filled Sky* · 3.4 · main | "the Ishgardian smugglers supplying the beast tribes with crystals have been identified as remnants of the criminal network established by [[Eline Roaille]]—the infamous [[Garlean]] spymaster, the Ivy." | #366 |
| M366e | They also sent crystals to refugees fighting for Ala Mhigan liberation. | *Beneath a Star-filled Sky* · 3.4 · main | "refugees devoted to the cause of [[Ala Mhigo|Ala Mhigan]] liberation" | #366 |
| M366f | The smugglers worked for a man in black robes. | *Beneath a Star-filled Sky* · 3.4 · main | "a man in black robes." | #366 |
| M366g | Alisaie treats Urianger with deep suspicion. | *Beneath a Star-filled Sky* · 3.4 · main | "her words and actions seem to indicate a deep-seated suspicion. But why?" | #366 |
| M366h | A masked man tells the Warriors' leader the Ardor is not the Ascians' alone to invoke. | *Beneath a Star-filled Sky* · 3.4 · main | "the power to invoke the Ardor belongeth not unto the Ascians alone." | #366 |
| M367a | Members of the Ala Mhigan Resistance in Little Ala Mhigo received large crystal shipments. | *When We Were Free* · 3.4 · main | "members of the Ala Mhigan Resistance operating here have taken receipt of several large shipments of crystals" | #367 |
| M367b | The Griffin is a masked revolutionary leader who has rallied many refugees. | *When We Were Free* · 3.4 · main | "a revolutionary leader who always wears a mask in public. A charismatic figure, he seems to have inspired a good many of the refugees to rally to his standard" | #367 |
| M367c | The Griffin and his Masks are said to have a secret weapon against the Garleans. | *When We Were Free* · 3.4 · main | "there are even rumors that he and his Masks have a secret weapon they plan to use against the [[Garlean]] occupiers" | #367 |
| M367d | The Masks most likely received the crystals from Ishgard. | *When We Were Free* · 3.4 · main | "it is highly likely that the Masks are the group who received the shipment of crystals from [[Ishgard]]" | #367 |
| M367e | At Qarn you find Yda and Papalymo. | *When We Were Free* · 3.4 · main | "and catches sight of two very familiar faces: [[Yda]] and [[Papalymo]]!" | #367 |
| M367f | Yda and Papalymo fled Ul'dah with old Resistance friends and have helped them since. | *When We Were Free* · 3.4 · main | "they slipped out of the city with the aid of some old friends from the Resistance" | #367 |
| M367g | The Crystal Braves were denounced and Raubahn reinstated, clearing the Scions. | *When We Were Free* · 3.4 · main | "the [[The Crystal Braves|Crystal Braves]] had been denounced and General [[Raubahn]] reinstated" | #367 |
| M367h | The man at the temple was an impersonator, not the Griffin. | *When We Were Free* · 3.4 · main | "the man you saw was not in fact the Griffin, but an impersonator" | #367 |
| M367j | Papalymo's letter to the Scions was likely intercepted by the Masks. | *When We Were Free* · 3.4 · main | "it seems likely that his missive was intercepted by the Masks" | #367 |
| M368a | The impostor speaks with the Griffin's voice and procured the crystals at his behest. | *Honorable Heroes* · 3.4 · main | "But I speak with his voice, and it was at his behest that we procured those crystals." | #368 |
| M368b | The Masks traded the crystals to the Amalj'aa for their aid. | *Honorable Heroes* · 3.4 · main | "In exchange for crystals to summon their god, they will aid us in the fight for Ala Mhigan liberation." | #368 |
| M368c | Yda names the god Ifrit and fears for the Resistance. | *Honorable Heroes* · 3.4 · main | "When people find out you helped the lizardmen summon Ifrit, they'll turn on the Resistance!" | #368 |
| M368d | Many joined the Masks because you saved them. | *Honorable Heroes* · 3.4 · main | "a great many who have joined us did so because you saved them" | #368 |
| M368e | Papalymo forbids Yda to join the Masks. | *Honorable Heroes* · 3.4 · main | "You cannot seriously be contemplating taking up arms with that band of cutthroats?" | #368 |
| M368f | Papalymo and Yda will search for the cache. | *Honorable Heroes* · 3.4 · main | "If there a cache to be found, Yda and I will find it." | #368 |
| M369a | The crystals are said to be in Zahar'ak, where the Amalj'aa may summon Ifrit. | *One Life for One World* · 3.4 · main | "where the [[Amalj'aa]] are presumably preparing to summon [[Ifrit]]" | #369 |
| M369b | The masked man calls himself author of the drama to come. | *One Life for One World* · 3.4 · main | "the drama of which I am author, gods forgive me, shall at last be performed." | #369 |
| M369c | In the Bowl of Embers the Warriors of Darkness come to kill you, to bring about a Calamity. | *One Life for One World* · 3.4 · main | "This time, however, they are come not to slay a primal, but you, the Warrior of Light, in the belief that your death will plunge the realm into chaos and bring about another Umbral Calamity." | #369 |
| M369d | Urianger breaks your chains, revealed as a double agent who manipulated the Warriors. | *One Life for One World* · 3.4 · main | "[[Urianger]] appears from nowhere, and breaks the aetheric chains around you, revealing himself to have manipulated the Warriors of Darkness as a double agent." | #369 |
| M369e | The masked man is Urianger. | *One Life for One World* · 3.4 · main | "By the Twelve—Urianger!" | #369 |
| M369f | The Warriors of Darkness are already dead. | *One Life for One World* · 3.4 · main | "these desperate men and women of [[the First]] are already dead" | #369 |
| M369g | They were adventurers chosen by their Mothercrystal as Warriors of Light, whose world fell to ruin anyway. | *One Life for One World* · 3.4 · main | "their leader declares that they were humble adventurers chosen by the Mothercrystal to serve as Warriors of Light. They did everything that was asked of them, and yet, for all their efforts, and all their many triumphs, their world still fell to ruin." | #369 |
| M369h | Urianger orchestrated events to grant Minfilia her freedom. | *One Life for One World* · 3.4 · main | "the [[Archon]] confesses to having orchestrated events to grant Minfilia her freedom" | #369 |
| M369i | Minfilia says Hydaelyn has freed her to serve as Her emissary. | *One Life for One World* · 3.4 · main | "She has grown strong enough to set me free, that I might serve as Her emissary." | #369 |
| M369j | She will go to the Warriors' world and take its rising Light into herself. | *One Life for One World* · 3.4 · main | "I will hie me to your world and there take unto myself the Light which riseth even now to drown it" | #369 |
| M369k | The Warriors ask to be taken home, and you bid them and Minfilia farewell. | *One Life for One World* · 3.4 · main | "Their journey at an end, the broken Warriors of Light have a simple request for your departing friend" | #369 |
| M369l | Minfilia promises to return one day. | *One Life for One World* · 3.4 · main | "Someday, when I have found a way to free this star from Her sorrow, I promise you I shall repay the favor." | #369 |
| M370a | Thancred goes back alone to secure the crystals in Zahar'ak. | *An Ending to Mark a New Beginning* · 3.4 · main | "Leave them to me. I will go back into Zahar'ak and secure them." | #370 |
| M370b | Alisaie cannot condone Urianger's deception. | *An Ending to Mark a New Beginning* · 3.4 · main | "Alisaie, however, cannot bring herself to condone his actions." | #370 |
| M370c | Papalymo says it is past time he and Yda rejoined the Scions. | *An Ending to Mark a New Beginning* · 3.4 · main | "I believe it is past time Yda and I rejoined the Scions." | #370 |
| M370d | Gundobald knew Yda's father, who fought in the revolution against the King of Ruin. | *An Ending to Mark a New Beginning* · 3.4 · main | "who is apparently acquainted with both Yda and [[Curtis Hext|her father]], with whom he fought in the revolution against the [[King Theodoric|King of Ruin]] twenty years ago." | #370 |
| M370e | Yda's work for the Circle of Knowing and the Scions required absolute secrecy. | *An Ending to Mark a New Beginning* · 3.4 · main | "her involvement in the [[The Circle of Knowing|Circle of Knowing]] and its successor, the Scions of the Seventh Dawn, necessitated absolute secrecy" | #370 |
| M370f | Alphinaud has each Scion pursue what best keeps the realm safe, rather than a strict hierarchy. | *An Ending to Mark a New Beginning* · 3.4 · main | "Rather than holding to a strict hierarchy, he believes that each member should be allowed to pursue whatever activities they think would best serve to ensure the safety and security of the realm." | #370 |
| M370g | Krile and Alisaie are welcomed as full Scions. | *An Ending to Mark a New Beginning* · 3.4 · main | "[[Krile]] and Alisaie are welcomed as a fully fledged Scions" | #370 |
| M370h | Urianger is haunted by guilt over Minfilia's fate. | *An Ending to Mark a New Beginning* · 3.4 · main | "plainly haunted by feelings of guilt towards his role in [[Minfilia Warde|Minfilia's]] fate" | #370 |
| M370i | Papalymo asks to keep Tupsimati, as Louisoix's disciple. | *An Ending to Mark a New Beginning* · 3.4 · main | "My friends. If I may, I would ask that you entrust Tupsimati to me. … as Master Louisoix's disciple, I would keep it close at hand." | #370 |
| M370j | Elidibus gives the Griffin the Eyes of Nidhogg, judging him most deserving. | *An Ending to Mark a New Beginning* · 3.4 · main | "The Eyes of Nidhogg. Many were the candidates considered, but by your deeds, you have proven yourself the most deserving." | #370 |
| M370k | Elidibus calls a world without equilibrium a void in waiting. | *An Ending to Mark a New Beginning* · 3.4 · main | "a world bereft of equilibrium is but a void in waiting." | #370 |
| M370l | Every surviving senior Scion is back at the Rising Stones. | *An Ending to Mark a New Beginning* · 3.4 · main | "With every surviving senior Scion now safely back at the Rising Stones" | #370 |
| M371a | Alphinaud proposes electing a coordinator of operations as Minfilia's successor. | *Tidings from Gyr Abania* · 3.5 · main | "Thus I propose we elect a successor to Minfilia—not to serve as a fully fledged Antecedent, perhaps, but as a coordinator of operations." | #371 |
| M371b | A Resistance messenger warns that the masked Griffin is set on a dangerous course. | *Tidings from Gyr Abania* · 3.5 · main | "one of the Resistance leaders—a masked man known as the [[The Griffin|Griffin]]—has embarked upon a course" | #371 |
| M371c | The Griffin means to assault the Wall from the Ala Mhigan side. | *Tidings from Gyr Abania* · 3.5 · main | "He means to assault Baelsar's Wall from the Ala Mhigan side." | #371 |
| M371d | He wants the fires of war to spread to Eorzea. | *Tidings from Gyr Abania* · 3.5 · main | "From what I understand, he wants the fires of war to spread to Eorzea." | #371 |
| M371e | Y'shtola sends you to urge Aymeric to send an envoy. | *Tidings from Gyr Abania* · 3.5 · main | "You will be our voice in Ishgard. Explain the situation to Ser Aymeric, and encourage him to send an envoy." | #371 |
| M371f | Aymeric agrees to send an envoy to the Alliance council. | *Tidings from Gyr Abania* · 3.5 · main | "the lord commander soon agrees to send an envoy to the Alliance council." | #371 |
| M371h | The Diamond Forge artisans measure you for clothes. | *Tidings from Gyr Abania* · 3.5 · main | "After being ambushed by the Diamond Forge artisans and their measuring tapes" | #371 |
| M372a | Aymeric will be Ishgard's envoy, with the House of Commons' agreement. | *An Envoy for Ishgard* · 3.5 · main | "my counterpart in the House of Commons agreed that I should act as Ishgard's envoy." | #372 |
| M372b | Emmanellain is the new garrison commander at Camp Dragonhead, with Honoroit's practical help. | *An Envoy for Ishgard* · 3.5 · main | "invite you to meet the newly appointed garrison commander. Much to your surprise, you discover that it is none other than Ser [[Emmanellain]]—ably abetted by the rather more practically minded [[Honoroit]]." | #372 |
| M372c | Aymeric readies the garrison against trouble in the Black Shroud. | *An Envoy for Ishgard* · 3.5 · main | "Given the trouble brewing in the Black Shroud, I would see that the garrison is ready to defend the border." | #372 |
| M372d | Emmanellain doubts he will feel worthy of the post. | *An Envoy for Ishgard* · 3.5 · main | "I doubt I'll ever feel worthy to sit where he sat..." | #372 |
| M373b | Raubahn warns the fighting will spill into the Black Shroud. | *An Allied Decision* · 3.5 · main | "the resulting fighting is all but certain to spill over into the Black Shroud..." | #373 |
| M373c | Aymeric asks leave to deploy an Ishgardian force in Gridania. | *An Allied Decision* · 3.5 · main | "Elder Seedseer—I do hereby request leave to deploy a defensive force within the borders of Gridania." | #373 |
| M373d | Yugiri offers the Alliance her seasoned shinobi. | *An Allied Decision* · 3.5 · main | "We are few, but our most seasoned shinobi are at your disposal." | #373 |
| M373e | The council agrees to reinforce Gridania's border. | *An Allied Decision* · 3.5 · main | "the council members agree that the [[Gridania]]n border must be preemptively reinforced." | #373 |
| M373f | Alphinaud fears the Alliance is giving the Griffin what he wants. | *An Allied Decision* · 3.5 · main | "In making ready for war, is the Alliance not granting the Griffin the very thing he desired?" | #373 |
| M373g | Riol reports that the missing Scions have been found. | *An Allied Decision* · 3.5 · main | "I was glad to hear from Riol that the missing Scions have now been found." | #373 |
| M373h | Alphinaud says the intelligence comes from the Resistance's inner circle. | *An Allied Decision* · 3.5 · main | "Our information comes directly from a member of the Resistance's inner circle, and we have no cause to doubt its veracity." | #373 |
| M374a | Papalymo, Yda, Thancred and Yugiri go through the tunnels to confront the Griffin. | *Griffin, Griffin on the Wall* · 3.5 · main | "it is decided that [[Papalymo]], [[Yda]], [[Thancred]], and [[Yugiri]] will brave the secret tunnels" | #374 |
| M374b | The Griffin's soldiers attack the Wall in Grand Company uniforms. | *Griffin, Griffin on the Wall* · 3.5 · main | "Scouts report that the Griffin has begun his assault, his soldiers apparently clad in [[Grand Company]] uniforms..." | #374 |
| M374c | Alphinaud sees the Wall was bait. | *Griffin, Griffin on the Wall* · 3.5 · main | "The Wall was never the Griffin's target—it was bait." | #374 |
| M374d | The Griffin is Ilberd, the disgraced captain of the Crystal Braves. | *Griffin, Griffin on the Wall* · 3.5 · main | "Beneath the mask, the Griffin is revealed to be none other than [[Ilberd]], the disgraced captain of the [[Crystal Braves]]." | #374 |
| M374e | Ilberd's ritual summons a primal empowered by Ala Mhigan desperation and Nidhogg's eyes. | *Griffin, Griffin on the Wall* · 3.5 · main | "a ritual to summon a nightmarish [[primal]] empowered by [[Ala Mhigo|Ala Mhigan]] desperation and the might of [[Nidhogg]]'s [[Eyes of Nidhogg|eyes]]." | #374 |
| M374f | Ilberd's death completes the ritual. | *Griffin, Griffin on the Wall* · 3.5 · main | "His death completed the ritual. The primal is taking shape." | #374 |
| M374g | Papalymo's forbidden magick swallows him and seals the primal in a cocoon of light. | *Griffin, Griffin on the Wall* · 3.5 · main | "the incantation swallows Papalymo and encases the newborn entity in a vast cocoon of light." | #374 |
| M374h | Papalymo works the magick with Tupsimati. | *Griffin, Griffin on the Wall* · 3.5 · main | "Papalymo steps forward with [[Tupsimati]], and grimly begins to weave a forbidden magick of confinement..." | #374 |
| M374i | You escape on Hilda's airship. | *Griffin, Griffin on the Wall* · 3.5 · main | "You barely have time to escape on [[Hilda]]'s [[airship]]" | #374 |
| M374j | Yda is broken by the loss of her dearest friend. | *Griffin, Griffin on the Wall* · 3.5 · main | "Stunned by the unexpected summoning and the loss of her dearest friend, Yda's irrepressible spirit seems to have been broken at last." | #374 |
| M374k | Ilberd calls on a deity more terrible than the black wyrm of the Calamity. | *Griffin, Griffin on the Wall* · 3.5 · main | "which is why I will call upon a deity more terrible than the very black wyrm of the Calamity itself!" | #374 |
| M375a | Alphinaud breaks the news of Papalymo's passing. | *Louisoix's Finest Student* · 3.5 · main | "Alphinaud finally breaks the news of [[Papalymo]]'s passing." | #375 |
| M375b | Ilberd's primal will not be contained forever. | *Louisoix's Finest Student* · 3.5 · main | "the [[primal]] summoned by [[Ilberd]] will not be contained forever..." | #375 |
| M375c | Y'shtola and Krile will inspect the cocoon. | *Louisoix's Finest Student* · 3.5 · main | "We must inspect the cocoon of light, and ascertain the state of the primal trapped within." | #375 |
| M375d | No one knows yet how Ilberd came by Nidhogg's eyes. | *Louisoix's Finest Student* · 3.5 · main | "I have as yet been unable to explain how Ilberd came to possess Nidhogg's eyes." | #375 |
| M375e | Urianger says the pupil followed the master even unto the grave. | *Louisoix's Finest Student* · 3.5 · main | "Thus did the pupil follow in the footsteps of the master...even unto the grave." | #375 |
| M375f | Alphinaud calls for preparations to face Ilberd's creation. | *Louisoix's Finest Student* · 3.5 · main | "We must begin preparations to face Ilberd's accursed creation without delay." | #375 |
| M376a | Y'shtola has confirmed a primal in the cocoon. | *The Obvious Solution* · 3.56 · main | "Archon Y'shtola has confirmed the presence of a primal entity..." | #376 |
| M376b | A Garlean airship that approached the cocoon was destroyed. | *The Obvious Solution* · 3.56 · main | "A Garlean airship was observed making an approach, but the vessel was destroyed when it drew near." | #376 |
| M376c | Nero, a former tribunus of the XIVth Legion, proposes Omega against the primal. | *The Obvious Solution* · 3.56 · main | "Nero tol Scaeva, former tribunus of the XIVth Legion of the Garlean Empire. … I speak, of course, of Omega." | #376 |
| M376d | Nero claims to have restored the Ultima Weapon. | *The Obvious Solution* · 3.56 · main | "the mechanical genius who restored the Ultima Weapon to full operational capacity!" | #376 |
| M376e | Nero studied beside Cid at the Academy. | *The Obvious Solution* · 3.56 · main | "All those years studying side by side at the Academy, sharing both trial and triumph." | #376 |
| M376f | Omega has lain beneath Carteneau since the Allagans. | *The Obvious Solution* · 3.56 · main | "That hulk has been gathering dust beneath the plains of Carteneau since the Allagans breathed their last!" | #376 |
| M376g | The council sends a contingent to Carteneau to take command of Omega. | *The Obvious Solution* · 3.56 · main | "Let the records show that we invest this contingent with the authority to enter Carteneau and take command of Omega." | #376 |
| M376h | Alisaie says Nero may be killed if he proves treacherous. | *The Obvious Solution* · 3.56 · main | "If you suspected any foul play from Nero, you would be welcome to kill him." | #376 |
| M376i | Alphinaud wonders how Nero learned of the council meeting. | *The Obvious Solution* · 3.56 · main | "not least among them how Nero chanced to learn of the council meeting in the first place." | #376 |
| M377a | Alisaie insists Nero never leave her sight. | *The Greater Obeisance* · 3.56 · main | "your sister was adamant that he would never leave her sight." | #377 |
| M377b | Gosetsu brings word that his master is in peril and Doma calls Yugiri home. | *The Greater Obeisance* · 3.56 · main | "Our master languishes in dire peril, and Doma calls her daughter home!" | #377 |
| M377c | Urianger came to Gosetsu's aid when he collapsed. | *The Greater Obeisance* · 3.56 · main | "Collapsed in the street like an unfed stray, I was, until Lord Urianger came to my aid." | #377 |
| M377e | Yugiri stays to play her part in the crisis. | *The Greater Obeisance* · 3.56 · main | "A crisis threatens all within this realm, Eorzean and Doman alike, and I go to play my part in its resolution." | #377 |
| M377f | Gosetsu's blade is oathbound against the imperials. | *The Greater Obeisance* · 3.56 · main | "My blade is oathbound to fall upon the ranks of the imperials wheresoever they march!" | #377 |
| M377g | Tataru sews a blood pearl charm into your new outfit. | *The Greater Obeisance* · 3.56 · main | "I also sewed in a blood pearl charm to...you know...bring you home." | #377 |
| M377h | The Omega contingent assembles at the airship landing. | *The Greater Obeisance* · 3.56 · main | "the [[Omega]] contingent is assembling at the [[airship]] landing." | #377 |
| M378a | Cid and Nero will rouse Omega while you, Yda and the Domans keep watch. | *Fly Free, My Pretty* · 3.56 · main | "Nero and I will focus on rousing Omega, while" | #378 |
| M378b | Nero has been to Omega's control room before. | *Fly Free, My Pretty* · 3.56 · main | "How convenient. ...You've been here before." | #378 |
| M378c | An imperial officer, Grynewaht, calls Cid a traitor. | *Fly Free, My Pretty* · 3.56 · main | "I chase down a suspect airship, and who should I find but the traitor" | #378 |
| M378d | Omega is expected to seek out and capture the primal at the Wall. | *Fly Free, My Pretty* · 3.56 · main | "Once we release Omega, we can be fairly certain it will attempt to capture the primal at Baelsar's Wall." | #378 |
| M378e | Yda launches Omega for Papalymo's sake. | *Fly Free, My Pretty* · 3.56 · main | "You gave too much for us to waste this chance, Papalymo. So this is for you...and me." | #378 |
| M378f | Omega's signal falls silent, and Cid reengages its stasis. | *Fly Free, My Pretty* · 3.56 · main | "the signal emanating from the weapon falls silent, prompting Cid to reengage its stasis systems." | #378 |
| M378g | Papalymo's spell, bound to his aether, is fading. | *Fly Free, My Pretty* · 3.56 · main | "Papalymo's spell is fading. It was bound to his aether, you see...and if the connection is broken..." | #378 |
| M378h | The Empire has long known of Omega. | *Fly Free, My Pretty* · 3.56 · main | "we can be sure that the Empire has long been aware of its existence." | #378 |
| M379a | The cocoon hatched as Omega arrived, and a great dragon rose from it. | *The Far Edge of Fate* · 3.56 · main | "the cocoon hatched even as Omega arrived, and from its midst rose a great dragon." | 3.56 |
| M379b | Omega and the primal fell together onto Ala Mhigan soil. | *The Far Edge of Fate* · 3.56 · main | "I saw the pair fall motionless from the sky, coming to rest somewhere on Ala Mhigan soil." | 3.56 |
| M379c | The Domans call the primal Shinryu. | *The Far Edge of Fate* · 3.56 · main | "the Domans were heard to whisper the name … Shinryu" | 3.56 |
| M379d | The Alliance moves to secure the Wall. | *The Far Edge of Fate* · 3.56 · main | "the Alliance now intends to exploit the resulting confusion by moving to secure [[Baelsar's Wall]]." | 3.56 |
| M379e | Omega's stasis was reengaged, so it is useless to the Empire. | *The Far Edge of Fate* · 3.56 · main | "we quickly reengaged Omega's stasis systems" | 3.56 |
| M379f | Aymeric proposes contacting the Resistance in Gyr Abania. | *The Far Edge of Fate* · 3.56 · main | "I humbly submit that the time has come for us to make contact with the Resistance representatives in Gyr Abania." | 3.56 |
| M379g | Yda is not Yda: she is Lyse, the fallen Archon's younger sister. | *The Far Edge of Fate* · 3.56 · main | "revealing at last that she is not Yda at all, but the fallen [[Archon]]'s younger sister, [[Lyse]]." | 3.56 |
| M379h | Her real name is Lyse. | *The Far Edge of Fate* · 3.56 · main | "I'm sorry for lying to you. My real name is Lyse." | 3.56 |
| M379i | Yda died six years ago smuggling refugees out of Ala Mhigo. | *The Far Edge of Fate* · 3.56 · main | "Yda died six years ago, on a mission to smuggle refugees out of Ala Mhigo." | 3.56 |
| M379j | Papalymo brought Lyse Yda's mask as a keepsake, and she chose to take up her sister's work. | *The Far Edge of Fate* · 3.56 · main | "When Papalymo brought me Yda's mask, it was meant as a keepsake. But I decided I wanted to be his new partner; to keep alive all the good that she had done." | 3.56 |
| M379k | Papalymo persuaded the others to keep up the charade. | *The Far Edge of Fate* · 3.56 · main | "'Twas Papalymo who persuaded us to maintain the charade." | 3.56 |
| M379l | Lyse will continue her family's fight for Ala Mhigo. | *The Far Edge of Fate* · 3.56 · main | "...And I choose to continue my family's fight." | 3.56 |
| M379m | Their father fought to overthrow the mad king Theodoric. | *The Far Edge of Fate* · 3.56 · main | "He had fought to overthrow the mad king, Theodoric." | 3.56 |
| M379n | The Wall has fallen to the Alliance, as an officer reports to the Viceroy. | *The Far Edge of Fate* · 3.56 · main | "My lord Viceroy—our scouts have confirmed the preliminary reports. Baelsar's Wall has fallen to the Eorzean Alliance." | 3.56 |
| M379o | The Viceroy, Zenos, has heard the Domans call the primal Shinryu, and expects interesting days. | *The Far Edge of Fate* · 3.56 · main | "My spies tell me the Domans call it" | 3.56 |
| M379p | Zenos expects the coming days to be most interesting. | *The Far Edge of Fate* · 3.56 · main | "Yes...the coming days promise to be most interesting." | 3.56 |
| M380a | After Ilberd's assault on the Wall and his summoning of the primal now called Shinryu, the Alliance had little choice but to occupy Castrum Oriens. | *Beyond the Great Wall* · 4.0 · main | "[[Ilberd]]'s assault on [[Baelsar's Wall]] and subsequent summoning of the primal now known as “[[Shinryu]]” left the [[Eorzean Alliance]] with little choice but to occupy [[Castrum Oriens]]" | #380 |
| M380b | Raubahn asks the Scions to secure the Ala Mhigan Resistance's support. | *Beyond the Great Wall* · 4.0 · main | "General [[Raubahn Aldynn]] has asked that [[the Scions of the Seventh Dawn]] secure the support of the [[Ala Mhigan Resistance]]" | #380 |
| M380c | Lyse leads the Scions to Ala Mhigo, given her ties to the Resistance. | *Beyond the Great Wall* · 4.0 · main | "with [[Lyse]] to lead the way, given her ties to the Resistance." | #380 |
| M380d | Yugiri and Gosetsu return to Doma to aid their comrades against Garlemald. | *Beyond the Great Wall* · 4.0 · main | "The pair have resolved to return to the nation of [[Doma]], in the Far East, to aid their comrades in the fight against Garlemald." | #380 |
| M380e | Raubahn will not proceed without the Ala Mhigan people's support, and makes the Scions his emissaries to the Resistance. | *Beyond the Great Wall* · 4.0 · main | "As stated in my message, the Alliance will not proceed without the support and cooperation of the Ala Mhigan people. We would have you serve as our emissaries and make contact with the Resistance." | #380 |
| M380f | Cid reports that the search for Omega and Shinryu has found nothing. | *Beyond the Great Wall* · 4.0 · main | "apprises you of the latest developments in the search for [[Omega]] and Shinryu: namely, that there have not been any, the fate and whereabouts of both remaining unknown." | #380 |
| M380g | Raubahn fears Omega fell in occupied territory and may be in imperial hands. | *Beyond the Great Wall* · 4.0 · main | "Then 'tis like Omega fell to earth in occupied territory...meaning it may already be in imperial hands." | #380 |
| M380h | Gyr Abania has languished under imperial rule for twenty years. | *Beyond the Great Wall* · 4.0 · main | "which has languished under imperial rule for twenty long years" | #380 |
| M380i | Raubahn expects Garlemald to answer the primal summoned on its doorstep. | *Beyond the Great Wall* · 4.0 · main | "Ilberd summoned a primal right on their doorstep—and Garlemald is not like to let such shows of defiance go unanswered." | #380 |
| M380j | Y'shtola goes with the Scions as a healer. | *Beyond the Great Wall* · 4.0 · main | "I too shall accompany you. Such endeavors are seldom hampered by a surfeit of healers." | #380 |
| M380k | Urianger stays as caretaker of the Waking Sands, watching for primals and Ascians. | *Beyond the Great Wall* · 4.0 · main | "Gladly shall I continue to serve as caretaker of the Waking Sands, and there keep watch for signs of primal and Ascian activity both." | #380 |
| M380l | Seeing Yugiri and Gosetsu off, Lyse says it is time she went home too. | *Beyond the Great Wall* · 4.0 · main | "I know how you feel. It's time I went home too..." | #380 |
| M380m | Thancred stays behind as well. | *Beyond the Great Wall* · 4.0 · main | "there are some troubles that may prove too much for a single Scion—on account of which, I mean to stay." | #380 |
| M380n | Cid says the Ironworks engaged Omega's stasis systems. | *Beyond the Great Wall* · 4.0 · main | "Even so, it bears repeating that we engaged the stasis systems." | #380 |
| M380o | Cid's people have followed the trail of destruction of Omega and Shinryu without result. | *Beyond the Great Wall* · 4.0 · main | "Omega and—Shinryu, I think the shinobi called it? Anyway, we've been studying their trail of destruction, hoping it might lead us somewhere—which it has singularly failed to do." | #380 |
| M380p | Gosetsu says the Domans' purpose and the Scions' are one, though their battlefields lie far apart. | *Beyond the Great Wall* · 4.0 · main | "Though our battlefields be a thousand malms apart, our purpose is one!" | #380 |
| M380q | Yugiri thanks the Scions for standing with her people from the first, when they came as refugees. | *Beyond the Great Wall* · 4.0 · main | "You and yours stood with us from the first, when we came to this land as refugees in search of sanctuary." | #380 |
| M380r | Yugiri's people have a new home and no longer need her; she and Gosetsu return to their master. | *Beyond the Great Wall* · 4.0 · main | "They have built a new home for themselves, and lo longer need me to shepherd them. The time has come for Gosetsu and I to return to our master—to our home." | #380 |
| M381a | Lyse takes the Scions to the most influential faction of the Resistance. | *Lyse Takes the Lead* · 4.0 · main | "there are plenty of factions in the Resistance, so I'm taking us to see the one with the most influence." | #381 |
| M381b | That faction's headquarters is Rhalgr's Reach, an old temple east across the Velodyna River. | *Lyse Takes the Lead* · 4.0 · main | "Their headquarters are located in a place called Rhalgr's Reach—an old temple, to the east of here, across the Velodyna River." | #381 |
| M381c | The imperials fortified the old bridge over the Velodyna as Castellum Velodyna. | *Lyse Takes the Lead* · 4.0 · main | "It's part of Castellum Velodyna. That's what the imperials decided to call the old bridge after they fortified it." | #381 |
| M381d | A glamour hides the way into Rhalgr's Reach. | *Lyse Takes the Lead* · 4.0 · main | "So you say, but all I see are rocks. … ...Indeed. A glamour." | #381 |
| M381e | Lyse's friend, the messenger who came to the Scions, belongs to this faction. | *Lyse Takes the Lead* · 4.0 · main | "my friend Naago, the messenger who came to the Rising Stones, is a member of this faction." | #381 |
| M381f | The Resistance in the Reach was waiting for the Scions. | *Lyse Takes the Lead* · 4.0 · main | "They were waiting for us there. Men and women whose souls still cried out for what was lost." | #381 |
| M382a | Conrad commands the Ala Mhigan Resistance in Rhalgr's Reach. | *The Promise of a New Beginning* · 4.0 · main | "[[Conrad Kemp]], the commander of the [[Ala Mhigan Resistance]] in [[Rhalgr's Reach]]" | #382 |
| M382b | Alphinaud thanks Conrad for sheltering Lyse and Papalymo at great risk. | *The Promise of a New Beginning* · 4.0 · main | "pray allow me to thank you for sheltering Yda—forgive me—Lyse and Papalymo at great risk to yourselves." | #382 |
| M382c | Lyse says Papalymo laid down his life to give them a chance at a better future. | *The Promise of a New Beginning* · 4.0 · main | "Papalymo laid down his life to save us—to give us a chance to make a better future for ourselves." | #382 |
| M382d | Ilberd led Resistance fighters to the Wall and sacrificed them for his ritual. | *The Promise of a New Beginning* · 4.0 · main | "Followed him all the way to the Wall. Good men and women who never came back. … how the bastard had made sacrifices of them for his twisted ritual" | #382 |
| M382e | Alphinaud formally offers the Resistance the Alliance's military assistance. | *The Promise of a New Beginning* · 4.0 · main | "On behalf of the Eorzean Alliance, we do hereby extend a formal offer of military assistance to the Ala Mhigan Resistance." | #382 |
| M382f | Conrad sees the offer as the Alliance fighting the Empire by proxy with the Resistance's weaker forces. | *The Promise of a New Beginning* · 4.0 · main | "The Alliance, hoping to avoid a direct confrontation with the superior forces of the Empire, wishes to engage them by proxy with our vastly inferior ones?" | #382 |
| M382g | Conrad and his officers must confer before they answer. | *The Promise of a New Beginning* · 4.0 · main | "My comrades and I must confer on your proposal." | #382 |
| M382h | Conrad notes Lyse is not the first to fight the Empire under a false name. | *The Promise of a New Beginning* · 4.0 · main | "You're not the first person to take up arms against the Empire under a false name." | #382 |
| M383a | Conrad encourages the Scions to look around Rhalgr's Reach. | *A Haven for the Bold* · 4.0 · main | "You are free to look around Rhalgr's Reach. In fact, I would encourage you to do so." | #383 |
| M383b | Hellsguard and Seekers of the Sun fight in the Resistance. | *A Haven for the Bold* · 4.0 · main | "We've got Hellsguard from all the way over by the Spine in here, and plenty of Seekers of the Sun like Naago." | #383 |
| M383c | One faction will have nothing to do with the Resistance, seeing it as no different from the Garleans. | *A Haven for the Bold* · 4.0 · main | "The rest won't have anything to do with us. We're no different than Garleans to them." | #383 |
| M383d | Rhalgr, the breaker of worlds, guided the Ala Mhigans' ancestors to these lands. | *A Haven for the Bold* · 4.0 · main | "The breaker of worlds, who guided our ancestors to these lands all those centuries ago." | #383 |
| M383e | Yda told Lyse Rhalgr's story on stormy nights. | *A Haven for the Bold* · 4.0 · main | "Yda used to tell me the story on stormy nights." | #383 |
| M383f | The Reach has an infirmary, since the Resistance's operations always cost casualties. | *A Haven for the Bold* · 4.0 · main | "No matter how carefully we planned our operations, casualties were inevitable." | #383 |
| M383g | The Resistance depends on brave merchants, its sutlers, for provisions. | *A Haven for the Bold* · 4.0 · main | "The Resistance would be in a bad way without brave merchants to keep it provisioned." | #383 |
| M384a | Conrad accepts the Eorzean Alliance's proposal. | *A Bargain Struck* · 4.0 · main | "I, Conrad Kemp, do hereby accept the Eorzean Alliance's proposal." | #384 |
| M384b | Conrad can speak only for the people of Rhalgr's Reach. | *A Bargain Struck* · 4.0 · main | "Simply put, I can only speak for the people of Rhalgr's Reach." | #384 |
| M384c | The Resistance is more fragmented than the Scions realize. | *A Bargain Struck* · 4.0 · main | "But the Resistance is far more fragmented than you realize." | #384 |
| M384d | Trusted veterans died at the Wall, and young recruits fill their posts. | *A Bargain Struck* · 4.0 · main | "Some of our most trusted veterans died at the Wall, and we've had to fill their posts with the young and inexperienced." | #384 |
| M384e | Alphinaud returns to the Scions' headquarters. | *A Bargain Struck* · 4.0 · main | "I, for my part, shall return to the Rising Stones." | #384 |
| M384f | Conrad has another task for the Scions, which M'naago and Meffrid will explain. | *A Bargain Struck* · 4.0 · main | "I had something else in mind for you lot. M'naago and Meffrid will tell you more." | #384 |
| M384g | Krile offers healers for the Reach's infirmary. | *A Bargain Struck* · 4.0 · main | "From what I saw, they could do with a few more healers." | #384 |
| M385a | M'naago, nursed back to health by the Scions, must deliver Conrad's formal reply to Raubahn, and asks for an escort. | *A Friend of a Friend in Need* · 4.0 · main | "After thanking the [[Scions]] for nursing her back to health, [[M'naago]] explains that she has been tasked with delivering [[Commander Kemp]]'s formal reply to [[General Aldynn]]." | #385 |
| M385b | Raubahn commands the forces at Castrum Oriens. | *A Friend of a Friend in Need* · 4.0 · main | "General Aldynn commands the forces at Castrum Oriens." | #385 |
| M385ba | Meffrid, first met in Quarrymill, dissolved his Resistance unit in Gridania and returned to join Conrad's people in Rhalgr's Reach. | *A Familiar Face Forgotten* · 4.0 · main | "how he dissolved his unit of [[Ala Mhigan Resistance|Resistance]] fighters in [[Gridania]] and resolved to return to [[Gyr Abania]] and join [[Conrad]]'s people in [[Rhalgr's Reach]]" | #385b |
| M385bb | Risen to a position of responsibility, Meffrid must find recruits, and goes to Ala Gannha, Lyse's birthplace. | *A Familiar Face Forgotten* · 4.0 · main | "Having subsequently risen to a position of responsibility, he has been tasked with finding new recruits, and wishes to pay a visit to the village of [[Ala Gannha]]—[[Lyse]]'s birthplace." | #385b |
| M385bc | A temple on the way was razed by the mad king Theodoric, not the Garleans. | *A Familiar Face Forgotten* · 4.0 · main | "was razed not by the [[Garlean Empire|Garleans]] but by the mad king, [[King Theodoric|Theodoric]], in the chaos that preceded the imperial invasion." | #385b |
| M385bd | Several youths of Ala Gannha died at the Wall, so others may sympathize with the Resistance. | *A Familiar Face Forgotten* · 4.0 · main | "Several youths hailing from Ala Gannha were said to have lost their lives at [[Baelsar's Wall|the Wall]], and it is possible that others in the village may be sympathetic to the Resistance's cause." | #385b |
| M385be | Meffrid says they must speak with the village elder first. | *A Familiar Face Forgotten* · 4.0 · main | "We must speak with the elder first. I know for a fact he can be trusted." | #385b |
| M385bf | Meffrid warns that an imperial spy may be listening. | *A Familiar Face Forgotten* · 4.0 · main | "You never know when an imperial spy is listening." | #385b |
| M385bg | Lyse finds it strange to come home. | *A Familiar Face Forgotten* · 4.0 · main | "It feels strange coming home like this." | #385b |
| M385bh | You first met Meffrid in Quarrymill. | *A Familiar Face Forgotten* · 4.0 · main | "whom [[Killing Him Softly|you first met]] in [[Quarrymill]] what feels like a lifetime ago" | #385b |
| M385c | Most soldiers in Gyr Abania are conscripts from other provinces. | *A Friend of a Friend in Need* · 4.0 · main | "M'naago tells you that most soldiers in [[Gyr Abania]] are conscripts from other provinces." | #385 |
| M385d | M'naago warns never to pity a man in magitek armor. | *A Friend of a Friend in Need* · 4.0 · main | "And don't you ''dare'' pity a man in armor." | #385 |
| M385e | Alisaie volunteers to escort M'naago too. | *A Friend of a Friend in Need* · 4.0 · main | "prompting [[Alisaie]] to volunteer as well." | #385 |
| M385f | On the way you ambush an imperial patrol and its magitek armor. | *A Friend of a Friend in Need* · 4.0 · main | "You ambush and destroy the magitek armor M'naago overheard." | #385 |
| M386a | M'naago presents the Resistance's formal response to Raubahn. | *Signed, Sealed, to Be Delivered* · 4.0 · main | "On behalf of Commander Kemp of the Ala Mhigan Resistance, I present to you our formal response." | #386 |
| M386b | Raubahn is glad they have reached an accord. | *Signed, Sealed, to Be Delivered* · 4.0 · main | "I am glad we have reached an accord." | #386 |
| M386ba | Raganfrid says the Empire took the Ala Mhigans' lands, traditions and god. | *The Prodigal Daughter* · 4.0 · main | "They took our lands, our traditions, even our god." | #386b |
| M386bb | Meffrid calls the Griffin a traitor and madman whose schemes his people had no part in. | *The Prodigal Daughter* · 4.0 · main | "The Griffin was a traitor and a madman, and we had no part in his schemes." | #386b |
| M386bc | Raganfrid recognizes Lyse as her father's daughter. | *The Prodigal Daughter* · 4.0 · main | "Wait... ''I know those eyes''. You're one of Curtis's girls!" | #386b |
| M386bd | Lyse says she has come to take back her homeland. | *The Prodigal Daughter* · 4.0 · main | "I'm here to take back my homeland." | #386b |
| M386be | Lyse almost did not come home, and her sister never will. | *The Prodigal Daughter* · 4.0 · main | "I almost didn't. And my sister never will." | #386b |
| M386bf | Raganfrid says the village can give no more. | *The Prodigal Daughter* · 4.0 · main | "But I say to you again: we can give no more." | #386b |
| M386bg | The Resistance party may stay in the village. | *The Prodigal Daughter* · 4.0 · main | "At least we've been granted permission to stay." | #386b |
| M386bh | Meffrid decides to approach the villagers directly. | *The Prodigal Daughter* · 4.0 · main | "It's risky, and it's hardly subtle, but I say we approach the villagers directly" | #386b |
| M386bi | Meffrid introduces you to the village elder. | *The Prodigal Daughter* · 4.0 · main | "Meffrid would like to introduce you to the village elder." | #386b |
| M386bj | Meffrid tells the elder the Resistance has an agreement with the Alliance this time. | *The Prodigal Daughter* · 4.0 · main | "We have made an agreement with the Alliance—this time it will be different!" | #386b |
| M386c | Many of the Resistance's finest died in the doomed assault on the Wall, and recruits are scarce since. | *Signed, Sealed, to Be Delivered* · 4.0 · main | "Some of our finest took part in the Griffin's doomed assault, and we've been struggling to find new recruits ever since." | #386 |
| M386d | A shipment of prototype magitek armor has reportedly reached Castellum Velodyna from research facilities near the capital. | *Signed, Sealed, to Be Delivered* · 4.0 · main | "Namely, the arrival of a shipment rumored to have come from the research and development facilities near the capital, containing prototype magitek armor." | #386 |
| M386e | Pipin proposes harrying patrols to bait the prototypes to East End, where the Resistance will strike from behind. | *Signed, Sealed, to Be Delivered* · 4.0 · main | "we harry their patrols to bait them into bringing their prototype weapons to East End, where our Resistance allies will take them from behind." | #386 |
| M386f | Raubahn sees a joint victory as the Resistance's rallying cry. | *Signed, Sealed, to Be Delivered* · 4.0 · main | "they will have their rallying cry." | #386 |
| M386g | Pipin calls Raubahn his father. | *Signed, Sealed, to Be Delivered* · 4.0 · main | "The people need proof of this, Father." | #386 |
| M386h | M'naago backs the vice marshal's plan. | *Signed, Sealed, to Be Delivered* · 4.0 · main | "Assuming the vice marshal's intelligence is accurate, I agree with his assessment and fully support his plan." | #386 |
| M387a | The officer field-testing the prototypes is Grynewaht, the brutish Roegadyn you faced at Carteneau. | *Best Served with Cold Steel* · 4.0 · main | "The imperial officer charged with overseeing the field testing of the prototype magitek weapons is none other than [[Grynewaht]], the brutish [[Roegadyn]] whom you faced on the [[Carteneau Flats]]." | #387 |
| M387b | Grynewaht means to bring your head to Zenos. | *Best Served with Cold Steel* · 4.0 · main | "I can't wait to see the look on Zenos's face when I bring him your head!" | #387 |
| M387ba | An imperial patrol took Griseldis's herb-gathering spear as a rebel's. | *Hard Country* · 4.0 · main | "My weapon marked me as a rebel, or so they said." | #387b |
| M387bb | Her father died before she limped home. | *Hard Country* · 4.0 · main | "And by the time I limped back to the hut, it was too late for my father." | #387b |
| M387bc | The imperials have made it known she is not to be helped. | *Hard Country* · 4.0 · main | "The imperials, they....they've made it known I'm not to be helped." | #387b |
| M387bd | Griseldis had gone out with her spear to gather herbs for her dying father. | *Hard Country* · 4.0 · main | "My father, gods rest his soul, was deathly sick at the time, and so I'd gone out into the forest, spear in hand, to gather herbs." | #387b |
| M387c | Grynewaht's weapon is destroyed; he flees, and the Alliance and Resistance celebrate. | *Best Served with Cold Steel* · 4.0 · main | "As the smoldering wreck of his beloved weapon belches smoke into the sky, the imperial officer flees, leaving the combined force of [[Eorzean Alliance|Alliance]] regulars and [[Ala Mhigan Resistance|Resistance]] fighters to celebrate a momentous victory." | #387 |
| M387ca | The sacks hold saltpeter for making firesand. | *Death by a Thousand Rocks* · 4.0 · main | "It's saltpeter for making firesand, in case you're curious what it's all for." | #387c |
| M387cb | The quarry brothers sell it to sutlers who trade with the Resistance. | *Death by a Thousand Rocks* · 4.0 · main | "sweeping bits and pieces off to the side, to bundle up and sell to the sutlers what trade with the Resistance." | #387c |
| M387cc | The Empire put their father in uniform and shipped him off fifteen years ago. | *Death by a Thousand Rocks* · 4.0 · main | "putting our father in an imperial uniform and shipping him off to some faraway province fifteen years ago." | #387c |
| M387cd | It is a living and a way of fighting back. | *Death by a Thousand Rocks* · 4.0 · main | "It's a living, and a way of fighting back besides." | #387c |
| M387d | Raubahn tells Grynewaht to run and tell his viceroy that the day belongs to Ala Mhigo. | *Best Served with Cold Steel* · 4.0 · main | "Run and tell your viceroy! The day belongs to Ala Mhigo!" | #387 |
| M387e | The operation cost no casualties. | *Best Served with Cold Steel* · 4.0 · main | "Not a single casualty, for which I'm grateful beyond words." | #387 |
| M387f | Conrad recalls the record of Raubahn, the Bull of Ala Mhigo, before he went west. | *Best Served with Cold Steel* · 4.0 · main | "The “Bull of Ala Mhigo” had an impressive military record before he went west, as I recall." | #387 |
| M387g | Grynewaht vows that your next meeting will be the last. | *Best Served with Cold Steel* · 4.0 · main | "You'll pay for this! Mark my words, the next time we meet will be the last!" | #387 |
| M388a | The victory counts for little in practice but much as a symbol. | *Let Fill Your Hearts with Pride* · 4.0 · main | "Our victory may not count for much in practical terms, but its symbolic value can't be overstated." | #388 |
| M388b | Conrad calls it the beginning of a united Eorzea's campaign to drive out the Garleans. | *Let Fill Your Hearts with Pride* · 4.0 · main | "the beginning of a campaign by a united Eorzea to drive the Garleans from our lands." | #388 |
| M388c | Conrad notes that the Alliance took the field and risked lives for the Resistance's cause. | *Let Fill Your Hearts with Pride* · 4.0 · main | "But the Alliance took the field and risked their lives for our cause." | #388 |
| M388d | M'naago will go from village to village spreading word of the victory and the new alliance. | *Let Fill Your Hearts with Pride* · 4.0 · main | "Which is why I intend to go from village to village and spread word of our victory and our new alliance." | #388 |
| M389a | Imperials raid villages to seize supplies and stop stockpiling. | *A Life More Ordinary* · 4.0 · main | "the imperials have been swooping into villages unannounced and seizing supplies in order to prevent the people from stockpiling" | #389 |
| M389b | A villager rages at Lyse for her twenty years away. | *A Life More Ordinary* · 4.0 · main | "And where were you these past twenty years!?" | #389 |
| M389c | Lyse accepts that she left them behind. | *A Life More Ordinary* · 4.0 · main | "No matter what I say or do, it doesn't change the fact I left them behind." | #389 |
| M389d | Qiqirn attacked villagers taking the tributum to the imperials and carried off a man and the money. | *A Life More Ordinary* · 4.0 · main | "we were taking it to the imperials when the Qiqirn attacked! they took my brother, and our money!" | #389 |
| M389e | The Qiqirn took him to the Ziggurat. | *A Life More Ordinary* · 4.0 · main | "The Ziggurat? If the ratmen took him there, he's as good as dead..." | #389 |
| M389f | Lyse sets off after them at once. | *A Life More Ordinary* · 4.0 · main | "We've got to get after them!" | #389 |
| M389g | Meffrid admits he ran from the Wall too. | *A Life More Ordinary* · 4.0 · main | "I ran too, aye, but if I hadn't, I'd've never met [Forename] or the rest of you" | #389 |
| M390a | Wercrata is held at the Ziggurat by Qiqirn bandits. | *The Color of Angry Qiqirn* · 4.0 · main | "So Wercrata's being held somewhere at the Ziggurat to the south, which has been taken over by a host of Qiqirn bandits." | #390 |
| M390b | Wercrata has gone hungry for months. | *The Color of Angry Qiqirn* · 4.0 · main | "I've gone to bed hungry for months. I'm skin and bones!" | #390 |
| M390c | He was warned last month for paying short. | *The Color of Angry Qiqirn* · 4.0 · main | "we were given a warning last month for being short!" | #390 |
| M390d | Wercrata runs off to deliver the tributum. | *The Color of Angry Qiqirn* · 4.0 · main | "Oh gods, the tributum! The imperials will be wondering where I am! I have to go!" | #390 |
| M390e | Lyse has a bad feeling about it. | *The Color of Angry Qiqirn* · 4.0 · main | "I've got a bad feeling about this." | #390 |
| M391a | The Skulls are a unit of Ala Mhigan conscripts. | *The Black Wolf's Pups* · 4.0 · main | "From a distance, you observe Wercrata meeting with the Skulls─a unit of Ala Mhigan conscripts, judging by their accents." | #391 |
| M391b | Meffrid stops Lyse from intervening, since a rescue would invite reprisals against Wercrata's family and village. | *The Black Wolf's Pups* · 4.0 · main | "Lyse rises to intervene but is stopped by Meffrid, who tells her that saving the man would only invite reprisals against his family, if not his whole village." | #391 |
| M391c | The Skulls' commander orders them to stop. | *The Black Wolf's Pups* · 4.0 · main | "the Skulls' commander eventually orders them to stop, as they are needed elsewhere." | #391 |
| M391d | Fordola calls them off, as Lord Zenos expects their report. | *The Black Wolf's Pups* · 4.0 · main | "That's enough! Lord Zenos is expecting our report." | #391 |
| M391e | It sickens Meffrid to see his countrymen doing the viceroy's bidding. | *The Black Wolf's Pups* · 4.0 · main | "It sickens Meffrid to the soul to see his countrymen doing the bidding of the viceroy." | #391 |
| M391f | The Skull beating Wercrata insists he is a citizen. | *The Black Wolf's Pups* · 4.0 · main | "I've worked and toiled harder than any man! I'm a citizen, godsdammit!" | #391 |
| M391g | Meffrid will cut down every last Skull if it comes to it. | *The Black Wolf's Pups* · 4.0 · main | "But they have made their choice, and he is prepared to cut down every last one of them if it comes to it." | #391 |
| M392a | Wercrata is alive and the tributum paid. | *Homeward Bound* · 4.0 · main | "Wercrata's alive, and the tributum's been paid." | #392 |
| M392b | Raganfrid says an intervention would have branded Wercrata and his sister insurgents, perhaps the whole village. | *Homeward Bound* · 4.0 · main | "Wercrata and his sister would've been branded insurgents, if not the whole lot of us." | #392 |
| M392c | Raganfrid tells Lyse she is strong like her father. | *Homeward Bound* · 4.0 · main | "And you're strong, like your father." | #392 |
| M392d | The village has nothing left to give. | *Homeward Bound* · 4.0 · main | "that we've got nothing left to give. We can't help you." | #392 |
| M392e | Raganfrid apologizes for judging Lyse. | *Homeward Bound* · 4.0 · main | "I'll admit I was quick to judge you, Lyse, and for that I apologize." | #392 |
| M392f | The young villager who scorned Lyse offers to fight. | *Homeward Bound* · 4.0 · main | "If I truly cared for my people, I'd fight for them. So I will...if you'll have me." | #392 |
| M392g | Meffrid welcomes the new recruits to Rhalgr's Reach. | *Homeward Bound* · 4.0 · main | "You and yours are more than welcome at Rhalgr's reach." | #392 |
| M392h | Lyse had never seen the Skulls in the flesh before. | *Homeward Bound* · 4.0 · main | "I'd heard about the Skulls, but that was the first time I'd seen them in the flesh." | #392 |
| M392i | Lyse says they could have stopped the Skulls beating Wercrata. | *Homeward Bound* · 4.0 · main | "We saw what happened when he met with the Skulls. How they beat him half to death. We could've stopped them." | #392 |
| M392j | Meffrid warns that the day they fight Garlean soldiers will come. | *Homeward Bound* · 4.0 · main | "''When'', not if. That day will come, Lyse." | #392 |
| M397a | Alphinaud brings Scions eager to join the endeavor with the Resistance. | *Where Men Go as One* · 4.0 · main | "There were several amongst the Scions' ranks that were quite eager to take part in our joint endeavor with the Resistance" | #397 |
| M397b | Arenvald, a half-breed, comes to fight for a free Ala Mhigo. | *Where Men Go as One* · 4.0 · main | "A...a half-breed, as you can doubtless tell. … I'm here to fight for a free Ala Mhigo." | #397 |
| M397c | Arenvald is among the Scions who came to fight for the Resistance; you remember him from the Waking Sands. | *Where Men Go as One* · 4.0 · main | "Alphinaud arrives with several other Scions of the Seventh Dawn who have apparently expressed a desire to fight for the Ala Mhigan Resistance, including one Arenvald" | #397 |
| M397d | Conrad welcomes Arenvald as a trueborn son of Gyr Abania. | *Where Men Go as One* · 4.0 · main | "You are a trueborn son of Gyr Abania, same as me." | #397 |
| M397e | Once the recruits are trained, Conrad will propose an assault on Castellum Velodyna. | *Where Men Go as One* · 4.0 · main | "Once our new recruits have received sufficient training, I will propose to General Aldynn that we draw up plans for an assault on Castellum Velodyna." | #397 |
| M397f | In Ala Mhigo, the Empire learns a prototype unit was destroyed. | *Where Men Go as One* · 4.0 · main | "A unit tasked with field-testing prototype magitek weapons was attacked." | #397 |
| M397g | Zenos kills the pilus prior, scorning cowards who hide in the castra. | *Where Men Go as One* · 4.0 · main | "''Zenos kills the pilus prior.'' … Cowards who defer critical missions to their subordinates" | #397 |
| M397h | Zenos's father has not granted leave to march on the Alliance's lands. | *Where Men Go as One* · 4.0 · main | "Because my honored father, in his infinite wisdom, has not seen fit to grant us leave to march on their lands." | #397 |
| M397i | Zenos has invited his neighbors to Gyr Abania for the sport of kings. | *Where Men Go as One* · 4.0 · main | "Accordingly, we have extended an invitation to our neighbors." | #397 |
| M397j | An officer sneers that Fordola, an Ala Mhigan, holds her place in name alone. | *Where Men Go as One* · 4.0 · main | "Silence, Ala Mhigan! You do forget yourself! … Only by the grace of Lord van Baelsar were you afforded a place here—and that in name alone." | #397 |
| M397k | Zenos remarks that Fordola yearns to hunt her own, and gives her the floor. | *Where Men Go as One* · 4.0 · main | "yearns to hunt her own...The floor is yours, Commander." | #397 |
| M397l | Zenos says the XIIth is no place for the weak. | *Where Men Go as One* · 4.0 · main | "However, the XIIth is no place for the weak." | #397 |
| M397m | The scene takes place in the Royal Palace of Ala Mhigo. | *Where Men Go as One* · 4.0 · main | "Meanwhile, in the Royal Palace of Ala Mhigo..." | #397 |
| M397n | Fordola has a proposal for Zenos. | *Where Men Go as One* · 4.0 · main | "My lord, I have a proposal!" | #397 |
| M398a | Beves was a historian before the occupation. | *Future Rust, Future Dust* · 4.0 · main | "Before the occupation I was a humble historian." | #398 |
| M398b | Hrotmar joined to avenge a friend the imperials butchered in Ala Mhigo. | *Future Rust, Future Dust* · 4.0 · main | "it was to avenge a friend the imperials butchered before my very eyes, back in Ala Mhigo." | #398 |
| M398ba | Ahelissa says there is never enough food or supplies in the Reach. | *A Dash of Green* · 4.0 · main | "It's always like this. Never enough food or supplies to go around." | #398b |
| M398bb | Villages give what they can, though many have more deserving children. | *A Dash of Green* · 4.0 · main | "Too many villages, too many children more deserving than us..." | #398b |
| M398bc | The Resistance never loses sight of the dream. | *A Dash of Green* · 4.0 · main | "And we never lose sight of the dream." | #398b |
| M398bd | Ahelissa needs herbs for her stew. | *A Dash of Green* · 4.0 · main | "could you go and ask Tebbe for a variety of herbs?" | #398b |
| M398c | Orella's father and brothers were worked to death building a castrum. | *Future Rust, Future Dust* · 4.0 · main | "My father and my brothers were worked to death while building a castrum." | #398 |
| M398ca | An Ananta battlemaid trains the Resistance's recruits, and five have not arrived. | *Ye Wayward Brothers* · 4.0 · main | "I am a trainer, and I was told to expect new recruitsss thisss eve, yet nary a one has come." | #398c |
| M398cb | One recruit was late from praying for a sister the imperials had wronged. | *Ye Wayward Brothers* · 4.0 · main | "After what the imperials did to her, death was a mercy..." | #398c |
| M398cc | Another says Ala Mhigo's government is moot until the imperials are driven out. | *Ye Wayward Brothers* · 4.0 · main | "until we drive the imperials from our lands, the question is moot." | #398c |
| M398cd | All five recruits are accounted for. | *Ye Wayward Brothers* · 4.0 · main | "All recruitsss are presssent and accounted for." | #398c |
| M398d | Hughoc would be fighting for the Empire if not for Ala Mhigo. | *Future Rust, Future Dust* · 4.0 · main | "if I wasn't fighting for Ala Mhigo, I'd be fighting for the Empire." | #398 |
| M398e | Beves keeps the fighters' accounts as a window into the struggle. | *Future Rust, Future Dust* · 4.0 · main | "Many years from now, when these tempestuous times pass into memory, their accounts will serve as a window into the struggle." | #398 |
| M398f | Hrotmar's friend was killed over a piece of fruit. | *Future Rust, Future Dust* · 4.0 · main | "a passing soldier snatched up a piece of fruit." | #398 |
| M401a | A fighter has lost his lucky ward. | *Token of Faith* · 4.0 · main | "Seven hells, that's the one! That's my lucky ward!" | #401 |
| M401b | The mad king Theodoric burned the Temple of the Fist. | *Token of Faith* · 4.0 · main | "And then Theodoric came and burned it all to the ground, the mad bastard." | #401 |
| M401c | The Garleans cared even less for Ala Mhigan beliefs. | *Token of Faith* · 4.0 · main | "And after him came the Garleans, who cared even less for our beliefs." | #401 |
| M401d | His mother made a pilgrimage to the Temple of the Fist before he was born. | *Token of Faith* · 4.0 · main | "Before I was born, my mother made a pilgrimage to the Temple of the Fist" | #401 |
| M401e | He still keeps to the old ways. | *Token of Faith* · 4.0 · main | "But I still keep to the old ways, as do many others." | #401 |
| M402a | For the first time the Resistance and the Alliance will go on the offensive together. | *Crossing the Velodyna* · 4.0 · main | "For the first time, the Resistance and the Alliance shall go on the offensive and attempt to capture an imperial outpost in occupied territory." | #402 |
| M402b | Conrad sends M'naago to present his proposal for an assault on Castellum Velodyna. | *Crossing the Velodyna* · 4.0 · main | "I would have you present my proposal for an assault on Castellum Velodyna to General Aldynn personally." | #402 |
| M402c | Y'shtola will not take the field this time. | *Crossing the Velodyna* · 4.0 · main | "I shall refrain from taking the field on this occasion." | #402 |
| M402d | Krile came for Minfilia, and that has not changed. | *Crossing the Velodyna* · 4.0 · main | "I came here for Minfilia, and that hasn't changed." | #402 |
| M402e | Recruitment has exceeded expectations. | *Crossing the Velodyna* · 4.0 · main | "Our recruitment efforts have exceeded expectations." | #402 |
| M402f | Raubahn accepts the proposal and has Pipin assess Velodyna's defenses. | *Crossing the Velodyna* · 4.0 · main | "The Alliance hereby accepts Commander Kemp's proposal. … Pipin—I want Velodyna watched. Assess its defenses." | #402 |
| M402g | Alisaie says the choice to fight was made for them before they were born, when the Empire marched on Eorzea. | *Crossing the Velodyna* · 4.0 · main | "The choice was made for us ere we were born, Brother, when the Empire marched on Eorzea." | #402 |
| M402h | Ilberd's primal complicates matters. | *Crossing the Velodyna* · 4.0 · main | "further complicated by the existence of Ilberd's primal." | #402 |
| M403a | Pipin takes you, Alphinaud and Alisaie to reconnoiter Castellum Velodyna. | *In Crimson It Began* · 4.0 · main | "Pipin would have you, Alphinaud, and Alisaie accompany him on a reconnaissance mission." | #403 |
| M403b | Cannon fire interrupts you: smoke is rising from Rhalgr's Reach. | *In Crimson It Began* · 4.0 · main | "Your mission is interrupted by the sound of cannon fire from the east. You turn to see a column of smoke on the horizon...rising from Rhalgr's Reach" | #403 |
| M403c | No one answers by linkpearl, confirming a coordinated attack. | *In Crimson It Began* · 4.0 · main | "all but confirming your suspicions that a coordinated attack is underway." | #403 |
| M403d | Y'shtola lies near death, struck down by Zenos, the imperial viceroy. | *In Crimson It Began* · 4.0 · main | "Y'shtola is on the verge of death, her lifeblood pooling beneath her prostrate form. You turn to confront the man who dealt the blow: Zenos yae Galvus, the imperial viceroy himself." | #403 |
| M403e | Lyse is unharmed and Conrad merely unconscious. | *In Crimson It Began* · 4.0 · main | "Lyse is unharmed, and Conrad merely unconscious" | #403 |
| M403f | Meffrid calls Fordola a traitor and kinslayer, and she cuts him down. | *In Crimson It Began* · 4.0 · main | "Traitor! Kinslayer! … You're no kin of mine! … ''Fordola cuts down Meffrid.''" | #403 |
| M403g | Zenos bests you, then turns his back and walks away. | *In Crimson It Began* · 4.0 · main | "the viceroy sends you flying─the only testament to your resilience the broken blade he flings to the earth as he turns his back on you and walks away." | #403 |
| M403h | Raubahn and the Immortal Flames arrive and let the imperials withdraw to tend the wounded. | *In Crimson It Began* · 4.0 · main | "Raubahn and his Immortal Flames soon arrive, but he has seen enough death for one day, and orders his men to let the imperials withdraw and instead attend to the wounded." | #403 |
| M403i | Zenos is the crown prince. | *In Crimson It Began* · 4.0 · main | "having come face-to-face with the crown prince himself" | #403 |
| M403j | Zenos bids Fordola, his pilus, stand aside, saying you have promise. | *In Crimson It Began* · 4.0 · main | "Stand aside, Pilus. This one has promise." | #403 |
| M403k | Krile and Arenvald are among those outside the Reach. | *In Crimson It Began* · 4.0 · main | "you encounter a number of fleeing Resistance fighters, as well as Krile and Arenvald." | #403 |
| M403l | Conrad and others made a last desperate stand. | *In Crimson It Began* · 4.0 · main | "of a last desperate stand by Conrad and the others" | #403 |
| M403m | Pipin and Alisaie fell to Zenos before you did. | *In Crimson It Began* · 4.0 · main | "Though you endure Zenos's onslaught far longer than Pipin and Alisaie, even you are no match for him." | #403 |
| M403n | Raubahn reminds you that your actions saved lives. | *In Crimson It Began* · 4.0 · main | "Raubahn reminds you that your actions saved lives" | #403 |
| M404a | Raubahn sends you to see to the wounded. | *The Fires Fade* · 4.0 · main | "See to the wounded, [Forename]. If they live, help them. If not, move on." | #404 |
| M404b | Meffrid lies dead. | *The Fires Fade* · 4.0 · main | "Meffrid lies motionless, having breathed his last." | #404 |
| M404c | Conrad calls Meffrid a worrier who always put others first. | *The Fires Fade* · 4.0 · main | "Meffrid was a worrier. Always thinking about others before himself." | #404 |
| M404d | Meffrid sent his men in Quarrymill away, thinking they fought for him rather than the cause. | *The Fires Fade* · 4.0 · main | "Did you know that he sent his men in Quarrymill away because he thought they were fighting for him, and not the cause?" | #404 |
| M404e | Alphinaud says Meffrid's sacrifice let many live. | *The Fires Fade* · 4.0 · main | "because of his sacrifice, many now live who otherwise would not" | #404 |
