| M404f | Conrad had high hopes for Meffrid. | *The Fires Fade* · 4.0 · main | "I had...high hopes for him, is all..." | #404 |
| M404g | Meffrid died with a charm in his fist, which goes to Conrad. | *The Fires Fade* · 4.0 · main | "That charm must've been important. You'd best take it to Conrad." | #404 |
| M405a | The Reach is no longer safe: the imperials could return. | *Bereft of Hearth and Home* · 4.0 · main | "The imperials may have withdrawn, but they could return at any time to finish what they began." | #405 |
| M405b | The wounded go to Castrum Oriens by chocobo carriage. | *Bereft of Hearth and Home* · 4.0 · main | "Those who are not fit to make the journey to Castrum Oriens will need to be taken by chocobo carriage." | #405 |
| M405c | Y'shtola is strapped into a carriage. | *Bereft of Hearth and Home* · 4.0 · main | "Y'shtola and the others are safely strapped in." | #405 |
| M405d | Conrad stays to finish his work and will meet them at the Wall. | *Bereft of Hearth and Home* · 4.0 · main | "I'll see you at the Wall when it's finished." | #405 |
| M405e | You gather medical supplies from the infirmary. | *Bereft of Hearth and Home* · 4.0 · main | "Search the infirmary for medical supplies." | #405 |
| M406a | The Resistance's forces were decimated; there will be no assault on Castellum Velodyna. | *Divide and Conquer* · 4.0 · main | "our forces were decimated. In this state, we'll not be mounting an assault on Castellum Velodyna or anywhere else." | #406 |
| M406b | Zenos and his men returned to Specula Imperatoris. | *Divide and Conquer* · 4.0 · main | "As far as we can tell, Zenos and his men returned straightways to Specula Imperatoris after quitting this place." | #406 |
| M406c | Raubahn says Zenos is no fool to walk away. | *Divide and Conquer* · 4.0 · main | "Only a fool would turn his back and walk away...but Zenos is no fool." | #406 |
| M406d | Lyse saw the Skulls' commander, an Ala Mhigan, cut down Meffrid. | *Divide and Conquer* · 4.0 · main | "When that Skulls commander cut down Meffrid. … It wasn't a Garlean who did it—it was an ''Ala Mhigan''." | #406 |
| M406e | The Skulls are Crania Lupi, the Black Wolf's legacy: children born to Ala Mhigan dignitaries during the occupation. | *Divide and Conquer* · 4.0 · main | "Crania Lupi. The Black Wolf's legacy, and our shame. A unit made up of children born to Ala Mhigan dignitaries who came during the age of occupation." | #406 |
| M406f | The Skulls are the youths who fight for Zenos. | *Divide and Conquer* · 4.0 · main | "You mean the Skulls... The youths who fight for Zenos." | #406 |
| M406g | Before taking charge in Ala Mhigo, Zenos crushed Doma's rebellion. | *Divide and Conquer* · 4.0 · main | "Zenos led the imperial army to Doma, where he crushed the rebellion utterly." | #406 |
| M406h | Doma is still in Zenos's charge, and he is Varis's heir. | *Divide and Conquer* · 4.0 · main | "As a matter of fact, Doma remains in his charge to this day. Suffice it to say, Varis's heir is a peerless warrior and an accomplished general." | #406 |
| M406i | Raubahn urges the Resistance to hold fast and rebuild, to defeat Zenos together. | *Divide and Conquer* · 4.0 · main | "Have faith in your people, Master Kemp. Them and the Scions. Hold fast, rebuild, and when all is in place, we shall defeat Zenos together." | #406 |
| M406j | Conrad agrees to fight on. | *Divide and Conquer* · 4.0 · main | "Very well. For all you have done for us, we will fight on." | #406 |
| M406k | Lyse will go to Doma too, to help friends there. | *Divide and Conquer* · 4.0 · main | "If you're going to Doma, I'm coming too. I want to help our friends there...and make sure nothing like this ever happens again." | #406 |
| M406l | Conrad grants the plan for Doma promise but fears the Resistance lacks the strength meanwhile. | *Divide and Conquer* · 4.0 · main | "Look, I'll not deny the plan has promise—and I feel for our brothers and sisters in Doma, truly I do—but I fear we lack the strength to see it through." | #406 |
| M406m | Zenos is next in line to the Garlean throne. | *Divide and Conquer* · 4.0 · main | "Gods help us if he's next in line to the Garlean throne." | #406 |
| M407a | Alisaie plans a journey to the Far East. | *Lies, Damn Lies, and Pirates* · 4.0 · main | "Alisaie is contemplating the logistics of a journey to the Far East." | #407 |
| M407b | Krile will look after Y'shtola and support Arenvald and the others. | *Lies, Damn Lies, and Pirates* · 4.0 · main | "Would you be willing to look after Y'shtola and provide support to Arenvald and the others?" | #407 |
| M407c | No airship can make the journey, and an Alliance ship would be noticed. | *Lies, Damn Lies, and Pirates* · 4.0 · main | "it is simply not feasible for an airship to travel such a distance without impossibly large fuel reserves" | #407 |
| M407d | Tataru presents Captain Carvallain. | *Lies, Damn Lies, and Pirates* · 4.0 · main | "May I present to you Captain Carvallain of the Kraken's Arms!" | #407 |
| M407e | Carvallain can carry them only as far as Kugane, in Hingashi. | *Lies, Damn Lies, and Pirates* · 4.0 · main | "I cannot deliver you to Doma itself. I can take you only as far as Kugane, in Hingashi." | #407 |
| M407f | Carvallain agrees to take them this once. | *Lies, Damn Lies, and Pirates* · 4.0 · main | "But so long as you are prepared to abide the capricious whims of fate, I am willing to oblige you this once." | #407 |
| M408a | Alphinaud sends you and Lyse to the Doman allies in Revenant's Toll. | *Tales from the Far East* · 4.0 · main | "I would like the two of you to return to Revenant's Toll and speak with our Doman allies." | #408 |
| M408b | The Domans suffered Garlean tyranny for twenty-five years. | *Tales from the Far East* · 4.0 · main | "For twenty-five years we suffered under their tyranny." | #408 |
| M408c | They rose up when the Emperor died and Garlemald fell into chaos. | *Tales from the Far East* · 4.0 · main | "And then we spied an opportunity—the Emperor, dead! Garlemald in chaos!" | #408 |
| M408d | Zenos and the XIIth Legion crushed the uprising in an instant. | *Tales from the Far East* · 4.0 · main | "But when Zenos arrived with the XIIth Legion, it was over in an instant..." | #408 |
| M408e | Doma Castle is home to the imperial viceroy's witch and her forces. | *Tales from the Far East* · 4.0 · main | "Now it is home to the imperial viceroy's witch, and her host of men and magitek." | #408 |
| M408f | Hozan rejoices that you go to join Yugiri and Gosetsu in the fight. | *Tales from the Far East* · 4.0 · main | "That you go to join Yugiri and Gosetsu in the fight fills me with such joy!" | #408 |
| M408g | Doma Castle, once the shining symbol of Doma's heritage, has become a den for Garleans. | *Tales from the Far East* · 4.0 · main | "To think that what was once the shining symbol of our heritage—and the seat of dear Lord Kaien—has become a den for Garlean dogs..." | #408 |
| M408h | Hozan's ancestors held positions in Doma Castle. | *Tales from the Far East* · 4.0 · main | "My ancestors held positions within Doma Castle, even." | #408 |
| M409a | Urianger gives Alisaie a rapier and a cryptic warning, and their rift may be healing. | *Not without Incident* · 4.0 · main | "Urianger arrives bearing a cryptic warning and a practical gift: a rapier made for your young companion. Moved by the gesture, her steely demeanor softens, and you sense that the rift between the two may have finally begun to heal." | #409 |
| M409b | Urianger's warning is a Far Eastern verse. | *Not without Incident* · 4.0 · main | "Look ye where the sun doth rise, see crimson embers, dark'ning skies..." | #409 |
| M409c | A mysterious force drags the ship off course. | *Not without Incident* · 4.0 · main | "speaking of strange things in the water, and a mysterious force dragging the ''Misery'' off course." | #409 |
| M409d | The being the sailors call Lorelei falls, and the voyage resumes. | *Not without Incident* · 4.0 · main | "With the demise of the mysterious being the sailors call Lorelei, the clouds part" | #409 |
| M409e | Kugane is Hingashi's only port open to outsiders, across the sea from Doma. | *Not without Incident* · 4.0 · main | "The only port open to outsiders in Hingashi, across the Ruby Sea from Othard. From Doma." | #409 |
| M409f | In Kugane's harbor Lyse stands apart, whispering a prayer or an oath. | *Not without Incident* · 4.0 · main | "From the corner of your eye you spy Lyse, standing apart from the others, her lips moving silently. A prayer or an oath, perhaps." | #409 |
| M409g | Carvallain and his crew sail the Scions from Limsa Lominsa. | *Not without Incident* · 4.0 · main | "you depart for Limsa Lominsa, where Captain Carvallain and the crew of the ''Misery'' are preparing for your journey to the other side of the world." | #409 |
| M409h | Kugane is the nation's only port open to foreign trade. | *Not without Incident* · 4.0 · main | "Regrettably, the only port in this nation open to foreign trade." | #409 |
| M410a | Carvallain pledged to take the Scions only as far as Kugane. | *The Man from Ul'dah* · 4.0 · main | "I realize you pledged only to take us as far as Kugane, and I have no desire to change the terms of our agreement." | #410 |
| M410b | Yugiri and Gosetsu must have found a way on, unless they are still in the city. | *The Man from Ul'dah* · 4.0 · main | "They must have found a way to Othard—assuming they are not still in the city." | #410 |
| M410c | Hancock of the East Aldenard Trading Company welcomes them on behalf of Chairman Lolorito. | *The Man from Ul'dah* · 4.0 · main | "Hancock, of the East Aldenard Trading Company, at your service. On behalf of Chairman Lolorito, I bid you welcome to Kugane" | #410 |
| M410d | Alphinaud distrusts those who serve a master with whom the Scions have a difficult relationship. | *The Man from Ul'dah* · 4.0 · main | "especially those who serve a master with whom we have such a difficult relationship" | #410 |
| M410e | Many nations, the Garlean Empire included, keep embassies in Kugane. | *The Man from Ul'dah* · 4.0 · main | "Many notable nations and empires have embassies here, you know—including of course, the Garlean Empire" | #410 |
| M410f | Beneath Kugane's trade, world powers wage a war for supremacy. | *The Man from Ul'dah* · 4.0 · main | "Beneath this veneer of bustling trade, a war for supremacy is being waged between world powers." | #410 |
| M410g | Alisaie accepts Hancock's invitation for now. | *The Man from Ul'dah* · 4.0 · main | "I say we accept his invitation—for now." | #410 |
| M411a | The Ijin-gai is where foreigners may live. | *Where the Streets Are Paved with Koban* · 4.0 · main | "This road leads to the Ijin-gai: the district in which" | #411 |
| M411b | The Sekiseigumi punish breaches of the peace severely. | *Where the Streets Are Paved with Koban* · 4.0 · main | "The Sekiseigumi punish breaches of peace quite severely." | #411 |
| M411c | A Sekiseigumi samurai cut down a foreign merchant with a single stroke. | *Where the Streets Are Paved with Koban* · 4.0 · main | "The samurai cut him in half with a single stroke." | #411 |
| M411d | The Garlean Consulate stands in Kugane. | *Where the Streets Are Paved with Koban* · 4.0 · main | "This, as you may have surmised, is the consulate of you-know-who." | #411 |
| M411e | Kugane's markets take every currency, Eorzean gil included. | *Where the Streets Are Paved with Koban* · 4.0 · main | "we accept all manner of currencies. Rest assured, your Eorzean gil are welcome here." | #411 |
| M412a | Hancock calls the company's aid Lolorito's contrition for the bloody banquet. | *By the Grace of Lord Lolorito* · 4.0 · main | "the chairman bids you consider this as an expression of his heartfelt contrition for the bloody banquet and the unpleasantness which followed." | #412 |
| M412b | The company's interests coincide with aiding the Doman Liberation Front. | *By the Grace of Lord Lolorito* · 4.0 · main | "By sheer coincidence, your purpose here happens to coincide with our own interests. That is, assuming you are come to aid the Doman Liberation Front?" | #412 |
| M412c | Yugiri and Gosetsu came on a company vessel but could not cross the Ruby Sea. | *By the Grace of Lord Lolorito* · 4.0 · main | "They came here aboard a company vessel, you see. However, they encountered difficulties securing passage across the Ruby Sea." | #412 |
| M412d | The Ruby Sea belongs to neither Hingashi nor Doma but to the Confederacy. | *By the Grace of Lord Lolorito* · 4.0 · main | "the Ruby Sea does not fall within the dominion of either Hingashi or Doma, being controlled since antiquity by a third party: namely, the Confederacy." | #412 |
| M412e | The Confederacy charges every ship in its waters a toll, the Ruby Tithe, for its protection. | *By the Grace of Lord Lolorito* · 4.0 · main | "demanding that any vessel entering its waters pay a toll referred to as the" | #412 |
| M412f | Doma's recently appointed acting viceroy has condemned the Confederacy. | *By the Grace of Lord Lolorito* · 4.0 · main | "The recently appointed acting imperial viceroy of Doma had other ideas, however. She has formally condemned the Confederacy" | #412 |
| M412g | Hingashi cut ties with the Confederacy and forbade the Ruby Tithe. | *By the Grace of Lord Lolorito* · 4.0 · main | "Bowing to this pressure, Hingashi severed ties with these toll-takers and forbade the payment of the Ruby Tithe" | #412 |
| M412h | Alphinaud will follow Yugiri and Gosetsu's footsteps to Doma. | *By the Grace of Lord Lolorito* · 4.0 · main | "I say we follow in Yugiri and Gosetsu's footsteps and attempt to secure passage to Doma for ourselves." | #412 |
| M412i | Alphinaud is reluctant to be in Lolorito's debt but takes the means available. | *By the Grace of Lord Lolorito* · 4.0 · main | "Though I am reluctant to take any action whatsoever that could be construed as placing ourselves in Lord Lolorito's debt" | #412 |
| M412j | Garlemald conquered Doma some twenty-five years ago. | *By the Grace of Lord Lolorito* · 4.0 · main | "Garlemald conquered Doma some twenty-five years past" | #412 |
| M412k | The Scions come to assist Yugiri and Gosetsu in their fight. | *By the Grace of Lord Lolorito* · 4.0 · main | "we are come in search of our Far Eastern allies, Yugiri and Gosetsu, whom we hope to assist in their fight." | #412 |
| M413a | Alphinaud sketched Yugiri and Gosetsu for the search. | *A Good Samurai Is Hard to Find* · 4.0 · main | "I took the liberty of preparing some few sketches of Yugiri and Gosetsu during our voyage." | #413 |
| M413b | The hostelry's keeper says the pair sought passage across the Ruby Sea. | *A Good Samurai Is Hard to Find* · 4.0 · main | "The two individuals of whom you speak did indeed come here. they sought passage across the Ruby Sea." | #413 |
| M413c | She will also answer those who ask after the Scions. | *A Good Samurai Is Hard to Find* · 4.0 · main | "But if I do, I must ''also'' oblige the ones who ask after ''you''." | #413 |
| M413d | A captain refused a Roegadyn who wanted to charter a ship across the Ruby Sea. | *A Good Samurai Is Hard to Find* · 4.0 · main | "He wanted to charter a ship to take him across the Ruby Sea, the mad devil." | #413 |
| M413e | A spice trader could not help the Roegadyn bound for Doma. | *A Good Samurai Is Hard to Find* · 4.0 · main | "He was for Doma, but I could not help him." | #413 |
| M413f | No captain knows the woman. | *A Good Samurai Is Hard to Find* · 4.0 · main | "The woman is unknown to me, but the man is not." | #413 |
| M414a | Gyodo claims to have met the Domans and found them a ship. | *It's Probably a Trap* · 4.0 · main | "claims not only to have met with your Doman friends, but to have helped them secure passage on a ship to Othard." | #414 |
| M414b | The Scions conclude it is a trap and spring it anyway. | *It's Probably a Trap* · 4.0 · main | "Your comrades quickly conclude that the Namazu is attempting to lure you into a trap...and that for lack of a better plan, you may as well spring it." | #414 |
| M414c | Gyodo betrays them, and Lyse boots him into the air. | *It's Probably a Trap* · 4.0 · main | "Gyodo's sudden but inevitable betrayal comes as no surprise, but the enthusiasm with which Lyse boots him into the air does." | #414 |
| M414d | The Sekiseigumi send patrols after you, perhaps taking you for foreign agents. | *It's Probably a Trap* · 4.0 · main | "Perhaps suspecting you of being the agents of foreign powers, the Sekiseigumi dispatch several patrols to the Ijin District" | #414 |
| M414e | Your savior is Soroban, a Kojin of the Blue and a merchant who wants an agreement with Hancock. | *It's Probably a Trap* · 4.0 · main | "at last learn the identity of your savior: Soroban, a Kojin of the Blue. … A merchant by trade, he seeks to broker an agreement with Hancock, who is more than happy to do so." | #414 |
| M414f | Soroban offers passage across the Ruby Sea. | *It's Probably a Trap* · 4.0 · main | "Ah, yes─you sought passage across the Ruby Sea, did you not?" | #414 |
| M414g | Kojin of the Red serve the Empire as mercenaries; the Blue walk a different path. | *It's Probably a Trap* · 4.0 · main | "Kojin of the Red may serve the Empire as mercenaries, but Kojin of the Blue walk a different path." | #414 |
| M414h | The viceroy was once a spy herself. | *It's Probably a Trap* · 4.0 · main | "The viceroy was once a spy herself, as I recall." | #414 |
| M414i | Lyse carries the unconscious Gyodo back. | *It's Probably a Trap* · 4.0 · main | "the unconscious Gyodo that Lyse carried on her shoulder to the Ruby Bazaar twitches" | #414 |
| M414j | Gyodo is a Namazu who frequents the Shiokaze Hostelry. | *It's Probably a Trap* · 4.0 · main | "Gyodo, a Namazu said to frequent the Shiokaze Hostelry" | #414 |
| M414k | Gyodo summons the ambushers on the bridge. | *It's Probably a Trap* · 4.0 · main | "To me, to me! These ones are for Doma!" | #414 |
| M415a | Gyodo met only the samurai and introduced him to the Garleans. | *Making the Catfish Sing* · 4.0 · main | "Only the samurai did I meet. Yes, yes, only him! He asked after a ship, so I provided an introduction to...to the Garleans..." | #415 |
| M415b | The Garleans offer a bounty for Domans found in Kugane. | *Making the Catfish Sing* · 4.0 · main | "So when the Garleans offered a bounty for any Domans found in Kugane─" | #415 |
| M415c | Blades were drawn and the Sekiseigumi took the samurai away. | *Making the Catfish Sing* · 4.0 · main | "Blades were drawn, men were cut down, and the Sekiseigumi came. They took the samurai away." | #415 |
| M415d | Hancock means to bribe the right official. | *Making the Catfish Sing* · 4.0 · main | "If he is in the custody of the Sekiseigumi, I need only see to it that the requisite funds find their way into the right official's hands" | #415 |
| M415e | The Sekiseigumi keep their word to free the samurai who defeated them, asking him to avoid altercations. | *Making the Catfish Sing* · 4.0 · main | "Our word, once given, may not be broken─but we pray you make every effort to avoid future altercations." | #415 |
| M415f | Hancock's help was not needed. | *Making the Catfish Sing* · 4.0 · main | "I gather my assistance was not required?" | #415 |
| M415g | Alphinaud keeps watch over Gyodo. | *Making the Catfish Sing* · 4.0 · main | "I will keep an eye on him for the time being." | #415 |
| M416a | Gosetsu won his freedom in a contest of strength just before the Scions arrived. | *Once More, to the Ruby Sea* · 4.0 · main | "he managed to win his freedom through a contest of strength mere moments before you and the others arrived to rescue him." | #416 |
| M416b | Gosetsu and Yugiri parted to improve their chances of finding Lord Hien. | *Once More, to the Ruby Sea* · 4.0 · main | "For a time, aye, but we parted ways some while ago, the better to improve our chances of finding Lord Hien." | #416 |
| M416c | Lord Hien is heir to Doma's throne and their master. | *Once More, to the Ruby Sea* · 4.0 · main | "Lord Hien is the heir to Doma's throne, and our master." | #416 |
| M416d | Hien sent Yugiri to sea with as many people as possible, and stayed to fight. | *Once More, to the Ruby Sea* · 4.0 · main | "Once Lord Hien understood this, he bade Yugiri take to the sea with as many of our people as possible. He, however, would stand and fight, that they might have more time to escape..." | #416 |
| M416e | Gosetsu lost Hien in battle, and knows only that he is beyond the Empire's reach. | *Once More, to the Ruby Sea* · 4.0 · main | "And then, one day, in a fierce battle, we were separated. I knew not what became of him─only that he was beyond the Empire's reach" | #416 |
| M416f | Doma's new viceroy, Yotsuyu, torments its people and pursues Hien. | *Once More, to the Ruby Sea* · 4.0 · main | "for the new viceroy, Yotsuyu, is a black-hearted villainess who delights in the torment of our people. There is naught she would not do to further her pursuit of Lord Hien." | #416 |
| M416g | The Scions come to help the Doman Liberation Front resume its fight. | *Once More, to the Ruby Sea* · 4.0 · main | "we are come to aid the Doman Liberation Front in resuming their efforts to overthrow their imperial oppressors." | #416 |
| M416h | Gosetsu joins them and will lead them to the Domans' headquarters and Yugiri. | *Once More, to the Ruby Sea* · 4.0 · main | "Then allow me to join you! … Once there, I can lead you to our headquarters in Yanxia, where we shall all be reunited with Yugiri!" | #416 |
| M416i | Alisaie sets Alphinaud to gather intelligence in Kugane with Tataru and Hancock. | *Once More, to the Ruby Sea* · 4.0 · main | "Alphinaud's talents would seem better applied here in Kugane, working closely with Tataru and Hancock to feed us intelligence." | #416 |
| M416j | Tataru and Alphinaud remain while you, Lyse, Alisaie and Gosetsu meet Soroban. | *Once More, to the Ruby Sea* · 4.0 · main | "it is decided that [[Tataru]] and Alphinaud will remain, while you, [[Lyse]], [[Alisaie]], and Gosetsu go to meet [[Soroban]] at [[Pier 2|the docks]]." | #416 |
| M416k | Hien survived, and the Empire seeks to make an example of him. | *Once More, to the Ruby Sea* · 4.0 · main | "only that he somehow survived, and that the [[Garlean Empire|Empire]] still seeks to make an example of him." | #416 |
| M416l | The crown prince and the XIIth left the Domans no chance. | *Once More, to the Ruby Sea* · 4.0 · main | "The crown prince was a merciless foe. Against him and the might of the XIIth, we had no chance." | #416 |
| M416m | Hien was a passionate voice for Doman liberation. | *Once More, to the Ruby Sea* · 4.0 · main | "His was a passionate voice for the cause of Doman liberation" | #416 |
| M416n | The Ruby Sea is named for the crimson it turns at sunrise. | *Once More, to the Ruby Sea* · 4.0 · main | "Before you lies the vast expanse of the Ruby Sea, so named for the shade of crimson it turns with the morning sun." | #416 |
| M417a | Setting out unprepared invites the Confederacy to claim your vessel and your life. | *Open Water* · 4.0 · main | "To set forth without making the necessary preparations is to invite the Confederacy to claim your vessel─and your life should you resist." | #417 |
| M417b | Paying the Ruby Tithe earns the pirate lords' blessing. | *Open Water* · 4.0 · main | "But this fate can be avoided by paying the Ruby Tithe, thereby earning you the blessing of the pirate lords." | #417 |
| M417c | Soroban leads them to an isle to the west. | *Open Water* · 4.0 · main | "So─let us go and greet them. Our destination is the isle to the west." | #417 |
| M417d | Tansui is a mate serving the Confederacy's captain. | *Open Water* · 4.0 · main | "I am Tansui, a humble mate who serves at the pleasure of the Confederacy's captain." | #417 |
| M417e | Tansui expects business from Soroban's company. | *Open Water* · 4.0 · main | "As you are in the company of the shellback, I presume we have business to discuss..." | #417 |
| M417f | Soroban says they must speak with Tansui. | *Open Water* · 4.0 · main | "The one with whom we must speak is a man named Tansui." | #417 |
| M417g | Tansui greets you by name. | *Open Water* · 4.0 · main | "Most honorable and esteemed [Forename] [Surname]." | #417 |
| M418a | Soroban offers to pay the Scions' passage. | *Boys with Boats* · 4.0 · main | "I would pay for their passage to Othard." | #418 |
| M418b | Tansui says the sum is too small for a safe crossing. | *Boys with Boats* · 4.0 · main | "I cannot say with confidence that you would reach your destination safely─unless you tripled it." | #418 |
| M418c | Tansui counts the Scions' tithe paid for what he expects they will do. | *Boys with Boats* · 4.0 · main | "I shall consider their tithe paid─not for what they have done, but for what I expect they will do." | #418 |
| M418d | An imperial officer scorns the Confederacy's extortion. | *Boys with Boats* · 4.0 · main | "Your feeble attempts at extortion will not work on us." | #418 |
| M418e | Defying the Empire would see the Confederacy branded its enemy. | *Boys with Boats* · 4.0 · main | "she'll accuse us of attempting to foment rebellion and brand us enemies of the Empire" | #418 |
| M418f | Many in the Confederacy are of Doman descent, and the acting viceroy is no friend of Domans. | *Boys with Boats* · 4.0 · main | "many of my comrades in the Confederacy are of Doman descent─and the acting viceroy...well, she's none too fond of Domans, to say the least." | #418 |
| M418g | The acting viceroy continues her purge of Doma's radical elements. | *Boys with Boats* · 4.0 · main | "continues her purge of Doma's “radical elements.”" | #418 |
| M418h | Tansui says the Confederacy is outmatched by the imperial army. | *Boys with Boats* · 4.0 · main | "Compared to them, we're boys with boats." | #418 |
| M418i | Gosetsu says the viceroy must be stopped. | *Boys with Boats* · 4.0 · main | "She must be stopped. Come, let us find Soroban and put to sea at once!" | #418 |
| M418j | Soroban did not want his Red brothers to see him with the Scions. | *Boys with Boats* · 4.0 · main | "As you may have guessed, I was reluctant to be seen in your company by my Red brothers." | #418 |
| M418k | With Tansui's business done, they can cross the Ruby Sea without fear. | *Boys with Boats* · 4.0 · main | "Now that our business with Tansui is concluded, we can traverse the Ruby Sea without fear!" | #418 |
| M419a | Soroban, relieved that you will not judge his Blue Kojin for the deeds of the Red, carries you on to Othard. | *To Bend with the Wind* · 4.0 · main | "Relieved that you will not judge him and his fellow Blue Kojin harshly for the deeds of the Red, Soroban is eager to take you the rest of the way to Othard" | #419 |
| M419b | Isari, a village on Othard's coast, is occupied by imperial forces. | *To Bend with the Wind* · 4.0 · main | "turn your attentions to Isari, which you soon discover to be occupied by imperial forces" | #419 |
| M419c | There you see Yotsuyu, the imperial viceroy, and Grynewaht, whom you have beaten in Gyr Abania and Carteneau. | *To Bend with the Wind* · 4.0 · main | "From afar, you catch your first glimpse of Yotsuyu, the imperial viceroy, and Grynewaht of all people, who seems none the worse for the trouncings you dealt him in Gyr Abania and Carteneau" | #419 |
| M419d | Yotsuyu forces one villager to execute another, then has him shot for refusing to kill his own parents. | *To Bend with the Wind* · 4.0 · main | "Yotsuyu forces one villager to execute another, before having him shot for refusing to kill his own parents." | #419 |
| M419e | Gosetsu surrenders himself to the viceroy, expecting her to question him and give you time to plan a rescue. | *To Bend with the Wind* · 4.0 · main | "Gosetsu resolves to end the madness by surrendering himself into the viceroy's custody, reasoning that she will wish to question him thoroughly, giving you time to formulate a rescue plan" | #419 |
| M419f | Gosetsu is led away into the village tavern. | *To Bend with the Wind* · 4.0 · main | "you can only watch as he is led away into the local tavern." | #419 |
| M419g | Gosetsu calls Yotsuyu the traitor who sold her homelands to the Garleans. | *To Bend with the Wind* · 4.0 · main | "The traitor who sold her homelands to the Garleans—Yotsuyu!" | #419 |
| M419h | The large settlement on Onokoro, with its own aetheryte, is under the Confederacy's control. | *To Bend with the Wind* · 4.0 · main | "It would seem that Gosetsu intended for you to seek the aid of the Confederacy, as the large settlement─complete with its own aetheryte─appears to be under their control." | #419 |
| M419i | Gosetsu bade you seek aid at the tower to the northeast. | *To Bend with the Wind* · 4.0 · main | "Recalling that Gosetsu bade you seek aid at the mysterious tower to the northeast" | #419 |
| M419j | Gosetsu thanks Soroban as a clever and industrious merchant. | *To Bend with the Wind* · 4.0 · main | "Truly, we are blessed to have befriended such a clever and industrious merchant!" | #419 |
| M419k | There seems little chance the Confederacy will answer your call. | *To Bend with the Wind* · 4.0 · main | "there is surely little chance that they will answer your call." | #419 |
| M419l | Gosetsu is a former leader of the rebellion and a known fugitive. | *To Bend with the Wind* · 4.0 · main | "As a former leader of the rebellion and known fugitive, they will wish to detain me until they are certain they have learned all I know." | #419 |
| M419m | Alisaie, frustrated, swims with Lyse for Onokoro. | *To Bend with the Wind* · 4.0 · main | "Though Alisaie is frustrated by this turn of events, she recognizes that what is done is done, and that you have no choice but to find a solution." | #419 |
| M419n | Tansui spoke of Doma's viceroy holding court in one of the fishing villages. | *To Bend with the Wind* · 4.0 · main | "Recalling that Tansui spoke of Doma's viceroy “holding court” in one of the fishing villages" | #419 |
| M419o | Alisaie and Lyse swim for the isle of Onokoro. | *To Bend with the Wind* · 4.0 · main | "she takes to the water with Lyse, and begins swimming for the isle of Onokoro." | #419 |
| M420a | Tansui takes you to the Confederacy's captain, Rasho. | *Confederate Consternation* · 4.0 · main | "Come with me. I will take you to the captain─Rasho." | #420 |
| M420b | Rasho knows Gosetsu as a former leader of the rebellion. | *Confederate Consternation* · 4.0 · main | "Gosetsu. A former leader of the rebellion. Aye, I know of him." | #420 |
| M420c | To join the Confederacy is to renounce all other ties; it is beholden to and allied with none. | *Confederate Consternation* · 4.0 · main | "to join the Confederacy is to renounce all other ties. We are beholden to none─allied with none." | #420 |
| M420d | The Confederacy did not rush to Doma's defense when the Empire invaded twenty-five years ago. | *Confederate Consternation* · 4.0 · main | "When Doma was invaded by the Empire twenty-five years ago, we did not rush to her defense." | #420 |
| M420e | Rasho says hate is a luxury they cannot afford, and refuses further aid. | *Confederate Consternation* · 4.0 · main | "Hate is a luxury we cannot afford.<br>You are free to come and go as you please, but you will receive no further aid." | #420 |
| M420f | Alisaie plans to win trust by helping people with mundane problems. | *Confederate Consternation* · 4.0 · main | "seeking out people with mundane problems and offering them our expert assistance" | #420 |
| M420g | Alisaie thinks the settlement may be the Confederacy's headquarters. | *Confederate Consternation* · 4.0 · main | "It would seem we have found a Confederate settlement─mayhap even their headquarters, judging by the size of the place." | #420 |
| M421a | Afumi says the captain was once of Doma, and cast all aside when the imperials took his lands and family. | *The Solace of the Sea* · 4.0 · main | "The captain too was once of Doma, though he cast all aside twenty and five years ago, when the imperials took his lands and the lives of his parents and brothers." | #421 |
| M421b | Afumi sends medicine to a seasick Doman at Quickscape Pier. | *The Solace of the Sea* · 4.0 · main | "Then to Quickscape Pier and the hapless Doman bemoaning his wretched fate." | #421 |
| M421ba | Kajika says they would not normally hunt gyuki as game, but these are not normal times. | *The Arrows of Misfortune* · 4.0 · main | "Under normal circumstances, we would not bother hunting gyuki as game, but as you may have heard, these are not normal circumstances." | #421b |
| M421bb | Even arrows must be reused. | *The Arrows of Misfortune* · 4.0 · main | "Arrows too must be reused when possible. A prudent measure become necessary, alas." | #421b |
| M421bc | Kajika sends you to a gyuki carcass on the beach to the west. | *The Arrows of Misfortune* · 4.0 · main | "On the beach far to the west lies the carcass of a gyuki, feathered with arrows." | #421b |
| M421c | Afumi wonders whether you too will renounce your ties and join them. | *The Solace of the Sea* · 4.0 · main | "Perhaps you too will renounce your ties and join us someday?" | #421 |
| M421ca | Aranami sends you after a recruit he ordered to patrol Onokoro's northern edge. | *The Last Voyage* · 4.0 · main | "A new recruit I ordered to patrol the northern edge of Onokoro." | #421c |
| M421cb | The recruit mourns a comrade who died hunting deadly game in treacherous waters. | *The Last Voyage* · 4.0 · main | "She hunted rare and deadly game in treacherous waters, and lost her life for it." | #421c |
| M421cc | Aranami admits he judged the recruit too quickly. | *The Last Voyage* · 4.0 · main | "Too quick was I to judge him." | #421c |
| M424a | Kojin chasing a group of pirates asked whether they were Doman. | *Alisaie's Stones* · 4.0 · main | "Before the Kojin started chasing us, their leader asked us if we were Doman..." | #424 |
| M424b | A pirate calls the Kojin Yotsuyu's catchers. | *Alisaie's Stones* · 4.0 · main | "They're Yotsuyu's catchers, I know it! First Isari, and now this!" | #424 |
| M424c | Alisaie sees Kojin mercenaries rounding up Doman Confederates. | *Alisaie's Stones* · 4.0 · main | "Kojin mercenaries rounding up Doman Confederates? Surely Yotsuyu has crossed the line now..." | #424 |
| M424d | Rasho will take no revenge until the enemy is weaker; he waits for a sign. | *Alisaie's Stones* · 4.0 · main | "Until the day our enemy is grown weaker, and we stronger, there can be no retribution. We will wait for a sign." | #424 |
| M424e | Alisaie vows to deprive the Garleans of a key ally, the Kojin. | *Alisaie's Stones* · 4.0 · main | "Accordingly, we shall demonstrate our seriousness by depriving the Garleans of a key ally: the Kojin." | #424 |
| M424f | The wager: if you succeed, the Confederacy helps liberate Isari. | *Alisaie's Stones* · 4.0 · main | "If we succeed, you help us liberate Isari. If we fail, you lose nothing." | #424 |
| M424g | Rasho will weigh you by your deeds. | *Alisaie's Stones* · 4.0 · main | "Words are air. We will weigh you by your deeds." | #424 |
| M425a | The Kojin mercenaries who serve the Empire are of the Red. | *Under the Sea* · 4.0 · main | "The mercenaries who serve the Empire belong to the Red." | #425 |
| M425b | The Blue Kojin are traders with no pact with the Empire, who may aid you. | *Under the Sea* · 4.0 · main | "Those who belong to the Blue are the traders. They have made no pact with the imperials, and so may be willing to aid your cause." | #425 |
| M425c | Soroban welcomes you to Tamamizu. | *Under the Sea* · 4.0 · main | "Just so. I bid you welcome to Tamamizu!" | #425 |
| M425d | Lyse says Yda and her father knew how to inspire people. | *Under the Sea* · 4.0 · main | "Yda and my father had a way with words. They knew how to inspire people─how to bring them together." | #425 |
| M425e | Lyse fears she lacks that gift. | *Under the Sea* · 4.0 · main | "I always hoped I might have something of that in me too, but I'm starting to think I don't." | #425 |
| M425f | Tamamizu lies inside a giant bubble. | *Under the Sea* · 4.0 · main | "We, uh...appear to be standing inside a giant bubble." | #425 |
| M426a | Soroban introduces Bunchin, leader of the Blue Kojin. | *Of Kojin and Kami* · 4.0 · main | "Very well. I shall introduce you to our leader, Bunchin." | #426 |
| M426b | The Blue uphold the old ways, trading and valuing peace; the Red live by the sword. | *Of Kojin and Kami* · 4.0 · main | "While our Red brothers live by the sword, we Blue Kojin uphold the old ways, engaging in trade and valuing peace." | #426 |
| M426c | The Red Kojin resolved to join the Empire. | *Of Kojin and Kami* · 4.0 · main | "Wiser to join with them. So resolved the Red Kojin." | #426 |
| M426d | Hingashi held the Yasakani-no-Magatama until it presented the jewel to the Garleans. | *Of Kojin and Kami* · 4.0 · main | "This jewel, this most sacred of Kojin relics, was in the possession of Hingashi─until they decided to present it to the Garleans in a gesture of conciliation." | #426 |
| M426e | In the chaos when the Red seized the ship, the Magatama was lost to the sea. | *Of Kojin and Kami* · 4.0 · main | "in the ensuing chaos the Magatama was lost to the sea." | #426 |
| M426f | The Blue want to recover the jewel before the Red. | *Of Kojin and Kami* · 4.0 · main | "We wish to recover the sacred jewel before our brothers." | #426 |
| M426g | The Red pose as mercenaries and never speak of their faith before their masters. | *Of Kojin and Kami* · 4.0 · main | "So the Red masquerade as mercenaries who seek money and power and never speak of their faith in the presence of their masters." | #426 |
| M426h | The people of Sui-no-Sato can guide you better than the Kojin. | *Of Kojin and Kami* · 4.0 · main | "The shell-less of Sui-no-Sato can provide you with better guidance than we." | #426 |
| M427a | Soroban's ritual lets you travel the depths to search for the Yasakani-no-Magatama. | *In Soroban We Trust* · 4.0 · main | "to let you traverse the depths as easily as any [[Kojin]], that you might begin your search for the [[Yasakani-no-Magatama]]." | #427 |
| M427b | On rays he summons, you set out for Sui-no-Sato. | *In Soroban We Trust* · 4.0 · main | "with the aid of the striped rays, you embark on your journey to [[Sui-no-Sato]]." | #427 |
| M427c | Alisaie: first win over the Confederacy, rescue Gosetsu and liberate your homelands. | *In Soroban We Trust* · 4.0 · main | "after we've won over the Confederacy, rescued Gosetsu, and liberated your respective homelands." | #427 |
| M427d | Sui-no-Sato is the underwater village of the reclusive Raen. | *In Soroban We Trust* · 4.0 · main | "the underwater village of the [[Raen]]. But will these reclusive people be willing to aid your cause?" | #427 |
| M428a | Shiosai is high priest of Shisui of the Violet Tides. | *Forever and Ever Apart* · 4.0 · main | "I am high priest of Shisui of the Violet Tides. Shiosai." | #428 |
| M428b | The Raen live under the Ruby Princess, sovereign of Shisui. | *Forever and Ever Apart* · 4.0 · main | "Under the benevolent leadership of the Ruby Princess, sovereign of Shisui, we Raen enjoy humble yet fulfilling lives." | #428 |
| M428c | When Doma fell twenty-five years ago, the Ruby Princess decreed the Raen would take no part. | *Forever and Ever Apart* · 4.0 · main | "So it was when Doma fell twenty-five years past. The Ruby Princess, in her wisdom, decreed that we would take no part." | #428 |
| M428d | Shiosai will not betray his people by letting outsiders stay. | *Forever and Ever Apart* · 4.0 · main | "For me to defy their will and permit outsiders to remain would be a gross betrayal of my responsibilities." | #428 |
| M428e | Yotsuyu presses Gosetsu about Doma's missing heir. | *Forever and Ever Apart* · 4.0 · main | "To wit, I would ask you again to help me resolve this vexing matter of Doma's missing heir." | #428 |
| M428f | The Garleans want the heir found, living or dead; Yotsuyu knows Gosetsu helped Lord Hien flee. | *Forever and Ever Apart* · 4.0 · main | "Living or dead, it makes no difference really … I know you helped him flee. So, tell me: what became of Lord Hien?" | #428 |
| M428g | Grynewaht beats the bound Gosetsu. | *Forever and Ever Apart* · 4.0 · main | "''Grynewaht beats up a bound Gosetsu.''" | #428 |
| M428h | Yotsuyu mocks the late Lord Kaien as the leader of a fallen nation. | *Forever and Ever Apart* · 4.0 · main | "Poor, poor Lord Kaien─kami rest his soul! Noble leader of a fallen nation..." | #428 |
| M428i | Gosetsu claims the details escape him. | *Forever and Ever Apart* · 4.0 · main | "To my dismay, the details escape me at present." | #428 |
| M429a | Ihanami and Yunagi's daughter fought in the rebellion and has not been heard from since. | *In Darkness the Magatama Dreams* · 4.0 · main | "Our daughter fought in the rebellion. We have heard naught from her since." | #429 |
| M429b | The Ruby Princess of the day forbade Yugiri's return. | *In Darkness the Magatama Dreams* · 4.0 · main | "When the then Ruby Princess learned of this, she forbade Yugiri's return, lest she bring misfortune to us all." | #429 |
| M429c | As a child Yugiri went to Doma and met a proud son of Yanxia. | *In Darkness the Magatama Dreams* · 4.0 · main | "To Doma they went, where they met a young boy─a proud son of Yanxia." | #429 |
| M429d | Ihanami sends you to search a sunken ship to the east. | *In Darkness the Magatama Dreams* · 4.0 · main | "There is a sunken ship to the east. Though the currents may have spirited away her cargo, you should begin your search there." | #429 |
| M429e | Alisaie confirms the jewel is the Yasakani-no-Magatama. | *In Darkness the Magatama Dreams* · 4.0 · main | "This...this is it! The Yasakani-no-Magatama, exactly as Soroban described!" | #429 |
| M429f | Yunagi asks you to tell Yugiri her family thinks of her every day. | *In Darkness the Magatama Dreams* · 4.0 · main | "pray tell her not a day goes by that her parents and her brothers do not think of her." | #429 |
| M429g | Lyse says you are looking for Yugiri too. | *In Darkness the Magatama Dreams* · 4.0 · main | "We're looking for her too, actually, but I'm sure she's all right." | #429 |
| M430a | The Magatama is one of three Kojin treasures; the other two lie in the vault. | *The Whims of the Divine* · 4.0 · main | "The Yasakani-no-Magatama is one of three most coveted treasures of the Kojin. The other two lie within the vault." | #430 |
| M430b | Bunchin's ceremonial knife opens the Kojin vault on the Isle of Zekki. | *The Whims of the Divine* · 4.0 · main | "Before this blade, that which is sealed shall open. The treasure vault of the Kojin, on the Isle of Zekki." | #430 |
| M430c | An attack on the vault would summon Kojin from all around to defend it. | *The Whims of the Divine* · 4.0 · main | "Were the vault to be attacked, their response would by necessity be immediate. Kojin would be summoned from malms around to defend their treasures." | #430 |
| M430d | Without the Kojin, the imperials lack the troops to hold Isari. | *The Whims of the Divine* · 4.0 · main | "Without the Kojin, the imperials don't have nearly enough troops in Isari to hold off the pirates." | #430 |
| M430e | Soroban will tell the Confederacy to be ready to act. | *The Whims of the Divine* · 4.0 · main | "I shall inform the Confederacy of your plans, and bid them make ready to act when the time is right." | #430 |
| M430f | Soroban says the Magatama chose you to bear it. | *The Whims of the Divine* · 4.0 · main | "The Magatama has chosen you to bear it to its brothers." | #430 |
| M431a | Alisaie's plan: two draw the guards while the third goes for the vault. | *Breaking and Delivering* · 4.0 · main | "How about two of us get the guards' attention while the third heads straight for the vault?" | #431 |
| M431b | The three treasures glow and rise into the air. | *Breaking and Delivering* · 4.0 · main | "''The three treasures begin glowing and become airborne.''" | #431 |
| M431c | A Red commander hails a great kami of the sacred treasures. | *Breaking and Delivering* · 4.0 · main | "Hark! A great kami stirs... Yes, yes, a great kami of the sacred treasures!" | #431 |
| M431d | The Red have guarded the kami's blade and glass for generations. | *Breaking and Delivering* · 4.0 · main | "We who have guarded your blade and glass for generations─your faithful Kojin!" | #431 |
| M431e | The kami names itself Susano. | *Breaking and Delivering* · 4.0 · main | "We are Susano, and in revelry do we rejoice!" | #431 |
| M431f | Alisaie resolves to defeat the primal. | *Breaking and Delivering* · 4.0 · main | "We have to defeat that primal!" | #431 |
| M431g | Susano marvels that you delivered the jewel to your enemy's vault. | *Breaking and Delivering* · 4.0 · main | "Thou didst freely deliver our jewel unto thine enemy's own treasure vault?" | #431 |
| M432a | Susano is a primal by another name, summoned when the treasures reacted to the Magatama. | *The Lord of the Revel* · 4.0 · main | "summoning forth from the aether the great kami [[Susano]]—a [[primal]] by another name." | #432 |
| M432b | The great kami is no more. | *The Lord of the Revel* · 4.0 · main | "The great kami is no more" | #432 |
| M432c | Alisaie stays to give you and Lyse an opening to escape. | *The Lord of the Revel* · 4.0 · main | "Alisaie steps forward, resolved to provide you and Lyse with an opening in which to escape." | #432 |
| M432d | Rasho means to keep the Confederacy's bargain with the Eorzeans. | *The Lord of the Revel* · 4.0 · main | "We made a bargain with the Eorzeans, and I mean to uphold our end of it." | #432 |
| M432e | Yotsuyu swears to find Lord Hien. | *The Lord of the Revel* · 4.0 · main | "Mark me, samurai─I will find Lord Hien, be it with your help or with your heads." | #432 |
| M432f | Grynewaht says Gosetsu is iron. | *The Lord of the Revel* · 4.0 · main | "This one is iron. My hands will break before he speaks." | #432 |
| M432g | Lyse meant to go back for Alisaie, who turns up right behind her. | *The Lord of the Revel* · 4.0 · main | "No sooner have you reached the beach south of Isari than Lyse declares her intention to return to the Isle of Zekki and help Alisaie." | #432 |
| M432h | The Red sounded the alarm and rushed to defend the Isle of Zekki. | *The Lord of the Revel* · 4.0 · main | "The Red have sounded the alarum. The Isle of Zekki under attack; fierce fighting in the stronghold." | #432 |
| M432i | Grynewaht addresses Yotsuyu as his lady. | *The Lord of the Revel* · 4.0 · main | "My lady, I... I'm going outside!" | #432 |
| M432j | A Red marine cries that Susano is slain. | *The Lord of the Revel* · 4.0 · main | "S-Susano is slain! Slain by the shell-less one!" | #432 |
| M432k | Rasho means to remind the Garleans that the sea belongs to the Confederacy. | *The Lord of the Revel* · 4.0 · main | "I mean to remind the Garleans that this is our sea─that all here belongs to the Confederacy!" | #432 |
| M432l | Alisaie is safe behind Lyse. | *The Lord of the Revel* · 4.0 · main | "as is the shock of hearing Alisaie's voice coming from right behind her." | #432 |
| M433a | The imperials fled Isari once their Kojin reinforcements failed to come. | *Tide Goes in, Imperials Go Out* · 4.0 · main | "Fled. They stood their ground for a time─until it became clear that their Kojin reinforcements would not be coming." | #433 |
| M433b | The Empire believes the Confederacy attacked Isari alone, in retaliation for the viceroy's actions. | *Tide Goes in, Imperials Go Out* · 4.0 · main | "So far as the Empire is concerned, the Confederacy attacked Isari in retaliation for the viceroy's actions─and without the aid of insurrectionists, I might add." | #433 |
| M433c | The Confederacy expects the Empire's undivided attention. | *Tide Goes in, Imperials Go Out* · 4.0 · main | "we are prepared to be the subject of their undivided attention for the foreseeable future." | #433 |
| M433d | Gosetsu and the captives are alive. | *Tide Goes in, Imperials Go Out* · 4.0 · main | "Gosetsu! You're alive! You're all alive..." | #433 |
| M433e | Rasho regrets letting Yotsuyu slip away; her bodyguard is dangerous in battle. | *Tide Goes in, Imperials Go Out* · 4.0 · main | "my deeper regret is having let Yotsuyu slip through my fingers. That bodyguard of hers may be a half-wit, but he's a mean bastard on the battlefield." | #433 |
| M433f | Gosetsu trusts his allies yet live, and will plan the next step once he finds them. | *Tide Goes in, Imperials Go Out* · 4.0 · main | "I have faith that our allies yet live. … Once we have found them, we will plan our next step." | #433 |
| M433g | Rasho calls Yanxia the crucible of the rebellion, where Yotsuyu did worse. | *Tide Goes in, Imperials Go Out* · 4.0 · main | "Yanxia was the crucible of the rebellion. What happened here pales in comparison to what Yotsuyu did there." | #433 |
| M433h | The Confederates rescued the fishermen and their kidnapped comrades. | *Tide Goes in, Imperials Go Out* · 4.0 · main | "Thanks to you, we managed to rescue the fishermen and my kidnapped comrades" | #433 |
| M434a | Confederate forces drove Yotsuyu from Isari, and the captives survived. | *A Silence in Three Parts* · 4.0 · main | "[[Yotsuyu]] and her imperials have been driven from [[Isari]], and their former captives managed to survive their ordeal." | #434 |
| M434b | Zenos left Doma after crushing the rebellion and named Yotsuyu to serve in his stead. | *A Silence in Three Parts* · 4.0 · main | "[[Zenos]] had no desire to remain in Doma after he crushed the rebellion, and so he named Yotsuyu to serve in his stead." | #434 |
| M434c | The acting viceroy waged an indiscriminate campaign of terror on the villages. | *A Silence in Three Parts* · 4.0 · main | "The acting viceroy waged an indiscriminate campaign of terror, marching into villages without warning" | #434 |
| M434d | The purges ceased, but their memory keeps the people from resisting. | *A Silence in Three Parts* · 4.0 · main | "After a time, these purges ceased, but the memory remains, and with it the promise of punishment should the people resist." | #434 |
| M434e | Gosetsu is still hurt from Grynewaht's interrogation. | *A Silence in Three Parts* · 4.0 · main | "Despite his assurances, [[Gosetsu]] is clearly still troubled by the injuries he sustained from [[Grynewaht]]'s brutal interrogation." | #434 |
| M434f | Grynewaht cuts down the Red Kojin there and destroys most of their treasure. | *A Silence in Three Parts* · 4.0 · main | "''Grynewaht defeats all of the Red Kojin and destroys most of the treasure.''" | #434 |
| M434g | Yotsuyu takes a katana, one of the Red's treasures. | *A Silence in Three Parts* · 4.0 · main | "And what do we have here? One of the vaunted treasures of the Red?" | #434 |
| M434h | Grynewaht wants Zenos to reinstate him so he can return to Eorzea. | *A Silence in Three Parts* · 4.0 · main | "How long before Lord Zenos reinstates me, d'you reckon? I've got unfinished business back in Eorzea..." | #434 |
| M434i | Gosetsu leads you to Namai, a farming village to the west. | *A Silence in Three Parts* · 4.0 · main | "Gosetsu leads you to [[Namai]], a humble farming village a little ways to the west." | #434 |
| M434j | Yotsuyu rebukes the Red Kojin for abandoning the posts she contracted them for. | *A Silence in Three Parts* · 4.0 · main | "So this is why you abandoned your posts─and without a word of explanation at that. … What is the point of contracting your services if you are not here when they are required?" | #434 |
| M435a | Isse names Gosetsu as Lord Kaien's retainer, a leader of the rebellion wanted by the Empire. | *Life after Doma* · 4.0 · main | "Lord Kaien's retainer and leader of the rebellion—Gosetsu! Notorious fugitive, wanted dead or alive by the Empire!" | #435 |
| M435b | The rebels died, and the imperials came looking for more. | *Life after Doma* · 4.0 · main | "They fought, and they died. Every last one. And then the imperials came looking for more. Because of them." | #435 |
| M435c | Gosetsu cannot leave things as they are. | *Life after Doma* · 4.0 · main | "surely he must know that I cannot leave things as they are." | #435 |
| M435d | The House of the Fierce is the Doman Liberation Front's headquarters. | *Life after Doma* · 4.0 · main | "We must continue on towards our final destination—the House of the Fierce. Proud headquarters of the Doman Liberation Front." | #435 |
| M435e | Isse wants Namai left out of the plotting. | *Life after Doma* · 4.0 · main | "Whatever it is you're plotting, leave us out of it!" | #435 |
| M435f | A secret entrance north of Namai leads through a small spring. | *Life after Doma* · 4.0 · main | "There is a secret entrance to the north of Namai, by way of a small spring." | #435 |
| M436a | Lord Hien is the son of Lord Kaien, who perished during the rebellion. | *A Glimpse of Madness* · 4.0 · main | "he speaks of Lord Hien, son of Lord Kaien, who perished during the rebellion." | #436 |
| M436b | Hien sent his people to safety, then fought on as heir until a grievous wound left him vanished. | *A Glimpse of Madness* · 4.0 · main | "When it became clear that all hope of victory had gone, Lord Hien bade us deliver as many of our people as we could to safety beyond our borders. But as his father's heir, he was determined to fight on, and so he did─until he suffered a grievous wound in battle and vanished without a trace..." | #436 |
| M436c | Yugiri has found Lord Hien on the Azim Steppe. | *A Glimpse of Madness* · 4.0 · main | "For I have found Lord Hien─seen and spoken with him. On the Azim Steppe." | #436 |
| M436d | Hien will offer Doma his blade if it still thirsts for freedom, or else his head to its conquerors. | *A Glimpse of Madness* · 4.0 · main | "If Doma yet thirsts for the blood of patriots and tyrants, I shall offer unto her my blade. But if she has had her fill of bitter draughts, I shall offer unto her conquerors my head." | #436 |
| M436e | Hien asks Yugiri to learn the truth of Doma's heart. | *A Glimpse of Madness* · 4.0 · main | "Learn the truth of her heart and return to me" | #436 |
| M436f | Zenos ended the Front's hopes with the secrets Yotsuyu sold. | *A Glimpse of Madness* · 4.0 · main | "But Zenos soon put paid to that, with the aid of Yotsuyu and the secrets she sold." | #436 |
| M436g | Zenos desired hate, not obedience. | *A Glimpse of Madness* · 4.0 · main | "He did not desire obedience. He desired hate...and men consumed by it." | #436 |
| M436h | Zenos has wielded Far Eastern blades ever since. | *A Glimpse of Madness* · 4.0 · main | "Since that day, he has ever wielded Far Eastern blades." | #436 |
| M436i | The Front is few and weak, its enemies legion. | *A Glimpse of Madness* · 4.0 · main | "We are few, and our enemies legion. We are weak, and they strong." | #436 |
| M436j | The Front disabled its aetheryte. | *A Glimpse of Madness* · 4.0 · main | "As you can see, they went to great lengths to disable the aetheryte." | #436 |
| M436k | Zenos made the Front's soldiers watch their comrades die. | *A Glimpse of Madness* · 4.0 · main | "One...after...another. He made us watch. Do you understand? He made us watch." | #436 |
| M437a | Yugiri proposes going among the people to witness their hardship. | *The Stubborn Remainder* · 4.0 · main | "Let us go among the people and with our own eyes bear witness to their hardship." | #437 |
| M437b | You pose as traveling merchants, with you as leader. | *The Stubborn Remainder* · 4.0 · main | "We shall pose as traveling merchants─[Forename] as our leader," | #437 |
| M437c | The samurai lords of Monzen all died in battle or in the purges. | *The Stubborn Remainder* · 4.0 · main | "Once, I would have directed you to the samurai lords of Monzen─had they not all died in battle or in the purges." | #437 |
| M437d | The Doman Enclave lies beyond the Dairyu Moon Gates. | *The Stubborn Remainder* · 4.0 · main | "There is the Doman Enclave, of course, but that lies beyond the Dairyu Moon Gates." | #437 |
| M437e | The villagers have been pushed beyond the brink. | *The Stubborn Remainder* · 4.0 · main | "Lyse and I encountered much of the same. They have been pushed beyond the brink." | #437 |
| M437f | Unless the villagers change their minds, Yugiri fears she cannot bring Lord Hien back. | *The Stubborn Remainder* · 4.0 · main | "Unless we can convince them otherwise, I fear I cannot convince Lord Hien to return" | #437 |
| M437g | The villagers question the wisdom of opposing imperial rule at all. | *The Stubborn Remainder* · 4.0 · main | "not so much that they would come to question the wisdom of opposing imperial rule in the first place" | #437 |
| M438a | Isse's parents believed in the rebellion and left to fight for Lord Kaien. | *The Ones We Leave Behind* · 4.0 · main | "My mother and father believed in the rebellion. They left to fight for Lord Kaien." | #438 |
| M438b | Imperial soldiers beat the rebels' families in the village square. | *The Ones We Leave Behind* · 4.0 · main | "They beat us in the village square─with their hands, with sticks, with anything." | #438 |
| M438c | A soldier threatened what he would do to Isse's sister. | *The Ones We Leave Behind* · 4.0 · main | "One leaned close and whispered in my ear what he'd do to my sister if they had to come back." | #438 |
| M438d | The imperials are never far, and the wrong word brings them back. | *The Ones We Leave Behind* · 4.0 · main | "They're gone, but they're never far. All it takes is the wrong word in the wrong ear, and they'll return." | #438 |
| M438e | Yugiri wonders whether Lord Hien foresaw how broken his people would be. | *The Ones We Leave Behind* · 4.0 · main | "Could Lord Hien have foreseen this? That our people's spirits would be broken so?" | #438 |
| M438f | Isse will not report you to the imperials. | *The Ones We Leave Behind* · 4.0 · main | "I'm not going to report you to the imperials, or anything stupid like that." | #438 |
| M439a | Imperial soldiers select villagers to serve the Garleans, ending with Isse. | *A New Ruby Tithe* · 4.0 · main | "the pilus prior begins to select villagers one by one, ending with Isse, the young man who implored you to leave Namai and never return." | #439 |
| M439b | The pilus calls the battle at Isari an act that will not go unanswered. | *A New Ruby Tithe* · 4.0 · main | "He tells the gathered people of the battle between Confederate and imperial forces in Isari, declaring it a brazen, unconscionable act that will not go unanswered." | #439 |
| M439c | Yugiri sees an attempt to intimidate those who supported the Confederacy. | *A New Ruby Tithe* · 4.0 · main | "This is but a thinly veiled attempt to intimidate those who supported the Confederacy's actions" | #439 |
| M439d | Yugiri gives you a blowgun and sleeping darts. | *A New Ruby Tithe* · 4.0 · main | "she furnishes you with a blowgun and darts that you can use to put imperial soldiers to sleep." | #439 |
| M439e | The villagers are held in the castrum Fluminis. | *A New Ruby Tithe* · 4.0 · main | "The villagers are being held in the castrum to the south─Fluminis." | #439 |
| M439f | The captives realize they have been sentenced to servitude. | *A New Ruby Tithe* · 4.0 · main | "it finally dawns on the gathered Domans that they have been effectively sentenced to a life of servitude." | #439 |
| M439g | Yugiri sends Lyse back to the House of the Fierce. | *A New Ruby Tithe* · 4.0 · main | "After Yugiri convinces Lyse to return to the House of the Fierce to inform the others of this development" | #439 |
| M440a | Yugiri means to infiltrate the castrum without raising the alarm. | *The Will to Live* · 4.0 · main | "To wit, we must infiltrate the castrum without raising the alarum." | #440 |
| M440b | You steal a uniform from a sleeping soldier. | *The Will to Live* · 4.0 · main | "Steal a suitable uniform from a sleeping soldier." | #440 |
| M440c | Isse says the villagers are lost and afraid and can change nothing. | *The Will to Live* · 4.0 · main | "We're lost and afraid! We hate this─all of this─but we can't do a damn thing to change it!" | #440 |
| M440d | An elder says the survivors are forced to bend the knee. | *The Will to Live* · 4.0 · main | "We who survived are forced to bend the knee─to sell our souls and surrender our dignity." | #440 |
| M440e | Yugiri swears by all the kami to free them. | *The Will to Live* · 4.0 · main | "I swear by all the kami in creation that I will free you from this torment." | #440 |
| M440f | Isse asks whether Yugiri is Lord Hien's retainer. | *The Will to Live* · 4.0 · main | "You are Lord Hien's retainer?" | #440 |
| M440g | Yugiri has dealt with the imperials and tells the villagers to flee. | *The Will to Live* · 4.0 · main | "We have dealt with the imperials for now, but you must flee this place, quickly!" | #440 |
| M441a | Yugiri was born beneath the waves of the Ruby Sea. | *Daughter of the Deep* · 4.0 · main | "in truth I am not of Doma. I was born beneath the waves of the Ruby Sea..." | #441 |
| M441b | Yugiri met Lord Kaien, his son and their loyal samurai Gosetsu. | *Daughter of the Deep* · 4.0 · main | "It was then that I met Lord Kaien and his son, and their loyal samurai, Gosetsu." | #441 |
| M441c | Yotsuyu governs Doma, but Zenos is its anointed viceroy and has come in person. | *Daughter of the Deep* · 4.0 · main | "Though Yotsuyu governs Doma, Zenos remains the province's anointed viceroy─and it would seem he has come to assess the current situation in person." | #441 |
| M441d | Zenos defeated the Alliance, the Resistance and the Scions. | *Daughter of the Deep* · 4.0 · main | "He faced the Alliance, the Resistance and the Scions, and he defeated us all." | #441 |
| M441e | The crown prince has come to Doma. | *Daughter of the Deep* · 4.0 · main | "The crown prince is come to Doma." | #441 |
| M441f | Gosetsu warns Zenos's death would not free Doma, more likely doom it. | *Daughter of the Deep* · 4.0 · main | "Even should you succeed, Zenos's death will not win Doma her freedom─more likely her doom." | #441 |
| M441g | Yugiri will not squander the chance. | *Daughter of the Deep* · 4.0 · main | "I will not squander this opportunity!" | #441 |
| M441h | An imperial airship is bound for Doma Castle. | *Daughter of the Deep* · 4.0 · main | "It is bound for Doma Castle. Quickly, we must rejoin the others!" | #441 |
| M441i | Yugiri learned the shinobi's secrets and was sent to assist the Doman Liberation Front. | *Daughter of the Deep* · 4.0 · main | "After a time, my skills were recognized, and I was dispatched to assist the Doman Liberation Front." | #441 |
| M441j | The Scions encountered Zenos at Rhalgr's Reach. | *Daughter of the Deep* · 4.0 · main | "You may recall that we encountered Zenos at Rhalgr's Reach." | #441 |
| M442a | Gosetsu will not pursue Yugiri. | *Path of No Return* · 4.0 · main | "I will not pursue her. It is for her to think on our master's words" | #442 |
| M442b | Zenos will inspect the Moon Gates this evening. | *Path of No Return* · 4.0 · main | "He wishes to inspect the Moon Gates this very eve." | #442 |
| M442c | Zenos arrived without warning, giving Yotsuyu no time to prepare. | *Path of No Return* · 4.0 · main | "He arrived without warning and gave Yotsuyu no time to prepare." | #442 |
| M442e | You are to meet Yugiri at Yuzuka Manor. | *Path of No Return* · 4.0 · main | "Rendezvous with Yugiri at [[Yuzuka Manor]]." | #442 |
| M442f | Isse overheard two rebels plotting to kill the crown prince. | *Path of No Return* · 4.0 · main | "No one knows you overheard two rebels plotting...to kill the crown prince..." | #442 |
| M442g | Yugiri thanks you for joining her. | *Path of No Return* · 4.0 · main | "Thank you. Thank you. I...I cannot well express what this means to me." | #442 |
| M443a | Yugiri resolved to assassinate Zenos over her comrades' protests, and only you agreed to help. | *The Time between the Seconds* · 4.0 · main | "Yugiri has resolved, over the protestations of your comrades, to assassinate him. You alone have agreed to support her in this endeavor" | #443 |
| M443b | Zenos's new katana unleashes crimson energy that fells Yugiri and the soldiers. | *The Time between the Seconds* · 4.0 · main | "until your foe elects to bring a new katana to bear against you. At once, his body begins to crackle with a crimson energy, which bursts forth and fells both Yugiri and the viceroy's few remaining soldiers." | #443 |
| M443c | Zenos recognizes you as the champion of the savages in Ala Mhigo. | *The Time between the Seconds* · 4.0 · main | "recognizes you as the champion of the savages in Ala Mhigo" | #443 |
| M443d | He urges you to live, grow stronger and face him again. | *The Time between the Seconds* · 4.0 · main | "he implores you to live on, to endure, to grow stronger, and to face him once more" | #443 |
| M443e | Armed villagers, Gosetsu and Alisaie stand ready to save Yugiri. | *The Time between the Seconds* · 4.0 · main | "pauses when confronted by a group of armed villagers, as well as Gosetsu and Alisaie, who are determined to save the ninja's life." | #443 |
| M443f | Zenos withdraws without a word. | *The Time between the Seconds* · 4.0 · main | "Nevertheless, Zenos withdraws without a word, leaving you and your comrades free to withdraw to safety." | #443 |
| M443g | Alphinaud convinced the others to rush to your defense. | *The Time between the Seconds* · 4.0 · main | "for it was he who convinced the others to rush to your defense." | #443 |
| M443h | Zenos says the Empire sowed hatred and fear to train the Domans as beasts. | *The Time between the Seconds* · 4.0 · main | "We labored long to sow the seeds of hatred, of fear─to train them as beasts." | #443 |

| M444c | Tataru and Hancock report that the Empire withdrew most of its forces from Doma this past year. | *All the Little Angels* · 4.0 · main | "according to Tataru and Hancock's intelligence, the Empire has withdrawn most of its forces from Doma in the past year" | #444 |
| M444d | Alphinaud believes an uprising led by the Liberation Front could threaten Doma Castle. | *All the Little Angels* · 4.0 · main | "a popular uprising spearheaded by the full might of the Doman Liberation Front may feasibly be sufficient to threaten the seat of provincial government, namely Doma Castle." | #444 |

| M444f | Gosetsu says the people's open defiance of Zenos is the proof Hien wanted. | *All the Little Angels* · 4.0 · main | "He desired proof of his subjects' conviction, and they have provided it─through no less a deed than openly opposing Zenos himself!" | #444 |
| M444g | Zenos chose Yotsuyu for her petty hatreds, to bleed her kinsmen of hope. | *All the Little Angels* · 4.0 · main | "Because of your petty hatreds.<br>They render you the perfect instrument to bleed your kinsmen of hope" | #444 |

| M444l | Isse apologizes for his earlier disdain. | *All the Little Angels* · 4.0 · main | "Isse offers an apology for his earlier disdain towards the adventurer." | #444 |
