| M445a | Convinced this is Lord Hien's sign, you set out for the Azim Steppe to bring him home to lead a new rebellion. | *Here There Be Xaela* · 4.0 · main | "Convinced that this is the sign that Lord [[Hien]] has been waiting for, you and your comrades embark on a journey to the [[Azim Steppe]] to seek out the young heir and convince him to return to lead his people in a new rebellion against the [[Garlean Empire|Empire]]." | #445 |
| M445b | The failed attempt on Zenos spurred Doma's people to openly oppose the imperials. | *Here There Be Xaela* · 4.0 · main | "it spurred the people of [[Doma]] to rise up and openly oppose the imperials in battle." | #445 |
| M445c | Domans rarely venture so far north. | *Here There Be Xaela* · 4.0 · main | "Despite the close proximity, Domans rarely venture this far north." | #445 |
| M445d | The search for Lord Hien begins at Reunion. | *Here There Be Xaela* · 4.0 · main | "Welcome to Reunion, my friends. Here is where we will begin our search for Lord Hien." | #445 |
| M445e | The route runs north from Isari through a mountain tunnel. | *Here There Be Xaela* · 4.0 · main | "From [[Isari]] you must continue north, towards a tunnel leading through the mountains." | #445 |
| M445f | Reunion is a marketplace filled with Xaela from across the Steppe. | *Here There Be Xaela* · 4.0 · main | "You come to the gates of what appears to be a bustling marketplace, filled with [[Xaela]] from all across the Steppe." | #445 |
| M446a | More than fifty Xaela tribes roam the Steppe, ranked through ritual combat. | *The Search for Lord Hien* · 4.0 · main | "More than fifty roam these lands, and though they do not constitute a nation, there is a hierarchy of sorts, determined through ritual combat." | #446 |
| M446b | The Steppe's thin soil and harsh weather were not made for men. | *The Search for Lord Hien* · 4.0 · main | "These lands were not made for men. The soil is thin, and the weather unforgiving." | #446 |
| M446c | The Doman comes and goes with a Mol girl. | *The Search for Lord Hien* · 4.0 · main | "I know of whom you speak. He comes and goes with a Mol girl." | #446 |
| M446d | Cirina is of the Mol. | *The Search for Lord Hien* · 4.0 · main | "Yes? I am Mol. My name is Cirina. May I help you?" | #446 |
| M446e | Cirina realizes you are looking for Hien. | *The Search for Lord Hien* · 4.0 · main | "Oh... Oh, I see. You are looking for Hien." | #446 |
| M446f | Cirina cannot leave Reunion without the kinsai. | *The Search for Lord Hien* · 4.0 · main | "I cannot leave Reunion without that kinsai..." | #446 |
| M447a | Cirina says the Mol are lambs among wolves, long content to live apart. | *A Season for War* · 4.0 · main | "The Mol are lambs among wolves. Long were we content to remain apart and live quietly, but..." | #447 |
| M447b | The Oronir and Dotharl are the two strongest tribes. | *A Season for War* · 4.0 · main | "Those boys are of the Oronir and Dotharl. The two strongest tribes." | #447 |
| M447c | The tribe that triumphs in the Naadam rules until the next Tsagaan Sar. | *A Season for War* · 4.0 · main | "The tribe which triumphs in the Naadam rules until the next Tsagaan Sar." | #447 |
| M447d | Fighting in Reunion means banishment. | *A Season for War* · 4.0 · main | "To fight in Reunion is forbidden, and to break the peace is to be banished forever." | #447 |
| M447e | Cirina knows where Hien may be. | *A Season for War* · 4.0 · main | "You seek Hien, yes? I know where he may be." | #447 |
| M448a | Guided by her grandmother's vision, Cirina found Hien near death in the southern mountains. | *An Impossible Dream* · 4.0 · main | "Long ago, guided by a vision received by her grandmother, she ventured into the southern mountains and there found Hien, near death." | #448 |
| M448b | The Mol nursed Hien back to health, and he has stayed to repay them. | *An Impossible Dream* · 4.0 · main | "Cirina brought the Doman lord to her village, where they nursed him back to health, and there he has remained ever since, until such time as he can repay his debt to them." | #448 |
| M448c | You meet the young heir to Doma's throne, who greets you as a friend. | *An Impossible Dream* · 4.0 · main | "you come face–to–face with the young heir to Doma's throne, who greets you as a friend." | #448 |
| M448d | Hien will not go home until he wins the Naadam and earns the right to lead the Xaela to Doma. | *An Impossible Dream* · 4.0 · main | "he states that he cannot go home─not until he has won the ceremonial battle known as the Naadam and thereby earned the right to bid the Xaela follow him to Doma." | #448 |
| M448e | The Liberation Front's forces are depleted. | *An Impossible Dream* · 4.0 · main | "given the Liberation Front's depleted forces." | #448 |
| M448f | Yugiri presents the Scions as friends who sheltered the Domans who fled to Eorzea. | *An Impossible Dream* · 4.0 · main | "The Scions of the Seventh Dawn, my lord─good and true friends who opened their hearts and homes to us when we fled to Eorzea." | #448 |
| M448g | Hien's father ruled in name only for twenty-five years. | *An Impossible Dream* · 4.0 · main | "For twenty-five years did my father rule in name only." | #448 |
| M448h | Hien sends Yugiri back to Yanxia to take charge of their forces. | *An Impossible Dream* · 4.0 · main | "Yugiri, I bid you return to Yanxia and take charge of our forces in preparation for my homecoming." | #448 |
| M448i | Hien's impossible dream is a revolution against the most powerful empire in the world. | *An Impossible Dream* · 4.0 · main | "For ours is an impossible dream─to set in motion a revolution that will rattle the very firmament, and shake off the yoke of the most powerful empire in the world!" | #448 |
| M448j | Yugiri says the people of Yanxia are ready to rise up and fight. | *An Impossible Dream* · 4.0 · main | "The people of Yanxia remain loyal to Doma. I have seen the fire in their eyes─they are ready to rise up and fight." | #448 |
| M449a | Hien asks to fight in the Naadam as a warrior of the Mol. | *Stars in the Dark* · 4.0 · main | "To that end, I wish to fight in the Naadam─as a warrior of the Mol. Will you grant me this honor?" | #449 |
| M449b | Victory beside a more favored tribe would be credited to it, and none would follow Hien. | *Stars in the Dark* · 4.0 · main | "Were we to join hands with a more favored tribe, our victory would inevitably be attributed to them. And no one would follow me to Doma." | #449 |
| M449c | No foreigner has ever taken part in a Naadam, let alone won. | *Stars in the Dark* · 4.0 · main | "To my knowledge no foreigners have ever participated, much less triumphed in a Naadam." | #449 |
| M449d | Cirina says the Mol are weak and few. | *Stars in the Dark* · 4.0 · main | "There is no path to victory! We are weak and our numbers few." | #449 |
| M449e | Temulun, khatun of the Mol, invites you to join them in the Naadam. | *Stars in the Dark* · 4.0 · main | "As khatun of the Mol, I invite you all to join with us in the Naadam." | #449 |
| M449f | Temulun says the gods and stars know you. | *Stars in the Dark* · 4.0 · main | "The gods know you, child...as do the stars in the heavens, which shone bright at your coming." | #449 |
| M449g | To be recognized as Mol warriors you must overcome a trial. | *Stars in the Dark* · 4.0 · main | "To be recognized as warriors of the Mol, there is a trial you must overcome." | #449 |
| M449h | The Mol hearken to the whispers of the elder gods. | *Stars in the Dark* · 4.0 · main | "The Mol hearken to the whispers of the elder gods, which guide us in our daily lives." | #449 |
| M450a | The trial sends you to the far reaches of the Steppe, as the warrior Bardam once went. | *A Warrior's Welcome* · 4.0 · main | "You will journey unto the far reaches of the Steppe to confront a terrible foe, as did the warrior Bardam in the days of yore." | #450 |
| M450b | Many who follow Bardam's footsteps perish. | *A Warrior's Welcome* · 4.0 · main | "Many who follow in his footsteps perish, lacking in strength of body and mind." | #450 |
| M450c | For the feast, gulo gulo are to be lured to slaughter. | *A Warrior's Welcome* · 4.0 · main | "For today's feast the gods decreed that we lure gulo gulo to slaughter with entrails." | #450 |
| M450d | Hien splits the bait between you for a contest. | *A Warrior's Welcome* · 4.0 · main | "I have six bags of entrails with which we may lure our gulo gulo. Three for you, and three for me." | #450 |
| M451a | A Mol boy says the soil belongs to one and all. | *The Heart of Nations* · 4.0 · main | "Ours is the soil of one and all." | #451 |
| M451b | Hien says he should have fled after his father died. | *The Heart of Nations* · 4.0 · main | "I know I should have fled with the others after Father died, instead of blindly fighting on." | #451 |
| M451c | His people called for the sword, so that is what he must be. | *The Heart of Nations* · 4.0 · main | "Instead they have called for the sword, so that is what I must be." | #451 |
| M451d | Gosetsu calls Hien Master Shun. | *The Heart of Nations* · 4.0 · main | "Mina's little Master Shun. What a fine young man you've become!" | #451 |
| M451e | Gosetsu has amends to make before he meets his ancestors. | *The Heart of Nations* · 4.0 · main | "I myself am in no hurry to meet those who have gone before—not while I have still to make amends." | #451 |
| M451f | Hien declares it time for your trial. | *The Heart of Nations* · 4.0 · main | "Then what better time than now to undertake our trial, and become full-fledged warriors of the Mol!" | #451 |
| M452a | To be warriors of the Steppe, you must follow in the footsteps of Bardam. | *A Trial Before the Trial* · 4.0 · main | "to be recognized as warriors of the Steppe, one must follow in the footsteps of the legendary warrior Bardam." | #452 |
| M452b | Bardam walked to a holy place, thereafter named Bardam's Mettle. | *A Trial Before the Trial* · 4.0 · main | "walked for many malms until he came to a holy place, thereafter named Bardam's Mettle." | #452 |
| M452c | You must tame a yol at the altar's breeding grounds. | *A Trial Before the Trial* · 4.0 · main | "The altar lies deep within the breeding grounds of the yol. To complete your trial, you must tame one." | #452 |
| M452d | On the eve of the Naadam, some tribes abduct and enslave other tribes' warriors. | *A Trial Before the Trial* · 4.0 · main | "On the eve of the Naadam, many strive to improve their fortunes—some by abducting and enslaving the warriors of other tribes." | #452 |
| M452e | Attackers left you no choice but to fight. | *A Trial Before the Trial* · 4.0 · main | "Ah, there you are. As you can see, they left us little choice." | #452 |
| M452f | You reach the pass to Bardam's Mettle. | *A Trial Before the Trial* · 4.0 · main | "Right. This must be the pass that leads to Bardam's Mettle." | #452 |
| M453a | To fight alongside the Mol, you must first be recognized as warriors of the Steppe. | *In the Footsteps of Bardam the Brave* · 4.0 · main | "If you are to participate in the [[Naadam]] and fight alongside the [[Mol]], you must first be recognized as a warrior of the [[Azim Steppe|Steppe]]." | #453 |
| M453b | You tame a wild yol in Bardam's Mettle. | *In the Footsteps of Bardam the Brave* · 4.0 · main | "At the end of a strange and bewildering journey through Bardam's Mettle, you manage to tame a wild yol in the traditional fashion." | #453 |
| M453c | Lyse completes her trial too, but Hien and Gosetsu have not emerged. | *In the Footsteps of Bardam the Brave* · 4.0 · main | "[[Lyse]] emerges from the pass not long after you do, her trial also complete. However, there is still no sign of [[Hien]] or [[Gosetsu]]..." | #453 |
| M453d | You are now warriors of the Steppe. | *In the Footsteps of Bardam the Brave* · 4.0 · main | "There can be no doubt: you are now warriors of the Steppe." | #453 |
| M453e | Bardam grants you the Steppe and everything in it. | *In the Footsteps of Bardam the Brave* · 4.0 · main | "Yours is the Steppe and everything in it!" | #453 |
| M454a | Hien and Gosetsu emerge, and Oronir surround you and take you to their khan. | *The Children of Azim* · 4.0 · main | "you find yourself surrounded by a party of Oronir, their leader demanding that you return with him to meet with his khan." | #454 |
| M454b | Magnai is leader of the Oronir and lord of the Dawn Throne. | *The Children of Azim* · 4.0 · main | "The most radiant brother Magnai, leader of the Oronir and lord of the Dawn Throne, recognizes you as warriors of the Steppe." | #454 |
| M454c | Magnai demands you acknowledge his supreme authority and pay tribute. | *The Children of Azim* · 4.0 · main | "He demands that you acknowledge his supreme authority and pay tribute" | #454 |
| M454d | Magnai says the Oronir are born of the Sun. | *The Children of Azim* · 4.0 · main | "Born of the Sun are Oronir, and born of the earth are you." | #454 |
| M454e | Magnai demands fealty to the Sun. | *The Children of Azim* · 4.0 · main | "Swear fealty to the Sun. Pledge to him your body and soul." | #454 |
| M454f | Baatu, who led your escort, will give you your duties. | *The Children of Azim* · 4.0 · main | "The details of the duties he would demand from you, however, shall be given by Baatu, the warrior who commanded your escort from Bardam's Mettle." | #454 |
| M454g | Hien sees a chance to learn of his foes and future allies. | *The Children of Azim* · 4.0 · main | "Hien considers this an opportunity to learn more of his foes and soon-to-be allies" | #454 |
| M455a | Baatu says you will not die today, but will serve the Oronir cause. | *The Labors of Magnai* · 4.0 · main | "By the grace of our most radiant brother, you will not die this day, but instead be granted an opportunity to contribute to our cause." | #455 |
| M455b | Lyse is set to milk the Oronir's beasts. | *The Labors of Magnai* · 4.0 · main | "You, the red woman. You will milk our beasts." | #455 |
| M455c | You are to gather swordgrass from the bed of Azim Khaat. | *The Labors of Magnai* · 4.0 · main | "You are to dive into Azim Khaat, below the Dawn Throne, and gather swordgrass from the lakebed." | #455 |
| M455d | The swordgrass makes paralysis potions. | *The Labors of Magnai* · 4.0 · main | "Lest you wonder, we will use it to prepare potions to induce paralysis." | #455 |
| M455e | Hien asks how the Oronir can be children of Azim if they are Xaela born of Nhaama. | *The Labors of Magnai* · 4.0 · main | "How can Oronir be the children of Azim, if they are Xaela born of Nhaama?" | #455 |
| M455f | Magnai sends Hien to the elders to learn the truth of the world. | *The Labors of Magnai* · 4.0 · main | "Very well. I bid you speak with our elders and learn the truth of this world." | #455 |
| M455g | Gosetsu is set to repair arms and armor. | *The Labors of Magnai* · 4.0 · main | "We have arms and armor in need of repair." | #455 |
| M456a | Udutai says Azim and Nhaama were lovers. | *For Love of the Moon* · 4.0 · main | "But what it does not mention is this: Azim and Nhaama were lovers." | #456 |
| M456b | Sun and moon must remain apart, lest creation end. | *For Love of the Moon* · 4.0 · main | "Alas, he was of the sun and she of the moon. Apart they must remain, lest day and night cease to be, and with them all creation." | #456 |
| M456c | Azim's avatar was the first Oronir. | *For Love of the Moon* · 4.0 · main | "Yea, he was the first Oronir. We are of his flesh and blood." | #456 |
| M456d | Udutai says the Xaela must be made to kneel. | *For Love of the Moon* · 4.0 · main | "Xaela are not wont to kneel. They must be made to—only then will they heed reason." | #456 |
| M456e | Hien says the Oronir's arrogance comes from seeing themselves as caretakers of all Xaela. | *For Love of the Moon* · 4.0 · main | "Their arrogance is rooted in the belief that they must act as caretakers of all Xaela." | #456 |
| M456f | Magnai requires one more thing before your freedom. | *For Love of the Moon* · 4.0 · main | "However, if it is freedom you must desire, then there is one more thing you must do..." | #456 |
| M456g | Hien seeks the elder Udutai as a teacher. | *For Love of the Moon* · 4.0 · main | "I met an elder—a storyteller—named Udutai. Mayhap he can be our teacher." | #456 |
| M457a | As prisoners of the Oronir, you have been coerced into menial tribute. | *Sworn Enemies of the Sun* · 4.0 · main | "As prisoners of the tribe, you have been coerced into performing menial tasks as expressions of tribute." | #457 |
| M457b | For the next task, two will go and two will remain. | *Sworn Enemies of the Sun* · 4.0 · main | "For this task, two will go, and two will remain." | #457 |
| M457c | If the two flee or fall, the others will be enslaved. | *Sworn Enemies of the Sun* · 4.0 · main | "Should the two who embark upon this expedition choose to flee, or be captured or killed by the Dotharl, then the others will be taken as slaves and serve the Sun unto death." | #457 |
| M457d | Sadu is khatun of the Dotharl. | *Sworn Enemies of the Sun* · 4.0 · main | "Sadu, khatun of the Dotharl, who spares but a moment for her fallen kinsman before demanding to know who you are." | #457 |
| M457e | Sadu grants you leave to walk among the Dotharl. | *Sworn Enemies of the Sun* · 4.0 · main | "For your deeds, I grant you leave to walk among us." | #457 |
| M457f | Sadu scorns the Mol as little lambs. | *Sworn Enemies of the Sun* · 4.0 · main | "The Mol? The little lambs who wander to and fro at the behest of their gods?" | #457 |
| M457g | Hien and Lyse may not take part in scouting the Dotharl. | *Sworn Enemies of the Sun* · 4.0 · main | "it seems he and Lyse will not be allowed to take part in your next duty─to reconnoiter the encampment of the Dotharl." | #457 |
| M457h | You saved one hunter, but his comrade was beyond help. | *Sworn Enemies of the Sun* · 4.0 · main | "You reach the embattled Xaela and slay the beasts in time to save his life. Alas, as you suspected, it is too late for his comrade." | #457 |
| M458a | Sadu says Geser will return. | *The Undying Ones* · 4.0 · main | "Geser was a great warrior. He will return to us in time." | #458 |
| M458b | The flesh rots, but the soul endures and returns. | *The Undying Ones* · 4.0 · main | "The flesh rots, but the soul endures. And ere the seasons have turned, so he shall return." | #458 |
| M458c | A returned warrior, glimpsed in a newborn's eyes, is given the same name. | *The Undying Ones* · 4.0 · main | "When he is glimpsed in the eyes of a newborn, he is blessed with the same name, that he may grow into a great warrior once more." | #458 |
| M458d | The Dotharl do not fear death. | *The Undying Ones* · 4.0 · main | "None are braver than the Dotharl, for we do not fear death." | #458 |
| M458e | Gosetsu sees the Dotharl are fewer. | *The Undying Ones* · 4.0 · main | "'tis plain to see that their numbers are inferior." | #458 |
| M458f | Gosetsu cannot ignore Sadu's callousness toward her kinsman. | *The Undying Ones* · 4.0 · main | "I cannot in good conscience ignore the callous disregard that woman showed for her kinsman!" | #458 |
| M459a | Sadu says the Dotharl need no tricks, only strength. | *A Final Peace* · 4.0 · main | "Others rely on tricks and traps. Subterfuge. Lies. We have need of naught but our own strength." | #459 |
| M459b | The Dotharl kill many, and many of them are killed. | *A Final Peace* · 4.0 · main | "While we kill many, many are killed in turn." | #459 |
| M459c | A warrior who dies before having children does not return. | *A Final Peace* · 4.0 · main | "Yet one cannot return unless there is a vessel to inherit the soul, and if warriors die ere they birth children, then that is that." | #459 |
| M459d | Sadu offers that you fight for the Dotharl and live. | *A Final Peace* · 4.0 · main | "Or you could fight for us and live." | #459 |
| M459e | Hien and Lyse are in prison. | *A Final Peace* · 4.0 · main | "''Hien and Lyse are in prison.''" | #459 |
| M459f | Hien was still in his mother's womb when Doma fell twenty-five years ago. | *A Final Peace* · 4.0 · main | "when my homeland fell twenty-five years past, I was still in my mother's womb." | #459 |
| M459g | Lyse means to seize the future she wants. | *A Final Peace* · 4.0 · main | "I'm going to seize the future I want with my own two hands!" | #459 |
| M459h | Gosetsu finds the fallen hunter left for the vultures. | *A Final Peace* · 4.0 · main | "Left for the vultures... It seems an insult to the man." | #459 |
| M460a | Magnai says the Dotharl are not schemers and live only for war. | *As the Gods Will* · 4.0 · main | "The Dotharl are not schemers. They live only for war—for the contest of strength and will." | #460 |
| M460b | Magnai called the Steppe's women to see whether Nhaama looked back from their eyes. | *As the Gods Will* · 4.0 · main | "The most radiant brother called to him the women of the Steppe, that he might look into their eyes and see if Nhaama looked back." | #460 |
| M460c | Sadu gave great offense after the last Naadam. | *As the Gods Will* · 4.0 · main | "After the last Naadam, she gave great offense." | #460 |
| M460d | Magnai demands you swear to the law of the Steppe. | *As the Gods Will* · 4.0 · main | "Swear, here and now, to abide by the law of the Steppe." | #460 |
| M460e | You may return to the Mol to make ready. | *As the Gods Will* · 4.0 · main | "You may return to the Mol and make ready for the Naadam...on one condition." | #460 |
| M460f | Cirina and the Mol are waiting. | *As the Gods Will* · 4.0 · main | "Come, come—Cirina and the others are waiting!" | #460 |
| M460g | Magnai has the others fetched from their cells. | *As the Gods Will* · 4.0 · main | "Go and fetch the others from their cells." | #460 |
| M461a | The Naadam is the ceremonial battle for rule of the Steppe. | *Naadam* · 4.0 · main | "The ceremonial battle for rulership over the [[Azim Steppe|Steppe]], or [[The Naadam|Naadam]], will commence with the rising of the sun" | #461 |
| M461b | You stand a khagan crowned. | *Naadam* · 4.0 · main | "As the ovoo evaporates into motes of fading light, you stand a khagan crowned." | #461 |
| M461c | The Mol now claim the Dawn Throne. | *Naadam* · 4.0 · main | "The Naadam is ended, and we Mol now claim the Dawn Throne." | #461 |
| M461d | Grynewaht arrives with an imperial squadron to kill you, but faces a united Steppe. | *Naadam* · 4.0 · main | "for [[Grynewaht]] suddenly appears at the head of an imperial squadron, and declares his intention to kill you once and for all. Yet in coming a moment too late, he faces a Steppe united." | #461 |
| M461e | Hien convinces the Oronir and Dotharl to march south and fight for Doma. | *Naadam* · 4.0 · main | "for [[Hien]] succeeds in convincing the two tribes to travel south with him and fight for [[Doma]]'s liberation." | #461 |
| M461f | Magnai pledges that the Sun will answer Hien's call. | *Naadam* · 4.0 · main | "The Sun shall answer your call, Doman." | #461 |
| M461g | Hien hands Cirina command of the gathered forces. | *Naadam* · 4.0 · main | "I have kept my promise, Cirina. They are yours to command." | #461 |
| M461h | Temulun says the Xaela were made for battle. | *Naadam* · 4.0 · main | "We were made for battle—to kill and be killed." | #461 |
| M461i | With the Oronir and Dotharl, you slay the invaders and send Grynewaht off again. | *Naadam* · 4.0 · main | "With the aid of the Oronir and the Dotharl, you slay the invaders and send Grynewaht on his way once more." | #461 |
| M461j | Sadu asks whether Hien would have the Dotharl fight his war for Doma. | *Naadam* · 4.0 · main | "To Doma would you have us go, to fight your war and win your freedom?" | #461 |
| M461k | Hien and the Liberation Front are a step closer to freeing Doma. | *Naadam* · 4.0 · main | "By your deeds are Lord Hien and the Doman Liberation Front one step closer to freeing their home from the yoke of imperial oppression." | #461 |
| M462a | Temulun credits you with the Mol's victory and the safe return of all her kinsmen. | *Glory to the Khagan* · 4.0 · main | "Not only did you bring us victory in the Naadam, but you ensured that every one of my kinsmen returned with their lives." | #462 |
| M462b | None of the Mol were lost. | *Glory to the Khagan* · 4.0 · main | "Some were more gravely wounded than others, but none were lost to us." | #462 |
| M462c | You were declared khagan at the ovoo. | *Glory to the Khagan* · 4.0 · main | "As well as the moment when you became one with the ovoo and were declared khagan!" | #462 |
| M462d | Cirina and her grandmother discuss how to thank you. | *Glory to the Khagan* · 4.0 · main | "Grandmother and I have been discussing how we might express our gratitude to you." | #462 |
| M462e | Cirina gives you a morin khuur. | *Glory to the Khagan* · 4.0 · main | "This is a morin khuur—a traditional instrument of our people." | #462 |
| M462f | The Mol preserve their heroes' feats in song. | *Glory to the Khagan* · 4.0 · main | "The feats of our greatest heroes, like Bardam and Chakha, are preserved in song." | #462 |
| M463a | Hien has his Xaela army and is ready to lead Doma to war. | *In Crimson They Walked* · 4.0 · main | "Lord Hien has his Xaela army, and is ready to return to Doma and lead his people to war against the Empire." | #463 |
| M463b | Magnai renews his vow to aid the Domans. | *In Crimson They Walked* · 4.0 · main | "Magnai renews his vow to render aid to the Domans without a second thought." | #463 |
| M463c | Magnai wonders whether Cirina is his long-sought moon. | *In Crimson They Walked* · 4.0 · main | "he then begins to muse aloud about whether or not Cirina could be his long-sought moon." | #463 |
| M463d | Cirina says Magnai is mistaken. | *In Crimson They Walked* · 4.0 · main | "I know not of what you speak, Brother Magnai, but I am certain you are mistaken." | #463 |
| M463e | Sadu reaffirms that the Dotharl will go to war for Hien. | *In Crimson They Walked* · 4.0 · main | "Sadu too does not hesitate to reiterate that the Dotharl will gladly go to war for Lord Hien" | #463 |
| M463f | The Dotharl sealed the southern passage with magicks during the Doman rebellion. | *In Crimson They Walked* · 4.0 · main | "The Dotharl sealed it with their magicks during the Doman rebellion, when more men in iron came from afar." | #463 |
| M463g | Geser's seed has quickened in his lover. | *In Crimson They Walked* · 4.0 · main | "Geser's seed has quickened in his lover." | #463 |
| M463h | Cirina gives Lyse a banner of the Mol. | *In Crimson They Walked* · 4.0 · main | "Wait, is this a banner of the Mol tribe?" | #463 |
| M463i | Magnai says you are not allies. | *In Crimson They Walked* · 4.0 · main | "We are not allies, traveler—far from it." | #463 |
| M463j | The Oronir yet occupy the Dawn Throne. | *In Crimson They Walked* · 4.0 · main | "The most radiant brother of the Oronir, who yet occupy the Dawn Throne, is to be first." | #463 |
| M463k | The long-sealed passage south is open. | *In Crimson They Walked* · 4.0 · main | "you resume your journey south towards a passage long sealed by the Dotharl's magicks, but no longer." | #463 |
| M463l | Cirina parts from you until you call the Mol to battle. | *In Crimson They Walked* · 4.0 · main | "It is here we must part ways, until the day you call us to battle." | #463 |
| M464a | Hien leads you to the House of the Fierce. | *The Hour of Reckoning* · 4.0 · main | "Right, then. To the House of the Fierce. With me, my friends!" | #464 |
| M464b | Yugiri greets Hien; Gosetsu told them of your trials on the Steppe. | *The Hour of Reckoning* · 4.0 · main | "Gosetsu told us of your trials and tribulations on the Steppe." | #464 |
| M464c | Hien meets Alphinaud and Alisaie, to whom he owes much. | *The Hour of Reckoning* · 4.0 · main | "I am told we owe you much. It is a pleasure to meet you both." | #464 |
| M464d | The Xaela tribes have pledged their might to the cause. | *The Hour of Reckoning* · 4.0 · main | "The Xaela tribes of the Azim Steppe have pledged their military might to the cause." | #464 |
| M464e | Alisaie secured the Confederacy and many Domans. | *The Hour of Reckoning* · 4.0 · main | "We, meanwhile, have secured the support of the Confederacy, and a not insignificant number of your countrymen." | #464 |
| M464f | The Blue Kojin will fight if Hien agrees to trade terms after Doma is freed. | *The Hour of Reckoning* · 4.0 · main | "The Blue Kojin, too, have expressed a willingness to join the fight—provided you agree to certain trade agreements following the liberation of Doma." | #464 |
| M464g | The shinobi will help, but Sui-no-Sato refused the call. | *The Hour of Reckoning* · 4.0 · main | "The shinobi will render what aid they can. However, Sui-no-Sato refused to answer our call." | #464 |
| M464h | Zenos left Doma soon after you went to find Hien, for Gyr Abania. | *The Hour of Reckoning* · 4.0 · main | "You should know that Zenos departed Doma not long after you left to find Lord Hien. We know not the reason why, but it would seem he was eager to return to Gyr Abania." | #464 |
| M464i | Yotsuyu remains acting viceroy, and Doma Castle's garrison is preparing a massive operation. | *The Hour of Reckoning* · 4.0 · main | "Yotsuyu retains her position as acting viceroy, and we have reports that the garrison at Doma Castle is preparing for a massive operation..." | #464 |
| M464j | The Scions suspect a purge of Doma's remaining resistance. | *The Hour of Reckoning* · 4.0 · main | "We suspect that the Empire's attempt to eliminate you on the Steppe may have been a prelude to a larger effort to purge the remaining pockets of resistance within Doma." | #464 |
| M464k | Hien rallies his people: twenty-five years of oppression end with them. | *The Hour of Reckoning* · 4.0 · main | "Twenty-five years of oppression, of tyranny, of shame—it ends with us! We will prevail!" | #464 |
| M464l | Alphinaud and Alisaie have worked to restore the aetheryte. | *The Hour of Reckoning* · 4.0 · main | "Alisaie and I have been hard at work attempting to restore the aetheryte here to its former glory" | #464 |
| M465a | Anticipating Lord Hien's return, Alphinaud has drawn up a plan to retake Doma Castle. | *The Room Where It Happened* · 4.0 · main | "In anticipation of Lord Hien's return, Alphinaud has developed a comprehensive strategy to retake Doma Castle." | #465 |
| M465b | Lyse and the Doman irregulars will open with diversions, then the Xaela will strike the castle's airborne defenses. | *The Room Where It Happened* · 4.0 · main | "After Lyse and the Doman irregulars carry out a series of diversionary attacks, the Xaela will launch a surprise attack on the castle's airborne defenses." | #465 |
| M465c | Ships will bombard the castle while sailing west toward the Doman Enclave. | *The Room Where It Happened* · 4.0 · main | "They will commence to bombard the castle with cannon fire, while sailing west towards the Doman Enclave." | #465 |
| M465d | Alphinaud wants the enclave secured before the imperials think to take its people hostage. | *The Room Where It Happened* · 4.0 · main | "Once pressed, the imperials may well think to take them hostage, and I would fain forestall any such attempt." | #465 |
| M465e | Alphinaud doubts he can disable the castle's field generators, so he has turned to Cid. | *The Room Where It Happened* · 4.0 · main | "Alphinaud lacks confidence in his ability to disable the field generators" | #465 |
| M465f | Tataru has taken after Hancock and dresses in the latest Hingan fashions. | *The Room Where It Happened* · 4.0 · main | "You are shocked to find Tataru a woman transformed, having taken after Hancock and dressed herself in the latest Hingan fashions." | #465 |
| M465g | Hien will carry out Alphinaud's plan to the letter. | *The Room Where It Happened* · 4.0 · main | "We shall carry out Alphinaud's plan to the letter." | #465 |
| M466a | Tataru has grown used to life in Kugane. | *How Tataru Got Her Groove Back* · 4.0 · main | "Tataru has grown rather accustomed to her life in Kugane." | #466 |
| M466b | Cid's hunt for the dragon and Omega keeps him from coming himself. | *How Tataru Got Her Groove Back* · 4.0 · main | "Apparently his hunt for the dragon and Omega demands his full attention, you see..." | #466 |
| M466c | The parcel holds the documents on the magitek field generators that Alphinaud asked for. | *How Tataru Got Her Groove Back* · 4.0 · main | "These are the documents detailing the operation of the magitek field generators Alphinaud requested!" | #466 |
| M466d | Hancock has arranged for the company to supply the Confederacy with arms. | *How Tataru Got Her Groove Back* · 4.0 · main | "we have brokered an arrangement between the company and the Confederacy. They require armaments, we provide." | #466 |
| M466e | Alphinaud can deactivate the barrier once the shinobi get him inside the Moon Gates. | *How Tataru Got Her Groove Back* · 4.0 · main | "Assuming our shinobi escort can see us safely inside the Moon Gates, I should have no trouble deactivating the barrier." | #466 |
| M467a | Since your failed assassination attempt the imperials have shown unusual restraint. | *Seeds of Despair* · 4.0 · main | "In the wake of your failed assassination attempt, the imperials demonstrated uncharacteristic restraint." | #467 |
| M467b | Intelligence suggests an imperial purge is imminent. | *Seeds of Despair* · 4.0 · main | "our latest intelligence does suggest a purge is imminent." | #467 |
| M467c | Imperial soldiers are said to be moving about disguised as villagers. | *Seeds of Despair* · 4.0 · main | "We've heard rumors of imperial soldiers skulking about disguised as villagers." | #467 |
| M467d | Alisaie caught one of the men who attacked you as he fled. | *Seeds of Despair* · 4.0 · main | "I saw them attack you, while this one fled. Since you seemed to have everything in hand, I decided to capture him." | #467 |
| M467e | In an Echo vision, a village mother keeps her late sister's daughter. | *Seeds of Despair* · 4.0 · main | "My sister's, not mine—kami rest her soul." | #467 |
| M467f | Alisaie saw you go somewhere else while the two of you were talking. | *Seeds of Despair* · 4.0 · main | "What happened? We were talking, and then all of a sudden you were somewhere else." | #467 |
| M468a | The villagers lack the armor the diversion needs. | *The Limits of Our Endurance* · 4.0 · main | "Well the thing is, we don't have enough armor to go around." | #468 |
| M468b | A craftsman who fled the enclave might make armor. | *The Limits of Our Endurance* · 4.0 · main | "There is one man. A craftsman who fled the enclave. He is experienced, and may be able to help." | #468 |
| M468c | The craftsman is Tsuranuki, who lives in Kusakari. | *The Limits of Our Endurance* · 4.0 · main | "His name is Tsuranuki, and he lives in Kusakari, in the south." | #468 |
| M468d | Since Lord Hien returned, everyone in the village has tried to help. | *The Limits of Our Endurance* · 4.0 · main | "Everyone's been trying to contribute since Lord Hien returned." | #468 |
| M468e | Tsuranuki scoffs at the rebels' foolish dreams. | *The Limits of Our Endurance* · 4.0 · main | "Surely they would not think to trouble me with such foolish dreams!" | #468 |
| M469a | Tsuranuki will make armor if you bring him steel. | *Broken Steel, Broken Men* · 4.0 · main | "Bring me my steel, and you will have your armor." | #469 |
| M469b | Tsuranuki made his bombs to kill imperials. | *Broken Steel, Broken Men* · 4.0 · main | "I made them to kill imperials, heh heh!" | #469 |
| M469c | Lord Kaien was restored to his throne years later as a paper tiger. | *Broken Steel, Broken Men* · 4.0 · main | "Restored to his throne years later, a paper tiger." | #469 |
| M469d | Tsuranuki's circle whispered dreams of freedom to Kaien. | *Broken Steel, Broken Men* · 4.0 · main | "After my lady died, we began to whisper in his ears dreams of freedom and liberation." | #469 |
| M469e | Tsuranuki blames himself and his circle for the deaths. | *Broken Steel, Broken Men* · 4.0 · main | "We murdered them all, don't you see?" | #469 |
| M469f | Tsuranuki believed Kaien and Hien both dead. | *Broken Steel, Broken Men* · 4.0 · main | "Lord Kaien, Lord Hien—all dead! Their blood is on our hands!" | #469 |
| M469g | Tsuranuki learns that Lord Hien lives. | *Broken Steel, Broken Men* · 4.0 · main | "Could...could it be? My lord Hien yet lives?" | #469 |
| M470a | After twenty-five years of oppression, the Domans will still stand up and fight. | *The Doma Within* · 4.0 · main | "Even after twenty-five years of oppression, they still have it in them to stand up and fight." | #470 |
| M470b | Great swaths of Monzen were razed and its people put to the sword. | *The Doma Within* · 4.0 · main | "Great swaths were razed, and people put to the sword." | #470 |
| M470c | Hien admits the Domans were complicit in the occupation. | *The Doma Within* · 4.0 · main | "But even then, the imperials walked among us. Sometimes openly, sometimes discreetly, but we were always aware of their presence. And we...we were complicit." | #470 |
| M470d | Hien says his father kept the peace and his people safe, at a cost. | *The Doma Within* · 4.0 · main | "He did what he had to do to preserve the peace. To keep our people safe. But at what cost..." | #470 |
| M470e | Doma Castle now belongs to Yotsuyu. | *The Doma Within* · 4.0 · main | "Now her every court and corridor belongs to Yotsuyu." | #470 |
| M470f | Hien names himself Kaien's son and a caretaker of Doma's story. | *The Doma Within* · 4.0 · main | "Hien, son of Kaien. Another caretaker of the story of Doma." | #470 |
| M470g | Hien knows how to improve the plan. | *The Doma Within* · 4.0 · main | "Ours is an excellent plan, but not so excellent that it cannot be improved—and I know how." | #470 |
| M471a | Soroban, the Kojin emissary, brings the elder's regards. | *On the Eve of Destiny* · 4.0 · main | "Greetings and well met, old friends! The elder sends his regards." | #471 |
| M471b | The Kojin are to advance underwater and breach the castle's outer wall. | *On the Eve of Destiny* · 4.0 · main | "I would have the Kojin advance upstream, beneath the water, and breach a segment of the outer wall." | #471 |
| M471c | Hien means to flood Doma Castle. | *On the Eve of Destiny* · 4.0 · main | "I mean to flood Doma Castle." | #471 |
| M471d | Gosetsu protests that destroying the castle is unthinkable. | *On the Eve of Destiny* · 4.0 · main | "To destroy it is unthinkable, unconscionable–you cannot do this!" | #471 |
| M471e | Flooding the castle removes most of the garrison and drives Yotsuyu to the top of the keep. | *On the Eve of Destiny* · 4.0 · main | "By flooding the castle we remove the better part of their forces from the field and force Yotsuyu to retreat to the highest levels of the keep." | #471 |
| M471f | Gosetsu's first failure was Doma's defeat twenty-five years ago and Lord Kaien's imprisonment. | *On the Eve of Destiny* · 4.0 · main | "The first, our defeat at the hands of the Empire twenty-five years past, and the subsequent imprisonment of your father." | #471 |
| M471g | His second was failing to protect Kaien and Hien in the rebellion. | *On the Eve of Destiny* · 4.0 · main | "when in the rebellion I failed to protect not only Lord Kaien—but Lord Hien too..." | #471 |
| M471h | Hien promises that every debt will be repaid. | *On the Eve of Destiny* · 4.0 · main | "All debts will be repaid. On that you have my word." | #471 |
| M472a | The Kojin are ready. | *The Die Is Cast* · 4.0 · main | "We received confirmation from the Kojin not long ago." | #472 |
| M472b | Every stage of the plan is carried out to perfection. | *The Die Is Cast* · 4.0 · main | "Every stage of the plan is carried out to perfection" | #472 |
| M472c | The river rushes into Doma Castle. | *The Die Is Cast* · 4.0 · main | "you and your comrades observe as the river rushes into Doma Castle" | #472 |
| M472d | Grynewaht, transformed by his vengeance into steel and flesh, falls in the castle's depths. | *The Die Is Cast* · 4.0 · main | "driven mad by his quest for vengeance, and transformed into a twisted mass of steel and flesh … he crumples to the ground, never to rise again." | #472 |
| M472e | Yotsuyu found a way to survive as a spy for the Empire. | *The Die Is Cast* · 4.0 · main | "But this time, this time, I found a way to live for myself. To survive. As a spy for the Empire." | #472 |
| M472f | Yotsuyu tells how her countrymen wronged her and how she came to delight in their suffering. | *The Die Is Cast* · 4.0 · main | "she speaks of the many ways in which she was wronged by her countrymen...and of the joy she one day found in their suffering." | #472 |
| M472g | Yotsuyu brings the keep down in a final act of defiance. | *The Die Is Cast* · 4.0 · main | "she gleefully reveals her plan to bring the keep down on your heads in a final suicidal act of defiance" | #472 |
| M472h | Gosetsu catches the falling debris. | *The Die Is Cast* · 4.0 · main | "Gosetsu, blessed with indomitable strength as he is, has somehow managed to catch the massive debris that threatened to crush you" | #472 |
| M472i | Yotsuyu survives and fires on Gosetsu until her pistol is spent. | *The Die Is Cast* · 4.0 · main | "Alas, Yotsuyu too has survived, as you learn to your dismay when she fires upon the [[samurai]], not stopping until her pistol is spent." | #472 |
| M472j | Gosetsu urges his lord to go while he can. | *The Die Is Cast* · 4.0 · main | "Go, my lord...while you still can. I cannot hold this forever." | #472 |
| M472k | Hien, Gosetsu and Yugiri form one group. | *The Die Is Cast* · 4.0 · main | "He, [[Gosetsu]], and [[Yugiri]] will comprise one" | #472 |
| M473a | The castle has fallen and the imperial occupation of Doma has ended. | *The World Turned Upside Down* · 4.0 · main | "The castle has fallen, and the imperial occupation of Doma is at an end." | #473 |
| M473b | No body was found, but it seems all but impossible that Gosetsu survived. | *The World Turned Upside Down* · 4.0 · main | "Though he found no body, it seems all but impossible that the samurai survived..." | #473 |
| M473c | Soroban thinks Gosetsu may have fallen into the river. | *The World Turned Upside Down* · 4.0 · main | "If the floor beneath him gave way before the rest...it is not impossible that he could have been sent tumbling into the river." | #473 |
| M473d | Hien was raised to rule. | *The World Turned Upside Down* · 4.0 · main | "Hien was raised to rule, and so the language of leadership comes naturally to him." | #473 |
| M473e | Hien urges his countrymen to come together and rebuild. | *The World Turned Upside Down* · 4.0 · main | "he implores his countrymen not to grow complacent, for the true challenge lies in the days ahead, when they must come together to rebuild their broken nation." | #473 |
| M473f | Zenos still rules Gyr Abania, so you must return west in haste. | *The World Turned Upside Down* · 4.0 · main | "Gyr Abania still suffers under the rule of Zenos … it is imperative that you return to the West with all possible haste." | #473 |
| M473g | Two Confederates take you to Kugane, to find passage to Limsa Lominsa. | *The World Turned Upside Down* · 4.0 · main | "Rasho and Tansui understand the urgency of your cause, and agree to take you to Kugane, where you might secure passage to Limsa Lominsa aboard a larger ship." | #473 |
| M473h | Cirina says the war in Doma has ended, and the Oronir and Dotharl have withdrawn. | *The World Turned Upside Down* · 4.0 · main | "The war here is ended, and there is no cause to remain. The Oronir and Dotharl have already withdrawn." | #473 |
| M474a | Tataru chooses to stay in Kugane. | *A Swift and Secret Departure* · 4.0 · main | "Yes. I gave it some thought, and I realized that even with Doma free, there's still a need for a woman in the middle." | #474 |
| M474b | Hien offers to repay the Scions by sending Doman forces to Eorzea. | *A Swift and Secret Departure* · 4.0 · main | "I think it only meet that we repay the favor by dispatching our own forces to Eorzea. If you would have us." | #474 |
| M474c | Hien will send shinobi to spread word of Doma's victory and inspire other provinces. | *A Swift and Secret Departure* · 4.0 · main | "we will send forth shinobi to provinces far and wide to spread word of our victory, that we might inspire others to take up arms as we did." | #474 |
| M474d | Fordola presents Zenos with the prisoner the Crania Lupi took. | *A Swift and Secret Departure* · 4.0 · main | "come at the command of the lord viceroy to present the prisoner taken by the Crania Lupi!" | #474 |
| M474e | Krile tells Zenos he has the eyes of a monster. | *A Swift and Secret Departure* · 4.0 · main | "It's true what they say. You do have the eyes of a monster." | #474 |
| M474f | Zenos says the beast yet lives and that it is time to sharpen his claws. | *A Swift and Secret Departure* · 4.0 · main | "The beast yet lives. Mayhap it is time I sharpen my claws." | #474 |
| M474g | Zenos rewards Fordola with a dead soldier's weapon. | *A Swift and Secret Departure* · 4.0 · main | "A reward given in recognition of your service. I trust you will prove more capable than its former owner." | #474 |
| M474h | Lyse wants to go back to Ala Mhigo and defeat Zenos. | *A Swift and Secret Departure* · 4.0 · main | "So let's do it! Let's get back to Ala Mhigo and defeat Zenos once and for all!" | #474 |
| M475a | Krile was ambushed while escorting the wounded Y'shtola to the Rising Stones. | *While You Were Away* · 4.0 · main | "Krile and a squadron of Resistance fighters were escorting Y'shtola and several other wounded to the Rising Stones for further treatment when they were ambushed by imperial forces." | #475 |
| M475b | The Alliance holds the Wall but cannot press further into occupied territory. | *While You Were Away* · 4.0 · main | "The Alliance still holds the Wall, despite several imperial counterattacks. But we have been unable to press further into occupied territory." | #475 |
| M475c | The Skulls knew a path across the Wall. | *While You Were Away* · 4.0 · main | "It was the Skulls. They knew a path across the Wall we hadn't found." | #475 |
| M475d | Zenos himself ordered Krile brought back alive. | *While You Were Away* · 4.0 · main | "It was Zenos himself that gave the order. Told her to return with Krile" | #475 |
| M475e | Fordola was promised education, training and citizenship if she sold her soul. | *While You Were Away* · 4.0 · main | "A woman promised the world—education, training, citizenship—allthat and more if she but sold her soul." | #475 |
| M475f | Thancred will slip past enemy lines to search on his own. | *While You Were Away* · 4.0 · main | "I intend to slip past enemy lines and see what information I can uncover on my own." | #475 |
| M475g | With the Flames' Ala Mhigan Brigade, the Resistance has freed countrymen from labor camps. | *While You Were Away* · 4.0 · main | "We've been blessed to receive the support of the Flames' Ala Mhigan Brigade, who've been instrumental in freeing our countrymen from labor camps throughout Gyr Abania" | #475 |
| M476a | The first objective is Castellum Velodyna, whose bridge commands the Fringes. | *Rhalgr's Beacon* · 4.0 · main | "Our first objective will be to capture Castellum Velodyna. Take the bridge, and the whole Fringes will be ours." | #476 |
| M476b | Pipin's agents report a flurry of messages between Garlemald and Ala Mhigo. | *Rhalgr's Beacon* · 4.0 · main | "Our agents report a flurry of communications between Garlemald and Ala Mhigo in recent days." | #476 |
| M476c | Zenos ordered Fordola and her men to defend the bridge. | *Rhalgr's Beacon* · 4.0 · main | "Zenos knows this, which is why he has ordered Fordola and her men to see to the bridge's defense." | #476 |
| M476d | Fordola, the Butcher, is decisive, ruthless and feared by friend and foe. | *Rhalgr's Beacon* · 4.0 · main | "The Butcher is not to be underestimated. She is decisive, ruthless, and feared by foes and friends alike." | #476 |
| M476e | The plan is a feint to convince the imperials Velodyna is lost. | *Rhalgr's Beacon* · 4.0 · main | "We convince them Velodyna is lost, hit them hard with our reinforcements, and watch them go running for the hills." | #476 |
| M476f | M'naago flies her griffin to the castellum's top to change its banner. | *Rhalgr's Beacon* · 4.0 · main | "who will fly to the top of the castellum with the aid of her griffin, and there employ a number of modified glamour prisms" | #476 |
| M476g | At Conrad's urging Lyse lets Fordola go; Castellum Velodyna is taken. | *Rhalgr's Beacon* · 4.0 · main | "At Conrad's urging, Lyse lets her go, for while capturing the Skulls commander would represent a fine achievement, the greater goal has been accomplished: Castellum Velodyna is yours." | #476 |
| M476h | The castellum's fall all but drives the imperials from the Fringes. | *Rhalgr's Beacon* · 4.0 · main | "the fall of the castellum marks a turning point in the campaign, for the imperials have been all but driven from the Fringes." | #476 |
| M477a | Imperials may still be holed up inside the castellum. | *The Fortunes of War* · 4.0 · main | "There's still a chance that some imperials are holed up inside the castellum." | #477 |
| M477b | Pipin has taken troops to occupy Velodyna. | *The Fortunes of War* · 4.0 · main | "Pipin left but a moment ago with a contingent of troops to occupy Velodyna." | #477 |
| M477c | If the imperials return, they will find the Alliance dug in behind their own walls. | *The Fortunes of War* · 4.0 · main | "If and when the imperials return, they'll find us dug in behind their own walls." | #477 |
| M477d | Raubahn warns that one castellum will not turn the tide. | *The Fortunes of War* · 4.0 · main | "It'll take more than one castellum to turn the tide." | #477 |
| M477e | Alphinaud says the Resistance allies are waiting. | *The Fortunes of War* · 4.0 · main | "Our work here is finished. Our Resistance allies are waiting." | #477 |
| M478a | M'naago flew to the Peering Stones, the M tribe village where she was born. | *Rising Fortunes, Rising Spirits* · 4.0 · main | "She took her bird and flew off towards the Peering Stones—the village of the M tribe, where she was born." | #478 |
| M478b | The Qalyana are the strongest and most influential Ananta tribe. | *Rising Fortunes, Rising Spirits* · 4.0 · main | "The Qalyana are another Ananta tribe—the strongest and the most influential." | #478 |
| M478c | The Qalyana swore fealty to the Empire and kept to their borders. | *Rising Fortunes, Rising Spirits* · 4.0 · main | "They swore fealty to the Empire and were content to remain within their borders." | #478 |
| M478d | The Butcher demanded that the Qalyana surrender a hostage. | *Rising Fortunes, Rising Spirits* · 4.0 · main | "She demanded the Qalyana sssurrender a hossstage, thisss Butcher." | #478 |
| M478e | When the Qalyana surrounded the imperials, one of them cut down the child. | *Rising Fortunes, Rising Spirits* · 4.0 · main | "The Qalyana sssurrounded the imperials, one of whom, whether out of fear or sssttupidity, cut the child down." | #478 |
| M478f | The broodmother begged Sri Lakshmi to restore her daughter to life. | *Rising Fortunes, Rising Spirits* · 4.0 · main | "the Qalyana broodmother cried out for her daughter to be ressstored to life. She bessseeched Sri Lakshmi's intercession." | #478 |
| M478g | The goddess appeared, if only for an instant. | *Rising Fortunes, Rising Spirits* · 4.0 · main | "That she did, if only for an inssstant." | #478 |
| M478h | Alphinaud says a summoned primal cannot be ignored. | *Rising Fortunes, Rising Spirits* · 4.0 · main | "if a primal has indeed been summoned, we can scarce afford to ignore it." | #478 |
| M478i | The Vira who fought with the Resistance know of you. | *Rising Fortunes, Rising Spirits* · 4.0 · main | "All who have fought with the Resissstance have heard tell of the warrior." | #478 |
| M479a | Alphinaud would learn Sri Lakshmi's nature from her summoners before challenging her. | *The Lure of the Dream* · 4.0 · main | "Ere we decide to challenge Sri Lakshmi, it would seem wise to learn more of her nature from those who summoned her unto this world." | #479 |
| M479b | The Vira were created in Sri Lakshmi's image. | *The Lure of the Dream* · 4.0 · main | "In the beginning was Sri Lakshmi. We were created in Her image─daughters blesssed with Her beauty." | #479 |
| M479c | To the Vira, Sri Lakshmi embodies freedom. | *The Lure of the Dream* · 4.0 · main | "To the Vira, Sri Lakshmi embodies freedom." | #479 |
| M479d | To the Qalyana she embodies beauty, and they turned inward and ignored the Vira's plight. | *The Lure of the Dream* · 4.0 · main | "To the Qalyana, however, She embodies transsscendental beauty, which they ssstrive to create through their craft. … The imperials do not interfere with thisss holy work, and ssso the Qalyana were content to turn inward and ignore our plight." | #479 |
| M479e | Vira who refuse the pilgrimage will be made to take part. | *The Lure of the Dream* · 4.0 · main | "Those who do not wish to partake of their primal's bliss will be made to do so." | #479 |
| M479f | Sarisha's sister Vajra will guide you to Djanan Qhat. | *The Lure of the Dream* · 4.0 · main | "My sssister Vajra will guide you to Djanan Qhat...and I shall pray for your successs." | #479 |
| M479g | Sarisha asks you to help her Qalyana kin see reason. | *The Lure of the Dream* · 4.0 · main | "Though our beliefsss are not the sssame, we are ssstill kin to the Qalyana. Will you help them to sssee reason?" | #479 |
| M480a | The broodmother saw her daughter killed by Fordola and her Skulls. | *The Lady of Bliss* · 4.0 · main | "Upon witnessing her daughter's death at the hands of [[Fordola]] and her Skulls, the [[Qalyana]] broodmother cried out to the heavens in despair..." | #480 |
| M480b | Sri Lakshmi is the patron deity of the Ananta. | *The Lady of Bliss* · 4.0 · main | "[[Sri Lakshmi]], patron deity of the [[Ananta]], came forth from the aether, and the imperials were put to flight." | #480 |
| M480c | Sri Lakshmi remade the daughter's body but not her soul. | *The Lady of Bliss* · 4.0 · main | "Flesh sssundered hath been made whole. … Only the vesssel may be remade. Not so the flame which flickered within." | #480 |
| M480d | Sri Lakshmi orders you to leave her lands and her dreamers. | *The Lady of Bliss* · 4.0 · main | "Leave these lands and my dreamers be. I will not warn you a second time." | #480 |
| M480e | Defeated, the Lady of Bliss departs, and her dreamers' succor with her. | *The Lady of Bliss* · 4.0 · main | "With a flash of light, the Lady of Bliss departs, and with her whatever succor her dreamers may have enjoyed." | #480 |
| M480f | Shanti blames you for slaying the goddess she calls her people's hope. | *The Lady of Bliss* · 4.0 · main | "You! You ssslew our Missstresss! She was our hope, our sssalvation!" | #480 |
| M480g | Zenos tells Fordola she provoked the Ananta into summoning an eikon. | *The Lady of Bliss* · 4.0 · main | "You misjudged the Ananta and spurred them into summoning an eikon." | #480 |
| M480h | Zenos offers Fordola a chance to transcend her mortal limits. | *The Lady of Bliss* · 4.0 · main | "Then I shall give you a chance. A chance to transcend your mortal limits." | #480 |
| M480i | Fordola reports to Zenos as commander of the Crania Lupi. | *The Lady of Bliss* · 4.0 · main | "commander of the Crania Lupi, reporting as ordered." | #480 |
| M480j | Shanti asks why her Anamika still slumbers. | *The Lady of Bliss* · 4.0 · main | "Why, Missstresss...Why does my Anamika ssslumber ssstill?" | #480 |
| M481a | Vajra and her people need no longer live in fear. | *The Silence of the Gods* · 4.0 · main | "Thanks to you, Vajra and her people need no longer live in fear." | #481 |
| M481b | Sri Lakshmi no longer holds the Qalyana in her thrall. | *The Silence of the Gods* · 4.0 · main | "The Lady of Bliss no longer holds our Qalyana sssisters in Her thrall. For thisss we thank you." | #481 |
| M481c | Alisaie fears desperation will drive people to beseech the gods again. | *The Silence of the Gods* · 4.0 · main | "However, I fear this is only the beginning, for there is no cure for that which drives the desperate to beseech the gods for salvation." | #481 |
| M481d | The Qalyana's crystals must stay limited and the aether watched. | *The Silence of the Gods* · 4.0 · main | "In any event, the Qalyana's supply of crystals must remain limited for the foreseeable future, and ambient aether levels closely observed." | #481 |
| M481e | The Skulls and the rest of the garrison have withdrawn into the Peaks. | *The Silence of the Gods* · 4.0 · main | "It seem as though the Skulls and the remainder of the garrison have fully withdrawn into the Peaks." | #481 |
| M481f | Pipin's father has decided to advance east. | *The Silence of the Gods* · 4.0 · main | "Father has decided that it is time to advance east." | #481 |
| M481g | Lyse's mother died when she was little, so her sister did most of the cooking. | *The Silence of the Gods* · 4.0 · main | "Our mother died when I was little, so she did most of the cooking..." | #481 |
| M481h | Lyse grieves that they did not have to die. | *The Silence of the Gods* · 4.0 · main | "But they didn't have to die..." | #481 |
| M482a | Ala Ghiri has been taken and is being fortified as the new base in the Peaks. | *The First of Many* · 4.0 · main | "You'll be happy to hear we've taken Ala Ghiri, and have already begun fortifying it as our new base of operations in the Peaks." | #482 |
| M482b | The imperials were beaten back and a village liberated. | *The First of Many* · 4.0 · main | "But we beat the imperials back, just like that, and we liberated a village." | #482 |
| M482c | Beyond the last line of defenses lie the Lochs and the city. | *The First of Many* · 4.0 · main | "Beyond this last line of defenses lay the Lochs and the city. Breaching it would be no small feat." | #482 |
| M482d | The imperials fled and Ala Ghiri was taken without a fight. | *The First of Many* · 4.0 · main | "The imperials fled at our coming, and we took the village without a fight." | #482 |
| M482e | The next strategy is to seize Specula Imperatoris. | *The First of Many* · 4.0 · main | "We were about to discuss our strategy to seize Specula Imperatoris." | #482 |
| M482f | Specula Imperatoris sits on a cliff's edge and cannot be attacked from the north. | *The First of Many* · 4.0 · main | "Sits right on the edge of a cliff, so attacking from the north is a fool's errand." | #482 |
| M483a | The path to Ala Mhigo runs through Specula Imperatoris and Castrum Abania. | *Strong and Unified* · 4.0 · main | "The path to Ala Mhigo is through the east—through Specula Imperatoris and Castrum Abania." | #483 |
| M483b | Castrum Abania holds a massive cannon of unparalleled range. | *Strong and Unified* · 4.0 · main | "the latter is home to a massive cannon with unparalleled range and destructive power." | #483 |
| M483c | Alphinaud cannot explain why Zenos gave up Ala Ghiri without a shot. | *Strong and Unified* · 4.0 · main | "For Zenos to surrender a fortified settlement of strategic importance without so much as firing a shot is...inexplicable." | #483 |
| M483d | The main host will assault Specula Imperatoris, with the Scions in reserve at Ala Ghiri. | *Strong and Unified* · 4.0 · main | "The main host will commence a direct assault on Specula Imperatoris. … I would hold the Scions in reserve at Ala Ghiri to deal with any imperial forces that may be dispatched in response." | #483 |
| M483e | Aulus says Garleans lack the traits to manipulate aether. | *Strong and Unified* · 4.0 · main | "we lack the hereditary traits required for the reliable manipulation of aether—hence our unique inability to wield magicks." | #483 |
| M483f | Aulus argued that changing the Garlean genome could let subjects wield magicks. | *Strong and Unified* · 4.0 · main | "Through modification of the Garlean genome, I argued that it might be possible to enhance a subject's ability to manipulate aether, effectively empowering them to wield magicks." | #483 |
| M483g | Only Zenos supported Aulus's research. | *Strong and Unified* · 4.0 · main | "Only Lord Zenos, with commendable foresight and wisdom, deigned to support my research." | #483 |
| M483h | Fordola learns she has been given the treatment, though she is not Garlean. | *Strong and Unified* · 4.0 · main | "...You gave me this treatment? But I'm not Garlean." | #483 |
| M483i | Aulus would test Fordola in the field at Ala Ghiri. | *Strong and Unified* · 4.0 · main | "This could provide and ideal opportunity to test her performance in the field." | #483 |
| M483j | An unconscious Krile is sent into a capsule while Thancred watches from afar. | *Strong and Unified* · 4.0 · main | "An unconscious Krile is sent into a capsule. Fordola lies on a surgical bed." | #483 |
| M483k | Thancred observes from a distance. | *Strong and Unified* · 4.0 · main | "Thancred observes from a distance." | #483 |
| M483l | Ala Ghiri was taken without losing a single man. | *Strong and Unified* · 4.0 · main | "Ala Ghiri is ours, and for a mercy we took it without losing a single man." | #483 |
| M484a | Alisaie sent word to Arenvald's squadron, which is searching nearby. | *Hells Open* · 4.0 · main | "I sent word to Arenvald's squadron. They are already searching other nearby areas" | #484 |
| M484b | The pair that spied on the Scions in the Fringes has followed them. | *Hells Open* · 4.0 · main | "That was Arenvald. The pair that was spying on us in the Fringes has followed us here..." | #484 |
| M484c | Conrad believes he can convince the remaining Skulls to lay down their arms. | *Hells Open* · 4.0 · main | "The Skulls have offered fierce resistance thus far, but Conrad seems to believe he can convince them to lay down their arms..." | #484 |
| M484d | Zenos sent no reinforcements and would have the main cannon destroy the installation. | *Hells Open* · 4.0 · main | "Lord Zenos said no reinforcements. He would have us use the main cannon to destroy the installation." | #484 |
| M484e | Fordola names her soldiers who will die, for their people's future. | *Hells Open* · 4.0 · main | "Ansfrid, Hrudolf, Emelin...<br>For us. For our people. For our future." | #484 |
| M484f | Fordola orders the cannon to fire on the main tower of Specula Imperatoris. | *Hells Open* · 4.0 · main | "Specula Imperatoris, main tower. Fire when ready." | #484 |
| M484g | Raubahn orders a full retreat, appalled that the imperials fired on their own. | *Hells Open* · 4.0 · main | "Those treacherous bastards...Their people were still fighting!<br>Full retreat, now! Relay the orders!" | #484 |
| M485a | Castrum Abania's cannon destroyed the main tower of Specula Imperatoris, killing Allied and imperial troops. | *Heavens Weep* · 4.0 · main | "when the massive cannon at Castrum Abania was used to destroy the main tower of Specula Imperatoris, killing countless Allied and imperial troops" | #485 |
| M485b | Dying, Conrad asks Lyse to succeed him as leader of the Resistance, and she accepts. | *Heavens Weep* · 4.0 · main | "He uses his final moments to implore Lyse to succeed him as leader of the Ala Mhigan Resistance, and she tearfully accepts, promising to lead their countrymen to freedom." | #485 |
| M485c | Conrad's wounds are mortal. | *Heavens Weep* · 4.0 · main | "Though the others escaped with only minor injuries, the grizzled commander's are unquestionably mortal." | #485 |
| M485d | Conrad chose Lyse for herself, not because she is Curtis's daughter or Yda's sister. | *Heavens Weep* · 4.0 · main | "Not because you're Curtis's daughter, or Yda's sister, but because...because you're you." | #485 |
| M485e | Raubahn is shocked that the imperials would kill their own men to kill the Alliance's. | *Heavens Weep* · 4.0 · main | "I didn't think they had it in them. To kill their own men just to kill us!" | #485 |
| M485f | Conrad had just convinced the last Skulls to surrender when the tower was struck. | *Heavens Weep* · 4.0 · main | "Conrad had just convinced the last few Skulls to surrender, when...when we heard the bang" | #485 |
| M485g | Estinien calls on Nidhogg and attacks the imperial facility. | *Heavens Weep* · 4.0 · main | "Come, Nidhogg. We are needed. … Estinien attacks the facility." | #485 |
| M485h | Alphinaud swears there will be a reckoning. | *Heavens Weep* · 4.0 · main | "Gods as my witness, there will be a reckoning!" | #485 |
| M485i | Alisaie, desperate to find her brother, runs into the ruins. | *Heavens Weep* · 4.0 · main | "Desperate to find her brother, she pushes through the gates before you can even think to stop her." | #485 |
| M486a | The imperials will fire on their own, so staying is dangerous. | *The Road Home* · 4.0 · main | "The imperials have demonstrated a willingness to fire on their own. So long as we remain here, we are in danger." | #486 |
| M486b | You are to find Alisaie and return to Ala Ghiri. | *The Road Home* · 4.0 · main | "Find my sister and return to Ala Ghiri." | #486 |
| M486c | A wounded imperial wishes he were home in Ilsabard, not under fire from his own side. | *The Road Home* · 4.0 · main | "To hells with this. I should be back home in Ilsabard, instead of taking fire from my so-called comrades." | #486 |
| M486d | Alphinaud sets aside vows of vengeance. | *The Road Home* · 4.0 · main | "Forgive me. Now is not the time for vows of vengeance." | #486 |
| M486e | Alphinaud says they must press forward and not despair. | *The Road Home* · 4.0 · main | "For now, however, we must carry on. We must press forward. We must not give in to despair..." | #486 |
| M487a | All Alliance and Resistance units have withdrawn from Specula Imperatoris. | *For the Living and the Dead* · 4.0 · main | "All Alliance and Resistance units have now withdrawn from Specula Imperatoris." | #487 |
| M487b | Raubahn calls it a grievous defeat but will not renounce the cause. | *For the Living and the Dead* · 4.0 · main | "We have suffered a grievous defeat. But we can not and will not renounce this cause." | #487 |
| M487c | Pipin notes the imperials stopped firing when they could have destroyed the Alliance's forces. | *For the Living and the Dead* · 4.0 · main | "Recall that the imperials could have obliterated our forces had they kept firing, but neglected to do so." | #487 |
| M487d | Lyse will see for herself whether the cannon still works. | *For the Living and the Dead* · 4.0 · main | "Then I'll just have to go and see for myself if the cannon is still operational." | #487 |
| M487e | M'naago reminds Lyse that Conrad put her in charge of the Resistance. | *For the Living and the Dead* · 4.0 · main | "Conrad put you in charge of the Resistance! We can't risk losing another commander so soon!" | #487 |
| M487f | Lyse says she is not Conrad and never will be. | *For the Living and the Dead* · 4.0 · main | "It may not be the decision Conrad would've made...but I'm not Conrad. I never will be." | #487 |
| M487g | Few of the Resistance are left, and many are wounded. | *For the Living and the Dead* · 4.0 · main | "There's not a lot of us left, and of the ones that are, many are still wounded." | #487 |
| M487h | Raubahn settles that you go with Lyse. | *For the Living and the Dead* · 4.0 · main | "Then it is settled. We will await your return." | #487 |
| M488a | Lyse doesn't want to be talked out of it. | *Above the Churning Waters* · 4.0 · main | "If you don't mind, I'd rather not wait around and have people try to talk me out of it." | #488 |
| M488b | Nyunkrepf's Hope is the ark one man built against the flood of the Sixth Umbral Era. | *Above the Churning Waters* · 4.0 · main | "The ark that one man made to save countless others from the flood during the Sixth Umbral Era." | #488 |
| M488c | Lyse says you helped those in need long before anyone called you a hero. | *Above the Churning Waters* · 4.0 · main | "Long before anyone started calling you a hero, you were there for those in need." | #488 |
| M488d | Lyse will never lead like Conrad, Hien or her father. | *Above the Churning Waters* · 4.0 · main | "I'll never be a leader like Conrad. Or Hien. Or my father..." | #488 |
| M488e | The cannon won't trouble them soon. | *Above the Churning Waters* · 4.0 · main | "it won't be giving us any trouble in the immediate future." | #488 |
| M488f | Lyse wants to take Castrum Abania and bring those who ordered the shot to justice. | *Above the Churning Waters* · 4.0 · main | "To take Castrum Abania and bring the bastards who gave the order to fire to justice!" | #488 |
| M488g | They must strike before the imperials fix the cannon. | *Above the Churning Waters* · 4.0 · main | "We need to convince the others to strike now, before the imperials have time to fix their cannon." | #488 |
| M489a | The imperials didn't fire again because they couldn't. | *The Path Forward* · 4.0 · main | "they didn't fire again because they couldn't." | #489 |
| M489b | Pipin's people have built a network of informants across Gyr Abania, Radiata included. | *The Path Forward* · 4.0 · main | "men and women under my command have labored long to cultivate a network of informants throughout Gyr Abania—including Radiata." | #489 |
| M489c | Many in Radiata work menial jobs at Castrum Abania. | *The Path Forward* · 4.0 · main | "Radiata is home to many who hold menial positions at Castrum Abania and other nearby installations." | #489 |
| M489d | Lyse insists the Resistance will handle the operation. | *The Path Forward* · 4.0 · main | "The Resistance will handle this. You may have recruited these informants, but they'll be just as happy working with fellow Ala Mhigans." | #489 |
| M489e | Raubahn readies the march on Castrum Abania. | *The Path Forward* · 4.0 · main | "We will make ready to march on Castrum Abania. Go, and may Rhalgr watch over and keep you." | #489 |
| M489f | Alphinaud thinks Krile is likely held at Castrum Abania, the largest facility in the Peaks. | *The Path Forward* · 4.0 · main | "Castrum Abania is the largest facility in the Peaks, you see, and so the likelihood that Krile is being held captive there is quite high..." | #489 |
| M490a | In Radiata you will meet a contact recruited by Marshal Tarupin's agents. | *With Tired Hands We Toil* · 4.0 · main | "we are to travel to Radiata, where we will meet with a contact recruited by Marshal Tarupin's agents." | #490 |
| M490b | Radiata's people fared better than most Ala Mhigans on the imperials' scraps. | *With Tired Hands We Toil* · 4.0 · main | "They got a better deal than most Ala Mhigans—feeding off what scraps the imperials gave 'em." | #490 |
| M490c | The contact is a Roegadyn woman who will ask a question. | *With Tired Hands We Toil* · 4.0 · main | "Our contact is a Roegadyn woman. She will ask us a question." | #490 |
| M490d | The smoke above Castrum Abania is gone. | *With Tired Hands We Toil* · 4.0 · main | "I don't see smoke above Castrum Abania anymore. Do you think they fixed the cannon already...?" | #490 |

| M491b | The cannon's ceruleum pipeline was destroyed by an unidentified male insurgent. | *Where Courage Endures* · 4.0 · main | "The ceruleum pipeline fueling the cannon was destroyed by an unidentified male insurgent, they said." | #491 |
| M491c | The Ala Mhigan workers have been sent home indefinitely. | *Where Courage Endures* · 4.0 · main | "To make matters worse, the Ala Mhigan help have been sent home indefinitely." | #491 |
| M491d | Lyse asks Stark's help to sneak into Castrum Abania. | *Where Courage Endures* · 4.0 · main | "We need to sneak inside Castrum Abania to ensure the cannon is no longer a threat to our troops. Can you help us?" | #491 |

| M492a | The Alliance will not march until the cannon can no longer be turned on them. | *The Price of Freedom* · 4.0 · main | "they will not march upon [[Castrum Abania]] until they can be certain the fortress's main cannon will not be brought to bear against them." | #492 |
| M492b | A single unknown assailant disabled the cannon. | *The Price of Freedom* · 4.0 · main | "Though the weapon was apparently disabled by a single [[Estinien|unknown assailant]], it is only a matter of time before it is restored to operational status." | #492 |
| M492c | The castrum's experiments seek to augment the Garleans' weakness. | *The Price of Freedom* · 4.0 · main | "It seems they seek to augment their people's weakness." | #492 |
| M492d | Fordola admits she gave the order to fire. | *The Price of Freedom* · 4.0 · main | "Aye, I gave the order to fire." | #492 |
| M492e | Fordola knew exactly what she was doing. | *The Price of Freedom* · 4.0 · main | "I gave the order that led to their deaths...and I knew exactly what I was doing." | #492 |
| M492f | Fordola cuts down Alisaie and escapes through the window. | *The Price of Freedom* · 4.0 · main | "After effortlessly evading her attacks and cutting down Alisaie with a single stroke, Fordola dives through the window and makes her escape" | #492 |
| M492g | Allied forces seize Castrum Abania. | *The Price of Freedom* · 4.0 · main | "With nothing to fear from the fortress's dormant cannon, Allied forces march on Castrum Abania and seize control of it in short order." | #492 |

| M493b | Alisaie will make a full recovery. | *Raubahn's Invitation* · 4.0 · main | "Regrettable, but I am pleased to hear that she will make a full recovery." | #493 |
| M493c | Raubahn accepts Zenos's invitation to the royal hunt. | *Raubahn's Invitation* · 4.0 · main | "then this bull would fain accept!" | #493 |
| M493d | Pipin's men search the castrum's archives for Krile. | *Raubahn's Invitation* · 4.0 · main | "Pipin has his best men scouring the castrum's archives as we speak." | #493 |
| M493e | The army needs time to occupy the castrum before advancing into the Lochs. | *Raubahn's Invitation* · 4.0 · main | "It will take time for us to fully occupy the castrum and prepare to advance into the Lochs." | #493 |
| M493f | Lyse feels the weight of what remains, Krile among it. | *Raubahn's Invitation* · 4.0 · main | "we're almost to the end, but we've still got to save Krile and do so much more..." | #493 |
| M494a | Raubahn reveals Coldhearth as his childhood home. | *Liberty or Death* · 4.0 · main | "which he reveals to be his childhood home." | #494 |
| M494b | Raubahn was wounded and sent home to Coldhearth to recover. | *Liberty or Death* · 4.0 · main | "I was wounded and sent home to recover. Back here..." | #494 |
| M494c | Curtis rallies the crowd to the creed of liberty or death. | *Liberty or Death* · 4.0 · main | "Liberty or death. Liberty or death! If this be your creed, then raise up your hands!" | #494 |
| M494d | Curtis teaches that liberty is the chance to build something better. | *Liberty or Death* · 4.0 · main | "Liberty is the chance to build something better." | #494 |
| M494e | Lyse insists the freedom they win be for every Ala Mhigan. | *Liberty or Death* · 4.0 · main | "But the freedom we win must be for every Ala Mhigan—even ones like Fordola, though they may hate us, and the Ananta, though they may fear us." | #494 |
| M494f | Youths who grew up in a castrum's shadow won't change for a few victories. | *Liberty or Death* · 4.0 · main | "They've been living in the shadow of a castrum their whole lives. A few victories won't change that." | #494 |
| M494g | Lyse vows to build a new home together. | *Liberty or Death* · 4.0 · main | "We're going to build a new home for ourselves. Together." | #494 |

| M494j | The Alliance agent explains that youths who know only the occupation distrust the Resistance. | *Liberty or Death* · 4.0 · main | "agent explains that many youths who have only ever known the occupation are unwilling to place their faith in the Resistance" | #494 |

| M495c | Yda wore the outfit whenever she could. | *The Lady in Red* · 4.0 · main | "Yda wore it whenever she could..." | #495 |
| M495d | Lyse thanks you as the kind of friend who will knock sense into her. | *The Lady in Red* · 4.0 · main | "You're the best kind of friend—the kind that's not afraid to knock some sense into you." | #495 |

| M496c | Reinforcements from all four Alliance nations have joined the main host in a new base in the Lochs. | *Upon the Great Loch's Shore* · 4.0 · main | "reinforcements from all four Alliance nations have arrived and joined the main host, which has established a new base of operations within the Lochs." | #496 |

| M496g | The Scions are held in reserve until their captured comrade is found. | *Upon the Great Loch's Shore* · 4.0 · main | "As for the Scions, you will be held in reserve as an irregular unit until we have more intelligence on your captured comrade." | #496 |
| M496h | Then the army will surround the palace and hunt down Zenos. | *Upon the Great Loch's Shore* · 4.0 · main | "Once both objectives are complete, we press on into the city, surround the palace, and hunt down Zenos." | #496 |
| M496i | Alphinaud says the stage is set. | *Upon the Great Loch's Shore* · 4.0 · main | "The stage is set, and the players assembled." | #496 |

| M497c | Wiscar reckons there is a way into the Ala Mhigan Quarter by Loch Seld. | *The Key to Victory* · 4.0 · main | "I reckon we can get inside the Ala Mhigan Quarter by way of Loch Seld." | #497 |

| M498b | Urianger made an aetheric siphon to strip Fordola of her preternatural prescience. | *The Resonant* · 4.0 · main | "he was inspired to fashion a new and improved aetheric siphon, which he believes will enable you to deprive the Skulls commander of her preternatural prescience" | #498 |
| M498c | You find Krile alive but weak, and take Fordola prisoner. | *The Resonant* · 4.0 · main | "To your relief, you find Krile alive, albeit weak from her ordeal, and together you make ready to leave, with Fordola as your prisoner." | #498 |
| M498d | Lyse declares that Fordola will live to see a free Ala Mhigo. | *The Resonant* · 4.0 · main | "declaring that Fordola will live long enough to see a free [[Ala Mhigo]]." | #498 |
