| M500e | Hakuro speaks of Gosetsu perishing in the keep's collapse. | *The Measure of His Reach* · 4.0 · main | "But for Lord Gosetsu to perish in the keep's collapse" | #500 |
| M500f | Hakuro names himself the White and challenges you. | *The Measure of His Reach* · 4.0 · main | "I am the White! I am Hakuro! And I will be your opponent! Have at you!" | #500 |
| M500g | Hakuro demands the tale be proved on the battlefield. | *The Measure of His Reach* · 4.0 · main | "Words are air! You will prove the truth of your tale on the battlefield, or die in dishonor!" | #500 |
| M500h | Hakuro vows to turn on the imperials when the gates are breached. | *The Measure of His Reach* · 4.0 · main | "When you breach the gates, we shall turn on the imperials as one." | #500 |
| M500i | Hien declares that Doma has come to pay her debts. | *The Measure of His Reach* · 4.0 · main | "Doma is come to pay her debts!" | #500 |
| M500j | The way through is clear. | *The Measure of His Reach* · 4.0 · main | "We're through! The way is clear!" | #500 |
| M501a | Allies from the Far East led by Lord Hien swoop in to the rescue at the gate. | *Stormblood (Quest)* · 4.0 · main | "allies, led by Lord [[Hien]], swoop in to the rescue" | 4.0 |
| M501b | The gate gives way under the renewed assault. | *Stormblood (Quest)* · 4.0 · main | "The gate buckles under the renewed assault, and soon gives way." | 4.0 |
| M501c | Shinryu, Ilberd's god of vengeance, is at the viceroy's mercy. | *Stormblood (Quest)* · 4.0 · main | "[[Shinryu]], [[Ilberd]]'s draconic god of vengeance, at the mercy of the viceroy." | 4.0 |
| M501d | Zenos uses his power as a Resonant to merge with the primal and bind it. | *Stormblood (Quest)* · 4.0 · main | "uses his power as a [[Resonant]] to merge with the primal, binding it to his will." | 4.0 |
| M501e | Zenos, legatus of the XIIth Legion and viceroy of Ala Mhigo, has fallen. | *Stormblood (Quest)* · 4.0 · main | "Legatus of the XIIth Legion, imperial viceroy of Ala Mhigo, has fallen." | 4.0 |
| M501f | Zenos takes his own life, at peace, before Lyse can stop him. | *Stormblood (Quest)* · 4.0 · main | "He brings the blade to his neck and smiles the smile of a man at peace, and before Lyse can intervene, the deed is done." | 4.0 |
| M501g | The people of Ala Mhigo sing as word of the viceroy's death spreads. | *Stormblood (Quest)* · 4.0 · main | "as the horns spread word of the viceroy's death, the people of Ala Mhigo raise their voices in song" | 4.0 |
| M501h | Lyse declares she will formally leave the Scions. | *Stormblood (Quest)* · 4.0 · main | "Lyse declares her intention to formally leave the Scions of the Seventh Dawn." | 4.0 |
| M501i | Lyse cannot serve her homeland and the order both. | *Stormblood (Quest)* · 4.0 · main | "she believes she cannot serve her homeland and the order both." | 4.0 |
| M501j | Y'shtola says that, Scion or no, they are comrades. | *Stormblood (Quest)* · 4.0 · main | "Scion or no, we are comrades. Now and ever after." | 4.0 |
| M501k | The rest of the XIIth surrendered, and the twenty-year occupation is ended. | *Stormblood (Quest)* · 4.0 · main | "With their commander fallen, the remainder of the XIIth chose to surrender to our forces. After twenty long years it's official: the occupation is ended." | 4.0 |
| M501l | The people have no consensus on what government they want. | *Stormblood (Quest)* · 4.0 · main | "there is no clear consensus on what sort of government they would prefer instead." | 4.0 |
| M501m | Hien says Zenos killed his father. | *Stormblood (Quest)* · 4.0 · main | "Zenos was a monster and a tyrant—the man who killed my father and countless other of my countrymen." | 4.0 |
| M501n | Hien urges Lyse to raise a cheer for all who fought for freedom. | *Stormblood (Quest)* · 4.0 · main | "Stand tall, Lyse! Now is the time to raise a cheer for all who fought for freedom!" | 4.0 |
| M501o | Estinien destroys the eyes of Nidhogg. | *Stormblood (Quest)* · 4.0 · main | "Estinien destroys the eyes of Nidhogg. … There. There ends your hateful legacy." | 4.0 |
| M501p | Elidibus offers condolences: a father should not outlive his son. | *Stormblood (Quest)* · 4.0 · main | "My condolences for your loss. A father should not outlive his son." | 4.0 |
| M501q | Elidibus calls Zenos Varis's son and heir, and an irreplaceable test subject. | *Stormblood (Quest)* · 4.0 · main | "He was your son and heir...and an irreplaceable test subject." | 4.0 |
| M501r | Varis says monsters are not made for thrones. | *Stormblood (Quest)* · 4.0 · main | "I know what he was. Monsters are not made for thrones." | 4.0 |
| M501s | Elidibus says his desires and the Emperor's are one: preservation and restoration. | *Stormblood (Quest)* · 4.0 · main | "Our desires are one and the same. Preservation. Restoration." | 4.0 |
| M501t | Gosetsu is alive on a desert island. | *Stormblood (Quest)* · 4.0 · main | "Gosetsu catches a fish on a desert island." | 4.0 |
| M501u | An unconscious Yotsuyu lies by a campfire. | *Stormblood (Quest)* · 4.0 · main | "An unconscious Yotsuyu is by a campfire." | 4.0 |
| M501v | Gosetsu laments that fortune tests him with this wretched soul. | *Stormblood (Quest)* · 4.0 · main | "O capricious fortune! How you test me with this wretched soul..." | 4.0 |
| M501w | At the Yawn, Cid finds the work of Shinryu or Omega and resolves to clean it up. | *Stormblood (Quest)* · 4.0 · main | "At the Yawn in the Fringes … This is indeed the work of either Shinryu or Omega. … This is a mess of our own making, so it falls to us to clean it up." | 4.0 |
| M501x | Nero joins the Ironworks in its uniform, eager for his first day. | *Stormblood (Quest)* · 4.0 · main | "Nero arrives in a red Ironworks uniform. … eager to begin his first day as a loyal member of the Ironworks team!" | 4.0 |
| M501y | Raubahn leaves Pipin the command of the Flames to join the charge. | *Stormblood (Quest)* · 4.0 · main | "The command of the Flames, I leave in your capable hands, Pipin." | 4.0 |
| M501z | Varis rejects false gods summoned by desperate savages. | *Stormblood (Quest)* · 4.0 · main | "Nor are stars made to sustain false gods who sup on their very essence." | 4.0 |
| M502a | Lyse says Doma had Lord Hien ready to rule, but Ala Mhigo has no one like him. | *Arenvald's Adventure* · 4.1 · main | "The Domans had Lord Hien ready and willing to take the throne, but we've got no one like that here." | #502 |
| M502b | Theodoric, Ala Mhigo's last ruler, was called the mad king for good reason. | *Arenvald's Adventure* · 4.1 · main | "Theodoric was our last ruler—and he wasn't called the mad king for nothing." | #502 |
| M502c | For now Ala Mhigo must lean on Raubahn and the Alliance for support. | *Arenvald's Adventure* · 4.1 · main | "For the time being, we're just going to have to keep leaning on General Aldynn and the Alliance for support" | #502 |
| M502d | Arenvald reminds you that you were adventurers before you were Scions. | *Arenvald's Adventure* · 4.1 · main | "we were "adventurers" before we were Scions, were we not?" | #502 |
| M502e | Theodoric had everyone with a claim to the throne executed, his own family included. | *Arenvald's Adventure* · 4.1 · main | "And so he ordered that every soul with a claim to the throne be executed—including his own family." | #502 |
| M502f | Theodoric is said to have hoarded the wealth of the kinsmen he executed. | *Arenvald's Adventure* · 4.1 · main | "[[King Theodoric]] is reputed to have hoarded the personal wealth of his executed kinsmen" | #502 |
| M502g | Soldiers who searched the palace after the liberation found no treasure. | *Arenvald's Adventure* · 4.1 · main | "But even after scouring every room from dawn till dusk, they uncovered not a single coin." | #502 |
| M502h | Arenvald is set on you and Alphinaud finding the mad king's trove. | *Arenvald's Adventure* · 4.1 · main | "Needless to say, Arenvald is determined that you should be the ones to uncover the mad king's trove." | #502 |
| M503b | Old Ernold was the king's head scribe. | *The Darkness Below* · 4.1 · main | "ask old Ernold—he was the king's head scribe." | #503 |
| M503c | The king had his court thaumaturge curse the remaining royal family. | *The Darkness Below* · 4.1 · main | "His Majesty ordered the court thaumaturge to unleash a vile curse upon the remaining members of the royal family" | #503 |
| M503d | The cursed monstrosities were cast into the darkness below the palace. | *The Darkness Below* · 4.1 · main | "The resulting...monstrosities were then cast into the darkness below the palace." | #503 |
| M503e | Arenvald's father was a Garlean soldier. | *The Darkness Below* · 4.1 · main | "My father was in the imperial army, a man of Garlean blood." | #503 |
| M503f | Arenvald's war paint covers a scar. | *The Darkness Below* · 4.1 · main | "War paint serves to cover the scar." | #503 |
| M503g | Alphinaud thinks Ernold's tale may show where the trove is hidden. | *The Darkness Below* · 4.1 · main | "Ernold's anecdote may have very well told us where the trove is hidden." | #503 |
| M504a | Alphinaud believes the darkness below the palace holds the drowned remnants of ancient Skalla. | *The Mad King's Trove* · 4.1 · main | "may contain the submerged remnants of the ancient city of [[The Drowned City of Skalla|Skalla]]" | #504 |
| M504b | Theodoric had his kin cursed and cast down to guard his treasures. | *The Mad King's Trove* · 4.1 · main | "had his kinsmen cursed and cast into the depths not only to punish them for their imagined crimes, but to have them serve as guardians over his treasures." | #504 |
| M504c | What is left of Theodoric's kin still haunts the drowned city. | *The Mad King's Trove* · 4.1 · main | "'Twould appear this place is yet inhabited by King Theodoric's kin—or what is left of them." | #504 |
| M504d | You find the mad king's trove. | *The Mad King's Trove* · 4.1 · main | "As you gaze upon the riches of the mad king's trove" | #504 |
| M504e | Alphinaud grudgingly declares you the winner of the race to the treasure. | *The Mad King's Trove* · 4.1 · main | "With grudging admiration, Alphinaud declares you the victor of the impromptu race to the treasure." | #504 |
| M505a | Lyse had thought the mad king's trove a myth. | *The Butcher's Blood* · 4.1 · main | "You're joking...The mad king's trove? I thought it was a myth!" | #505 |
| M505b | A mob outside the Resistance's headquarters demands Fordola, the Butcher. | *The Butcher's Blood* · 4.1 · main | "We demand vengeance! Bring her out! Today we butcher the Butcher!" | #505 |
| M505c | Raubahn tells the mob the prisoners must have a fair trial before all Ala Mhigo. | *The Butcher's Blood* · 4.1 · main | "it is our responsibility to give these prisoners a fair trial, that they might answer to all of Ala Mhigo!" | #505 |
| M505d | In Fordola's past, a stone thrown by Ala Mhigans killed her father. | *The Butcher's Blood* · 4.1 · main | "A large stone hits Fordola's father and kills him." | #505 |
| M505e | Fordola joined the legion to make her people respect her. | *The Butcher's Blood* · 4.1 · main | "So I'll play the part. I'll join the legion and I'll make them respect me." | #505 |
| M505f | Fordola herself asked to be put to death. | *The Butcher's Blood* · 4.1 · main | "She herself asked to be put to death." | #505 |
| M505g | Fordola's Echo shows her your own past. | *The Butcher's Blood* · 4.1 · main | "Fordola has an Echo flashback and sees glimpses of the player's experiences with Gaius" | #505 |
| M505h | Lyse tells Fordola to think how she wants to spend the time she has. | *The Butcher's Blood* · 4.1 · main | "You still have time, Fordola. Think about how you want to spend it." | #505 |
| M506b | The procedure fed one candidate aether siphoned from many "supply subjects". | *Echoes of an Echo* · 4.1 · main | "The enhancement procedure entailed infusing a single candidate with aether siphoned from a multitude of "supply subjects."" | #506 |
| M506c | Krile was held in the Resonatorium's master device. | *Echoes of an Echo* · 4.1 · main | "The device in which Krile was held seems even more ominous now you know its purpose." | #506 |
| M506d | The procedure likely gave Fordola the same acute sensitivity. | *Echoes of an Echo* · 4.1 · main | "it seems probable that the procedure engendered the same acute sensitivity in Fordola." | #506 |
| M506e | Raubahn will not grant Fordola's wish to die, not yet. | *Echoes of an Echo* · 4.1 · main | "She'll not be getting her wish. Not yet." | #506 |
| M506f | Pipin brings word that the sultana desires an audience with you. | *Echoes of an Echo* · 4.1 · main | "Her Grace desires an audience with you." | #506 |
| M506g | Arenvald's own visions are rare, but Fordola seems to have them constantly. | *Echoes of an Echo* · 4.1 · main | "From the way Fordola kept wincing, though, I get the feeling she's having them almost all the time." | #506 |
| M506h | The imperials were trying to recreate Krile's Echo. | *Echoes of an Echo* · 4.1 · main | "they were trying to recreate Krile's Echo?" | #506 |
| M507a | Alphinaud and Arenvald puzzle over how to use the mad king's treasures. | *A Sultana's Strings* · 4.1 · main | "Well, the question of how to put the mad king's treasures to good use shows no sign of answering itself." | #507 |
| M507b | Nanamo takes you on a private outing as her advisor. | *A Sultana's Strings* · 4.1 · main | "Ordinarily, Papashan would accompany me, but for this particular outing I need an advisor—not a minder." | #507 |
| M507c | No one knows whether the rockslide that killed Nanamo's parents was an accident or an assassination. | *A Sultana's Strings* · 4.1 · main | "To this day, it is not certain if the incident was simple misfortune or an expertly planned assassination." | #507 |
| M507d | Those who meant to make Nanamo a puppet on the throne were always beyond reach. | *A Sultana's Strings* · 4.1 · main | "The true villains—those who plotted to put me on the throne as a biddable puppet—were ever beyond our reach." | #507 |
| M507e | At the Coliseum, Raubahn swore to one day offer Nanamo his blade. | *A Sultana's Strings* · 4.1 · main | "he swore that he would one day offer me his blade in service." | #507 |
| M507f | Raubahn, with his Syndicate seat, was the first sword Nanamo could wield. | *A Sultana's Strings* · 4.1 · main | "Raubahn, with the authority of his Syndicate position, was the first sword I could wield." | #507 |
| M507g | Nanamo is unsure whether Raubahn will stay in Ul'dah or return to Ala Mhigo. | *A Sultana's Strings* · 4.1 · main | "Whether Raubahn chooses to remain in Ul'dah or return to Ala Mhigo, I only wish that he do so with a heart unburdened by guilt or regret." | #507 |
| M507h | A gambling ring had arranged for Raubahn to die on the Coliseum's sands. | *A Sultana's Strings* · 4.1 · main | "It was not until later that I learned of the gambling ring which had arranged for Raubahn to die on the sands that day." | #507 |
| M508a | With Ala Mhigo free, Nanamo sees a way to aid the refugees. | *A Sultana's Duty* · 4.1 · main | "With Ala Mhigo now freed from imperial chains, I finally see a way to aid the refugees." | #508 |
| M508b | The refugees will need shelter and work. | *A Sultana's Duty* · 4.1 · main | "These people will need shelter and employment if they are to survive" | #508 |
| M508c | Nanamo seeks advice from Godbert. | *A Sultana's Duty* · 4.1 · main | "I wonder, are you acquainted with Godbert Manderville?" | #508 |
| M508d | Godbert calls taxing Ul'dah's wealth to save the refugees a terrible idea. | *A Sultana's Duty* · 4.1 · main | "Taxing Ul'dah's wealth to save Ala Mhigan refugees is a terrible, terrible idea." | #508 |
| M508e | Nanamo decides her support must make the refugees self-sufficient. | *A Sultana's Duty* · 4.1 · main | "Then any support I pledge to the refugees must promote self-sufficiency" | #508 |
| M509a | Nanamo distrusts the East Aldenard Trading Company but trusts Hancock. | *A Sultana's Resolve* · 4.1 · main | "Ordinarily, I would not trust any agent of the East Aldenard Trading Company" | #509 |
| M509b | Hancock names Lolorito the best adviser on matters of profit. | *A Sultana's Resolve* · 4.1 · main | "Upon matters of profit, there is no living soul better-qualified to advise Her Grace than Chairman Lolorito" | #509 |
| M509c | Nanamo had hoped to keep the Monetarists, and Lolorito above all, at arm's length. | *A Sultana's Resolve* · 4.1 · main | "I had hoped to keep the Monetarists at arm's length, and ''him'' in particular..." | #509 |
| M509d | They meet at the Waking Sands as neutral ground. | *A Sultana's Resolve* · 4.1 · main | "The Waking Sands would seem a suitably ''neutral'' venue for negotiations, don't you think?" | #509 |
| M509e | The plan is funded partly from the late Teledji's seized estate. | *A Sultana's Resolve* · 4.1 · main | "together with the fortune seized from the late Teledji Adeledji's estate." | #509 |
| M509f | In return for aiding the refugees, Ul'dah is to receive a share of later profits. | *A Sultana's Resolve* · 4.1 · main | "you ask that a proportion of all subsequent profits be promised to Ul'dah." | #509 |
| M509g | Lolorito will send people to extract Ala Mhigo's "white gold". | *A Sultana's Resolve* · 4.1 · main | "I shall dispatch representatives well versed in the extraction of this “white gold,”" | #509 |
| M509h | Nanamo and Lolorito strike a deal. | *A Sultana's Resolve* · 4.1 · main | "A deal is struck, then!" | #509 |
| M510a | Raubahn says he will return to Ul'dah, but the sultana is unsure he wants to. | *Securing the Saltery* · 4.1 · main | "Raubahn ''says'' he's going to return to Ul'dah...but she isn't sure he wants to." | #510 |
| M510b | Alphinaud insists Ala Mhigo cover part of the investment. | *Securing the Saltery* · 4.1 · main | "Ala Mhigo must be permitted to cover a portion of the investment." | #510 |
| M510c | The saltery has lacked the coin and hands to rebuild, until now. | *Securing the Saltery* · 4.1 · main | "We've never had the coin or the hands to put the place back to how it was─but it sounds like that's about to change!" | #510 |
| M510d | Watt has no patience for folk in the city hungry for revenge. | *Securing the Saltery* · 4.1 · main | "Too often of late, I hear tales of folk over in the city, drunk on victory and hungry for revenge, their heads stuck firmly in the past." | #510 |
| M511a | Arenvald and his comrades captured men in the Peaks. | *A Blissful Arrival* · 4.1 · main | "We must speak of the men Arenvald and his comrades apprehended in the Peaks." | #511 |
| M511b | The captives are Yuyuhase and Laurentius, who conspired with Captain Ilberd. | *A Blissful Arrival* · 4.1 · main | "I daresay you remember Yuyuhase and Laurentius─the fugitives who conspired with Captain Ilberd in the Crystal Braves' betrayal." | #511 |
| M511c | They orchestrated the slaughter of Resistance fighters before the Griffin's ritual. | *A Blissful Arrival* · 4.1 · main | "It was they who orchestrated the slaughter of the Resistance fighters prior to the Griffin's infernal ritual." | #511 |
| M511d | The Alliance will pass judgment on them. | *A Blissful Arrival* · 4.1 · main | "When the time comes for the Alliance to pass judgment, I'll see that your opinion is heard." | #511 |
| M511e | Raubahn bears a share of the blame for Ilberd's atrocities. | *A Blissful Arrival* · 4.1 · main | "I bear a share of the blame for Ilberd's atrocities." | #511 |
| M511f | Raubahn will soon return to Ul'dah and the sultana's side. | *A Blissful Arrival* · 4.1 · main | "Soon, I will return to Ul'dah, and take my place at the sultana's side." | #511 |
| M511g | Raubahn will not betray his oath to Nanamo. | *A Blissful Arrival* · 4.1 · main | "But I pledged my blade to Nanamo. And I will not betray that oath." | #511 |
| M511h | The Qalyana at the gates are the ones who summoned the primal. | *A Blissful Arrival* · 4.1 · main | "These are ''Qalyana''─the ones that summoned the primal." | #511 |
| M511i | Lyse welcomes the Ananta but will not condone summoning. | *A Blissful Arrival* · 4.1 · main | "But we cannot condone the summoning of primals." | #511 |
| M511j | Raubahn fears they have welcomed a viper. | *A Blissful Arrival* · 4.1 · main | "Why do I have the feeling we've welcomed a viper into our midst...?" | #511 |
| M512b | The Qalyana broodmother summons an incarnation of Sri Lakshmi in the throne room. | *Return of the Bull* · 4.1 · main | "the Qalyana broodmother somehow summons forth an incarnation of [[Lakshmi|Sri Lakshmi]], plunging the throne room into chaos." | #512 |
| M512c | The guards meant to keep crystals out have themselves been enthralled. | *Return of the Bull* · 4.1 · main | "Too late, you realize that the guards entrusted with keeping the area free of crystals have themselves been enthralled." | #512 |
| M512d | Fordola arrives to even the odds. | *Return of the Bull* · 4.1 · main | "[[Fordola]] arrives to even the odds." | #512 |
| M512e | Lyse told Fordola she could end it like Zenos or fight for Ala Mhigo. | *Return of the Bull* · 4.1 · main | "You can end it like Zenos...or you can fight for Ala Mhigo. Your choice." | #512 |
| M512f | Afterwards Fordola surrenders her sword and returns to her cell of her own accord. | *Return of the Bull* · 4.1 · main | "After a taut moment, Fordola surrenders her sword, and returns to her cell of her own accord." | #512 |
| M512g | Nanamo dismisses Raubahn as General of the Immortal Flames and takes his Syndicate seat. | *Return of the Bull* · 4.1 · main | "You are hereby dismissed as General of the Immortal Flames, and relieved of your seat on the Syndicate." | #512 |
| M512h | The envoys choose an elected government modeled on Ishgard's House of Commons. | *Return of the Bull* · 4.1 · main | "The envoys have chosen to instate a government modeled on Ishgard's House of Commons─a ruling body of representatives elected by the people." | #512 |
| M512i | Nanamo tells Raubahn to stay and rebuild his homeland. | *Return of the Bull* · 4.1 · main | "Stay here, in your homeland. Work with your brethren. Rebuild Ala Mhigo!" | #512 |
| M512j | Lyse ends her time as Resistance commander and means to invite Raubahn to replace her. | *Return of the Bull* · 4.1 · main | "Lyse declares that her time as commander of the Resistance is over, and that she means to invite Raubahn to take her place." | #512 |
| M512k | Pipin will assume his father's duties. | *Return of the Bull* · 4.1 · main | "I shall be assuming my father's duties." | #512 |
| M512l | An imperial officer hears that Zenos lives. | *Return of the Bull* · 4.1 · main | "Nay... Lord Zenos ''lives''?" | #512 |
| M512m | Gosetsu, calling his companion Tsuyu, seeks a ship across the Ruby Sea. | *Return of the Bull* · 4.1 · main | "Come, Tsuyu. We shall find a ship to carry us across the Ruby Sea!" | #512 |
| M512n | Gosetsu and an amnesiac Yotsuyu are in Kugane. | *Return of the Bull* · 4.1 · main | "Gosetsu and an amnesiac Yotsuyu are in Kugane." | #512 |
| M512o | Raubahn strikes down Shanti, the Qalyana broodmother. | *Return of the Bull* · 4.1 · main | "Raubahn strikes down Shanti." | #512 |
| M512p | Gosetsu surrendered his blade for a few coins. | *Return of the Bull* · 4.1 · main | "I surrendered my blade to secure what few coins we have!" | #512 |
| M513a | The council recognizes the Resistance as Ala Mhigo's standing army. | *Tidings from the East* · 4.2 · main | "The council has officially recognized the Resistance as our standing army." | #513 |
| M513b | A commander has been appointed, and Lyse expects you can guess who. | *Tidings from the East* · 4.2 · main | "And I expect you can guess who's been appointed commander." | #513 |
| M513c | Lyse will keep leading the Resistance's forces in the Fringes. | *Tidings from the East* · 4.2 · main | "Lyse will continue to lead our forces in the Fringes─a job she's been doing tirelessly." | #513 |
| M513d | The Qalyana's new leader is Shanti's daughter. | *Tidings from the East* · 4.2 · main | "It's, um...Shanti's daughter. You know, the half-dead one." | #513 |
| M513e | Garleans are hunting Yotsuyu, Doma's late acting imperial viceroy. | *Tidings from the East* · 4.2 · main | "The late acting imperial viceroy of Doma─Yotsuyu!" | #513 |
| M513f | Hancock hears of a woman like Yotsuyu with a grizzled Roegadyn samurai. | *Tidings from the East* · 4.2 · main | "But a woman who resembles Yotsuyu in the company of a grizzled Roegadyn samurai? I daresay that thickens the plot." | #513 |
| M513g | Yugiri is bound by duty to learn the truth. | *Tidings from the East* · 4.2 · main | "I am bound by duty to ascertain the truth of the matter." | #513 |
| M513h | The informant is Gyodo, the Namazu who once betrayed you to the Empire. | *Tidings from the East* · 4.2 · main | "the [[Namazu]] who unwisely betrayed you to the Empire during your first visit to Kugane." | #513 |
| M513i | An old Roegadyn samurai sold his katana to a pawnbroker on Kogane Dori. | *Tidings from the East* · 4.2 · main | "They say an elderly Roegadyn samurai appeared on Kogane Dori with a beauteous maiden, and sold his katana to a pawnbroker." | #513 |
| M513j | A large detachment of soldiers has arrived at the Garlean embassy. | *Tidings from the East* · 4.2 · main | "Some few days ago, a large detachment of soldiers was seen arriving at the Garlean embassy." | #513 |
| M514a | Yugiri confirms the katana is Gosetsu's. | *The Sword in the Store* · 4.2 · main | "There can be no doubt. It is Gosetsu's blade." | #514 |
| M514b | Alphinaud buys the katana for the Scions as a gift. | *The Sword in the Store* · 4.2 · main | "The Scions will buy the katana. Pray consider it a gift." | #514 |
| M514c | Gosetsu meant to take the girl across the Ruby Sea, out of her family's reach. | *The Sword in the Store* · 4.2 · main | "he was planning to spirit her away across the Ruby Sea─out of the reach of her family, no doubt." | #514 |
| M514d | Yugiri wonders whether the girl can truly be Yotsuyu. | *The Sword in the Store* · 4.2 · main | "The more I hear about her, the more I wonder if she can truly be Yotsuyu." | #514 |
| M515a | An imperial contingent hunting Yotsuyu prepares to deploy to the Ruby Sea. | *Hope on the Waves* · 4.2 · main | "the imperial contingent charged with finding Yotsuyu is preparing to deploy to the [[The Ruby Sea|Ruby Sea]]." | #515 |
| M515b | Alphinaud admits he bought Gosetsu's katana with Scion funds. | *Hope on the Waves* · 4.2 · main | "[[Alphinaud]]'s subsequent admission that he purchased Gosetsu's katana with [[The Scions of the Seventh Dawn|Scion]] funds" | #515 |
| M515c | The Empire attacks the Confederacy's stronghold on Sakazuki. | *Hope on the Waves* · 4.2 · main | "the Empire has launched an attack against the [[The Confederacy|Confederate]] stronghold on [[Sakazuki]]." | #515 |
| M515d | Unable to decide Yotsuyu's fate, Gosetsu resolved to deliver her to Hien for judgment. | *Hope on the Waves* · 4.2 · main | "Gosetsu, unable to decide her fate, resolved to deliver her to [[Hien]] for his judgment." | #515 |
| M515e | Her kimono saved Yotsuyu from Hien's blade. | *Hope on the Waves* · 4.2 · main | "'Twas her kimono which saved her from Hien's blade, believe it or not." | #515 |
| M515g | Imperial troops come to take Yotsuyu back. | *Hope on the Waves* · 4.2 · main | "Lady Yotsuyu, we're here to rescue you! Please come with us!" | #515 |
| M515h | Alisaie will not take her eyes off Yotsuyu. | *Hope on the Waves* · 4.2 · main | "I won't be taking my eyes off you. Not for a moment." | #515 |
| M515i | Yotsuyu survived the fall but woke with no memories, speaking like a child. | *Hope on the Waves* · 4.2 · main | "Though she survived, she awoke bereft of all her memories, and speaking like a child." | #515 |
| M516a | Yugiri brings Gosetsu back to their master. | *Elation and Trepidation* · 4.2 · main | "Yugiri is eager to see Gosetsu safely back to their master." | #516 |
| M516b | Hien says Yotsuyu still haunts Doma's dreams and cannot roam free. | *Elation and Trepidation* · 4.2 · main | "Her face yet haunts their dreams. She cannot roam free." | #516 |
| M516c | As acting viceroy, Yotsuyu would know imperial secrets. | *Elation and Trepidation* · 4.2 · main | "As acting viceroy, Yotsuyu would have been privy to imperial secrets." | #516 |
| M516d | Hien places Yotsuyu in Jifuya's care, out of sight. | *Elation and Trepidation* · 4.2 · main | "Place her in Jifuya's care, with the express instruction that she is not to be seen." | #516 |
| M516e | Gosetsu doubts Yotsuyu's claim to have lost her memory. | *Elation and Trepidation* · 4.2 · main | "She claims to have lost her memory, though I know not if she speaks true." | #516 |
| M516f | Jifuya was Yotsuyu's master when she was a courtesan. | *Elation and Trepidation* · 4.2 · main | "I was... I was her master. When she was yet a courtesan." | #516 |
| M516g | Yotsuyu became an imperial informant and won the office of acting viceroy. | *Elation and Trepidation* · 4.2 · main | "The rest you know. She became an imperial informant, and for her loyal service won the office of acting viceroy." | #516 |
| M516h | Hien says Yotsuyu's fate must wait. | *Elation and Trepidation* · 4.2 · main | "But the question of what is to be done with her must wait." | #516 |
| M517a | The Empire is on the move. | *Storm on the Horizon* · 4.2 · main | "The Empire is on the move, and Hien would not be caught on the back foot." | #517 |
| M517b | Yugiri names the envoy as heir to the Naeuri clan and Yotsuyu's stepbrother. | *Storm on the Horizon* · 4.2 · main | "He is heir to the Naeuri clan... and Yotsuyu's stepbrother." | #517 |
| M517c | Emperor Varis personally sanctioned Asahi's mission and lets him speak with his voice. | *Storm on the Horizon* · 4.2 · main | "Indeed, His Radiance, Emperor Varis zos Galvus personally sanctioned this mission, granting me the authority to speak with his voice." | #517 |
| M517d | Hien notes that such talks are held between sovereign nations. | *Storm on the Horizon* · 4.2 · main | "Unless I am mistaken, such negotiations are typically conducted between ''sovereign'' nations." | #517 |
| M517e | Asahi calls eikons a blight upon the star. | *Storm on the Horizon* · 4.2 · main | "Eikons are a blight upon this star." | #517 |
| M517f | Asahi is of the Populares, who speak for the common man. | *Storm on the Horizon* · 4.2 · main | "I am of the Populares, a collective which represents the interests of the common man." | #517 |
| M517g | The Optimates, pure-blooded Garleans, oppose the Populares' reforms. | *Storm on the Horizon* · 4.2 · main | "A collection of pure-blooded Garleans who seek to consolidate their own supremacy: the Optimates." | #517 |
| M517h | Asahi proposes trading Doman conscripts for imperial captives. | *Storm on the Horizon* · 4.2 · main | "We would repatriate them in return for those of ours you captured in the recent conflict." | #517 |
| M517i | The Optimates tried and failed to take Yotsuyu by force. | *Storm on the Horizon* · 4.2 · main | "The Optimates tried—and failed—to take my sister by force." | #517 |
| M517j | Asahi's mention of Yotsuyu left Hien uneasy. | *Storm on the Horizon* · 4.2 · main | "But the ambassador's mention of Yotsuyu left me ill at ease." | #517 |
| M517k | Hien needs time to consider the proposal. | *Storm on the Horizon* · 4.2 · main | "Your proposal has merit, but I will need time to consider it." | #517 |
| M518a | While Hien deliberates, Asahi tours Yanxia. | *His Forgotten Home* · 4.2 · main | "While [[Lord Hien]] deliberates over the proposed prisoner exchange, Asahi apparently wishes to take a trip around [[Yanxia]]." | #518 |
| M518b | Doma Castle is gone, and Asahi laments it. | *His Forgotten Home* · 4.2 · main | "Doma Castle is no more, and Asahi can but lament the loss of the great keep which once graced Yanxia's skyline." | #518 |
| M518c | Red Kojin surround two villagers. | *His Forgotten Home* · 4.2 · main | "surrounded by a group of [[Kojin of the Red|Red Kojin]]." | #518 |
| M518e | Yugiri reminds Asahi of the Empire he is sworn to. | *His Forgotten Home* · 4.2 · main | "The Empire to which you have sworn allegiance." | #518 |
| M518f | Asahi says the Empire must change from within. | *His Forgotten Home* · 4.2 · main | "You and yours have fought fiercely to change the Empire from without, but if we are to end the cycle of conflict, the Empire must change from within." | #518 |
| M519a | Susano was summoned with relics from the Kojin's vault on the Isle of Zekki. | *A Guilty Conscience* · 4.2 · main | "Susano was summoned forth using the power of relics locked away in the Kojin's treasure vault on the Isle of Zekki." | #519 |
| M519b | Hien will enlist the Blue Kojin. | *A Guilty Conscience* · 4.2 · main | "For this, I plan to enlist the aid of our friends the Blue Kojin." | #519 |
| M519c | Hien had Yotsuyu brought to the Kienkan in secret. | *A Guilty Conscience* · 4.2 · main | "I had her brought here in secret while the three of you kept our guest company." | #519 |
| M519d | Hien calls Yotsuyu the living proof of his failure. | *A Guilty Conscience* · 4.2 · main | "Yet here you are, the living, breathing proof of my failure." | #519 |
| M519e | Hien leaves Yotsuyu's life for Gosetsu to safeguard. | *A Guilty Conscience* · 4.2 · main | "If so, her life is not mine to take. It is yours to safeguard." | #519 |
| M519f | If her memory has not returned by the exchange, Yotsuyu may live in Doma as Tsuyu. | *A Guilty Conscience* · 4.2 · main | "Come the hour of the exchange, if her memories have not returned, she may remain here in Doma to live out her days as Tsuyu." | #519 |
| M519g | Gosetsu asks why the heavens denied him his rest. | *A Guilty Conscience* · 4.2 · main | "I ask why the heavens saw fit to deny me my rest." | #519 |
| M519h | Hien thinks Gosetsu should not even be on his feet. | *A Guilty Conscience* · 4.2 · main | "after all he has suffered, even he should not be on his feet." | #519 |
| M520a | Hien's condition: Yotsuyu is exempt if her memory has not returned in time. | *Rise of a New Sun* · 4.2 · main | "that [[Yotsuyu]] be granted exemption from the transaction should her memory fail to return by the appointed hour." | #520 |
| M520b | Hien tells Asahi that Yotsuyu remembers nothing, not even her name. | *Rise of a New Sun* · 4.2 · main | "She was inside Doma Castle when it collapsed. Though she survived, she remembers nothing of her past life─not even her name." | #520 |
| M520c | Asahi warns you privately of a day of retribution. | *Rise of a New Sun* · 4.2 · main | "Mark me, savior of the savages. There will be a reckoning." | #520 |
| M520d | Asahi is a fervent admirer of Zenos. | *Rise of a New Sun* · 4.2 · main | "you discover he is in fact a fervent admirer of the late Lord [[Zenos]]." | #520 |
| M520e | In the Echo, the masked swordsman was Zenos, legatus of the XIIth and crown prince. | *Rise of a New Sun* · 4.2 · main | "Surely you jest? That was Zenos yae Galvus, legatus of the XIIth─the crown bloody prince!" | #520 |
| M520f | Hien must play Asahi's game to bring Doma's conscripts home. | *Rise of a New Sun* · 4.2 · main | "But if by treating with them there is even the faintest hope we might secure the return of our conscripted brothers and sisters, I must play this game." | #520 |
| M520g | Alisaie asks Hien to send for the Scions if anything happens. | *Rise of a New Sun* · 4.2 · main | "If anything should happen─anything at all─you must send for us at once." | #520 |
| M520h | Alphinaud apologizes to Tataru for the purchase. | *Rise of a New Sun* · 4.2 · main | "It was reckless of me to make such a purchase without first consulting you." | #520 |
| M520i | Asahi reports that everything is going according to plan. | *Rise of a New Sun* · 4.2 · main | "Yes, my lord will be most pleased. Everything is going according to plan." | #520 |
| M520j | Hancock will pass any urgent word from Doma to the Scions first. | *Rise of a New Sun* · 4.2 · main | "Well, should we receive any urgent missives from Doma, rest assured you will be the first to know of it." | #520 |
| M521a | Hien asks for you in Doma at this critical juncture. | *Gosetsu and Tsuyu* · 4.3 · main | "Lord Hien requests your presence, and I share his view that you should be on hand at this critical juncture." | #521 |
| M521b | Gosetsu has been ailing. | *Gosetsu and Tsuyu* · 4.3 · main | "a worrying incident involving an ailing [[Gosetsu]]" | #521 |
| M521c | Gosetsu is bedridden but recovering. | *Gosetsu and Tsuyu* · 4.3 · main | "Confined to bed and grumbling without cease, but he has shown signs of recovery." | #521 |
| M521d | Gosetsu will never again be the warrior he was. | *Gosetsu and Tsuyu* · 4.3 · main | "he will never again be the warrior he once was." | #521 |
| M521e | Yugiri's spying convinces her Yotsuyu's mind is truly broken. | *Gosetsu and Tsuyu* · 4.3 · main | "I can but conclude that her mind is truly broken." | #521 |
| M521f | Hien allows Yotsuyu to live out her days in Doma as Tsuyu. | *Gosetsu and Tsuyu* · 4.3 · main | "he decides to allow her to live out her days in Doma as" | #521 |
| M521g | A ship of Doman conscripts is due in Yanxia. | *Gosetsu and Tsuyu* · 4.3 · main | "a vessel bearing Doman conscripts is soon to arrive in Yanxia." | #521 |
| M521h | Tsuyu has crossed the river. | *Gosetsu and Tsuyu* · 4.3 · main | "'Twould seem that Tsuyu has crossed the river." | #521 |
| M522a | Hien is desperate to find Yotsuyu. | *Gone Like the Morning Dew* · 4.3 · main | "Though his face betrays little sign of panic, Hien is plainly desperate to find Yotsuyu before something terrible happens." | #522 |
| M522b | Yotsuyu and Gosetsu survived the keep's fall, at the cost of her memory. | *Gone Like the Morning Dew* · 4.3 · main | "By some miracle, both she and Gosetsu were spared when the keep collapsed─though Yotsuyu's preservation came at the cost of her memory." | #522 |
| M522c | Hien cut Yotsuyu down and left her to her fate. | *Gone Like the Morning Dew* · 4.3 · main | "'Twas I who cut her down. I who left her to her fate." | #522 |
| M522d | The villagers of Namai were told she was dead. | *Gone Like the Morning Dew* · 4.3 · main | "They told us she was dead!" | #522 |
| M522e | Isse is appalled that she has forgotten what she did. | *Gone Like the Morning Dew* · 4.3 · main | "You're saying she's ''forgotten''? Forgotten everything she's done!?" | #522 |
| M522f | Until her memories return, she is to be Tsuyu, a citizen of Doma. | *Gone Like the Morning Dew* · 4.3 · main | "Until such time as her memories return, this woman shall be known as “Tsuyu,” and treated as a citizen of Doma." | #522 |
| M522g | Azami sees that she is frightened and not the same. | *Gone Like the Morning Dew* · 4.3 · main | "Can't you see how scared she is? How can you be scared of her? She's not the same." | #522 |
| M523a | Asahi brings special guests in case Yotsuyu has not recovered. | *Fruits of Her Labor* · 4.3 · main | "In anticipation of this tragic turn of events, I took the liberty of inviting some special guests..." | #523 |
| M523b | Yotsuyu wishes she had not remembered. | *Fruits of Her Labor* · 4.3 · main | "If only I hadn't remembered." | #523 |
| M523c | Yotsuyu means to savor her vengeance against Doma. | *Fruits of Her Labor* · 4.3 · main | "For years I knew naught but the taste of pain and humiliation, but the time has come to savor my vengeance against Doma." | #523 |
| M523d | Asahi offers Yotsuyu a deeper vengeance and the power to take it, after she murdered their parents. | *Fruits of Her Labor* · 4.3 · main | "But surely you can't be satisfied with murdering a pair of doddering elders. You yearn for a deeper vengeance...and the power to see it through." | #523 |
| M523e | Hien knew what would happen if she recovered. | *Fruits of Her Labor* · 4.3 · main | "I knew what would happen if she recovered, and ''still'' I did nothing." | #523 |
| M523f | Asahi was willing to pay for her with his parents' lives. | *Fruits of Her Labor* · 4.3 · main | "Whatever he wants with her, he was willing to pay for it with his parents' lives..." | #523 |
| M523h | Yotsuyu is gone from the Kienkan. | *Fruits of Her Labor* · 4.3 · main | "My lord! Forgive me, but the lady Yotsuyu─she's gone!" | #523 |
| M524a | Yotsuyu has regained her memory and returned to the imperial fold. | *Conscripts and Contingencies* · 4.3 · main | "Yotsuyu has regained her memory and returned to the imperial fold." | #524 |
| M524b | By the contract, Doma must surrender Yotsuyu if she recovers before the exchange. | *Conscripts and Contingencies* · 4.3 · main | "According to the terms of the contract, we were bound to surrender Yotsuyu into the Garleans' hands should her condition improve prior to the hour of the exchange." | #524 |
| M524c | Hien no longer trusts Asahi, given his admiration for Zenos. | *Conscripts and Contingencies* · 4.3 · main | "Given his recent conduct, and his apparent admiration for Zenos, it is plain he cannot be trusted." | #524 |
| M524d | Asahi brings unexplained containers to every meeting. | *Conscripts and Contingencies* · 4.3 · main | "And that is to say nothing of the unexplained containers he insists on bringing to our meetings." | #524 |
| M524e | Rasho says the Confederacy aided Doma's liberation only to save itself. | *Conscripts and Contingencies* · 4.3 · main | "Doma's liberation was but a means to an end. We aided you only to save ourselves." | #524 |
| M524f | The Confederacy agrees to lend a ship. | *Conscripts and Contingencies* · 4.3 · main | "The Confederacy agrees to your request. You will have your ship." | #524 |
| M525a | Asahi says his crates hold materials for Doma's restoration. | *The Primary Agreement* · 4.3 · main | "Oh, the supply crates? They are filled with materials we hoped might be of use in Doma's restoration." | #525 |
| M525b | Drawing on crystals hidden in the crates, Yotsuyu becomes the primal Tsukuyomi. | *The Primary Agreement* · 4.3 · main | "begins to draw upon crystals hidden within the surrounding supply crates, transforming at last into the [[primal]] [[Tsukuyomi]]." | #525 |
| M525c | Asahi declares the summoning a violation of the agreement. | *The Primary Agreement* · 4.3 · main | "A Doman citizen has called forth an eikon in direct violation of our primary agreement." | #525 |
| M525d | Yotsuyu says Zenos himself ordered her to purge resisters. | *The Primary Agreement* · 4.3 · main | "Such was the order given to me by Lord Zenos himself!" | #525 |
| M525e | In the Echo, Zenos foresees the eikon leaving Yotsuyu a husk. | *The Primary Agreement* · 4.3 · main | "She will be no more than a husk, a slave to whim and desire." | #525 |
| M525f | Maxima says Zenos is alive and received his party before they left. | *The Primary Agreement* · 4.3 · main | "Lord Zenos is very much alive—he granted our party an audience prior to our departure." | #525 |
| M525g | Hien saw Zenos take his own life after the battle in Ala Mhigo. | *The Primary Agreement* · 4.3 · main | "Zenos is dead. He took his own life after the battle in Ala Mhigo." | #525 |
| M525h | Alphinaud suspects an Ascian behind Zenos's teaching of summoning. | *The Primary Agreement* · 4.3 · main | "Impostor or no, if Zenos was instructing Asahi on the finer points of ritual summoning, then experience tells us there is an Ascian waiting in the wings." | #525 |
| M525i | Yotsuyu thanks her brother for the gift of vengeance. | *The Primary Agreement* · 4.3 · main | "Thank you, dear brother... for this precious gift. Vengeance." | #525 |
| M525j | Alphinaud goes with Maxima to Garlemald. | *The Primary Agreement* · 4.3 · main | "Suspecting [[Ascian]] involvement, [[Alphinaud]] offers to accompany Maxima back to Garlemald, and he duly accepts." | #525 |
| M525k | Gosetsu leaves to spend his remaining days on pilgrimage. | *The Primary Agreement* · 4.3 · main | "Thus did I decide to devote my remaining days to pilgrimage." | #525 |
| M525l | Gosetsu lost his wife and daughter in the invasion. | *The Primary Agreement* · 4.3 · main | "I believe the answer lies in past tragedy—specifically, the death of his wife and daughter during the invasion." | #525 |
| M525m | The conscripts are home, and Doma's restoration is closer. | *The Primary Agreement* · 4.3 · main | "Our brothers and sisters are returned to us, and the dream of Doma's restoration is that much closer to being realized." | #525 |
| M525n | The exchange is held with the Populares delegation at Castrum Fluminis. | *The Primary Agreement* · 4.3 · main | "At Castrum Fluminis, you meet with the [[The Populares|Populares]] delegation" | #525 |
| M525o | Maxima, the late ambassador's second-in-command, now speaks for the Empire. | *The Primary Agreement* · 4.3 · main | "as the late ambassador's second-in-command, it falls to me to speak on the Empire's behalf." | #525 |
| M525p | Yotsuyu impales Asahi. | *The Primary Agreement* · 4.3 · main | "Yotsuyu impales Asahi and lifts him in the air." | #525 |
| M525q | Yotsuyu returns to her own form and dies. | *The Primary Agreement* · 4.3 · main | "Yotsuyu reverts back to her normal form and dies." | #525 |
| M525r | Asahi dies. | *The Primary Agreement* · 4.3 · main | "Asahi dies." | #525 |
| M526a | Alisaie declares the Scions' work in Doma done. | *Under the Moonlight* · 4.3 · main | "Alisaie declares the Scions' work in Doma done, and turns her gaze towards Rhalgr's Reach." | #526 |
| M526b | Through the Echo you saw the crown prince in a recent meeting. | *Under the Moonlight* · 4.3 · main | "saw the crown prince through the Echo─in a meeting that could only have taken place in the recent past." | #526 |
| M526c | Alphinaud has gone to Garlemald. | *Under the Moonlight* · 4.3 · main | "he's off on what will almost certainly turn out to be a once-in-a-lifetime trip to Garlemald." | #526 |
| M526d | Zenos's coffin is empty. | *Under the Moonlight* · 4.3 · main | "The coffin is empty." | #526 |
| M526e | Thancred notes a missing corpse. | *Under the Moonlight* · 4.3 · main | "Oh dear. We seem to be missing a corpse." | #526 |
| M526f | Thancred leans toward Alphinaud's theory of Ascian possession. | *Under the Moonlight* · 4.3 · main | "More and more, however, I find myself siding with Alphinaud's theory of Ascian possession." | #526 |
| M526g | Y'shtola concludes that an Ascian now inhabits Zenos's body. | *Under the Moonlight* · 4.3 · main | "Given all that we know, I too would conclude that an Ascian now inhabits Zenos's body." | #526 |
| M526h | Zenos tells the Emperor the eikon was destroyed by you. | *Under the Moonlight* · 4.3 · main | "The eikon in question was summarily dispatched by the Warrior of Light." | #526 |
| M526i | Zenos claims everything went according to plan. | *Under the Moonlight* · 4.3 · main | "Everything proceeded according to plan─every party behaving exactly as required." | #526 |
| M527a | Thancred will infiltrate the Empire's western provinces. | *Emissary of the Dawn* · 4.3 · main | "Ascian involvement seems all but certain at this point, and Thancred offers to infiltrate the Empire's western provinces to seek out further information." | #527 |
| M527b | Y'shtola goes to Doma, and Alisaie holds the Rising Stones. | *Emissary of the Dawn* · 4.3 · main | "With Y'shtola's time already committed to a scholarly expedition to Doma, Alisaie reluctantly agrees to hold the fort at the Rising Stones." | #527 |
| M527c | Tsukuyomi was summoned through a sacred relic, as Susano was. | *Emissary of the Dawn* · 4.3 · main | "So this Tsukuyomi was summoned in much the same manner as Susano─via the medium of a sacred relic." | #527 |
| M527d | Emperor Solus declared eikons a threat to the star and ordered them eradicated. | *Emissary of the Dawn* · 4.3 · main | "He declared eikons a threat to our very star's existence, and issued an Empire-wide decree ordering the eradication of all such entities." | #527 |
| M527e | Alphinaud's airship is shot down by attackers who knew where to strike. | *Emissary of the Dawn* · 4.3 · main | "Damn it! They knew exactly where to hit us." | #527 |
| M527f | The attackers' commander says the prince wants no survivors. | *Emissary of the Dawn* · 4.3 · main | "The prince wishes none left alive!" | #527 |
| M527g | The attackers bore the insignia of the Emperor's personal guard. | *Emissary of the Dawn* · 4.3 · main | "The soldiers bore the insignia of the Emperor's personal guard..." | #527 |
| M527h | Shadowhunter carries an Ascian mask: the face of their prey. | *Emissary of the Dawn* · 4.3 · main | "An Ascian mask! … The face of our prey." | #527 |
| M527i | Alphinaud accepts Shadowhunter's offer. | *Emissary of the Dawn* · 4.3 · main | "We accept your gracious offer, uh..." | #527 |
| M527j | Emperor Solus reeled at the devastation he saw in Othard. | *Emissary of the Dawn* · 4.3 · main | "When Emperor Solus first came to Othard and beheld this blight, he is said to have reeled at the scale of the devastation." | #527 |
| M528a | A council in Ala Mhigo will weigh how to combat the Ascians. | *Sisterly Act* · 4.4 · main | "a council is to be held in [[Ala Mhigo]] to deliberate how best to combat the [[Ascian]] menace" | #528 |
| M528b | The Alliance acts on the Resistance's intelligence about Zenos's remains. | *Sisterly Act* · 4.4 · main | "Having assessed intelligence provided by the Ala Mhigan Resistance concerning the whereabouts of Lord Zenos's remains" | #528 |
| M528c | The Emperor razed the Dalmascans' capital for their defiance. | *Sisterly Act* · 4.4 · main | "For their defiance, the Emperor made a show of razing their capital to the ground" | #528 |
| M528d | The Alliance supplies resistance movements abroad. | *Sisterly Act* · 4.4 · main | "The Alliance has already begun supplying materiel to resistance movements abroad" | #528 |
| M528e | Garleans whisper that Doma has summoned a primal. | *Sisterly Act* · 4.4 · main | "Talk is rife that Doma has summoned a primal" | #528 |
| M528f | Thancred: an Ascian wears Zenos's skin. | *Sisterly Act* · 4.4 · main | "Yes. An Ascian wears his skin." | #528 |
| M528g | The impostor wearing Zenos's face moves in plain sight. | *Sisterly Act* · 4.4 · main | "the impostor wearing [[Zenos]]'s face continues to move in plain sight" | #528 |
| M528h | Alphinaud's linkpearl message said only "The Burn". | *Sisterly Act* · 4.4 · main | "Two words were all I could make out. … “The Burn.”" | #528 |
| M528i | Alisaie means to search the Burn for her brother. | *Sisterly Act* · 4.4 · main | "Alisaie declares her intention to search for her brother there" | #528 |
| M528j | Relics the Kojin collect let a summoner call a primal without fervent faith. | *Sisterly Act* · 4.4 · main | "Such sacred relics as the Kojin collect obviate the need for religious fervor in the summoner" | #528 |
| M528k | Yotsuyu used an artifact Asahi gave her. | *Sisterly Act* · 4.4 · main | "She had but to associate the artifact given to her by Asahi with the divine" | #528 |
| M529a | Hien guesses Alphinaud met trouble. | *Feel the Burn* · 4.4 · main | "My guess is they encountered some manner of trouble there." | #529 |
| M529c | A sandstorm scatters you on arrival at the Burn. | *Feel the Burn* · 4.4 · main | "Scant moments after arriving at the Burn, you are caught in the midst of a fierce sandstorm which scatters your comrades in all directions." | #529 |
| M529d | You fight through Allagan ruins in the Burn. | *Feel the Burn* · 4.4 · main | "you fight your way through hordes of fearsome desert creatures, as well as machina, inside what appear to be [[Allagan]] ruins." | #529 |
| M529e | The wreck is the ship that bore Alphinaud away. | *Feel the Burn* · 4.4 · main | "Judging by its class, it must be the vessel that bore Master Alphinaud away." | #529 |
| M529f | The dead are of the Emperor's personal guard. | *Feel the Burn* · 4.4 · main | "The insignia on this man's uniform identifies him as one of the Emperor's personal guard." | #529 |
| M529g | The Garleans were fighting their own. | *Feel the Burn* · 4.4 · main | "That would explain why all the casualties are Garlean. They were fighting their own." | #529 |
| M529h | There is no sign of Alphinaud or Maxima. | *Feel the Burn* · 4.4 · main | "There's no sign of Alphinaud. Nor of Maxima and his people." | #529 |
| M529i | The search is left to the shinobi. | *Feel the Burn* · 4.4 · main | "any subsequent search may safely be left in the hands of the shinobi." | #529 |
| M530a | Asahi proclaimed Tsukuyomi's summoning a Doman violation of the agreement. | *Shadows in the Empire* · 4.4 · main | "When Yotsuyu summoned Tsukuyomi, Asahi was quick to proclaim that a Doman citizen had violated the terms of our agreement." | #530 |
| M530b | Hien suspects Zenos, or whoever wears his face. | *Shadows in the Empire* · 4.4 · main | "Most likely Zenos—or whoever it is that wears his face." | #530 |
| M530c | Ala Mhigo has agreed to join the Eorzean Alliance. | *Shadows in the Empire* · 4.4 · main | "Right, so, the big news is that Ala Mhigo has agreed to join the Eorzean Alliance." | #530 |
| M530d | Hien accepts the Alliance's invitation but first has matters at home. | *Shadows in the Empire* · 4.4 · main | "I accept your invitation. I must, however, ask for time to attend to some pressing matters here." | #530 |
| M530e | Hien would reach out to Hingashi, Sui-no-Sato, Nagxia and Dalmasca. | *Shadows in the Empire* · 4.4 · main | "I would reach out to Hingashi and Sui-no-Sato, and further afield, to the myriad peoples of Nagxia and Dalmasca." | #530 |
| M530f | Y'shtola concludes Azys Lla was made from land taken from the Burn. | *Shadows in the Empire* · 4.4 · main | "I conclude therefore that the Allagans created the floating continent with land taken from the Burn." | #530 |
| M530g | You saw something like an Allagan field generator in the Burn. | *Shadows in the Empire* · 4.4 · main | "the Warrior of Light ''did'' report seeing a structure resembling other known Allagan field generators." | #530 |
| M530j | Lyse sees that Alisaie is barely coping. | *Shadows in the Empire* · 4.4 · main | "Alisaie. She's acting as if everything's all right, but it's clear she's barely coping." | #530 |
| M531a | Hien will journey to the Azim Steppe with the Scions. | *A Power in Slumber* · 4.4 · main | "It is settled, then. I will journey to the Azim Steppe with the Scions." | #531 |
| M531b | Alisaie engages the Ironworks to overhaul the field generators. | *A Power in Slumber* · 4.4 · main | "I will engage our friends at Garlond Ironworks to undertake a complete overhaul of the field generators." | #531 |
| M531c | In a Mol myth, a shard of Nhaama's moon cleaved a tainted land from the earth. | *A Power in Slumber* · 4.4 · main | "Venturing into the northern crag, he received of Nhaama a sliver of her essence—a shard of the shining moon—and with it clove the tainted land from the earth." | #531 |
| M531d | Y'shtola links the myth to Azys Lla and Dalamud. | *A Power in Slumber* · 4.4 · main | "A tainted land cloven from the earth, and an offering of blood to the heavens... Azys Lla and Dalamud." | #531 |
| M531e | Legend holds the House of the Crooked Coin's pillars are the source of Nhaama's power. | *A Power in Slumber* · 4.4 · main | "Inside this cavern are pillars of stone that legend holds to be the source of Nhaama's power." | #531 |
| M531f | Cirina warns that Nhaama's faithful would spurn the alliance if the pillars were disturbed. | *A Power in Slumber* · 4.4 · main | "Should you proceed as you propose, such tribes are like to spurn an alliance, prompting others to follow their example." | #531 |
| M531h | Cirina says the Mol reign over the Steppe today. | *A Power in Slumber* · 4.4 · main | "Though the Mol may reign over the Steppe today, this decision will shape the days to come" | #531 |
| M532a | Hien and Y'shtola test the pillars as an energy source. | *The Will of the Moon* · 4.4 · main | "resolved to determine the pillars' suitability as an energy source." | #532 |
| M532b | The pillars are an Allagan artifact regulating the flow of aether. | *The Will of the Moon* · 4.4 · main | "This is an Allagan artifact, most likely built to regulate the flow of aether." | #532 |
| M532c | None revere Nhaama more fervently than the Dotharl. | *The Will of the Moon* · 4.4 · main | "none are more fervent in their faith than the [[Dotharl]]." | #532 |
| M532d | Sadu is the Dotharl's khatun. | *The Will of the Moon* · 4.4 · main | "you set out for [[Dotharl Khaa]] to treat with the tribe's khatun, [[Sadu]]." | #532 |
| M532e | Sadu will back Hien only if you beat her in single combat. | *The Will of the Moon* · 4.4 · main | "Sadu agrees to support Hien's cause only if you first face her in single combat." | #532 |
| M532f | Magnai, first among his Oronir brothers, claims the petition is his to judge. | *The Will of the Moon* · 4.4 · main | "forget that all Nhaama's children are wards of the Oronir. As first among my brothers, your petition is mine alone to judge." | #532 |
| M532g | You secure the allegiance of the Dotharl and the Oronir. | *The Will of the Moon* · 4.4 · main | "securing the allegiance of the Dotharl and the [[Oronir]]" | #532 |
| M532h | Magnai asks whether Y'shtola is his Nhaama. | *The Will of the Moon* · 4.4 · main | "Long had I wondered if my Nhaama might not be a woman of the Steppe. … Are you my Nhaama?" | #532 |
| M532i | Cirina formally commits the Mol to Hien's alliance. | *The Will of the Moon* · 4.4 · main | "formally commits the [[Mol]] to Hien's nascent alliance." | #532 |
| M532j | The Oronir respect strength, and the Sun will answer Hien's call. | *The Will of the Moon* · 4.4 · main | "It is the way of the Oronir to accord recognition and respect to the strong." | #532 |
| M533a | Aether flows freely to the Burn again. | *The Call* · 4.4 · main | "It did. Aether may flow freely to the Burn once more." | #533 |
| M533b | Alisaie and Tataru commission the Ironworks to ready the field generators. | *The Call* · 4.4 · main | "Tataru and I have commissioned Garlond Ironworks to ensure that the field generators function as they should." | #533 |
| M533c | Hingashi and Sui-no-Sato reject the alliance. | *The Call* · 4.4 · main | "From Hingashi and Sui-no-Sato, however, we received outright rejections." | #533 |
| M533e | Thancred would spread word that a corpse inhabited by a servant of darkness walks as Zenos. | *The Call* · 4.4 · main | "the man parading around is in fact a corpse inhabited by a servant of darkness." | #533 |
| M533f | A voice warns of a Calamity and twin dooms only you can forestall. | *The Call* · 4.4 · main | "Ahead looms a Calamity. … Twin dooms only you can forestall. Only you." | #533 |
| M533g | Thancred lies in an unresponsive slumber. | *The Call* · 4.4 · main | "Master Thancred remains in slumber. Though his vital signs appear stable, he is unresponsive." | #533 |
| M533h | Kan-E-Senna calls it a soul plucked from the flesh: Called. | *The Call* · 4.4 · main | "your soul being plucked from your flesh. ''Called''." | #533 |
| M533i | Thancred may suffer worst because Lahabrea once possessed him. | *The Call* · 4.4 · main | "a consequence of his prior possession by the Ascian Lahabrea." | #533 |
| M533j | Urianger heard the voice too, so the Scions were targeted. | *The Call* · 4.4 · main | "As we alone were afflicted at the meeting, I had my suspicions, but if the voice also spoke to Urianger, there can be little doubt: the Scions were targeted specifically." | #533 |
| M534a | Urianger heard the voice at the same moment. | *Prelude in Violet* · 4.4 · main | "adding that he heard it at what was all but certainly the selfsame moment." | #534 |
| M534b | Urianger and Y'shtola collapse. | *Prelude in Violet* · 4.4 · main | "you open your eyes to see Urianger and Y'shtola collapsed upon the ground" | #534 |
| M534c | Ga Bu is mute and lost within his own mind. | *Prelude in Violet* · 4.4 · main | "mute and oblivious to the world outside the walls of his mind." | #534 |
| M534d | Alisaie has visited Ga Bu since the Maelstrom took him in. | *Prelude in Violet* · 4.4 · main | "Ever since the Maelstrom took him in, I've tried to visit as often as I can." | #534 |
| M534e | Alisaie will seek help from Eorzea's guilds and research institutions. | *Prelude in Violet* · 4.4 · main | "I will begin by reaching out to the myriad guilds and research institutions here in Eorzea." | #534 |
| M534f | Elidibus has possessed Zenos's body. | *Prelude in Violet* · 4.4 · main | "A flashback image reveals that Elidibus has possessed Zenos's body." | #534 |
| M534g | The one wearing Zenos's face swore to use his power for the Empire's cause. | *Prelude in Violet* · 4.4 · main | "When first I took this face, I swore to use all of my knowledge, all of my power, to further the cause of the Empire." | #534 |
| M534h | Solus says he built the Empire. | *Prelude in Violet* · 4.4 · main | "That is why this empire exists-why ''I'' built it." | #534 |
| M534i | The founding father was an Ascian who made the Empire to sow chaos. | *Prelude in Violet* · 4.4 · main | "The founding father was an Ascian! And he created the Empire solely for the purpose of sowing the seeds of chaos!" | #534 |
| M534j | Solus calls Varis his dearest grandson. | *Prelude in Violet* · 4.4 · main | "Now, my dearest grandson. Let me remind you of your place, in the simplest of terms." | #534 |
| M534k | Varis calls Solus an Ascian and insists man is master of his destiny. | *Prelude in Violet* · 4.4 · main | "Mark me, Ascian. Man is the master of his own destiny!" | #534 |
| M534l | An identical Solus reappears. | *Prelude in Violet* · 4.4 · main | "An identical Solus reappears." | #534 |
| M534m | Solus reminds Varis that he is Emperor now. | *Prelude in Violet* · 4.4 · main | "Lest you forget, you are Emperor now." | #534 |
| M534n | Black Rose is an imperial gas that kills whoever breathes it. | *Prelude in Violet* · 4.4 · main | "it was called, and to breath it is to breathe your last." | #534 |
| M534o | Shadowhunter says the Ascians seek strife, not rule. | *Prelude in Violet* · 4.4 · main | "Their objective was never to rule, but to sow strife and discord." | #534 |
| M534p | Zenos urges the Emperor to unleash the army on Ala Mhigo. | *Prelude in Violet* · 4.4 · main | "and the imperial army will fall upon Ala Mhigo as a pack of bloodthirsty wolves and tear that feeble nation apart." | #534 |
| M534q | Varis shoots Solus. | *Prelude in Violet* · 4.4 · main | "Varis shoots Solus." | #534 |
| M535a | The stricken Scions' souls seem to have left their bodies. | *Soul Searching* · 4.5 · main | "it's as if the souls of their companions have taken leave of their bodies." | #535 |
| M535b | The threads of aether come to an abrupt end. | *Soul Searching* · 4.5 · main | "the threads of aether come to an abrupt end." | #535 |
| M535c | Death has not taken them to the aetherial sea. | *Soul Searching* · 4.5 · main | "Death has not taken them to the aetherial sea, yet there are no tracks left for us to follow." | #535 |
| M535d | Populares have defected to Ala Mhigo, Maxima among them. | *Soul Searching* · 4.5 · main | "Apparently, a group of Populares have defected to Ala Mhigo, and Maxima─the envoy Alphinaud left with─is one of them!" | #535 |
| M535f | Alisaie counts it progress that their souls are still out there. | *Soul Searching* · 4.5 · main | "knowing their souls are still out there is progress of a sort." | #535 |
| M535g | Lakshmi resurrected the Qalyana girl, who was already dead. | *Soul Searching* · 4.5 · main | "The Qalyana girl was already dead, body and soul, when Lakshmi effected her resurrection." | #535 |
| M536a | Lyse asks you and Alisaie to take the news to Hien. | *A Defector's Tidings* · 4.5 · main | "Would you and Alisaie head to Doma and let Lord Hien know about this?" | #536 |
| M536b | Alphinaud declined to flee with the Populares, to keep investigating the Ascians. | *A Defector's Tidings* · 4.5 · main | "Master Alphinaud, however, declined the invitation to join us, preferring to continue his investigation into the Ascian threat." | #536 |
| M536c | The Emperor's personal guard fired on Maxima's party over the Burn. | *A Defector's Tidings* · 4.5 · main | "While crossing the Burn, we were fired upon by the Emperor's personal guard, and forced to make an emergency landing." | #536 |
| M536d | Their rescuers root out Ascians but will not identify themselves. | *A Defector's Tidings* · 4.5 · main | "Beyond the fact that they root out and destroy Ascians, they were unwilling to divulge anything which might serve to identify them." | #536 |
| M536e | The imperials have brought a formidable new weapon against uprisings. | *A Defector's Tidings* · 4.5 · main | "It would seem my former comrades grew tired of putting down uprisings in the conventional manner, and chose instead to bring a formidable new weapon to bear." | #536 |
| M536f | Maxima judges the Empire is mobilizing for a large-scale war. | *A Defector's Tidings* · 4.5 · main | "It is my assessment that the Empire's forces are mobilizing for a large-scale military engagement." | #536 |
| M536g | Raubahn asks the Alliance's leaders for reinforcements. | *A Defector's Tidings* · 4.5 · main | "Dispatch messengers to the Alliance leadership requesting reinforcements." | #536 |
| M536h | Nagxia and Dalmasca have joined Hien's alliance. | *A Defector's Tidings* · 4.5 · main | "We have also been joined by our neighbors from Nagxia, and the indomitable citizens of Dalmasca..." | #536 |
| M536i | The Ironworks engineers are ready to test the generators. | *A Defector's Tidings* · 4.5 · main | "The Ironworks engineers report that they have finished calibrating the generators, and are ready to proceed to the testing stage." | #536 |
| M536j | Hien suggests naming the barrier for the Four Lords. | *A Defector's Tidings* · 4.5 · main | "A name drawn from the Four Lords of legend, perhaps─“Seiryu's Aegis” or some such." | #536 |
| M536k | The voice speaks again of untold sorrow. | *A Defector's Tidings* · 4.5 · main | "Untold sorrow... must be changed." | #536 |
| M537a | The barrier holds at five thousand yalms. | *Seiryu's Wall* · 4.5 · main | "The barrier is holding steady at five thousand yalms! We've done it!" | #537 |
| M537b | Alphinaud is not dead, only locked in slumber. | *Seiryu's Wall* · 4.5 · main | "The lad is not dead—merely locked in slumber." | #537 |
| M537c | Alphinaud cannot be woken, like the others. | *Seiryu's Wall* · 4.5 · main | "Alphinaud is lost in a sleep from which he cannot be awoken...just like the others." | #537 |
| M537d | The Black Wolf has shed his pelt and will never return to Garlemald's legions. | *Seiryu's Wall* · 4.5 · main | "The Black Wolf has shed his pelt, never to return to Garlemald or her legions." | #537 |
| M537g | A recent Black Rose directive bears Zenos's signature. | *Seiryu's Wall* · 4.5 · main | "authorized with the signature of one Zenos yae Galvus." | #537 |
| M537h | Every incubator in the plant held a young Emperor Solus. | *Seiryu's Wall* · 4.5 · main | "And what should we find in each and every incubator...but a young Emperor Solus." | #537 |
| M537i | Solus says the civil war was intended. | *Seiryu's Wall* · 4.5 · main | "Well, of course it was! Though I will admit the resulting panic exceeded even my wildest expectations." | #537 |
| M537j | Solus thanks Varis for the surplus of vessels. | *Seiryu's Wall* · 4.5 · main | "I really must thank you for this surplus of vessels." | #537 |
| M537k | Hien sends troops to bolster his allies in Eorzea. | *Seiryu's Wall* · 4.5 · main | "As promised, we wills end troops to bolster our allies in Eorzea without delay." | #537 |
| M537l | Shadowhunter answers to the Black Wolf's name, a title he has long relinquished. | *Seiryu's Wall* · 4.5 · main | "That was the title I was given. One I have long since relinquished." | #537 |
| M537m | He dragged himself from the rubble and swore vengeance on the Ascians. | *Seiryu's Wall* · 4.5 · main | "It was for them that I dragged myself free of the rubble, and swore vengeance on the Ascians." | #537 |
| M537n | He lives now only for revenge. | *Seiryu's Wall* · 4.5 · main | "I live now only to exact revenge." | #537 |
| M537o | He will continue the partnership Alphinaud began and share his intelligence. | *Seiryu's Wall* · 4.5 · main | "Then I shall continue the partnership the boy began, and share what intelligence I have acquired." | #537 |
| M537p | He thinks Elidibus the likeliest culprit. | *Seiryu's Wall* · 4.5 · main | "Elidibus seems the most likely culprit." | #537 |
| M537q | They destroyed the Black Rose stores and the plant. | *Seiryu's Wall* · 4.5 · main | "We infiltrated the production facility, and destroyed all existing stores of the chemical along with the plant itself." | #537 |
| M537r | Shadowhunter arrives carrying an unconscious Alphinaud. | *Seiryu's Wall* · 4.5 · main | "The Shadowhunter arrives, carrying an unconscious Alphinaud." | #537 |
| M538a | The Alliance has a base camp near Ala Mhigo's northeastern border. | *Parley on the Front Lines* · 4.5 · main | "The Alliance has established a base camp near Ala Mhigo’s northeastern border." | #538 |
| M538b | The Empire wants a Scion at the talks. | *Parley on the Front Lines* · 4.5 · main | "the Empire has requested that a member of the Scions be present." | #538 |
| M538c | The Empire also asks for Eorzea's champion. | *Parley on the Front Lines* · 4.5 · main | "the Empire also requested the presence of "Eorzea's champion"..." | #538 |
| M538d | Hien cannot trust a man whose son's skin an Ascian wears. | *Parley on the Front Lines* · 4.5 · main | "knowing that an Ascian walks in his son's skin, I do not see how we can trust him" | #538 |
| M538e | Varis says his grandsire, the former emperor, is an Ascian. | *Parley on the Front Lines* · 4.5 · main | "My grandsire, the former emperor, is of their number." | #538 |
| M538f | Solus decreed that eikons be eradicated. | *Parley on the Front Lines* · 4.5 · main | "Recognizing the threat eikons pose to the world, Solus zos Galvus decreed that they were to be eradicated." | #538 |
| M538g | Varis says the worlds must be rejoined for lasting peace. | *Parley on the Front Lines* · 4.5 · main | "To bring about everlasting peace, our worlds must be rejoined." | #538 |
| M538h | Doma has a concord with the nations of Eorzea. | *Parley on the Front Lines* · 4.5 · main | "Doma has entered into a concord with the nations of Eorzea." | #538 |
| M538i | Varis invites the Alliance to join a new nation. | *Parley on the Front Lines* · 4.5 · main | "I bid you join me! Not as subjects of Garlemald, but of a new nation!" | #538 |
| M538j | Varis ends the talks; their next meeting will not be at a table. | *Parley on the Front Lines* · 4.5 · main | "This discussion is at an end. I bid you make ready for our next meeting. It will not be at the negotiating table." | #538 |
| M538k | The delay lets Doma's vanguard arrive. | *Parley on the Front Lines* · 4.5 · main | "the delay has allowed the vanguard from [[Doma]] time to arrive before hostilities begin in earnest..." | #538 |
| M539a | You and Alisaie join an irregular unit. | *The Face of War* · 4.5 · main | "The two of you are to join an irregular unit and support the main host." | #539 |
| M539c | The imperials concede defeat and retreat. | *The Face of War* · 4.5 · main | "They eventually concede defeat and begin a hasty retreat." | #539 |
| M539d | The voice says the Light will expunge all life. | *The Face of War* · 4.5 · main | "The Light...will expunge all life..." | #539 |
| M539e | Alisaie lies in a slumber with no outward cause. | *The Face of War* · 4.5 · main | "She is locked in a slumber without any outward cause." | #539 |
| M539f | Five Scions are incapacitated, and Raubahn cannot spare men to seek a cure. | *The Face of War* · 4.5 · main | "Five Scions incapacitated... And with the enemy on our doorstep, I cannot spare the men to assist in the search for a cure." | #539 |
| M539h | Zenos walks onward, saluted by the imperials. | *The Face of War* · 4.5 · main | "Zenos walks onward. He is saluted by the imperials." | #539 |
| M540a | The Scions' condition has not changed. | *A Brief Reprieve* · 4.56 · main | "Sadly there has been no change in the Scions' condition." | #540 |
| M540b | Maxima shares all he knows with the Alliance. | *A Brief Reprieve* · 4.56 · main | "That is why I chose to share all that I know with the Alliance." | #540 |
| M540c | Maxima crossed the Burn in the Black Wolf's company. | *A Brief Reprieve* · 4.56 · main | "To think I walked halfway across the Burn in the presence of the Black Wolf..." | #540 |
| M540d | Maxima marvels that the Emperor claimed the Ascians founded Garlemald. | *A Brief Reprieve* · 4.56 · main | "Did His Radiance ''really'' claim that Garlemald was founded by the Ascians?" | #540 |
| M540e | Cid calls himself an imperial defector who aided another. | *A Brief Reprieve* · 4.56 · main | "I am but an imperial defector who thought to aid another imperial defector." | #540 |
| M540f | Cid pledges the Ironworks to thwarting the Empire. | *A Brief Reprieve* · 4.56 · main | "The Ironworks will spare no effort to achieve that end." | #540 |
| M540g | Raubahn summons you to Alliance Headquarters in Ala Mhigo. | *A Brief Reprieve* · 4.56 · main | "Commander Aldynn requests your presence at Alliance Headquarters in Ala Mhigo." | #540 |
| M540h | Cid offers you a ride aboard the Excelsior. | *A Brief Reprieve* · 4.56 · main | "could I tempt you to a ride aboard the ''Excelsior?''" | #540 |
| M540i | Maxima says the Ascians must be stopped to save all peoples. | *A Brief Reprieve* · 4.56 · main | "The Ascians must be stopped. To save my people, to save all peoples." | #540 |
| M540j | Cid has heard from Raubahn that Gaius still lives. | *A Brief Reprieve* · 4.56 · main | "Is it true? Gaius still lives?" | #540 |
| M540k | Cid thinks Gaius should have had the sense to stay dead after the Praetorium. | *A Brief Reprieve* · 4.56 · main | "After his humbling at the Praetorium, one would think he'd have the good sense to stay dead." | #540 |
| M541a | The Emperor has retreated to Garlemald. | *A Requiem for Heroes* · 4.56 · main | "our latest intelligence suggests the Emperor has retreated back to Garlemald." | 4.56 |
| M541b | Imperials led by a man who appears to be Zenos break through the Alliance's defenses. | *A Requiem for Heroes* · 4.56 · main | "imperial soldiers have broken through the Alliance's defenses, being led by a man who appears to be Lord Zenos." | 4.56 |
| M541c | Over the fallen stands Zenos, or the Ascian who wears his face. | *A Requiem for Heroes* · 4.56 · main | "Towering over them is Lord Zenos, or the Ascian who wears his face." | 4.56 |
| M541d | A voice tells you it has found you. | *A Requiem for Heroes* · 4.56 · main | "At last, I've found you." | 4.56 |
| M541e | The figure bids you go to the Crystal Tower, where it left something near the base. | *A Requiem for Heroes* · 4.56 · main | "Go to the Crystal Tower. I have left something for you near its base." | 4.56 |
| M541f | The figure says that, win or lose, your path leads only to oblivion. | *A Requiem for Heroes* · 4.56 · main | "Win or lose, the path you walk leads only to oblivion." | 4.56 |
| M541g | Zenos says only your death will restore the balance. | *A Requiem for Heroes* · 4.56 · main | "Equilibrium must be restored... And only your death will redress the balance." | 4.56 |
| M541h | Estinien snatched you to safety as the Ascian struck. | *A Requiem for Heroes* · 4.56 · main | "He explains that, just as the Ascian was about to strike, Estinien intervened and whisked you away to safety." | 4.56 |
| M541i | Zenos retreated soon after your rescue. | *A Requiem for Heroes* · 4.56 · main | "Though Zenos bested all before him, the battle clearly took its toll, for he retreated shortly after your rescue." | 4.56 |
| M541j | Varis left the front because of a rumor that a demon possesses his son. | *A Requiem for Heroes* · 4.56 · main | "How could I remain there while the rumor that my son is possessed by a demon spreads like a sickness here at home?" | 4.56 |
| M541k | Zenos asks Varis where his grandsire is. | *A Requiem for Heroes* · 4.56 · main | "Where is your grandsire? I would have words with him." | 4.56 |
| M541l | Tataru tells you to find the others and bring them home. | *A Requiem for Heroes* · 4.56 · main | "Go and find the others. Bring them home." | 4.56 |
| M541m | Tataru thinks the figure's may be the voice the Scions heard. | *A Requiem for Heroes* · 4.56 · main | "If his is the voice you've all been hearing, perhaps the others are with him." | 4.56 |
| M541n | Engineers stand ready to make Black Rose on Varis's order. | *A Requiem for Heroes* · 4.56 · main | "We stand ready to begin production of Black Rose, upon your order." | 4.56 |
| M541o | Tataru names the five you will need: Thancred, Y'shtola, Urianger, Alphinaud and Alisaie. | *A Requiem for Heroes* · 4.56 · main | "Thancred, Y'shtola, Urianger, Alphinaud, Alisaie... You're going to need all of them on your side to defeat him." | 4.56 |
| M541p | You find Lyse and Yugiri down and Hien unable to rise. | *A Requiem for Heroes* · 4.56 · main | "You arrive on the battlefield to find Lyse and Yugiri motionless on the ground, Lord Hien struggling and failing to get on his feet." | 4.56 |
| M541q | Your foe's blade comes down on you. | *A Requiem for Heroes* · 4.56 · main | "Your foe standing over you, the world goes dark as his blade comes crashing down on you." | 4.56 |
| M541r | You wonder whether the hooded figure was a dream. | *A Requiem for Heroes* · 4.56 · main | "Was the hooded figure a dream?" | 4.56 |
